tamiḻmaṇ patippakam

veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
2, ciṅkārāvēlar teru
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi -600 017
to.pē: 2433 9030

moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit  tamiḻ vitti ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. ati muḷikkac ceytavar paritimāl kaliñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟimaliyām maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkiḷi nili nāṭṭiya maliyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavi eḻutiyavi aṉittum meymmiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭit tam āyvu matukiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuriyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uriyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavi alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpiyāka avaṟṟi yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉi akara niral tokiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kāliyil, tamiḻ moḻiyiyum tamiḻ maṇṇiyum tamiḻ iṉattiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uriyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉi vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkarutti yāṉ urikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi tōṟṟu vippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷi amittār.
tēvanēyar paṭippukaḷ aṉittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷit tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷiyum kaṭṭurikaḷiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉivar kikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya niliyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpati nilippaṭuttum vakiyil avarkkup paṭiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumiyarum toṇṭaru māvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!	iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


patippuri
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutiyum celaviṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatit tam paṉmoḻip pulamiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ aṉittiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatit tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻarkaḷukkuk kiṭitta viraccuraṅkam. it tokuppukaḷi veḷiyiṭuvatil perumip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlatti vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

uvakkāṇ 
unta iṭam
uva eṉṟatu oru cila aṭit toliyē uḷḷa muṉṉili iṭam. im muṉmic cuṭṭu iṟṟit tamiḻakattil vaḻak kiḻantatu.  (ti.ma.1185)
uvami
uvami -upamā
uttal - poruntutal, u-ō-ōppu- oppumi.
u- uv -uva. uvattal = ottal, virumputal, makiḻtal.
uva-uvami. uva-uvaki. uvami=oppu (tivā).
makiḻccikku viruppamum viruppattiṟku maṉapporuttamuṅ kāraṇamām.
nāṭa, viḻiya, vīḻa eṉṉum uvamavurupukaḷ viruppak karuttiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. uvami eṉṉuñ col viruppap poruḷi uṇarttuvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, oppumip poruḷi iyalpākak koṇṭum uḷḷatu.
uvami- uvamam- uvamaṉ
ō.nō : parumi - parumam - parumaṉ
muḻumi - muḻumam- muḻumaṉ - muḻuvaṉ
uvami - uvami. o. nō:  orumi - orumi.
uvamittal = oppākkutal, oppākak koḷḷutal, oppiṭutal - tolkāppiyattil āḷap peṟṟa vaṭivam uvamam eṉpatē. (va.va.)
uvami eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyil upamā eṉṟu tiriyum. ati upa+mā eṉṟu pirittu irucoṟ kūṭṭākki, otta aḷavu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar. upa=uṭaṉ. mā=aḷavu. tamiḻil, uvami eṉpatu.,  upa eṉṟum mutaṉiliyum mi eṉṉum īṟuṅkoṇṭa oru collē.
irukku vētattil, upamā eṉpatu kūṭṭuc collāyiruntu muṉ colliṟ poruḷ ciṟantu, aḷattil. paṅkiṭutal, aḷittal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭa tākat terikiṉṟatu, catapata pirāmaṇam, makāpāratam mutaliya piṉṉūlkaḷil, atu tamiḻt toṭarpiṉāl oppumip poruḷpeṟṟu viṭṭatu.
oppu, oppup poruḷ eṉa irukūṟu koṇṭatu uvami eṉṉum aṇi, oppu uvamam eṉṟum oppup poruḷ poruḷ eṉṟum tamiḻṟ collappeṟum, vaṭamoḻiyār, piṟkālat til piramāṇa(m)- pirēmēya(m) eṉṉum muṟiyil, uvamat tiṟkum poruḷukkum upamāṉa- upamēya eṉṉum col liṇiyi ākkik koṇṭaṉar. itilum, māṉa eṉpatu aḷavu kuṟitta māṉam eṉṉum teṉcollē.  (va.va: 96-97)
uvamiyāku peyar
  āṭṭuk kompu (avari)
  kākkik kāl (keṇṭi)
  kiḷimūkku (māṅkāy)
  kuruvittali (matiluṟuppu)  (col.16)
uvami  teṉcollē
āṅkilak kalviyum aṟiviyal ārāycciyum nam nāṭṭil pukuntu oṉṟari nūṟṟāṇṭum, tamiḻ taṉi vaḷamoḻiyeṉak kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyār nāṭṭi oru nūṟṟāṇṭum ākiyum iṉṟum tamiḻiṉ taṉittaṉmiyip paṟṟip pala pērāciriyarum aiyuṟṟu varuvatāl ovvoru tamiḻc colliyum, vērum ākkamum kāṭṭi iṉṉiṉṉatu teṉ collē yeṉa nāṭṭa vēṇṭum nilimi nērntuḷḷatu.
kaṇṇutaṟ peruṅ kaṭavuḷuṅkaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇuṟat terintāyntip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇiṭic cila ilakkaṇavarampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇiṭip paṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō!
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar pāṭumāṟu tamiḻilakkaṇam. taṉippaṭṭatāyum piṟa moḻikaṭkillāta poruḷilakkaṇattit taṉṉakattuk koṇṭu talimi vāyntatāyum ki.mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta toṉmiyuḷḷatāyum, iruntum ataṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷil oṉṟākavum poruṭ paṭala iyaṟpeyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum uḷḷa uvami eṉṉum col vaṭa collā teṉ collā eṉṉum aiyuṟaviṟku vaṭacollē eṉṉum valippiṟkum collārāycciyum moḻiyārāycciyum mikka ikkālattu iṭantantu niṟpatu mikamika iraṅkattakka ceytiyākum.
uvamiyeṉṉuñ col oppumi yeṉṉuñ poruḷatu. ip poruḷ ataṉ muḻuvaṭivil maṭṭumiṉṟi ataṉ aṭivērāṉa ukarattilēyē karuk koṇṭuḷḷatu. ukaramākiya ōreḻut  toru moḻi oppumik karuttuk koṇṭi ruppati utti yeṉṉuñ collāl uṇaralām. uttal =  poruntutal utti = poruttam. matikkum aṟiviṟkum poruttamāṉa ceyti utti eṉappaṭum. avvuttiyi aṟiyum akakkaraṇattiyum utti yeṉpatu ākupeyar. nūliṟkum urikkum payaṉpaṭum utti kaḷāṉa neṟimuṟikaḷ muppattiraṇṭākat tokukkap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvuttiyi yukti eṉat tirittāḷvar vaṭanūlār.
iru kaṭciyar viḷiyāṭum ellā viḷiyāṭṭukaḷilum kaṭci yamittaṟku ivviruvarāyp porunti varuvati utti kaṭṭutal eṉpar. kaṭṭutal eṉṉum col vaḻakkē uttiyeṉ ṉum coṟkup poruttap poruḷuṇmiyi uṇarttap pōtiya cāṉṟākum. kaṭṭutal, poruttutal, kaḷḷutal, porun tutal,  kaḷḷa eṉpatu porunta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ōr uvama urupu. kaḷḷak kaṭuppa āṅkavi eṉāa eṉṟu tol kāppiyaṅ kūṟutal kāṇka (1232) kaḷ  eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantatē kaḷam (kūṭṭam. avi) eṉṉum tokutip peyarum. kaṭci (kaḷ+ci) eṉṉum peyarum ippakutiyatē.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akarāti viḷiyāṭṭu uttic collit teluṅkuc colleṉṟu piṟaḻakkoṇṭatōṭu, nūluttic colliṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa teṉavuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷatu. utti yeṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa untu eṉṟum col poruntutaṟ poruḷpaṭuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
tāṉantōṟum untiṭuṅ karaṇam eṉpatu civañāṉa cittiyār. (4.34)
tamiḻil ēṟattāḻa mukkāṟ pakuti ukaravaṭic coṟkaḷ.
ukaravaṭi, collākkattil
(1)	muṉṉit tiripu
(2)	aḷḷit tiripu
(3)	piṉṉit tiripu
eṉa mūvakit tiripaṭiyum, ukaram ūkāra okara ōkāraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāvatu muṉṉit tiripu:  ukaram akaramāvatu aḷḷit tiripu:  ukaram ikaramāvatu piṉṉit tiripu: muṉṉit tiripu eṉṟum mōṉiyākavēyirukkum.
e-ṭu :
	puḻi	-	pūḻi
	kuṭṭu	-	koṭṭu	muṉṉit tiripu
	muṭaṅku	-	maṭaṅku	aḷḷit tiripu
	puraḷ		-	piṟaḻ		piṉṉit tiripu
ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉiraṇṭum mōṉiyākavum toṭarum. avi aḷḷi mōṉi, piṉṉi mōṉi eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu :	kuḷiyam	-	kūḷi,koḷ	-	kōḷ	- 	muṉṉi mōṉi
	pacumi	-	pāci	-	pimi	- 	aḷḷi mōṉi
	civaṉ	-	ceyyāṉ	-	cēyōṉ	-	piṉṉimōṉi
kuḷiyam = uruṇṭi, kuḻiyam = vaḷitaṭi, kūḷi= vaḷintatāyuḷḷa vāḻi vaki, koḷ= vaḷinta kāyuḷḷa kāṇam, koṭkutal = vaḷital, cuṟṟutal, cuḻalutal, kōḷ = vaṭṭam, katiravaṉic cuṟṟum kirakam, kōḷ, kōṇ = kōṭu, kuḷ - kūḷ.
ukarac col mutalāvatu muṉmik karuttiyum, piṉpu ataṉ vaḻiyākap piṉmi, uyarcci, oppumi, ākiya karuttukaḷiyum koṇṭuḷḷatu.
oppumik karuttil ukarac col u - ō - ō eṉa mōṉit tiripaṭiyum. ottal pōlutal, oviyam = oppumi paṟṟiya varivukkali. ō + tal =  ottal. ō + iyam = ōviyam. ōkaravaṭi. pala meykaḷoṭuñ cērntu pala oppumi allatu poruntaṟ karuttuc coṟkaḷi uṇṭupaṇṇum. okkal, ōcci, oṭṭu, oṇṭu, oṇṇu, ottu, oppu, ompu, ollu, ovvu, oṟṟu. oṉṟu, oṉṉu ākiya coṟkaḷi nōkkuka.
moḻi vaḷarcciyil, coṟkaḷ palkutaṟ poruṭṭu aṟuvakic ceyyuḷ tiripukaḷum mūvakip puṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷum mukkuṟikaḷum. muccērkki kaḷum muṉṉōrāl kiyāḷap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. muccērkkiyāvaṉa. mutaṟ cērkki iṭic cērkki, kaṭic cērkki eṉpaṉa.
oppumik karuttuḷḷa ukarac col kaṭic cērkkiyāka lakaramey peṟṟup palvēṟu tiripaṭintu paṟpala coṟkaḷip piṟappikkum.
ul - uḷ - uṭu - uṭaṉ
uṭu - oṭu - ōṭu
ōṭutal = ottal, poruttutal, kūṭutal, ōṭa eṉpatu ōr uvama urupu.
ōṭap puriya eṉṟavi eṉāa  eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka. (1232)
ōṭāvi = ōviyaṉ. ōṭu - ōṭṭam - āṭṭam  = ōppu
kuraṅkāṭṭam ōṭukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
oṭṭi - uṭaṉ, otta.
ul - ur - uṟu- uṟaḻ
ur - uri - uriñ - uriñcu
ur - ur- uri- urāy.
ul  aṭiyiṉ aḷḷit tiripu

aḷ - aṇ - aṇavu, aṇi
aḷ - aṭṭu - accu (otta poruḷamikkuṅ karuvi)
aṇ - aṇṭu
 attu, am mutaliya coṟkaḷum aḷ aṭiyiṉiṉṟu amintaviyē, attutal - oṭṭutal. iṇittal. am- amartal - ottal. poruntutal.
uḷ aṭiyiṉ piṉṉit tiripu
uḷ - iḷ, iṇ - iṇar, iṇaṅku, iṇi
iḷ - (iy) - iyi - ici
iḷ - iḻi
iy - ey - ēy.
ḷakara meyyīṟu pala coṟkaḷil yakaramey yīṟākat tiriyum.
e-ṭu : toḷ- toy. poḷ - poy. vaḷ - vay - vi = kūrmi
iḻital = urāytal, uḷam poruntutal, oṉṟip paḻakutal
eytal = ottal tēṉey mārpakam (cīvaka 2382)
ukarac col, muṉmik karuttilum uyarccik karuttilum vakarameyc cērkki peṟuvatu pōṉṟē, oppumik karuttilum peṟum.
uv, uvakkāṇ, uvi - muṉmiccuṭṭu.
uvvi (tali) uva, uvaṇ, uvaṇi - uyarccik karuttu
(uv) - uva - (uvar) - ivar - oppumik karuttu, ivartal = ottal.
uvami eṉṉuñ col oppumik karuttuḷḷa uva eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu piṟantatākum, uvattal eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarkku virumputal, piriyamātal, makiḻtal eṉṉum poruḷ kaḷum uvaki eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarkku aṉpu, kāmam, iṉpac cuvi (ciruṅkāra racam) makiḻcci eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum, uvappu eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarkku viruppam, makiḻcci, polivu, eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum, palkalikkaḻakam akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṉam ottirāviṭṭāl, viruppamum aṉpum kāmamum iṉpamum makiḻcciyum eṅṅaṉam nikaḻum? ivaṟṟiṟ kellām aṭippaṭi uḷam ottal aṉṟō!
uvaki  uvappu mutaliya coṟkaḷ pōṉṟē uvami eṉpatum toḻiṟ peyarākum, uva  eṉṉum pakuti tal, ki, pu, u, mi, mam, māṉam, eṉṉum pala vikutikaḷi yēṟṟu muṟiyē uvattal, uvaki, uvappu, uvavu, uvami, uvamam, uvamāṉam, eṉṉum pala toḻiṟ peyarkaḷit tōṟṟuvikkum, ivaṟṟuḷ mutal nāṉkum, uvamip poruḷ iḻantuviṭṭaṉa,  allatu avaṟṟi apporuḷil āḷum ilakkiyap pakutikaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
mam oru toḻiṟ peyarīṟu eṉpatu,  urumam, parumam, mutaliya coṟkaḷāl uṇaralām. uruttal, veppamātal, urumam, veppamāṉa naṇpakal.
mam īṟu mi  yīṟṟut tiripeṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu.
o.nō : ceyyāmi - ceyyāmal.
ceyyāmi pōṉāṉ. ceyyāmē pōṉāṉ, ceyyāmaṟ pōṉāṉ, eṉṉum mut toṭarum oru poruḷaṉavātal kāṇka.
maṉṉutiru vaṇṇā maliccampan tāṇṭāṟkup
paṉṉutalic cavaram paṇṇuvatēṉ - miṉṉiṉ
iḷittaviṭi mātar ivaṉkuṭumi paṟṟi
vaḷittiḻuttuk kuṭṭā malukku
eṉṉum kāḷamēkar pāṭalil, kuṭṭāmal eṉpatu kuṭṭā malukku eṉṟu urupēṟṟu varuvatāl ceyyāmal eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu etirmaṟi viṉiyecca vaṭivam, viṉi eccamāy āḷappaṭum. ceyyāmi eṉṉum etirmaṟit toḻiṟ peyariṉ tiripāyirukkalām eṉṟu uyttuṇarap peṟum
aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷiṉ makaraviṟuti ṉakaraviṟutiyāvatu perumpāṉmi yātalāl uvamam eṉṉum peyar uvamaṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
ō.nō : 	parumam	-	parumaṉ	toḻiṟ peyar
		taṭumam	-	taṭumaṉ
		tiṟam	-	tiṟaṉ
		kaṭam	-	kaṭaṉ		piṟa peyar
viḻumam eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyar viḻu + mam eṉṟum, viḻumu + am eṉṟum pirittaṟku iṭantarum, āyiṉ urumam, parumam eṉpaṉa iṭantarā, viḻuttal = ciṟattal, viḻumutal = ciṟattal.
māṉam eṉṉum col toḻiṟ peyarīṟāy varuvati, aṭimāṉam, kaṭṭumāṉam tīrmāṉam, paṭimāṉam, piṭimāṉam, peṟumāṉam, varumāṉam mutaliya toḻiṟ peyarkaḷiṉṟu aṟika.
uvami eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu uvamittal eṉṉum viṉi piṟakkum. orumi eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṉu orumi-ttal eṉṉum viṉi piṟantiruttal kāṇka, uvamittal, oṉṟaṉi oṉṟiṟku oppākak kūṟutal.
iṅṅaṉamē, uvamāṉam eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu uvamāṉi - ttal eṉṉum viṉi piṟakkum. tīrmāṉam eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu tīrmāṉi-ttal eṉṉum viṉi piṟantiruttal kāṇka. tīrmāṉam, muṭivu tīrmāṉittal muṭipu ceytal, uvamāṉittal, uvamittal.
uvappu eṉṉum col muṟkūṟiyavāṟu uyarccip poruḷiyuntarum.
uva - uvar - ivar. ivartal = uyartal, ēṟutal
uva - uvappu = uyaram, uva-uva-ukappu = uyarvu
uva - uvaṇ = mēliṭam uvaṇ - uvaṇi = tēvarulakam.
uyarntataṉ mēṟṟē uḷḷuṅkāli
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl (1224) uyarnta poruḷi oppākak koḷvatē uvamiyiṉ aṭippaṭi neṟimuṟi eṉṟu koṇṭu uvamic colliṟku uyarvup poruḷ koḷvatu poruntātu. tolkāppiyam uyarntatu eṉṟu kuṟittatu. ciṟantatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.  ciṟappu, uyarvu ciṟappu, iḻivu ciṟappu eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. uyarnta poruḷukku mikavuyarnta poruḷi yum iḻinta poruḷiyum uvamaṅkūṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatē tolkāppiyar karuttu.
e-ṭu:	ūraṅ kaṇanīr uravunīr cērntakkāl
		pērum piṟitākit tīrttamām
kaṅkiyāṟṟilum cākkaṭi kalantu kaṅki nīrām eṉṉum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvamiyil mēlōrkkuk kaṅkiyum kīḻōrkkuc cākkaṭiyum uvama mātal kāṇka. vēṟṟumi kuṟikkum col vēṟṟumi eṉṟē peyar peṟṟāṟ pōl oppumipaṟṟiya aṇiyum oppumi kuṟikkum collālēyē aḻikkap peṟutalē muṟimiyām.
vaṭamoḻiyil upamā upamāṉa eṉṉum iru vaṭivukaḷē uḷa. ivi muṟiyē, uvami, uvamāṉam, eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripukaḷ, uvamam, uvamaṉ, uvami, uvamāṉi eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷum coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyililli. aṟiyuṅkaruvi allatu aḷavip peyar āṉa eṉṟu iṟiṉ. ataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum poruḷiṉ peyar ēya eṉṟu iṟuvatu vaṭamoḻi marapu.
e-ṭu : 	ñāṉa(m)		-	ñēya(m)
	aṉumāṉa(m)	-	aṉumēya(m)
	piramāṇa(m)	-	piramēya(m)
immuṟippaṭi upamāṉattiṉāl aṟiyappaṭuvatu upamēya(m) eṉṟāyiṟṟu. itu piṟkāla  vaḷarcci māṉam eṉpatu taṉiniliyil aḷavu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tūya teṉcol. paṭiyi iṉṟum māṉam eṉpar vaṭārkkāṭṭār.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl uvami teṉ collē yeṉṟum atu uva eṉṉum mutaṉiliyaṭip piṟanta toḻiṟ peyareṉ ṟum ataṉ aṭivēr ukarac col eṉṟum ataṉ kaṇṇum ottaṟ karuttu koṇṭuḷḷateṉṟum ataṉāl atu ataṉiṉṟu mōṉi aḷḷi piṉṉit tiripākat tōṉṟiyuḷḷa ottaṟ karuttuc coṟkaḷiyellām tāṅkiniṟkum taṉipperum tūṇ eṉṟum aṇi kaṭkellān tāyāṉa uvami yilakkaṇat tiyē aṇiyilakkaṇamākavuṅ kūṟum tolkāppiya uvama iyal vaṭamoḻiyil piṟkālattu virivākat tōṉṟiya aṇi nūlkaṭ kellām mūlameṉṟum varalāṟṟu aṭippaṭiyil collārācciyum moḻiyārāycciyum ceytal vēṇṭu meṉṟum ārāycciyiṉṟik kuṟaṭum pētiyum pōlak koṇṭatu viṭāmi āciriyark kaḻakaṉ ṟeṉṟum aṟintu koḷka.
uvami teṉ collē eṉpataṟku vēṟu cila cāṉṟukaḷumuḷa.
tamiḻil  uvami eṉṉum collil uva eṉpatu viṉip pakuti; mi eṉpatu vikuti; ākavē, uva  eṉpatē uyirnāṭi yāṉa uṟuppu.
mi vikuti, vantami, varukiṉṟami eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil toḻiṟpeyar vikutiyākavum, ciṟumi, poṟumi eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil toḻiṟ paṇpup peyar vikutiyākavum, naṉmi, tīmi eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil paṇpup peyar vikutiyākavum irukkum.
vaṭamoḻiyil upamā eṉṉum collil  upa eṉpatu iṭiccollāṉa muṉṉoṭṭu (upacarkkam. Prefix) eṉṟum mā eṉpatu aḷavu kuṟitta colleṉṟum koṇṭu upamā eṉpataṟku otta aḷavu oṉṟiṉik koṇṭu, oṉṟi aḷattal, oppu nōkku, oppumi, uvami eṉa muṟiyē poruḷ kūṟappaṭum. ākavē, mā  eṉpatē ciṟanta uṟuppām. itaṉāl tamiḻil nilimoḻi allatu piṉ moḻip poruḷ ciṟantum vaṭamoḻiyil varumoḻi allatu muṉmoḻip poruḷ ciṟantum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum teḷivām.
uvamāṉam eṉṉum tamiḻc collil māṉam  eṉpatu aṭimāṉam (aṭivu) paṭimāṉam (paṭivu) eṉpavaṟṟiṟpōl oru vikuti. upamāṉa eṉṉum vaṭacollil, māṉa eṉpatu aḷavu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuttappaṭuñ col.
iṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭṭiruppatāl, tamiḻ uvami  vēṟu vaṭa moḻi. upamā  vēṟu eṉṟu karutaṟka. iraṇṭum oṉṟē vaṭa moḻiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭacollēyeṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟku, atil uḷḷa teṉ coṟkaḷellām poruntap poyttalākavum poruntāp poyttalākavum poruḷ kūṟuvatum, atuvum iyalākkāl iṭukuṟi (ruṭam) eṉṟu muttiriyiṭṭu viṭuvatum, vaṭanūlār vaḻakkam. veḷḷiyāṉa ciṟu tavaca vakiyoṉṟik kuṟikkum cāmi eṉṉum collic ciyāmā eṉṟu tirittuk karuppāṉatu eṉṟum;  naṉṟāy eṇṇeyil ventapiṉ tiṉṉum vaṭi vaki yoṉṟik kuṟikkum āmivaṭi eṉṉum colli āmavaṭa eṉat tirittu naṉṟāka vēkātatu  eṉṟum; mukam eṉpataṉ kaṭip pōliyāṉa mukaṉ eṉṉum colli mu + kaṉ  eṉap pirittu. tōṇṭap peṟṟa kiṭaṅku pōṉṟa vāyiyuṭiya uṟuppu eṉṟum, vaṭamuṉiyāy tōṉṟum oruvaki neruppik kuṟikkum vaṭavi eṉṉum collip paṭapā eṉṟu tirittu, ataṉoṭu mukam eṉpatic cērttup paṭapāmukam eṉa ākki, peṭṭik kutiri mukam pōṉṟatu eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟuvār vēṟu eṉtāṉ collār!
tolkāppiyattilum toṉṉūlkaḷilum uvami aṇiyilak kaṇa muṟiyiṟ kūṟappaṭāmal poruḷilakkaṇa muṟiyilēyē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tōḻi, talivaṉ taliviyoṭu, ciṟappākat talivaṉoṭu uriyāṭum pōtu uḷḷuṟiyuvamam ēṉi yuvamam eṉṉum iruvaki yuvamikaḷiyum āḷuvatu marapu. ivaṟṟi viḷakka eḻuntatē uvamaviyal, piṟkālattil vaṭa nūlār aṇiyilakkaṇam vakuttapōtu tamiḻilakkaṇa uvama viyalip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭatuṭaṉ uvami eṉṉum peyariyum upamā eṉat tirittuk koṇṭaṉar. tolkāp piyam pāṇiṉīyattiṟku muntiyateṉṉum uṇmi oppuk koḷḷappaṭāviṭiṉum, tolkāppiyam kaṭikkaḻakat toṭakkat teḻunta vaḻinūlātalāl ataṟku muntiya akattiyam mutaliya nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaṭku mutaṉūl eṉpati evarum maṟukka muṭiyātu. mēlum aintiram pāṇi ṉīyam mutaliya vaṭamoḻi viyākaraṇaṅkaḷ ellām naṉṉūl pōl eḻuttum collum ākiya iraṇṭē kūṟuvaṉaveṉ ṟum tolkāppiyam mutaliya toṉṉūlkaḷ yāvum eḻuttuc coṟ poruḷ mūṉṟiyuṅ kūṟum piṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟum vēṟu pāṭaṟital vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil, yāppilakkaṇam cantacu allatu cantōpiciti eṉṉum nūlkaḷilum, aṇiyilakkaṇam alaṅkāram eṉṉum nūlkaḷilum vēṟākak kūṟappaṭum. tamiḻilō, yāppum aṇiyum poruḷilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ, avi taṉinūṟkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭaviṭattum, poruḷ ilak kaṇak kūṟukaḷākavē eṉṟuṅ koḷḷappeṟum. uvami aṇi kaṭkellām tāyātalāl, aṇiyilakkaṇam uvamaviyalil aṭakkamām.
tolkāppiya uvamaviyalil nālvaki ēṉiyuvamaṅkaḷuṭaṉ aivaki uḷḷuṟi uvamaṅkaḷum kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa; uvami uṟuppukaḷ māṇavarkku eḷitāy viḷaṅkuvaṉa vātalāl kūṟap peṟavilli. āyiṉ, eccaviyalil uvamattoki kūṟappaṭ ṭiruppatāl ataṉ maṟutaliyākiya uvama viriyum kūṟāmal kūṟappaṭṭatākak koḷḷappeṟum.
uvamam, uvamavurupu, potuttaṉmi, poruḷ eṉṉum nāṉkum uvami uṟuppukaḷām. innāṉkum amivatu viriyuvami: uvama urupu allatu uvamavurupum potut taṉmiyum kuṟivatu tokiyuvami. vaṭamoḻiyil in nāṉkiyum muṟiyē, upamāṉa, upama vācaka, cātāraṇa tarma, upamēya eṉṟum: viriyuvamattip pūrṇōpamā eṉṟum; tokiyuva matti luptōpamā eṉṟum aḻippar. ivi yellām piṟkālattu vaḷarcciyum moḻipeyarppumākum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl uvami teṉ collē eṉṟum uvamaviyal teṉmoḻi yilakkaṇak kūṟē eṉṟum tērntu teḷika.
uḻavuṅ kittoḻilum
uḻavaṉ uyarvu:  uṇaviṉṟi uyir vāḻkkiyiṉmiyiṉ, av vuṇavi viḷikkum uḻavar maṉṉuyirt tērukku accāṇi yākavum, maṉpati marattiṟku āṇi vērākavum karutappaṭ ṭaṉar.
uḻuvār ulakattiṟ kāṇiyaḵ tāṟṟā
teḻuvāri yellām poṟuttu
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
ciṟappāka irappārkkoṉṟīpavarum viruntiṉarip pēṇupa varum uḻavarēyāvar.
iravā rirappārkkoṉ ṟīvar karavātu
kiceytūṇ māli yavar
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvarum, irappōr cuṟṟam eṉṟu iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷum,
vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpāṉ viruntirukka vuṇṇātāṉ eṉṟu nallātaṉārum,
ennāḷum - kāppārē vēḷāḷar kāṇ  eṉṟu kamparum kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
viruntōmpalum irappōrk kītalumākiya vēḷāṇmi ceyvatiṉā lēyē, uḻavar vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. vēḷāṇmiyāvatu piṟari virumpip pēṇu taliyāḷun taṉmi. vēḷ- viruppam.
uḻavar amitik kālattil uḻavut toḻilic ceytu vantatōṭu, pōrkkālattil pōrppaṇiyum purintu vantaṉar.
vēntuviṭu toḻiliṉ paṭiyuṅ kaṇṇiyum
vāyntaṉar eṉpa avar peṟum poruḷē
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅkūṟutal kāṇka (1582).
antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉṟum nāṟ peruṅkulat tāruḷ, vēḷāḷar ēṉi mukkulattillaṟattāriyum tāṅki vantatiṉāl, vēḷāḷarē ciṟanta illaṟattārākak karutappaṭ ṭaṉar. marutanilattūril niliyāka vacittu āṟiloru kaṭamiyi aracaṉukku oḻuṅkāka iṟuttuvantavarum vēḷāḷarē.
vaṇṇāṉ, mayirviṉiñaṉ, cemmāṉ, kuyavaṉ, kottaṉ, kollaṉ, kaṉṉāṉ, taṭṭāṉ, taccaṉ, kaṟṟaccaṉ, cekkāṉ, kikkōḷaṉ, pūkkāraṉ, kiṇiyaṉ (kiṇip paṟiyaṉ), pāṇaṉ, kūttaṉ, vaḷḷuvaṉ, maruttuvaṉ ākiya patiṉeṇ toḻilārum;  uḻavaṉukkup pakkat tuṇiyāyiruntu tattam toḻilic ceytu avaṉiṭam kūli allatu tām ceyta poruṭku vili peṟṟuvantaṉar. itaṉāl, avar patiṉeṇ kuṭimakkaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭṭu vēḷāḷaruḷ aṭakkap paṭṭaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam palvakuppāriyum uṇavaḷittuk kāttatiṉālēyē, vēḷāṇ viṉiyit tirukki vaḻakkam eṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟiṉar kampar. teyvattiṟkup paṭitta tiruccōṟṟip palarkkum vaḻaṅkuvatu tirukki vaḻakkam eṉappaṭum.
teṉpulattār teyvam viruntokkal tāṉeṉṟāṅ
kimpulattā ṟōmpal tali
eṉṟu ilvāḻkki yatikārattum (43)
iruntōmpi yilvāḻva tellām viruntōmpi
vēḷāṇmi ceytaṟ poruṭṭu
ilvāḻvā ṉeṉpāṉ iyalpuṭiya mūvarkkum
nallāṟṟi ṉiṉṟa tuṇi
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil (41), iyalpuṭiya mūvar eṉpatu atikāra viyi pāl antaṇar aracar vaṇikar eṉṉum mukkulattillaṟat tāriyē kuṟikkum, piramacāriyaṉ vāṉap pirattaṉ canniyāci eṉṟu parimēlaḻakar kūṟiyiruppatu poruntātu. tuṟantārkkum tuvvāta varkkum  eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiluḷḷa tuṟantār  eṉṉuñ collē tuṟaviyarik kuṟikkum.
vittu miṭal vēṇṭuṅ kollō viruntōmpi
miccil micivāṉ pulam
eṉṟu viruntōmpalatikārattum (81,85)
uḻutuṇṭu vāḻvārē vāḻvārmaṟ ṟellān
toḻutuṇṭu piṉcel pavar
eṉṟu uḻavatikārattum (1033), vaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruppatāl, uḻavaṉē talimik kuṭivāṇaṉ (Chief Citizen) eṉṟu avar koṇṭami pulaṉākum.
makkaḷ vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta ūṇuṭik karuvip poruḷkaḷum, araciyaṟkiṉṟiyamiyāta iṟiyum, pōrukku nērvakiyum nēralvakiyumāṉa paṇiyum uḻavarāl amivati nōkkum pōtu.
palakuṭi nīḻalun taṅkuṭikkīḻk kāṇpar
alakuṭi nīḻa lavar
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvarum (1034),
porupaṭi taruuṅ koṟṟamum uḻupaṭi
yūṉṟucāl maruṅki ṉīṉṟataṉ payaṉē
..........................................................................................
pakaṭupuṟan tarunar pāra mōmpik
kuṭipuṟan tarukuvi yāyiṉiṉ
aṭipuṟan tarukuvar aṭaṅkā tōrē
eṉṟu veḷḷikkuṭinākaṉārum (puṟam. 35),
purappōr koṟṟamum - uḻaviṭi viḷippōr
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum (cilap.10: 149 - 50), kūṟiyiruppatu oru ciṟitum mikiyākātu.
iruvaki vēḷāḷar :  vēḷāḷar, uḻutuṇpārum uḻuvittuṇ pārum eṉa iruvakiyar, uḻutuṇpārukkuk karuṅkaḷamar kārāḷar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, uḻuvit tuṇpārukku veṇ kaḷamar veḷḷāḷar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, uriyaṉa. uḻavar, kaḷamar, kaṭiyar, vēḷāḷar eṉpaṉa irucārārukkum potu vākum.  āyiṉum, īṟṟup peyar tavira ēṉiyavellām uḻu tuṇpārkkē ciṟappāka vaḻaṅkiṉa. avarukku maḷḷar eṉṉum peyarumuṇṭu. avar tannilattil uḻuvārum piṟar nilattil uḻuvārum eṉa iru nilimiyar.
uḻuvit tuṇpār palar vēḷeṉavum aracu eṉavum paṭṭa meyti, amiccarum paṭittalivarum maṇṭalat talivarum ciṟṟaracarumāki, mūvēntarkkum makaṭkoṭi nērum takutiyarāyiruntaṉar, kaṭiyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷuṭ  perumpālār vēḷirē.
nilavaki :  nilaṅkaḷ iṉṟiruppatu pōṉṟē, naṉcey puṉcey vāṉāvari (vāṉaṅkāṇi) eṉa mūvakip paṭṭiruntaṉa, uḻutu payiriṭappaṭuvatu uḻavukkāṭu eṉṟum, kottip payiriṭap paṭuvatu kottukkāṭu eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa.
ceyaṟki nīrvaḷam : uḻavut toḻiṟku iyaṟki nīrvaḷam pōtāviṭattu, aracarāl ceyaṟki nīrvaḷam amikkap paṭṭatu, veḷḷaccētam nērāvāṟum, pāyccalukku vēṇṭiya nīr ōṭumāṟum, āṟṟiṟkuk kari kaṭṭalum; nīrit tēkka vēṇṭu miṭattil āṟṟiṟkuk kuṟukkē aṇi kaṭṭalum;  pērāṟṟi ṉiṉṟu kaṇṇāṟum, kaṇṇāṟṟiṉiṉṟu kālvāyum, kālvāyi ṉiṉṟu vāykkālum veṭṭalum;  ivi iyalāviṭattu, ēri kuḷam toṭutalum;  akkālattaracar mēṟkoṇṭa ceyal kaḷām. kaṇṇāṟuṅ kālvāyum perumpālum cōḻanāṭ ṭilum, ēri kuḷam perumpālum pāṇṭi nāṭṭilum veṭṭap paṭṭaṉa.
karikālvaḷavaṉ īḻattiṉiṉṟu paṉṉīrāyiram makkaḷic ciṟi piṭittuk koṇarntu kāvirikkuk karikaṭṭuvittāṉ. irācēn tiraccōḻaṉ cōḻakaṅkam eṉṉum ēriyi veṭṭiṉāṉ. muṭikoṇṭāṉ āṟum avaṉāl veṭṭappaṭṭatu pōlum!  kāvirik kallaṇi ki.pi. 1068il vīrarācēntiraṉāl veṭṭappaṭṭa tākat terikiṉṟatu.
cōḻanāṭṭil, iṉṉiṉṉa kaṇṇuṟu iṉṉiṉṉa vaḷanāṭṭiṟ kum, iṉṉiṉṉa kālvāy iṉṉiṉṉavūrukkum, iṉṉiṉṉa vāykkāl iṉṉiṉṉa pāṭakavaricikkum, pāyavēṇṭumeṉ ṉum ēṟpāṭiruntatu. ataṉāl, vēliyāyiram viḷiyavum, orupiṭipaṭiyuñ cīṟiṭam eḻukaḷiṟupurakkavum, irupūvum muppūvum eṭukkappaṭavum, iyalvatāyiṟṟu, pūveṉpatu veḷḷāṇmi.
aracariṉ ūkkuvippu :  nilam cariyāy viḷiyātaviṭattum, kaṭuntaṇṭalāḷarip paṟṟi muṟiyiṭṭa viṭattum, aracar varinīkkañ ceytaṉar. ayal nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa ariya viḷi poruṭkaḷik koṇṭu vantum, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ payiriṭac ceytaṉar.

tarumpeṟa lamiḻta maṉṉa
karumpivaṭ ṭantōṉ perumpiṟaṅ kaṭiyē
eṉṟu atiyamāṉmakaṉ pokuṭṭeḻiṉiyi auviyār pāṭiyiruppatāl (puṟam. 392), avaṉ muṉṉōruḷ oruvaṉ cīṉattiliruntō cāvakattiliruntō, karumpit tamiḻ nāṭ ṭiṟkuk koṇṭu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu. *karumpu kīḻnāṭṭil mutalāvatu cīṉattilum piṉpu cāvakattilum payirāyatu.
cīṉari vāṉavar eṉṟum cīṉatti vāṉavar nāṭu eṉṟum aḻittaṉar muṉṉōr. iṉi, cāvakam nākanāṭṭic cērnta tātalāṉum, tēvarulakiṟku nākanāṭu eṉṉum peyaruṇmi yāṉum, cāvakanāṭṭu nākapurattaracar intiraṉ eṉṟa kuṭippeyar koṇṭiruntamiyālum, cāvakatti intira nāṭu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar muṉṉōr. marutanilat teyvamākiya vēntaṉ eṉṉum intiraṉukkup pukārc cōḻar viḻāk koṇṭāṭiyatum, avaṉ uḻavut toḻiṟku vēṇṭum maḻivaḷantaruvaṉ eṉṉum kuṟikkōḷ paṟṟiyatē pōlum!

puyavaliyi nīyuruva nōkkiyā   (kamparā. kulamuṟi. 26)
itukāṟum kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷellām, tuḷittal, tuḷik kuṅ karuvi, tuḷi, tuḷikkuṭ cellal, tuḷi yiṉiṉṟu varutal, tuḷiyūṭuruvutal ākiya peruṅkaruttuk kaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ kiḷippukkaḷiyumē koṇṭu orē toṭarpuṟ ṟiruttal kāṇka.
tuḷiyeṉiṉum paḷḷameṉiṉum ōkkum.

uḷ - uṣ (i.vē.)
uṣ- uṣṇa.
uḷ-ur-uru-urum-urumpu-uruppu- uruppam= veppam.
urum-urumi. urum- urumu- urumam= veppam mikka uccivēḷi.  (va.va: 91)
uḷḷīṭṭu vaki  
cāṟu nīrpōliruppatu; cōṟu kaṭṭic cōṟupōliruppatu; (kattari muruṅki kaṟṟāḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ uḷḷīṭu cōṟu eṉappaṭum)  cati vāḻi mā mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ uḷḷīṭu;  cuḷi cīttā palā mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ uḷḷīṭu; arici nel kampu mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ uḷḷīṭu;  paruppu avari tuvari mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ  uḷḷīṭu.  (col : 69)
uṟkam
uṟkam - ulka (i.vē.)
ul- ular. ul-cul- culli= aṭuppu.
ul- uḷ-uṇ-uṇa. uḷ-ur- urum- urumpu = kotippu,
ul-uṟku- uṟkam=1. aṉaṟṟiraḷ, 2. kaṭikkoḷḷi,
3. viṇvīḻkoḷḷi.
uṟkutal = erikoḷḷi vīḻtal.
ticiyiru nāṉkum uṟkam uṟkavum (puṟam. 41: 4). vaṭa moḻiyil uṣ eṉpatiyē mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar.  (va.va.96)
uṟaṅkum nilikaḷ
paṭuttal kiṭappu niliyaṭital;  cāytal kāl nīṭṭiyiruntu koṇṭu taliyic cāyttal;  kiṭattal uṟaṅkātu paṭukki niliyiliruttal;  kaṇvaḷartal kaṇṇi mūṭutal; tuñcutal kaṇṇi mūṭittūṅkutal; tūṅkutal toṭṭililāvatu tūṅku kaṭṭililāvatu kaṇpaṭi ceytal. uṟaṅkutal oṭukka nili yaṭintu ayarntu tūṅkutal.  (col : 56)
uṟuppu vaki
ciṉi uyirpporuḷiṉ uṟuppu; uṟuppu uyirp poruḷ uyirillāp poruḷ ākiya iraṇṭiṟkum potuvāṉa uṟuppu.  (col : 52)
uṟikkālam (Bronze Age) 
(tōrā. ki.mu. 15, 000- 10,000).
uṟi yeṉpatu veṇkalam. itu muṟiyeṉavum paṭum.
kañcam uṟiveṇ kalamā kummē.   (piṅ. 6: 140)
veṇkalap peyarum viḻumamum perumiyum
...............................................................................................................
ōriṭic collum vāḻnāḷum uṟiyeṉal  (piṅ 10 :  184).
eṭṭup paṅku cempum oru paṅku takaramuṅ kalanta kalappu māḻiyē uṟi. itu cempu pōlat taṉimāḻi yaṉmi yālum, veṇṇiṟak kalavaṭivilēyē makkaḷ itik kaṇṭa miyālum, karumiya (kāriya) vākupeyarāka veṇkalam eṉṉum peyar peṟṟatu.
cempu, kumpā, kiṇṇam, kuṭam, vaṭṭil mutaliya kalaṅkaḷum;  māṭaviḷakku, kuttuviḷakku, pāvi viḷakku mutaliya tiriviḷakku vakikaḷum;  vāḷ kaṟaṇṭi mutaliya karuvikaḷum;  uṟiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
veṇkala ēṉattil vārtta allatu vitta nīruṅkaṭṭiyumāṉa uṇavup poruṭkaḷ, ciṟappākap puḷippup paṇṭaṅkaḷ, kittuṅ keṭṭum pōkāvātalāl, poṉṉum veḷḷiyum kiṭiyāta ēḻimāntarkkum iṭittiṟa vakuppārkkum veṇkala ēṉamē ciṟanta māḻikkalamāka iruntu vantatu. ceppukkuṭam eṉṉum nīrkkalam cempiṉāṟ peyar peṟiṉum, veṇkalattiṉālēyē iṉṟuñ ceyyappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.
veṇkala vōci muḻaṅkuvatālum mika nīṇṭu niṟpatālum, kittāḷam, vaṇṭittāḷam, kimmaṇi, nāḻimaṇi, āṉmaṇi, kutirimaṇi, yāṉimaṇi, tērmaṇi, kōpuramaṇi, cēmakkalam, palakimaṇi, mutaliya icikkaruvikaṭku uṟiyiyē mutaṟ karuviyākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. iṭikkural muracam iḻumeṉ pāṇṭil  (cilap. 26: 194) itiṟ pāṇṭil eṉṟatu vaṇṭit tāḷatti.
uvaccarum piṟa pūcakarum pūciyiṟ payaṉpaṭuttuvatu kimmaṇi.
kimmaṇic cīraṉṟic cīraṟi yākkampa nāṭaṉ coṉṉa
mummaṇik kōvi mutaṟcīr piḻi.....

tātuṇ paṟavi pētuṟa lañci
maṇinā ārtta māṇviṉit tēraṉ
eṉpatil (akam. 4:10-12) kuṟittatu tērmaṇi.
yāṉiyi maṇikaṭṭātu teruvaḻic cella viṭuvatilli. ataṉāl, yāṉivarum piṉṉē, maṇiyōci varummuṉṉē eṉṟa paḻamoḻi yeḻuntatu.
maṇiyilāk kuñcaram vēntūrta liṉṉā eṉṟār kapilar. (iṉṉā. 14)
vaḻakkiḻantō peruntīṅku ceyyappaṭṭō aracaṉiṭattil muṟiyiṭu vōr acikkumāṟu, araṇmaṉi vāyiliṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa ārāycci maṇi veṇkala maṇiyē.
vāyiṟ kaṭimaṇi naṭunā naṭuṅka
āviṉ kaṭimaṇi yukunīr neñcucuṭat tāṉṟaṉ
arumpeṟaṟ putalvaṉi yāḻiyiṉ maṭittōṉ   (cilap. 20:53-5)
āṭuṅ kaṭimaṇi nāvaci yāmal akilameṅkum
nīṭuṅ kuṭiyit tarittapi rāṉinta nīṇilattil
pāṭum pulavar pukaḻoṭṭak kūttaṉ patāmpuyattic 
cūṭuṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉeṉ ṟēyeṉic colluvarē.
eṉpatu, oṭṭakkūttarum iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻa ṉum muṉṉīraṭiyum piṉṉīraṭiyumākap pāṭiya taṉippāṭal.
potumakkaḷ nēramaṟiyumāṟu, nāḻiki tōṟum aṭikkak kōpurattiṟ kaṭṭiya perumaṇi kōpura maṇiyām.
uṟutiyum pārviyumuḷḷatum, pōtiya aḷavu kiṭikkak kūṭiyatum, perumpāluṅ kaḷavuṅ koḷḷiyum pōkātatum, āṉa māḻi uṟiyē yātalāl, atilēyē ciṟiyavum periyavu māṉa teyvap paṭimikaḷum makkaṭpaṭimikaḷum vārkkap paṭṭaṉa.
uṟik kālattil, neytal nila makkaḷ kappal nāvāy mutaliya peruṅ kalaṅkaḷiṟceṉṟu, akkari nāṭukaḷilum avaṟṟi yaṭutta tīvukaḷilumuḷḷa palvaki arumpaṇṭaṅ kaḷik koṇṭu vantu nīrvāṇikattip perukkiṉar.
āṭcit tuṟiyil, kōviṉum periya vēntaṉ tōṉṟi, aintiṇi yaracariyum aṭakki yāṇṭāṉ. taṉakkoru taṉic ciṟappu vēṇṭi, muṭiyaṇiyum urimiyit taṉakkē koṇṭāṉ. itaṉālēyē, avaṉ vēntaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
mē=mēl, mē-mēy, mēytal= vilaṅku pulliṉ mēṟpakutiyit tiṉṉutal, kūriyiṉmēl vikkōliṭutal.
mēy-vēy. vēytal = kūriyiṉmēl vikkōliṭutal, oṟṟaṉ oru kōlatti mēṟkoṇṭu uḷavaṟital, talimēl makuṭamaṇital.
vēy- vēyntōṉ= makuṭamaṇintōṉ.
vēyntōṉ- vēyntaṉ - vēntaṉ.
koṉṟivēyntōṉ= koṉṟi māliyi allatu malarit taliyiṟ cūṭiya civaṉ.
koṉṟivēyntōṉ - koṉṟivēntaṉ- civaṉ.
vēntaṉ- vēntu. ō.nō:  aracaṉ - aracu,
amiccaṉ - amiccu, pāṅkaṉ - pāṅku, pārppāṉ - pārppu.
muṭiyuṭi mūvēntar eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
vaṇpoḻil mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ (1336) kūṟuvatāl, ki.mu.7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari icciṟappurimi kākkappaṭṭu vantateṉa aṟiyalām. ataṉpiṉ, kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil kuṟunila maṉṉar taliyeṭuttatāl, mūvēntarum ivvatikāratti iḻantu viṭṭaṉar.
paḵṟuḷi  yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ pāṭuvatālum, tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam kumarināṭā talālum, kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻ nila muḻutum paḻampāṇṭi nāṭātalālum, mūvēntaruḷḷum mutalil tōṉṟiyavaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉē eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. piṟkālat til, nāvalantēyattiṉ kīḻppākattiyum mēṟpākattiyum tuṇiyaracarāka allatu maṇṭilat talivarāka āḷumāṟu, amarttap peṟṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṭiyiṉar iruvarē, cēracōḻa rāka māṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭi = vaṇṭi (cakkaram, cakaṭam).
paṇṭi- pāṇṭi= 1. vaṭṭamāṉa viḷiyāṭṭuc cil.
2. atik koṇṭu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭu (vaṭṭāṭṭu).
3. māṭṭu vaṇṭi. akava varum pāṇṭiyum  (paripā. 10: 16).
4. kūṭārappaṇṭi. (cilap. 14: 168. arum.)
5. (uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa) erutu. (paripā. 20:17. kuṟippu)
o.nē. kuṇṭu- kuṇṭi= erutu.
pāṇṭi - pāṇṭiyam = 1. erutu. ceñcuvaṟ pāṇṭiyam
(peruṅ. uñcik. 38:32).
2. (erutu koṇṭu uḻum) uḻavu. ceñcuvaṟ pāṇṭiyam
	(peruṅ. uñcik. 38:32)
3. (erutukoṇṭu uḻum) uḻavu.  pāṇṭiyañ ceyvāṉ poruḷiṉum  (kalit. 136)
pāṇṭi- pāṇṭil = 1. vaṭṭam. polampacum pāṇṭiṟkācu (aiṅ. 310). 2. vaṭṭakkaṭṭil. pēraḷa veytiya perumpeyarp pāṇṭil (neṭunal. 123). 3. vaṭṭat tōl. puḷḷi yiralit tōlū ṉutirttut tītukaḷit teytiya tikaḻviṭu pāṇṭil (patiṟṟup. 74). 4. vaṭṭat tāḷam. iṭikkuraṉ muraca miḻumeṉ pāṇṭil (cilap. 26:194). 5. vaṭṭak kaṇṇāṭi. oḷirum... pāṇ ṭil (pu.ve.6:12). 6. vaṭṭakkiṇṇi. pāṇṭileṭutta paḵṟā mari kīḻum paḻaṉaṅkaḷē (tirukkō. 249). 7. viḷakkut takaḻi. (piṅ.) 8. tērvaṭṭi. (cilap. 14:168. uri). 9. irucakkara vaṇṭi. viyamum pāṇṭilum (cilap.14:168). 10. kutiripūṭṭiya tēr (tivā.) 11. nāṭu. o. nō: maṇṭalam, vaṭṭāram, 12. erutu, kāḷi.
kāḷi maṟam viñciya tātaliṉ, pōrmaṟavaṉ kāḷiyeṉap paṭṭāṉ.
kāḷi = 1. iḷaverutu. 2. kaṭṭiḷam paruvattiṉaṉ. (tivā.) 3. pāli nilattalivaṉ. (tivā.) 4. pōr maṟavaṉ. uravuvēṟ kāḷiyum (puṟam. 334).
kāḷi maṟam viñciyatu maṭṭuṉṟu; kaṟpāṟiyilum āṟṟu maṇalilum cēṟṟu nilattilum mēṭṭilum paḷḷattilum, poṟivaṇṭiyi maruṅkoṟṟi mūkkūṉṟit tāḷ tavaḻntu iḻuttuc celluṅ kaṭippiṭiyumuḷḷatu. ataṉāl, 
maṭuttavā yellām pakaṭaṉṉāṉ uṟṟa
iṭukkaṇ iṭarppā ṭuṭittu.
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. (kuṟaḷ.624).
accoṭu tākkiya pāruṟ ṟiyaṅkiya
paṇṭac cākāṭ ṭāḻcci colliya
varimaṇaṉ ñemarak kaṟpaka naṭakkum
perumitap pakaṭṭukkut tuṟiyu muṇṭō
eṉṟār auviyār. (puṟam.90).
aracaṉ  pōrmaṟamum āṭcittiṟaṉum oruṅkē yuṭiyavaṉ eṉpati yuṇarttaṟku, kumarinili mutal tamiḻavēntaṉ pāṇ ṭiyaṉ eṉak kuṭippeyar peṟṟāṉ. ceḻiyaṉ, vaḻuti, māṟaṉ mutaliya kuṭippaṭṭaṅkaḷum piṉṉart tōṉṟiṉa. pāṇṭināṭu veppanāṭātalāl, kuḷirveṇmati kulamutalākak koḷḷap paṭṭatu pōlum! vēppantāri yaṇintataṟkum kayaṟkoṭi koṇṭataṟkum ituvē karaṇiya māyirukkalām. tārālum koṭiyālum vēmpaṉ mīṉavaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum eḻun taṉa. kayalmīṉ yāṉimīṉum paṉimīṉum pōlap parumīṉaṉ ṟātaliṉ, atik koṉṟataṉāṟ piṭitta veṟṟicciṉṉamākak kayaṟkoṭiyik karuta iṭamilli. kuḷirnta pēroḷi vīcum muḻumati pōṉṟavaṉ kuṭikaḷ pēriṉpamum aṟivumpeṟa āḷum aracaṉ, eṉṉuṅ karuttiṟ piṭikkappaṭṭa mativaṭṭakkuṭi allatu veṇkoṟṟak kuṭiyum, tamiḻakattiṉ, ciṟappākap pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ, veppa niliyik kuṟippāka vuṇarttum.
naṇṇilakkōṭu kumarimuṉikkut teṟkiṟ patiṉpākikkuḷ iruppatum, iṟṟit tamiḻakattilum vaṭavāṇariṉum teṉ vāṇar perumpāluṅ karuttiruppatum, muḻukippōṉa teṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ vemmi mikutiyi yuyttuṇara ētuvākum.
cōḻa pāṇṭiyar pāṇṭavarkkut tuṇiyākavum cēraṉ naṭu niliyākavum pāratappōriṟ kalantu koṇṭataṉālum, mūvēntar kuṭikaḷum pāṇṭava kuravarkku muṉpē varalāṟ ṟiṟkeṭṭāt toṉmutu paḻaṅkālattil tōṉṟiyamiyālum. pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum collip pāṇṭavaṉ eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟu tirippatu, varalāṟṟaṟivum ārāyccittiṟaṉum illātavar ceyaleṉak kūṟi viṭukka.
iṉi, paḻiyaṉ eṉṉum kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ oruvaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭil vāḻntu, vēmpik kāvalmaramāka vaḷarttatiyum, pāṇṭiya vēntariṉ paḻimiyiyum nōkki; paṇṭu eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉuñ collit tirippar cilar. pāṇṭiya vēntaṉ iṉṟu tāṉ namakkup paḻimiyāṉavaṉē yaṉṟi, avaṉ mutaṉmutalākak kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya pōtu oruvarukkum paḻimiyāṉavaṉ allaṉ eṉṟum, paḻiyaṉ eṉṉuñ col oruvaṉatu iyaṟpeyarukkē yaṉṟi, ōr āḷkuṭip peyarukku ēṟkāteṉṟum, avar aṟital vēṇṭum.
pāṇṭiya vēntaṉ aintiṇi nilaṅkaḷiyuṅ kippaṟṟi āṇṭa taṉāl, karuṅkaṭalmutal karumalivari nāṭu muḻuvatuṅ kāvaṟ kuṭpaṭṭatu. pāli nilavāṇar vēntaṉiṉ paṭimaṟavarāyiṉar. annilattalivaṉ kāṭṭup paṭit talivaṉāṉāṉ. ēṟ keṉavēyirunta marutanilappaṭi nāṭṭuppaṭi yeṉappaṭṭatu. pōrkkālattilum amitik kālattilum niliyāka virunta paṭi nilippaṭi yeṉṟum, avvappōtu pōriṉ tēvik kut takkavāṟu kūlik kamarttappaṭṭa paṭi kūlip paṭiyeṉ ṟum, peyarpeṟṟaṉa.
kāval mikuntu kaḷavuṅkoḷḷiyum pōrum nīṅkavē, nāṭṭil amitiyum iṉpamum nilaviṉa. toḻilkaḷ perukiṉa. vaḻippōkkum iṭam peyarvum accam nīṅkiṉa. vaṇikam vaḷarntatu. makkaḷ perukap peruka mella mella vaṭakkē ceṉṟu paraviṉar. nāṭṭuc celvatti mikukkavum, nākarika vāḻkkik kēṟṟa poruḷkaḷit tokukkavum, kāṭṭuvaḻi kaḷilellām kāvaṟpaṭiyi niṟuvi nila vāṇikattiyum, kaṭaṟkari vaḷintuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilellām tuṟinakarkaḷi yamittu nīrvāṇikattiyum, vēntaṉ ūkkiṉāṉ.
ēṟkeṉavē, iyaṟki moḻikkālattiṟ pirintu ceṉṟa māntariṉaṅkaḷ āppirikkā āttirēliya nilappakutikaḷilum, iḻittal moḻiyiṉ aci nilik kālattiṟ pirintuceṉṟa māntariṉaṅkaḷ cīṉa maṅkōliya nilappakuti kaḷilum, koḷuvu nilittoṭakkak kālattiṟ pirintuceṉṟa māntariṉaṅkaḷ mēliyāciya nilappakutikaḷilum, cempukkālattil ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ kuṟuku muṉṉum ḷa ḻa ṟa ṉa tōṉṟumuṉṉum pirintu pōṉa māntariṉaṅkaḷ vaṭintiyāvilum, perukip paravi vantaṉa. perunilaṅkaḷil vāḻntavaruṭ cilar avaṟṟi yaṭutta ciṟunilaṅkaḷāṉa tīvukaḷiluṅ kuṭiyēṟiṉar.
perunilattiṉiṉṟum tīrntu nāṟpuṟamum nīrāṟ cūḻappaṭ ṭirukkum ciṟunilamē tīvākum. tīr-tīrvu-tīvu. o.nō. kōr-kōrvi-kōvi. kuru(vu)-kōr-kō. tīvu-tīvam = peruntīvu. vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tīvu eṉṉuñ collit tvīpa eṉṟu tirittu, irupakkam nīrāṟ cūḻappaṭṭateṉap poruṭkaraṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar. itaṉ poruntāmiyik kaṇṭu koḷka.
nilavaṇikattār pātukāppum utaviyum nōkki, eppōtuṅ kūṭṭamākavē ceṉṟu vantaṉar. akkūṭṭattiṟkuc cāttu eṉṟa peyar. cārtal = cērtal, kalattal, kūṭutal.
nalleḻil mārpiṉic cārntu   (kalit. 142).
cār-cārttu-cāttu = 1. kūṭṭam curivaḷic cāttu niṟimati tavaḻum (kallā. 63:32). 2. vaṇikar kūṭṭam.
cāttoṭu pōntu taṉittuya ruḻantēṉ   (cilap 11: 190).
cāttu-va. sārtta.
cāttu-cāttaṉ = 1. vaṇikak kūṭṭattalivaṉ, (naṉ. 130, mayili), 2. vaṇikar teyvamāṉa aiyaṉār. (85.ni.) 3. vaṇikarkkiṭum iyaṟ peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. 4. cīttaliccāttaṉār. avaṉuḻiyirunta taṇṭamiḻc cāttaṉ (cilap.pati.10). 5. yārēṉum oruvaṉik kuṟikkum potup peyar. akkaṭavuḷāṟ payaṉpeṟa niṉṟā ṉōr cāttaṉi (tol.po.422, uri). 6. uḻavar erutiṟ kiṭum viravuppeyar.
vaṭavar aiyaṉārik kuṟikkumpōtu cātā eṉṟum, cātru eṉṟum, tirippar. itiṉiṉṟu avar ēmāṟṟi aṟintu koḷka.
cāttar = veḷināṭu ceṉṟuvarum vaṇikak kūṭṭattār.
atarkeṭut talaṟiya cāttaro ṭāṅku   (akam. 39).
cāttavar = cāttar. paḻutil cāttavarkaḷ cūḻa (tiruvālavā. 27:1)
cāttaṉ eṉṉuñ col piṟkālattiṟ cāttuvaṉ eṉṟum cāttuvāṉ eṉṟun tirintatu. kōvalaṉiṉ tanti mācāttu vāṉ (peruñcāttaṉ) eṉṟu iyaṟpeyar peṟṟiruntami kāṇka.
cāttar tam vaṇikac carakkukaḷi ēṟṟic cella mutaliṟ poti yerutukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. piṉṉar mēli yāciyāviṉiṉṟu kaḻuti, kutiri, oṭṭakam, kōvēṟukaḻuti ākiyavaṟṟip paṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭuvantu paḻakkiṉar.
kaḻutiyiṉ piṟappiṭam, vaṭakiḻakku āppirikkāvil ettiyōp piyā cōmāli mutaliya nāṭukaḷum, mēliyāciyāvil cīriyā pāracīkam peluccittāṉam mutaliya nāṭukaḷum, naṭu āciyāvil tipēttum maṅkōli yāvum, ākum.
kutiriyiṉ piṟappiṭam āciyāviṉ vaṭanaṭuppāka meṉṟum, aṅkiruntu atu kiḻakkē cīṉa maṅkōliya nāṭukaṭkum, mēṟkē airōppāviṟkum, teṉmēṟkē pāracīkam arapiyā mutaliya nāṭukaṭkum, ceṉṟatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
oṭṭakattil, oṟṟit timilikku arapiyāvum iraṭṭit timilikkup pakattiriyāvum (Bactria), piṟappiṭamākac collap paṭukiṉṟatu.
kōvēṟu kaḻuti, piṟkālattiṟ ki.mu. īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē, ciṉṉa āciyāviṟ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. atu āṇkaḻutikkum peṇ kutirikkum piṟanta kalappiṉamākum.
vaṇikac cāttiṟkuk kaḻutiyum kutiriyumē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
taṭavunilip palaviṉ muḻumutaṟ koṇṭa
ciṟucuḷip perumpaḻaṅ kaṭuppa neriyaṟ
puṇarppoṟi tāṅkiya vaṭuvāḻ nōṉpuṟat
taṇarccevik kaḻutic cāttoṭu vaḻaṅkum.  (perumpāṇ. 77-80).
arapik kutirikaḷ periyaṉavum pēṇutaṟ kariyaṉavumātalāl, perumpālum paṭikaṭkum aracar ūrtaṟkumē payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭirukkum. nāṭṭuttaṭṭu eṉṟum accimaṭṭam eṉṟum collappaṭum ciṟutarak kutirikaḷiyē, cāttukkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkum. nāṭṭuttaṭṭu intiyā vilēyē vaḷarkkap paṭuvatu. accimaṭṭam cumaturāt (Sumatra) tīviṉ vaṭamēṟkup pakutiyākiya acciyiliruntu (Achin) vantatu.
cāttukkaḷ kutirikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyati, peruñ cāli vaḻikaḷil āṅkāṅkuk kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum cāttaṉār (aiyaṉār) kōviṟkumuṉ, cutiyālum cuṭumaṇṇālum ceytu niṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷa kutiriyuruvaṅ kaḷiṉiṉṟu aṟintu koḷḷa lām.
kutiri tamiḻakattiṟku vanta piṉṉarē, kumarar kātal vāḻvil maṭalēṟṟam eṉṉum maṇamuṟiviṉi tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ōr iḷiñaṉ tāṉ kātalitta peṇṇi avaḷ peṟṟōr tara iciyāviṭiṉ, nīrccīli maṭṭum aṇintu uṭampu muḻutum cāmpaṟpūci erukkamāliyaṇintu, ūr naṭuccantiyiṟ paṉaṅkarukku maṭṭiyāṟ ceyta kutirimē lamarntu, tāṉ kātalitta peṇṇiṉ uruvappaṭatti valakkiyilēnti, ati uṟṟu nōkkiya vaṇṇamāyiruppāṉ. ūrpperumakkaḷ ataṉip pārtatavuṭaṉēyē, avaṉ ellā vakiyilum peṇṇiṟkut takutiyuḷḷavaṉeṉṟum avaṉiluñ ciṟantavaṉ illiyeṉṟuṅ kāṇiṉ, peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōriṭam pēcit tirumaṇattiṟku ēṟpāṭu ceytirukkavuṅ kūṭum. atu attuṇi eḷitāka muṭivatāyillā viṭiṉ, avar avaṉi nōkki nī āyvu tarukiṉṟiyā? eṉa viṉavuvar. avaṉ tarukiṉṟēṉ. eṉiṉ, avaṉi ammaṭaṟ kutirimītu amarntiruntavāṟē perunteru vaḻiyāka ūric cuṟṟiyiḻuppar. paṉaṅkarukkāṟ kāyam paṭuntoṟuṅ kātal maṟam (vīriyam) kiḷariṉ, avaṉ kaṭippiṭiyiyum avaṉ eṇṇam niṟivēṟāviṭiṉ iṟantu paṭum nilimiyiyum uṉṉi, peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōri vaṟ puṟutti icivittu maṇatti muṭittu vippar. itu ik kālattu uṇṇānōṉpum pāṭukiṭappum (cattiyākkirakamum) pōṉṟatu. paḻiya vuriyiluḷḷa vīriyam eṉṉum coṟku vintu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷuvatu poruntātu.
peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr ūrip pakittuk koḷḷa muṭiyāta. ataṉāl, maṭalēṟṟattāṟ kātalaṉ karutiyatu kikūṭum vāyp puṇṭu. āyiṉ, atē camiyattil, kātalaṉ taṉmāṉattit tuṟakkavum nōvit tāṅkavum kāyam mikiṉ uyiri iḻakkavum aṇiyamāyiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṉ arumi nōkkiyē ellārum iti mēṟkoḷvatilli. cilar veṟṟaraṭṭāka viḷampic collaḷavilēyē niṉṟu viṭuvar.
immaṭalēṟṟa vaḻakku nākarikam nirampāta paḻaṅkālattiṟkuriya tātalāl, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē aṟṟuppō yiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, iṉṟum oruvar kōvi pāṭiṉ, maṭalūrtalum oru tuṟiyāka amital vēṇṭumeṉpatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākum. ātalāl, ayalnāṭṭār akapporuṭ ceyyuṭkaḷip paṭittavuṭaṉ paṇṭittamiḻ nākarikattip paṟṟit tavaṟāṉa eṇṇaṅ koḷḷāvāṟu, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ vaḻippaṭṭa taṉittamiḻāciriyariṭamē tamiḻ payilumāṟu ceytal vēṇṭum.
arapik kutirikaḷ vantapiṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉukkum avaṉ ciṟ ṟaracarkkum, kālāṭpaṭi, kutirippaṭi, yāṉippaṭi, tērp paṭi eṉṉum nālvakip paṭikaḷ amintaṉa.
āṭcittuṇiyāka, aimperuṅ kuḻuvum eṇpērāyamum aivaki yuṟuticcuṟṟamum eṉap patiṉeṇ kuḻuvār amintaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ āḷnilatti mūṉṟu maṇṭalaṅkaḷākavum, ovvoru maṇṭalattiyum palanāṭukaḷākavum, ovvoru nāṭṭiyum pala kūṟṟaṅkāḷavum, pirittāṇṭatākat terikiṉ ṟatu. maṇṭalam eṉṉuñcol maṇṭilam eṉṟun tiriyum.
maṇṭalam eṉpatu vaṭṭam. accol vaṭṭam, vaṭṭaki, vaṭṭāram eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pōṉṟu nāṭṭup pakutiyik kuṟittatu. atu tūya teṉcollē.
muṇṭu = uruṭṭuk kaṭṭi. muṇṭu-muṇṭam = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kaṭṭi, catittiraḷ, ki kāl taliyillā vuṭal, periya vuruṭṭuk kaṭṭi. muṇṭu-muṇṭi = muṭṭi. muṇṭu-muṭṭu-muṭṭi. muṇṭu-maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = vaḷital. maṇṭu-maṇṭi. maṇṭiyiṭutal = kāli maṭakkutal.
maṇṭu-maṇṭalam = 1. vaṭṭam. (piṅ.). cuṭar maṇṭalam (tirunūṟ.80). 2. vaṭṭa vaṭivam. (tivā.). 3. mantirac cakkaram. 4. katiravaṉi allatu tiṅkaḷic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa ūrkōḷ (vaṭṭa voḷikkōṭu). 5. kātalaṉ kātaliyiṉ uṭampiṟ poṟikkum vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa ukirkkuṟi. 6. kayiṟu pāmpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ cuṟṟu. maṇṭalam payilurakar (pārata. kurukulu.3) 7. vaṭṭa vaṭiva māṉa paṭi vakuppu. (kuṟaḷ.767, uri). 8. kutiri vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟi yōṭutal. paṇṇiya vītipaṟṟi maṇṭalam payiṟṟuṉāṉē (cīvaka. 795). 9. naṭuviralnuṉiyum peruviralnuṉiyum kūṭi vaḷin tirukka maṟṟa viralkaḷum okka vaḷintu niṟkum iṇiyā viṇikki (cilap. 3:12, uri). 10. villōr irukāliyum vaḷittu niṟkumnili.
maṇṭalam-maṇṭali.
maṇṭalittal = 1. vaḷittal. 2. kāli vaḷittu niṟṟal. 3. cuṟṟic cuṟṟi vaṭṭamiṭutal. 4. oru pāṭṭiṉ iṟuti eḻuttu aci cīr col eṉpavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu aṭutta pāṭṭiṉ mutalil amiyap pāṭutal.
maṇṭalam-maṇṭilam = 1. vaṭṭam. 2. vaṭṭavaṭivam. 3. katiravaṉ. pakalcey maṇṭilam pārittāṅku (perumpāṇ. 442). 3. tiṅkaḷ. ceyvuṟu maṇṭilam (kalit. 7). 4. vaṭṭak kaṇṇāṭi. miyaṟu maṇṭilam pōlak kāṭṭa (maṇi. 25:137). 5. vāṉam. (piṅ.) 6. pār vaṭṭam. ceññāyiṟṟu ................................................................................. parippuc cūḻnta maṇṭilamum (puṟam. 30). 7. pārnilam kaṭalcūḻ maṇṭilam (kuṟun. 300). 8. vaṭṭamā yōṭuki. celavoṭu maṇṭilañ ceṉṟu (pu.ve.12, veṉṟip. 14). 9. irukālum vaḷittu niṟkum nili. irukāl maṇṭalat tiṭutalmaṇ ṭilanili (piṅ. 6:369).
maṇṭalam-va. maṇṭala.
maṇṭalam  eṉṉum col maṭṭumaṉṟu, vaṭṭam eṉṉum collum vaṭacoṟ ṟiripākavē ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakaramutaliyiṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
val-vaḷ-vaṇṭu, vaṭṭu-vaṭṭam.
vaṭṭam-vaṭṭa (pirā.) - vrutta (va.) L.verto (turn).
ittiripit talikīḻāka vrutta-vaṭṭa-vaṭṭam eṉṟu kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu ce.pa.ka.ka.ta. akaramutali.
maṇṭalam vaṭṭam eṉṉum irucoṟkum aṭivēr mul eṉpatē.
uḻavu, kittoḻil, vaṇikam, araciyal mutaliyavaṟṟāl, ulakiyal aṟivu palatuṟiyilum vaḷarntu vantatu. ataṉ viḷi vāka, matattuṟiyilum pala māṟutalkaḷ nērntaṉa. aintiṇi nilattārum, aimpūtaṅkaḷiyum naṉmiyun tīmiyum ceyyum uyirikaḷiyum iṟanta muṉṉōriyum pōriṟpaṭṭa peru maṟavariyum pēykaḷiyum potuvāka vaṇaṅki vantālum, ovvoru tiṇinilattiṟkum ovvoru teyvam ciṟappāka nilittuviṭṭatu.
kuṟiñci nilattil mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟi, ēṉi nilaṅkaḷilum maṭṭumaṉṟi ulakamuḻutum paraviya mutal teyvam tīyākum. ataṉāl, teyvam eṉṉum potuppeyarē tīyiṉ peyari ṉiṉṟutāṉ tirintatu.
tēytal = maraṅkaḷ allatu kaṟkaḷ urāytal, urāyntu tīppaṟṟutal.
tēy-tēyu = urāyntu paṟṟum neruppu.
tēyu-tēcu = neruppiṉ oḷi. tēcu-va. tēja. tēy-tē = teyvam. (piṅ.) tēpūci ceyyuñ cittiracāli (civaraka. nimica. 20). 2. nāyakaṉ. (ilak. 81.).
tē-tī = 1. neruppu. vaḷittaliiya tīyum (puṟam.2). 2. viḷakku. tītturīi yaṟṟu (kuṟaḷ. 929). 3.. vayiṟṟuttī, kaṭumpaci. vayiṟṟuttīt taṇiya (puṟam. 74). 4. ciṉattī, ciṉam. maṉṉartī yīṇṭutaṅ kaḷiyōṭu merittiṭum (cīvaka. 250). 5. tīyiṉtaṉmi, tīmi. tīppāl tāṉpiṟarkaṭ ceyyaṟka (kuṟaḷ. 206). 6. nañcu. vēkaven tīnākam (maṇi. 20:98). 7. narakattī. aḻukkāṟu ......................................... tīyiḻi yuyttuviṭum. (kuṟaḷ : 168) o.nō : tēmpāl-tīmpāl.
tēy-tēyvu-tēvu = 1. teyvam, (piṅ.). narakarit tēvucey vāṉum (tēvā. 696:2). 2. teyvat taṉmi.
tēvu-tēvaṉ = kaṭavuḷ. oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum (tiruman. 2104). tēvaṉ-va. tēva.
tēyvu-teyvu-teyvam = 1. vaṇaṅkappaṭum poruḷ. teyva muṇāvē (tol.po.18). 2. teyvat taṉmi. 3. teyvattaṉmi yuḷḷatu. 4. kaṭavuḷ. 5. kaṭavuḷ ēṟpāṭu, ūḻ. teyvam-va. tiva.
tēy eṉṉum mūlattiluḷḷa yakaramey, teyvam eṉṉuñ collilu miruttal kāṇka.
kuṟiñci nilattār, teyvam tīvaṭiviṉatu allatu tīyottatu eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉarēṉum, teyvam allatu tēvaṉ eṉpatu potuppeyarāki viṭṭataṉāl, tīyippōṟ civantavaṉ eṉṉuṅ karuttil tam tēvaṉic cēyōṉ eṉṟaṉar. avaṉ tammippōl maṟavaṉāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu karuti, maṟattiṟkuc ciṟanta iḷamip peyarāl avaṉi murukaṉ eṉṟaṉar. muruku iḷami. aḻaku eṉpatu ataṉ vaḻip poruḷ. tam paṭikkalamākiya vēliyēntiyum, kuṟiñcikkuriya kaṭappa māliyi yaṇintum, iruppatākak karuti, vēlaṉ eṉṟum kaṭampaṉ eṉṟum irupeyar cērttaṉar. mēleḻuntu viṇṇulakañ cellavutavumeṉṟu karuti, kuṟiñcikkuriya aḻakiya perumpaṟaviyākiya mayili murukaṉukku ūrtiyākak koṇṭaṉar.
mullinilattār, tam kaṉṟukālikaṭkup pulvaḷaravum vāṉā vārik kolliyiṟ payirkaḷ viḷiyavum, iṉṟiyāmiyāta maḻi peyyum karumukili allatu atu tiraḷum nīlavāṉit tam tēvaṉ vaṭivākak koṇṭu, avaṉi māyōṉ eṉṟum kariyavaṉ eṉṟum māl eṉṟum kuṟittaṉar.
marutanilattār, viṇṇulakak koḷki koṇṭataṉāl, immiyiṟ ciṟanta nalviṉi ceyyum potumakkaḷ tēvarākavum vēntaṉ tēvarkōṉāka vum, maṟumiyil viṇṇulakattiṟcērvar eṉṉum nampikkiyiṉāl, oruṅkē viṇṇulaka vēntaṉākavum maḻit teyvamākavuṅ koṇṭa tam tēvaṉi, vēntaṉ eṉṟē viḷampiṉar.
neytal nilattār, kaṭalpaṭu celvattiṟkum nīrvāṇi kattiṟkum kaṭaliyē nampiyiruntataṉāl, kaṭal talivaṉē tam tēvaṉ eṉakkoṇṭu, avaṉi vāraṇaṉ eṉṟaṉar. vāraṇam kaṭal. vāraṇaṉ kaṭal talivaṉ. vārtal vaḷital. vaḷ-var-vār-vāraṇam. kaṭal nilattic cūḻntiruppatāl vāraṇam eṉappaṭṭatu. vaḷikaṭal valiyiṟ cūḻntu (cīvaka. 1115). kēṭakamum caṅkum vāraṇam eṉappaṭuvatum vaḷintiruttal paṟṟiyē. vāraṇaṉ-va. varuṇa. kaṭaṟkō = vāraṇaṉ (upatē. urut. 230).
pālinilattār aṭikkaṭi āṟalittum cūṟiyāṭiyum pōriṭṭum makkaḷik koṉṟu piṇamākkiyataṉāl, appiṇaṅkaḷit tiṉpatākak karutappaṭṭa kūḷikaḷiṉ (pēykaḷiṉ) taliviyākiya kāḷiyi, tamakkup pōril veṟṟi tarupavaḷākak karuti, avaḷi avar teyvamākak koṇṭu kāviṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. pēyniṟam  karuppeṉpatu paṟṟi, avaḷ kāḷi eṉappaṭṭāḷ. kaḷ-kāḷ-kāḷam-kāḷi = karuppi. avaḷi māyōḷ eṉpatum anniṟam paṟṟiyē. māmi karumi. kāḷi-va. kālī. karuppāy eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
kāḷi vaṇakkam aṭippaṭiyiṟ pēy vaṇakkamāyiṉum, atu tāy vaṇakkamum kaṇṇakipōlum kaṟpuṭit tēvi vaṇakkamuṅ kalantatākum. kāḷiyammi koṟṟavi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ tāy vaṇakkak karuttik kāṭṭum. koṟṟam + avvi = koṟṟavi (veṟṟittāy). iṉi, ammi yeṉṉum peyar aṭiyaṭukkātu taṉittum avaḷik kuṟikkum. ataṉāl, avaḷāl nērvatākak karutappaṭṭa koppuḷa nōy, ammi yeṉṟē peyar peṟṟatu.
iṅṅaṉam aintiṇi vaṇakkamum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, marutanilap pārppāruṭ ciṟanta aṟiñar cilar. piṟaviyaṟukkum vīṭu pēṟṟum karuttuṅkoṇṭu, appēṟṟip peṟum civa neṟi tirumāl neṟi ākiya iru mataṅkaḷiyuṅ kaṇṭaṉar. kuṟiñci nilatti liruntu āyntavar civa neṟiyiyum, mulli nilattiliruntu āyntavar tirumāl neṟiyiyum, kaṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. vīṭupēṟṟu muyaṟciyi mēṟkoṇṭavar, ulakap paṟṟi muṟṟat tuṟantu ellāvuyirkaḷiṭattum aruḷ pūṇṭamiyāl, antaṇar eṉappaṭṭār.
antaṇa reṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum
centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ.   (kuṟaḷ. 30).
iṅṅaṉam, kalvit toḻilāḷar, illaṟam pēṇum pārppār eṉṟum tuṟavaṟam pūṇum antaṇareṉṟum, iru pāṟpaṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉamē, aracarum kuṟunila maṉṉar perunila maṉṉar eṉṟum, vaṇikarum nila vaṇikar nīr vaṇikar eṉṟum, uḻavarum uḻutuṇpōr (karuṅkaḷamar) uḻuvit tuṇpōr (veṇkaḷamar) eṉṟum, ivviru tiṟappaṭṭaṉar. karuṅkaḷamar kārāḷar eṉṟum, veṇkaḷamar veḷḷāḷar eṉṟum, collappaṭṭaṉar. ivviru cārārkkum vēḷāḷar eṉpatu potuppeyarākum. ēṉi mūvar pōlātu, putitāy vantavarkkum aṉpuṭaṉ viruntōmpi vēḷāṇmi ceyvataṉāl, uḻavar vēḷāḷar eṉac ciṟappikkappaṭṭaṉar. vēḷāṇmi piṟari virumpi yāḷun taṉmi.
vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpāṉ viruntirukka vuṇṇātāṉ.   (tirikaṭukam. 12).
kuṟiñci nilattār, kuṟavar kuṉṟavar iṟavuḷar, kāṉavar vēṭṭuvar eṉṟum; mullinilattār. āṭṭiṭiyar māṭṭiṭiyar eṉṟum; neytal nilattār, nuḷiyar, timilar, paratar (paravar) eṉṟum; pālinilattār, eyiṉar, maṟavar, vēṭṭuvar, vēṭar eṉṟum; iṭampaṟṟiyum toḻil paṟṟiyum karuvi paṟṟiyum ciṟcila vakuppārāyp pirintu pōyiṉar.
āyar, iṭiyar, kōvar, toṟuvar, potuvar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, peyarvēṟṟumiyē yaṉṟik kulavakuppik kuṟiyā.
āṉvallōr (tivā.) eṉṟum, āṉvallavar (cūṭā.) eṉṟum, āyarkkup peyaruṇmiyāl, kōvalaṉ eṉpatu kō mēyppatil vallavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum teṉ collē yaṉṟi, kōpālaṉ eṉṉum irupiṟappi vaṭacolliṉ tiripākātu.
pālinilam kuṟiñciyi yaṭuttatātaliṉ, vēṭṭuvar eṉṉum peyar koṇṭa vakuppār irunilattilum iruntaṉar. kaṭiya neṭu vēṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉuṅ koṭiyāḷi kōṭi malikkut talivaṉ. cilappatikārattiṟ pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷa vēṭṭuvavari pālivāṇarip paṟṟiyatu.
uṟikkālattu moḻit tuṟiyil, ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷum ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa meykaḷun tōṉṟi neṭuṅkaṇakku nirampiyiruttal vēṇṭum. veṇpā vañcippāvun tōṉṟi nālvakip pāvilum ilakkiyam naṭipeṟṟirukkum. ici nāṭakak kalikaḷum vaḷarcciyuṟṟu ilakkiya vilakkaṇam peṟṟirukkum. eḻuttu col poruḷ eṉṉum mukkūṟut taḻuviya piṇṭa vilakkaṇamun tōṉṟiyirukkum.
tamiḻar mīṇṭum vaṭakkē ceṉṟu paraviyiruppar.
vaṭintiyā tiraviṭanāṭākavum, naṭuvintiyā moḻi peyar tēyamākavum, māṟiyirukkum.
makkaḷ peruki āḷnilam mika virivaṭintuviṭṭataṉāl, pāṇṭiyaṉ, kumariyāṟṟiṟku vaṭakkiṟ paṉimali variyuḷḷa nilappākattiṟ kīḻppātiyi oruvaṉum mēṟpātiyi oruvaṉum āḷumāṟu, irutuṇiyariyari amarttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
veḷḷi (veṇpoṉ)
cempum veḷḷīyamuṅ kalantu uṟiyeṉṉuṅ kalappup poṉṉam (metal), amittataṟku  muṉṉō piṉṉō, veḷḷi allatu veṇpoṉ eṉṉum poṉṉamam tamiḻakattiṟ kiṭittup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. veṇṇiṟa māyiruppatu veḷḷi. veḷ-veḷḷi.
irumpallāta poṉṉat taṉimaṅkaḷuḷ (elements) taliciṟantu payaṉpaṭupavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟāṉa cempu, kaṟkālattiṉ piṟpakutiyil ki.mu. 8000 pōl putuk kaṟkāla māntaṉāṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu mutaṉmutalākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. cempu iyaṟkiyil taṉippoṉṉa niliyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivviyaṟkic cempu putuk kaṟkāla māntaṉ kalliṟ kīṭākap payaṉpaṭuttiya karuvipporuḷākum. atiṉiṉṟu avaṉ muruṭṭuc cammaṭṭi kaḷiyum kattikaḷiyum piṉṉar maṟṟak kalaṅkaḷiyum amit tāṉ. ataṉ camaṭṭap paṭuntaṉmiyāl, ati vēṇṭiya vaṭivattiṟku aṭittuk karuvikaḷi uruvākkuvataṟku mika eḷitāyiruntatu. taṭṭuvataṉāṟ cempu iṟukik kūrmi mikkaṉa vum nīṇṭuḻippaṉavumāṉa ōraṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. appoṉ ṉattiṉ paḷapaḷappāṉa cempaṭṭi niṟamum niliyāṉa taṉmikaḷum ati mikunta vilimatippuḷḷa tākkiṉa.
im muntu kālattiṟ cempiṟkākac ceyta tēṭuki, iyaṟkic cempik kaṇṭu piṭikkavum ataṉ vippukkaḷiliruntu atiyeṭukkavum vaḻi vakuttatu. ki.mu.6000 āṇṭu kaṭkuc caṟṟuppiṉpu, appoṉṉattik kuṭiyirupput tīkkaḷattil urukki vēṇṭiya vaṭivattil vārttuk koḷḷa muṭiyumeṉ patu, kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉpu, poṉṉaviyaṟ cempiṟkum cempuḷḷa pāṟikkumuḷḷa uṟavum, cempu kalanta maṇaliliruntu neruppiṉālum kariyiṉālum cempip pirit teṭukka muṭiyumeṉpatum, kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. itiṉiṉṟu poṉṉavūḻi toṭaṅkip poṉṉakkali piṟantatu.
cempuk kālat toṭakkattil ataṉ māperu vaḷarcci ekiptuvil iruntirukkak kūṭum. ki.mu. 5000 āṇṭuk kālatti lēyē, kallaṟikaḷil iṟantavar payaṉpāṭṭiṟku vikkappaṭṭa paṭikkalaṅkaḷum karuvikaḷum cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭirun taṉa. ki.mu. 3200 pōl, cīṉāyt tīvakkuṟiyil (Peninsula), ciṉep puru (Snefru) aracaṉāl, ceppuc curaṅkaṅkaḷ naṭattappaṭṭatip paṟṟiya tiṭṭamāṉa eḻuttēṭukaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iccuraṅkaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷa kukikaḷ, ap poṉṉattip piritteṭukkuṅkali tūymippaṭuttaliyum uṭkoṇṭa teṉpatik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkali, cempic caṉṉattakaṭukaḷāka aṭittu, avaṟṟik kuḻāy kaḷākavum piṟavuruppaṭikaḷākavum ceykiṟa aḷavuṟku vaḷarcciyaṭin tiruntatu. akkālattil uṟi (veṇkalam) mutaṉmutal tōṉ ṟiṟṟu. ammūlakkaruvip poruḷiṉ aṟiyappaṭṭa mutaṟpaḻan tuṇṭu, metum (Medum) kūmpuk kōpurattiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷa oru veṇkalap pāriyākum. ataṉtōṟṟakkālam tōrā. ki.mu. 3700 eṉṟu potuvāka oppukkoḷḷap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
cempum veḷḷīyamuṅ kalanta aḷiyam (alloy) ākiya uṟi, cempiṉum uyarnta vaṉmiyum viṟappum ākiya taṉmikaḷi uṭiyatu. itu potuvākap paṭikkalaṅkaḷum kaliyuruppaṭi kaḷum ceyyuṅ karuvipporuḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. itu mikutiyākavum ciṟappākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kālam uṟikkālam (Bronze Age) eṉappaṭum. uṟiyip payaṉpaṭut tuki, ekiptuviliruntu kirēttāviṟkuk (crete) ki.mu. 3000-ilum, cicilikkuk (Sicily) ki.mu. 2500-ilum, pirāṉciṟkum airōppāviṉ piṟaviṭaṅkaṭkum ki.mu. 2000-ilum, pirittaṉuk kum kāṇṭiṉōviyap parappiṟkum ki.mu. 1200-ilumāka, naṇṇilakkaṭaṟkari nāṭukaṭku virintu paraviyatu.
ki.mu. 3000 pōl cempu ceppaṟit (Cyprus) tīvil ērāḷamāka eṭukkappaṭṭatu. aṅkuḷḷa cempu vippukkaḷ mikap periyaṉa vākavum mikavuyarnta vilimatippuḷḷaṉavākavum iruntataṉāl, attīviṉ āṭci, aṭuttaṭuttu, ekiptiyarkkum āciriyarkkum pīṉiciyarkkum kirēkkarkkum pāracīkarkkum, urōmarkkum tāviṟṟu. urōmarkku vēṇṭiya cempu ēṟattāḻa muṟṟilum attīviṉiṉṟē vantatu. ataṉāl atu ceppaṟip poṉ maṇal (acs cyprium) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. apporuḷuḷḷa ilattīṉap peyar kipriyam eṉṟu kuṟukap piṉṉark kupram eṉṟu tirintatu. ip peyariṉiṉṟē kāppar eṉṉum āṅkilac col vantatu. ilattīṉap peyariṉ mutalīreḻuttukkaḷuñ cērntu ku (cu) eṉṉum itaḷiyak (Chemical) kuṟi yamik kiṉṟatu.
āciyāviṟ cempum uṟiyum eppōtu mutaliṟ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa veṉpatu, teriyavilli. cu ciṅṅiṉ (Shuching) pāviyaṅkaḷ (epics), cīṉattil ki.mu. 2500-ilēyē cempu payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉ, akkaliyiṉ aṟṟi niliyip paṟṟiyāvatu, appoṉṉam ataṟkumuṉ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatip paṟṟiyāvatu, oṉṟum aṟitaṟkilli. ki.mu. 1765-iliruntu 1122 vari yāṇṭa caṅ (Shang) āḷkuṭik (dynasty) kālattiṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavum, pēraḻakuṭiyaṉavum, uyarnta kalittiṟaṉik kāṭṭu vaṉavumāṉa uṟikkalaṅkaḷ, kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉum, ippoṉṉaṅ kaḷiṉ mūlattip paṟṟiya marumam, muntukālac cīṉariṉ tirukkallaṟikaḷil eṉṟeṉṟikkum pūṭṭi vikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu pōlum! (- pirittāṉiyak kalik kaḷañciyam (1970) 6, pak. 468.)
veṇkalam tiṇmiyum vaṉmiyum uṭimiyiṉālēyē uṟiyeṉap paṭṭatu. muṟiyeṉpatum apporuṭṭē. uṟattal ceṟital. uṟutal uṟutiyātal, uṟital iṟukutal. muṟattal viṟappātal. muṟuku tiṇmi.
veḷḷi, poṉṉiṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāka aḻakuḷḷatum vili yuyarntatum aṇikalattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuvatumāṉa poṉṉa mākum. atu poṉpōl attuṇi yuyarntataṉṟēṉum, ulakat tiṟ kiṭikkumaḷavu mikak kuṟivākavē yuḷḷatu. ñālattiṉ kaṟpuṟaṇiyiṟ kiṭikkum arikkōṭip paṅku irumpiṟku, irupaṅkutāṉ veḷḷi kiṭikkiṉṟatu.
veḷḷiyiṉ naṭimuṟip payaṉpāṭṭut toṭakkam, toṉmikkuḷ mikat tolivu celkiṉṟatu. eṉiṉum, māntaṉ mutaṉmutaṟpaṇikkup payaṉpaṭuttiya poṉṉaṅkaḷ, poṉṉum cempumēyeṉṟu nampappaṭukiṉṟatu. ki.mu. 4000 vari paḻimiyāṉa aracar kallaṟikaḷil, veḷḷiyaṇi kalaṉkaḷum cuvaṭippukkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.mu. 3100 pōl ekipti āṇṭatākak karutappaṭum meṉeciṉ (Menes) neṟiyīṭṭut tokuppil, orupaṅku poṉ, vili matippil iraṇṭarip paṅku veḷḷikkuc camameṉṟu tīrkkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ituvē mutaṟpoṉṉaḷavit tiṭṭamāka irukkalām. ki.mu. 700-ām āṇṭu, cintāṟṟiṟkum nīlāṟṟiṟkum iṭip paṭṭa ellā nāṭukaḷilum, poṉṉum veḷḷiyum paṇamāka vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpatu perumpālum tēṟṟam. urōmar, tam kālam vari, veḷḷip paṇikkaliyilum aṟiviyalilum vēṟen nāṭṭāriṉum mikat tērcci peṟṟiruntirukkalām.  (pirit tāṉiyak kalikkaḷañciyam (1970), 20, pak. 536.)
mēṟkāṭṭiya mēṟkōṭpakutikaḷ, tamiḻaṟiyātavarāṟ perum pālum mēṉāṭukaḷē paṟṟi yeḻutappaṭṭa viyātalāl, avaṟ ṟuḷ tamiḻar varalāṟṟiṟku ovvātaṉa vellām aṟiyāmiyiṉ viḷiveṉṟu karutiyamika.
iṟṟiyulakil mikapperiya veḷḷiccuraṅkam mekacik kōvil (Mexico) uḷḷatu. amerikka oṉṟiya nāṭukaḷilum (U.S.A.) kāṉaṭāvilum veḷḷi peruvāriyākak kiṭikkiṉṟatu. teṉṉamarikkāvilum veḷḷi eṭukkappaṭu kiṉṟatu. mutaṉmi yāṉa veḷḷiyīya nāka maṇalkaḷ āttirēliyāviṟ kiṭikkiṉ ṟaṉa. ciṟṟaḷavāka veḷḷi kiṭikkumiṭaṅkaḷ ulakeṅkum paraviyuḷḷaṉa.
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil, veḷḷik kalaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatu maṭṭumiṉṟi, veḷḷi vēynta māṭaṅkaḷum ampalaṅ kaḷum araṇmaṉikaḷilum kōvilkaḷilum amintiruntaṉa.
amiḻtaṉa marapi ṉūṉṟuvi yaṭicil
 veḷḷi veṇkalat tūṭṭa laṉṟi   (puṟam. 390).
viḷaṅkila vanti veḷḷi māṭat
 tiḷaṅkō vēṇmā ḷuṭaṉirun taruḷi   (cilap.25:4-5).
veḷḷiyam palattu naḷḷiruṭ kiṭantēṉ   (cilap.pati. 41).
veḷḷi ciṟṟaḷavākak kiṭittataṉāl, aracarālum peruñ celvarālum aṇivakip poruḷkaṭkaṉṟi, potu makka ḷāṟ palvakik karuvikaḷ ceyyappayaṉpaṭuttap paṭavilli. ataṉāl, veḷḷikkālam eṉa oru kālamum ēṟpaṭavilli.
5. irumpukkālam (Iron Age) (tōrā. ki.mu. 10,000-ulaka muṭivu.)
uṟikkup piṉ taḷḷip poṉṉamākak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa tum, ulakeṅkum ērāḷamākak kiṭippatum, ellāk karuvi kaḷuñ ceytaṟ kēṟṟa uṟutimikkatum, nākarikamum aṟivum virintu vaḷartaṟ kētuvāyiruntatum, karumpoṉ eṉṉum irumpākum. ataṉ karumi niṟattāl irumpeṉappaṭṭatu. ir-iru-iruḷ. irumi karumi. iru-irum-irumpu. E.iron. OE iren.
acīriyarum ekiptiyarum irumpi mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭut tiṉar. tiruppottakam, paṭippiyal (ātiyākamam), 4-ām atikāram 22-ām tirumoḻiyil, tūpāl kāyīṉ irumpu cempu viṉiñarkkellām payiṟṟāḷaṉāka iruntāṉeṉṟu collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tāvītiṉ kālattiṟ karuvikaḷum paṭik kalaṅkaḷuñ ceyya irumpu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ilakkiyak kirēkkariṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷāṉa tōriyap paṭiyeṭuppāḷar, iruppuk karuvi kaḷiyum paṭikkalaṅkaḷiyuṅ koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. ākkileciṟku, avaṉatu valluṭampāṇmip paricāka ōr iruppup pantu aḷikkap paṭṭa ceytiyi ōmar kuṟittirukkiṉṟār. kirēkkar karuṅkaṭaliṉ teṉkariyiṉiṉṟum, urōmar icuppāṉiyā viṉiṉṟum elpāviṉiṉṟum, irumpip peṟṟaṉar. teḷivāka vuḷḷapaṭi, ataṟku muntiya mūlaṅkaḷ intiyāvil iruntaṉa.  (pirittāṉiyak kalikkaḷañ ciyam (1970), 12, pak. 598.)
tūpāl kāyīṉ kālam ki.mu. 3874. ki.mu. 1425-il kāṉāṉiyar irupputtēr vittiruntaṉar. 1296-il kāṉāṉ aracāṉāṉa yāpīṉ 90 irupputtēr uṭiyavaṉā yiruntāṉ. irumpu maṭṭu miṉṟit tērum mutaṉmutal tamiḻakattilēyē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻ, ulakil mutaṉmutal tāṉē taṉiyākat tōṉṟiya moḻi yātalāl, nīṇṭa kālamākac ciṟappup peyariṉṟi moḻi eṉṉum potup peyarālēyē vaḻaṅki vantatu. ōr ūril orē āṟiruppiṉ pōy vantēṉ eṉṟē colvar. atupōl orē maramiruppiṉ, marattaṭikkup pō eṉṟē colvar. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻum tamiḻakattil orē moḻiyāyiruntataṉāl, moḻi yeṉṟē mutalil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
teṉṉila makkaḷ vaṭakkē ceṉṟapiṉ, taṭpaveṭpanili cuṟṟuc cārpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭṭālum, pēccuṟuppuk kaḷ vaṉmi peṟṟuviṭṭa taṉālum, ilakkiya vilakkaṇaṅkaḷum pulavaru miṉmiyālum, vāykkicinta vāṟu pēci vantataṉālum, avar moḻi vallōciyum koccit taṉmiyum peṟṟut tiraviṭa niliyaṭintu viṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉ aṅkuc ceṉṟa teṉṉila vaṇikar, vaṭanāṭṭil moḻipeyarn tiruntatik kaṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl annāṭṭi moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟaṉar. ammoḻipeyar tēyat teṉṉelli varavarat teṟkē taḷḷi vantatu.
kullik kaṇṇi vaṭukar muṉiyatu
valvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum   (kuṟun. 11).
villalit tuṇṇum vallāṇ vāḻkkit
tamiḻkeḻu mūvar kākkum
moḻipeyar tēetta paṉmali yiṟantē.   (akam.31).
paṉipaṭu cōli vēṅkaṭat tumpar
moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum   (akam. 211).
toṭiyami pakaḻit tuvaṉṟunili vaṭukar
piḻiyār makiḻar kaliciṟan tārkkum
moḻipeyar tēem iṟantaṉa rāyiṉum   (akam.295).
ivi piṟkālattaṉavāyiṉum, muṟkālattu nilimiyiyum oruvāṟu kuṟikkum.
nilavāṇikam pōṉṟē nīrvāṇikamuñ ciṟantatu. āṟṟik kaṭakkap parical, ampi, ōṭam, paḷḷiyōṭam mutaliya kala vaki kaḷum; kālvāy āṟu kariyōrak kaṭal ākiyavaṟṟiṟ carakkuk kaḷik kaṭattat tōṇi, paḵṟi mutaliya kaṭattu vakikaḷum; kaṭalil uṭceṉṟu mīṉpiṭikkak kaṭṭumaram, mēṅkā, timil, paṭaku mutaliya ciṟukala vakikaḷum; muttuk kuḷikkac calaṅkup paṭakum; āḻkaṭalik kaṭantu akkari nāṭukaḷil vaṇikañ ceytu mīḷak kappal, nāvāy, vaṅkam mutaliya peruṅkala vakikaḷum; ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
neytal nilattil mīṉpiṭikkum toḻilarāyirunta paṭavar allatu paravar cilar, neṭuṅkaṭal cellum nīrvaṇikarāyiṉar. avaruḷ talivar kuṟunilamaṉṉarumāyiṉar. avar kuṭippeyar paratar, paratavar eṉavun tirintatu.
paṭam = tuṇi, cīli, pāy. paṭam-paṭavu-pāy kaṭṭiya tōṇi. paṭavatēṟi (tiruvāca. 43:3).
paṭavu-paṭaku. paṭavu-paṭavaṉ = paṭakōṭṭi. paṭavarmaṭamakaḷir (tiruppō. can. piḷḷit. mut. 4).
paṭavaṉ-paravaṉ = paṭakēṟi mīṉ piṭippavaṉ. mīṉpala paravaṉ vilikoṇarn tiṭṭaṉaṉ (tiruman. 2031).
paravaṉ-paratavaṉ = 1. mīṉ piṭippōṉ. mīṉvilip paratavar (cilap. 5:25). tiṇṭimil vaṉparatavar (puṟam. 24:4). 2. nīrvaṇikaḷ, vaṇikaṉ. (cilap. 5:157, uri). 3. kuṟunilamaṉṉaṉ. teṉparatavar miṭalcāya (puṟam.378).
paratavaṉ-parataṉ = 1. mīṉ piṭippōṉ. paṭartirip paratar muṉṟil (kamparā. kārkāla. 74). 2. kaṭalōṭi. paratar malinta payaṅkeḻu mānakar. (cilap. 2:2). 3. vaṇikaṉ. parata kumararum (cilap. 5:158).
paratavar kaṭalōṭikaḷum (Mariners) cuṟṟukkaṭalōṭikaḷumā yiruntaṉāl (Circumnavigators), vaṭaticic ceṉṟu vaṭapār muṉiyil ciṟcila vēḷikaḷil tōṉṟum vaṇṇavoḷiyik kaṇṭu, ataṟku vaṭavi yeṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar.
vaṭam-vaṭavi = vaṭatici neruppu.
vaṭavik kaṉalip piḻinteṭuttu  (taṉippāṭal).
vaṭavaṉal = vaṭavi.
akkaṭaliṉ mītu vaṭavaṉalniṟka viliyō  (tāyu. paripūra. 9).
veḷḷat tiṭivāḻ vaṭavaṉali  (kamparā. tilamā. 86).
vaṭavaṉalam = vaṭavi.
kaṭukiya vaṭavaṉa lattiṭi vittatu  (kaliṅ. 402).
vaṭam-vaṭanti = vaṭaticiyiluḷḷatu, vaṭakāṟṟu.
vaṭantittī = vaṭavi. cuṭarnteri vaṭantit tīyum (kāñcippu, irupat. 384).
uttara maṭaṅkal = vaṭavi (tivā.) uttiram = vaṭakku.
maṭaṅkal = kūṟṟuvaṉpōl ulakiyaḻikkum ūḻittī.
uttaram = vaṭakkiluḷḷa ūḻittī, vaṭavi (piṅ.)
pāriṉ teṉmuṉiyilum vaṭavi pōṉṟa oḷi tōṉṟu mēṉum, āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyam muṟṟum aḻiyuṇṭu pōṉami yāl, atippaṟṟiya ilakkiyak kuṟippum iṟantupaṭṭatu.
vaṭavaṉal kumarināṭu muḻukumuṉṉarē kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭatēṉum, atip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ iṟṟip paṇṭinūl 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa tivākaramē. eṉiṉum, vaṭaviyi mutaṉmutaṟ kaṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē eṉpataṟku, atuvē pōtiya cāṉṟām. ēṉeṉiṉ, iṟṟi yaṟiviyalkaḷik kaṇṭa mēṉāṭṭārum ati 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē aṟintaṉar. kācanti (Gassendi) eṉṉum pireñciya aṟiviyalār 1621-il atikkaṇṭu ataṟku vaṭaviṭiyal (Aurora Borealis)  eṉṟu peyariṭṭār. iṉṟu appeyarkku vaṭavoḷi yeṉṟē poruḷ koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉ viḷakkam.
“A luminous atmospheric phenomenon, now considered to be of electrical character, occuring in the vicinity of, or radiating from, the earth’s northern or southern magnetic pole, and visible from time to time by night over more or less of the adjoining hemisphere, or even of the earth’s surface generally; popularly called the Northern (or Southern) Lights............” eṉṟu erutan tuṟi āṅkilap pērakaramutaliyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tamiḻppeyarum ilattīṉap peyarum, ēṟattāḻa muṟṟum poruḷot tiruttal kāṇka.
nilavaṇikar vaṭakkiṟ paṉimali variyum vaṭamēṟkil mēliyāciyā variyum, nīrvaṇikar palticiyilumuḷḷa akkari nāṭukaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum, pēcappaṭum moḻikaḷi yellāṅ kēṭṭapiṉ, tammoḻi oṉṟilēyē ḻakaram  ciṟappāyolittalik kaṇṭu, ataṟkut tamiḻ eṉṟu peyariṭṭirukkalām.
tam-tami = 1. taṉimi. taminiṉṟu (tirukkō. 167).  2. oppiṉmi. (caṅ. 81).
tami+ḻ = tamiḻ (taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭa moḻi). tamiḻ - tamiḻam.
tamiḻiṉ iṉimiyiyum tamiḻap paṇpāṭṭiṉ cīrmiyiyum nōkki,
iṉimiyu nīrmiyun tamiḻeṉa lākum. (10:580) eṉṟār piṅkala muṉivar. avviraṇṭum vaḻipporuḷē yaṉṟit tamiḻ eṉṉuñ colliṉ vērpporuḷākā.
collamippum, cemmarapum, ilakkaṇa voḻuṅkum, aivakit toṭiyaminta nālvakippāviṉ iṉṉōciyum, poruḷilakkaṇamum, ciṟappāka akapporuṭṭuṟikaḷum, iṉkati taḻuviya vaṉappiyalum, tamiḻiṉ iṉimiyiyum; poruṇmoḻip pākkaḷum araciyal nūlum aṟanūlum meyp poruḷnūlum tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭiṉ nērmiyiyum, kāṭṭum.
akapporuṭṭuṟikaḷiṉ iṉimiyi nukarntē, māṇikka vācakar yāviyum pāṭiyapiṉ kōviyum pāṭi, atil,
ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟillic ciṟṟam palattumeṉ cinti yuḷḷum
uṟivāṉ uyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāyntavoṇ ṭīntamiḻiṉ
tuṟivāy nuḻintaṉiyō ..........................................................
eṉṟu (tirukkō. 20), tam ciṟappāṉa īṭupāṭṭik kuṟippākat terivittār.
irumpuk kālattil, veṇpā mutaliya nālvakip pākkaḷuḷ mutal iraṇṭaṉ kalappāl maruṭpā eṉṉum kalavippāvum, kalippāviṉ tiripāl paripāṭal eṉṉum tiripāvum, tōṉṟiṉa. ici, nāṭakam, maruttuvam, camiyal, kaṇakku, kaṇiyam, ēraṇam, maṟi, meypporuḷ mutaliya paltuṟik kalikaḷum aṟiviyalkaḷum paṟṟiya nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa nūlkaḷum; ammi, aḻaku, toṉmi, tōl, viruntu, iyipu, pulaṉ, iḻipu eṉṉum eṇvaki vaṉappiyalum; tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. moḻiyuṭaṉ iciyum nāṭakamum iṇikkappaṭṭu, iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻ vaḻakkum ēṟpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. aintiṇic coṟkaḷum cērntu, tamiḻ peruvaḷam peṟṟiruntatu.

kuṟippu : ū eṉpatu mutaṟkālattil peyarum peyareccamum kuṟippu viṉiyeccamumākiya cuṭṭuc collāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.

(2) muṉṉilip peyar
orumi 	paṉmi
ūṉ	ūm (mutal nili)
nūṉ	nūm (iraṇṭām nili)
nīṉ	nīm (mūṉṟām nili)
muṉṉilip peyariṉ vēṟṟumiyaṭi
orumi	paṉmi
ūṉ - uṉ,	ūm - um
nūṉ - nuṉ,	nūm - num
nīṉ - niṉ,	nīm - nim.  (mu.tā.)
kuṟippu : (1) mūviṭap patiṟpeyarkaḷum neṭumutal kuṟukiyē vēṟṟumi yēṟkum. mutaṟkālappaṭarkkip patiṟpeyar tāṉ tām eṉpaṉa. piṉpu avi taṟkaṭṭup peyarāyiṉa.

(2) mūviṭap patiṟpeyarkaḷilum, ṉakaravīṟu orumiyiyum makaravīṟu paṉmiyiyum uṇarttum.
(3) nīṉ, nīm eṉṉum irupeyarkaḷum, iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ vaḻakkil uḷḷaṉa.
(4) nīm eṉpataṉ vēṟṟumiyaṭi vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
(5) nīṉ nīm nīṅkaḷ eṉṉum muppeyarum, muṟiyē, iḻintōṉ ottōṉ uyarntōṉ ākiyōrik kuṟikka āḷappeṟum.
(ii) muṉṉili yorumippeyar
nīṉ-nī (nāṉkām nili)
o.nō. tampiṉ - tampi (kaṭikkuṟi)
nī eṉṉum peyar vēṟṟumiyēlātiruppatu, atu nīṉ eṉṉum peyariṉ tiripātali uṇarttum.

(v)  muṉṉilip paṉmip peyar
nī + ir = nīyir
            = nīvir (ilakkaṇap pōli) 5-ām nili
            = nīr (tokuttal)
nī eṉṉum orumip peyaraṭiyākap paṉmip peyar amikkak karuti, avar eṉṉum paṭarkkip peyaruk koppa rakara viruti yēṟṟi, nīyir eṉa amittaṉar iṭikkālattiṉar. nīm eṉṉum col palavakuppāriṭi vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉamiyē itaṟkuk kāraṇam, nīm eṉṉuñ collip pōṉṟē nīr eṉpatum ot tōṉik kuṟittaliyum, ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉatu nir eṉa vēṟ ṟumit tiripu koḷḷāmiyiyum, nōkkuka.
nīyir nīvir nāṉ eḻuvā yalapeṟā
eṉṉum nūṟpāvil, nīr eṉpatiyuñ cērttuk koḷḷāmi naṉṉūlāriṉ moḻiyārāycciyiṉmiyiyē kāṭṭum.

(3) muṉmi (kālamum iṭamum)
ūṅku = muṉpu. (ūṅkaṇ - ūṅkaṇōr = muṉṉōr.)
puram = muṉ; 
mukam = muṉpu, kāraṇam.

mutal - muṉ, toṭakkam, aṭi, kāraṇam.
(mutali = mutaliluḷḷavaṉ-ḷ-tu).

(munturi-muntiri = mutaṟ ciṟṟilakkam)
muṉ muṉṉam - muṉam. muṉṉam - muṉṉar.
muṉ - muṉpu. muṉ muṉṟu.

muṉmiyik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ, muṉvāyāṉa utaṭṭiṟ piṟakkum pakara makara mutalavāyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
(4) mutaṉmi
muṉṉiṭattil vikkappeṟuvatu ciṟanta poruḷātalāl, muṉmik karuttil mutaṉmikkaruttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
mukam = talimi. mukam - mukami - mukāmi.
mukam - mukar - mukari = talimi.
mukam - mukaṉ - mukaṉi = talimi.
muku - mukku - mukkiyam (va.)
mutal - mutaṉmi. mutaṉ - mutali = talivaṉ. paṭittalivaṉ. paṭimutali cēṉimutali eṉpaṉa paṭittalivaṉ peyar. mutaliyār = paṭittalivar vaḻiyiṉar

(5) mutumi
muṉ piṟantavaṉ piṉ piṟantavaṉi nōkka mutiyavaṉātalāl, muṉmik karuttil mutumik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. tuyppu, (aṉupava) mikutiyāl aṟivu vaḷarttaliṉ, mutumip peyar aṟiviyum kuṟikkum.
puri = paḻimi.
mukam - mukar - mukari = paḻimi, kiḻam. mukari - mūri = kiḻam, kiḻaverutu, paḻimi.
mukarimi = pēraṟivu.
mutu - mutumi. mutu - mutār - mutāri = mutiya kaṉṟu, mutumi, mutircci.
mutu - muti = paḻaṅkolli. muti - mutiyal = paḻaṅkāṭu.
mutu - mū - mūppu.
mutu - mutuval = paḻimiyāṟ paḻutāṉatu.
mutu - mutir - mutircci = mūppu, muṟṟiya viḷivu.
mutu = mūppiṉāluṇṭākum aṟivu. mutu - mutukku = aṟivu. mutukkuṟital = makaḷir ulakiyalaṟivaṭital, pūppaṭital.
mutuvaṉ = mūttōṉ, aṟiñaṉ.
mutu - (muttu) - muṟṟu. muṟṟutal = mutirtal.

(6) muṉṉuṟuppu
uttam = muṉ taḷḷiya muntirik koṭṭi.
utaṭu = vāyiṉ muṟpakuti, utaṭu (utaḻ) - itaḻ = utaṭu, utaṭu pōṉṟa pūvitaḻ.
tuti = yāṉikku, muṉṉirukkuṅ ki. tutikki = tutiyākiya ki.
(nutti) = netti - neṟṟi.
nutal = maṇṭiyiṉ muṟpākam.
mukam = mukattil muṉ nīṇṭirukkum mūkku, mūkkum vāyum cērnta mūñci (muzzle) mūñciyum kaṉṉamuñ cērnta mukari, mukariyum neṟṟiyuñ cērnta mukam (face) mukam - mukaṉ.
kuṟukkāka vaḷarum aḵṟiṇi yuyirikaḷuṭiya uṭaliṉ muṉpuṟattil mukamum, mukattiṉ muṉpuṟattil mūñciyum. mūñciyiṉ muṉpuṟattil mūkkum, iruttal kāṇka. paṟaviyiṉ mūñcikku mūkku allatu alaku eṉṟa peyar. makkaḷ mukam taliyiṉ muṉpuṟattiliruppatu.
mukam mukar = mūkku. mukar - mukari = mūkkiṉ aṭi.
mukam - muka. mukattal = mūkkāl maṇatti nukartal.
muka - mō - mōppu - mōppam.
mukam - mukar, mukartal = maṇatti nukartal.
mukar - nukar. mukar - mōr.
mukam - mukar - mukari - mōri. mukavāy - mōvāy.
muku - mukku - mūkku = mukarum uṟuppu, paṟaviyalaku,
mūkku - (mūṅku) - mūṅkā = mūkku nīṇṭa kīri.
mutāri = muṉ ki vaḷiyal.
munturi - muntiri = muṉtaḷḷiya koṭṭiyuḷḷa paḻam, appaḻamaram (aṇṭimā). muntiri - muntiriki.
kuṟippu : mukam eṉṉuñ colliṉ aṭi muku eṉṟum, ataṉ mukkiyamāṉa eḻuttu mu eṉṟum ataṟkum uyir nāṭiyāṉatu u eṉṉum uyir eṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum. mukaṉ eṉṉuṅ kaṭip pōli vaṭivatti mu+kaṉ eṉṟu pirittu. mu muṉṉoṭṭu (Prefix) eṉṟum. kaṉ eṉpatu tōṇṭutalik kuṟikkum viṉiccol eṉṟum, mukaṉ eṉpatu tōṇṭap paṭṭatu pōṉṟa vāyik kuṟikkum peyar eṉṟum, poruntāp puḷukalākak kūṟuvar vaṭanūlār. mukam eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyilum mukattik kuṟikkumeṉṟum, kaṉ eṉṉuñ collum kal eṉpataṉ tiripēyeṉṟum aṟintukoḷka. kallutal tōṇṭutal.
mukam eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻiyil vāyik kuṟittaṟkuk kāraṇam. paṟavikaṭku mūkkum vāyum iṇintiruppatum. avaṟṟiṉ vāyāṉa alaku mūkku eṉṟu aḻikkappeṟutalumē. 


(7) muṟpakuti
mukam uṭaliṉ allatu taliyiṉ muṟpakutiyātalāl, mukam eṉṉuñ col iṭam poruḷkaḷiṉ muṟpakutiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
urimukam tuṟimukam nūṉmukam pōrmukam, mukavuri mukatali mukamaṇṭapam, mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.
mukam - mukappu = muṉ maṇṭapam, muṉippakuti.
mukam - mukaṉ - mukaṉi - mōṉi = cīrkaḷiṉ mutaliṭam, avaṟṟiṉ mutaleḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟivaral, mukappu.
mukam - mukar - mukari = muṉpuṟam.
muntu - munti = muṉṟāṉi.
muṉ - muṉi. (pōr muṉi - War front)
muṉṟu - (muṉṟam) - muṟṟam. muṉṟu + il = muṉṟil.
iṉi, kuvinta allatu kūriya poruḷkaḷiṉ muṟpakutiyik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷāvaṉa : 
kuṉi - koṉi = nuṉi (vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku).
tuḷ - tūy = nuṉi.
nuti = nuṉi.
(nuṉ) - nuṉi, nuṉi.
muṉ - muṉi, muṉi.
muḷ - muḷi = muṉi.

	ūkāraccuṭṭu - 2) muṉṉuṟavu
(1) tōṉṟutal
oṉṟiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟu muṉvarutalē tōṉṟutalātaliṉ, muṉmik karuttil tōṉṟutaṟ karuttup piṟantatu. oru poruḷ etticiyil tōṉṟiṉum. ataṉ mukatti nōkka atu varun tici ataṟku muṉmiyātal kāṇka. itaṉāl, tōṉṟutali mukañceytal eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkiyum, nōkkuka.
muṉmi eṉpatu muṉṉākavuḷḷa nilimiyiyum, muṉṉuṟal eṉpatu muṉvarum iyakkattiyum, kuṟikkumeṉa vēṟupāṭaṟika..
ummi viṉivanturutta loḻiyāteṉum
(maṇi. 26; 82) uruttal = tōṉṟutal (u.vē.cā.uri).
ūḻviṉi yuruttu vantūṭṭum  (cilap. patikam, 52)  (mu.tā.)
uruttu = veḷippaṭṭu (arumpatavuri;)
uruttu = urukkoṇṭu (aṭiyārkku nallāruri).
uruttal = tōṉṟutal, muḷittal, uruveṭuttal.
uru = tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, uṭal, paṭimi, teyvac cili, ōviyam, ōviyavēliyāṉa pūttoḻil, vaṭivuḷḷa taṉipporuḷ.
uru-uruvu - uruvam.
uruppaṭi = ōr uruviṉ paṭi (copy) yāṉa poruḷpaṭiyāṉa taṉipporuḷ, taṉipporuḷ (article).
uruppaṭutal urukkulital eṉpaṉa, uṭampiṉ vaḷarcci taḷarccikaḷik kuṟikkum vaḻakkāṟukaḷ.
uruppōṭutal = maṇimāliyiluḷḷa ovvōr uruviṟkum ovvoru muṟiyāka oru mantiratti ōtutal, allatu oru mantirattiṉ olivaṭivip perukkutal, mantiram pōla oṉṟi maṉappāṭañ ceytal. uruvēṟṟutal eṉpatum ituvē.
uru = oru taṉiyici vaṭivu. paṇ, pāṭṭu, meṭṭu. tōṟṟa vakiyik kuṟikkum niṟam vaṇṇam mutaliya coṟkaḷum, ici vakiyik kuṟittal kāṇka.
uru - uruvu - urupu = peyar vēṟṟumiyiṉ vaṭivāṉa īṟu, uvamiyiṉ vaṭivāṉa col;
akattiyatti nōkka vaḻi nūlum ataṟku muntiya nūlkaḷi nōkkac cārpu nūlumāṉa tolkāppiyattil, urupu eṉpatu ōr ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭākak kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppataṉālum, atu tamiḻilakkaṇan tōṉṟiya toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum marapuc collātalālum, atu tūya tamiḻc col eṉpatu oru 
tali. tōṉṟutalik kuṟikkum vēṟu pala coṟkaḷum ukarattiyēṉum ukaramōṉiyiyēṉum ukarattiripi yēṉum mutaṟ koṇṭiruttal kāṇka.
uru - (urumpu) - arumpu. arumputal = tōṉṟutal: kuruttal = tōṉṟutal;
atiṉiṉṟu morupuruṭaṉ kuruttāṉ  (vināyakapu. 72, 4)
(kuru) - karu = mutaṟ poruḷil tōṉṟum poruḷ.
tuḷirttal = tōṉṟutal.
nuṉi - naṉi. naṉital = tōṉṟutal.
nutalutal = tōṟṟuvittal.
pūttal = tōṉṟutal. poṭittal = tōṉṟutal.
mukiḻttal = tōṉṟutal
muḷittal = tōṉṟutal
muṟital = tōṉṟutal
ic coṟkaḷellām muṉvarutal allatu muṉ taḷḷutal eṉpatiyē vērpporuḷākak koṇṭavi eṉpatu, tuḷ (taḷ), muḷ (muṭṭu) mutaliya coṟkaḷāl uṇarappaṭum. tuḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟanta turuttu eṉṉum collum, muḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟanta muṭṭu eṉṉum collum, muṉ taḷḷutalik kuṟittal kāṇka. tōṉṟutali (muṉ varutali)k kuṟikkum. uruttal eṉṉum viṉiccol vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkāmiyiyum, uruvam eṉṉum paṇpup peyariṉ tiripāṉa rūpa eṉpatu maṭṭum atil vaḻaṅkutaliyum, nōkkuka.

(2) iḷami
oru poruḷ tōṉṟiya nili ataṉ iḷamparuvamātaliṉ, tōṉṟaṟ karuttil iḷamik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. 
(i) iḷami (nilittiṇi)
ullari = taḷir. (ulir) - ilir. ilirttal = taḷirttal.

(kuḷ) - koḻuntu = taḷir. kuḷ - keḻu - keḻumu. keḻumutal = muḷittal.

muṭṭu - moṭṭu = arumpu.
mutaḷ = arumpu.
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa coṟkaḷellām, muṉ varutal allatu muṉṟaḷḷutal eṉṉuṅ karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭavi. (Shoot, bud mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷuṭaṉ ivaṟṟi oppu nōkkuka.).
