(3) maṭami
evvaki yuyiriṉattilum iḷamiyil aṟiyāmi mikkiruppatu iyaṟkiyātalāl, iḷamik karuttil maṭamik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
maḻa - mata - maṭa - maṭam = iḷami, maṭami.
maṭa - maṭappu = maṭappam = iḷami, maṭami.
maṭa - maṭi - maṭimi.
iru tiṇiyilum, peṇpālum āṇpāliṉum maṭam mikka tākak karutappaṭuvatāl maṭavaral maṭanti (pēti, ēḻi) mutaliya peyarkaḷ uyartiṇiyilum, manti mūṭu mutaliya peyarkaḷ aḵṟiṇiyilum, peṇpālik kuṟikka veḻuntaṉa.
iḷamiyilēyē aḻakum ciṟantiruppatāl, muruku matavu maṭam mutaliya coṟkaḷ aḻakiyum uṇarttiṉa.

(4) putumi
etuvun tōṉṟiyavuṭaṉ putumiyāyirukkumātalāl, tōṉṟaṟ karuttil putumik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. oru māṇavaṉ oru kalvi niliyattil cērnta tuvakkattil putumāṇavaṉ eṉap paṭutaliyum, oru karuvi mutaṉmutaṟ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭavuṭaṉ, putukkaruviyeṉappaṭutaliyum, nōkkuka.
(kutu) = kottam = putumi. iccol ivvaṭivil iṉṟu teluṅkil maṭṭum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
kotta = putiya. kottakā = putitāy.

iṉṟu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum vaṭivukaḷāvaṉa : 
kutu - (kuṭu) - kaṭu - kaṭi = putumi.
kaṭimalarp piṇṭi (cīvaka. 2739).
(kuṭu) - (koṭu) - kōṭu - kōṭakam = putumi.
kōṭu - kōṭi = putumi, puttāṭi.
nutu eṉṉum tamiḻ aṭiyiṉiṉṟē nūtaṉam eṉṉum vaṭacol tirintuḷatu.
putu - putumi putu - putukku - putukkam. putuval = putitāyt tiruntiya nilam. putir = putunel.
putumutal = putiyavarpōṟ pēcutal, putiṉam = putumi, putuc ceyti.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maḻa - maḻali = iḷami, putumi. maḻalittēṉ = pututtēṉ.
maḻa - (vaḻa) - vaḻi = putumi.
vaḻi matu nukarpu (paripā. 11, 66)
vaḻi - vaḻiccu = putumi.
vaḻiccaṟa viḷinta (perumpāṇ. 280)

(5) pacumi
putumiyāṉa muṉ paruvattil, atāvatu iḷamparuvattil, payir paccikaḷ pacumiyāyiruppatāl, putumik karuttil pacumik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. pacumi ceḻippik kuṟikkumātalāl, putumiccol uṭaṟ ceḻippiyum uṇarttum.
uṭal ceḻimpāyiruttalip putupputu veṉṟiruttal eṉpatu marapu. putu eṉṉum col ipporuḷil perumpālum pucu eṉṟu tiriyum. pucuppucu veṉṟiruttal = makkaḷ uṭal ceḻimpāyiruttal. putu - pucu - pucuppu = uṭaṟceḻippu.
pucu - pacu - pacumi = putumi, iḷami, meṉmi, pacci niṟam, ceḻimpu.
putumeḻukkip pacumeḻukku eṉṟār kaṭiyalūr
uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār  (paṭṭiṉappāli, 166.)
pacu - pacca - pacci = pacumi, paciya poruḷ. pacumi kaṇṇukkiṉiya niṟamātalāl, kaṇṇukkiṉitāyiruttalip pacceṉṟiruttal eṉpar.
paccuṭampu = iḷanta uṭampu.

paccu - paccaṭi = vēvikkāmaṟ pacciyākac ceyyum kūṭṭu. camikkāta kāykaṟikaḷ perumpālum pacci niṟamāyirup patāl, pacumiccol camiyāmip poruḷit tantatu. pacciveṭṭu paccūṉ paccarici pacci veṇṇey paccit taṇṇīr mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷ ikkaruttuppaṟṟiyaṉa. pacci eṉṉuñcol, uṇaviṉ tiruntā niliyik kuṟittalpōl, moḻiyiṉ tiruntā niliyiyum kuṟikkum. iṭakkarppēccu paccippēccu eṉappaṭum.
pacu - paca. pacattal = pacciyātal, paca - pacaḷ - pacaṇṭi = pacami.
paca - pacal - pacali = paciya poṉṉiṟat tēmal, pacali - payali = tēmal.
pacu - pācu - pācam (moss). pācu - pāci = pācam, paccippayaṟu.
pācu - pācavaṉ = paccūṉ viṟpavaṉ.
pacu - (paci) - (payi) - pi. pimi = pacumi, iḷami. meṉmi.
akara ikaram aikāramākum (tol. 54.)
pi - pittu = pacumi. pi - piyal = iḷiñaṉ, ciṟuvaṉ. piyal - piyaṉ.
piyal - payal - payaṉ - pacaṉ.
pi - pitu - pital = piyaṉ, kuḻavi.
pi - payir = pacciyāṉa pulceṭi koṭi. payirpacci piṅkūḻ eṉpaṉa vaḻakku.
pi - payaṟu = paccippayaṟu, piṟapayaṟu, payaṟu; payaṟupacci eṉpatu vaḻakku. payaṟu - payaṟi = payaṟṟammi.

(6) meṉmi
iḷamiyil evvuyiriyum meṉmiyāyiruppatāl. iḷamik karuttil meṉmik karuttum tōṉṟum. kutali = meṉmi, meṉmoḻi.
maḻa - maḻamaḻappu = meṉmi. maḻali = meṉmi, meṉmoḻi, maḻaṟutal = meṉmiyātal.
maḻa - mata - maṭa - maṭam = meṉmi.
piya = pacumi, iḷami, meṉmi.
pimi = pacumi. iḷami, meṉmi.
piya = mella. piyap piya - payappaya - pippaya.
pi - piyā. piyāttal = ciṟumippaṭutal, tuṉpattit tāṅkamuṭiyāvāṟu meṉmiyātal, tuṉpuṟutal. piyuḷ = tuṉpam.

(7) muṉ taḷḷi varutal
tōṉṟiya poruḷ muṉṉāka nīṇṭu varutal muṉ taḷḷi varutalākum.
tuntu - tunti = muṉtaḷḷiya vayiṟu. tunti - tonti.
turuttutal = muṉ taḷḷutal. vayiṟu turuttik koṇṭirukkiṟatu eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
(tuḷ) - taḷ.
puṭittal - muṉ taḷḷutal, vīṅkutal.
muli = muṉ taḷḷiya mārpu. (moñcu) - moñci = muli.

(8) tarutal
vāḻi kuliyīṉutalik kulitaḷḷutal eṉṟum, teṉṉi paṉi pāḷi viṭutalip pāḷi taḷḷutal eṉṟum, nel pul katir īṉutalik katir taḷḷutal eṉṟum, kūṟuvar. marañceṭi koṭikaḷ kāy kaṉiyōṭirukkum pōtu makkaḷ avaṟṟip paṟittal avi koṭukka avar koḷvatu pōliruttalāl; avi kāyttuk kaṉitalip palaṉ tarutal allatu palaṉ koṭuttal eṉpar. itaṉāl, taḷḷutaṟ karuttil tarutaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
taḷ - taru - tār - tā - ta. tār - tar.
taḷ eṉṉum viṉi tarutaṟ poruḷil taru eṉat tirintu, ataṉ mēlum, etirmaṟi viṉivil tār allatu tā eṉṟum (tārāṉ, tarāṉ), ēvalviṉiyil tā eṉṟum, iṟanta kāla viṉiyil ta eṉṟum (tantāṉ), tirintuḷḷatu.
yakara meyyīṟākat tiriyāta ḷakara meyyīṟṟu iyaṟ coṟka ḷellām, potuvāka rukaravīṟṟavākat tirikiṉṟaṉa.
v.L.: kaḷ - karu - kār (karumi).
iḷamiyi uṇarttum kuru nuru muru mutaliya aṭikaḷellām, kul nul pul mul mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tiripāṉa kuḷ nuḷ puḷ muḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tirintaviyē.
kaḷ - karu - kār eṉṉum tiripu pōṉṟatē taḷ - taru - tār eṉpatum, muḻuttiripu niliyilum, tarutal viṉi varutal viṉiyi ottuḷḷatu.
vaḷ - varu - vār - vā - va - vār - var.
arumpaṇṭattik kuṟikkum tāram eṉṉum col. tār eṉṉum tiripaṭiyākap piṟantatu. kaṭal tarum pala poruḷ kaṭaṟpaḵṟāram (puṟam. 30).
uyartiṇiyilum iyaṅkutiṇiyāṉa aḵṟiṇiyilum, piḷḷiyi allatu kuṭṭiyi īṉum tāy kuli taḷḷum allatu kāyīṉum marattip pōliruppatāl, tāykkut taḷḷi eṉṟu peyar. oru peṇṭu piḷḷi peṟṟāl, avaḷ pukuntakattiṟku allatu kaṇavaṉ kuṭikku oru piḷḷi tanta tākak karutappaṭuvāḷ. makap peṟutal tarutal eṉappaṭum.
puyaṅkamelāñ cutiyeṉṉumātu tantāḷ  (kampa. caṭāyukāṇ. 28).
piḷḷi peṟuvatil tāy tantiyar iruvar viṉiyuṅ kalan tiruttalāl, avviruvarukkum tā eṉpatu potup peyarām. āyiṉum, īṉṟāḷukku āy, tāy (tam + āy) mutaliya taṉip peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkutalālum, piḷḷiyit tōṟṟu vippavaṉ tantiyēyātalāṉum, tā eṉpatu perumpālum tantiyiyē kuṟikkum.
tātā - tāttā = tantiyiṉ tantiyākiya pāṭṭaṉ. tātā - tāti.
pukunta kuṭikkup piḷḷiyit tarupavaḷ tāyeṉṟum, maṉivikkup piḷḷiyit tarupavaṉ tanti yeṉṟum, vēṟu pāṭaṟiyappaṭum. tanti taruvatu vintu nili; tāy taruvatu piḷḷi nili.
(9) muṟcelavu
nutalutal = muṟkūṟutal, kūṟit toṭaṅkutal.
nutalippukutal = iṉṉatu kūṟuvēṉ eṉṟu kūṟappōkum poruḷic collit toṭaṅkum nūlutti.
tūtu = muṉviṭukkum ceyti. acceyti colpavar, tūtu - tūtaṉ tūtuvaṉ. aracarum kātalarum oruvaroruvariṭic celvati muṉṉaṟivittaṟkāka viṭukkum ceytiyē, mutaṉmutal tūtu eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉpu atu ceyti eṉṉum potup poruḷil vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭatu. kaṇṇaṉ tūtu, aṅkataṉ tūtu mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkuka.

	ūkāraccuṭṭu - 3) muṟcelavu
iyakkattil muṉṉuṟalukku aṭuttatu muṟcelavu. muṉṉatu iṭattu nikaḻvum piṉṉatu iṭam peyarvum ākum.
muṟcelavu, tāṉē cellalum taḷḷappaṭṭuc cellalum eṉa iruvakittu.
(1) muṉtaḷḷal
(i) kāṟṟi mūkkālum vāyālum taḷḷal
uy - uyir. uyirttal = mūccuviṭutal. mūccē uyirnāṭiyum uyirnilikku aṭiyāḷamumātalāl, āvi uyir eṉappaṭṭatu. uḷḷē pōṉāla mūccu, veḷiyē pōṉāl pōccu eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyi nōkkuka. uyiriyum uyirppiyum vaṭanūlār pirāṇaṉ eṉṟu kūṟutaliyuṅ kāṇka. uyttal = muṉ taḷḷal, celuttutal.
kāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuppatum veḷiviṭuvatum ākiya iraṇṭum cērntatē mūccāyiṉum mika veḷippaṭiyāṉatum oli viḷippatum veḷiyiṭuvatē yātaliṉ, mūccu uyirppeṉap paṭṭatu. mūccu viṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkum ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyatē.
untu - unti = pēcumpōtu vāyvaḻik kāṟṟit taḷḷuvatākak karutappaṭum koppūḻiṉ uṭpakkam. koppūḻ.
unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (piṟappiyal 1).
untiyiliruntu eḻuppappaṭum kāṟṟik kuṟikkum utāṉaṉ eṉṉum vaṭacol, utu eṉṉum aṭiyik koṇṭiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
untiyil tōṉṟum utāṉa vaḷippiṟantu
eṉpatu nēminātam (6).
tummutal = mūkku vaḻiyāyk kāṟṟit taḷḷutal.
turattutal = vāyvaḻit tīya kāṟṟi vaṉmiyāyc celuttutal (Cough).
mucumucuttal = kuṟaṭṭi viṭutal.
muka - mūcu. mūcutal = mūcci viṭutal, mōppam piṭittal.
mūcu - mūccu. mūcu - mūñcu - mūñci = mūkku, mūkkuḷḷa mukappakuti (muzzle). 
mūñcutal = mūccu viṭutal, mōppam piṭittal.
mūñcu - mūñcuṟu - mūñcūṟu = mūñci nīṇṭa allatu etiyum mōppam piṭikkiṉṟa eli; teṉṉāṭṭār itaṉi mūñceli eṉpar.
mūñcu - mūñci = nīṇṭa mūkku allatu mukam.
oruvaṉ iḷittuk kaḷitta niliyil virintu valitāy mūccu viṭiṉ, mūcu mūceṉṟu iḷikkiṟāṉ eṉpar. ivvolik kuṟippu oppoliyum cuṭṭoliyum kalantatākum.
muka - muci, mucittal = mūcciḻittal, kaḷittal, iḷittal, melital.
mucippāṟutal = iḷippāṟutal.
(ii) kāṟṟik kiyāl taḷḷal
ūtutal = vāyāl ūtutalpōl kollaṉ turuttiyāṟ kāṟṟit taḷḷutal.

turuttu - turutti = ūtulik kuruku, turutti pōṉṟa tōṟpik kuḻal (Bag-pipe). turutti - tutti - titti.
(iii) piṟa poruḷkaḷik kāl kiyāl taḷḷal
utittal = muṟceluttutal, kālāl muṉ taḷḷutal.
ciliyutitta kōṟ kilakkam  (kampa. kārmuka. 9)
untutal = muṉ taḷḷutal, taḷḷutal.
untu - uñcu - uñcal = untappaṭum tāppici.
uñcal - ūñcal - ūcal.
uṉṉutal = untutal.
(untu - unti = taḷḷit taḷḷip paṟakkum taṭṭāṉ).
ūṅkutal = ūñcalāṭutal. ūkkutal = ūñcalāṭṭutal.
koḻittal = muṉ taḷḷutal. puṭippavaḷ kūlattōṭu kalantuḷḷa maṇṇiyum, nīrali aṭimaṇṇiyum, muṉ taḷḷutal koḻittal eṉappaṭum. 
cuṇṭutal = viralāl muṉ taḷḷutal allatu teṟittal.
(tuḷ) - taḷ. taḷḷutal eṉṉum viṉi mutalāvatu muṉ taḷḷutaliyē kuṟittatu.
tūṇṭutal = viḷakkut tiriyi muṉ taḷḷutal, ūrti vilaṅki muṟceluttutal, ēvalaṉi muṉ taḷḷuvatu pōl oru viṉimēl ēvutal.
tūṇṭu - tīṇṭu. tūṇṭāviḷakku tīṇṭāviḷakku eṉappaṭutal kāṇka.
nuntutal = tiriyit tūṇṭutal. nuntu - nontu. nuntāviḷakku nontāviḷakku eṉavum paṭum.
nūṅku - nūkku. nūkkutal = muṉ taḷḷutal, taḷḷutal.
nūvutal = tiriyit tūṇṭutal, nīri muṉ iṟittup pāyccutal.
nūvu - nīvu; nīvāta tīpam (matāṉ. 140)
(nū) - nī. nīttal = taḷḷutal, celuttutal,

nīkāṉ - nīkāmaṉ - mīkāmaṉ.
(2) nīkkal
vēṇṭāveṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭa poruḷ nīkkap paṭṭatātaliṉ, taḷḷaṟkaruttu nīkkaṟkaruttit taḻuviyatākum.
uttutal = kaḻittal, nīkkutal.
uttu - ottu. ottutal = taḷḷutal. otti vittal= taḷḷi vittal.
otuṅkutal = tāṉē taḷḷatal (ta.vi.) otukkutal = oru poruḷit taḷḷutal, oru kuṟippiṭṭa payaṉukkāka oṉṟit taḷḷi vittal. (pi.vi.)
otuṅkavittal = vaḻiyilum aṟi naṭuvilum uḷḷavaṟṟi oru puṟamākat taḷḷi vittal.
otuṅku - otukku - otukkam.
koḻi - kaḻi. kaḻital = nīṅkutal. kaḻittal = nīkkutal. oru toki yiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoru tokiyi nīkkutal pōṟ kuṟital.
(tuḷ) - taḷ. taḷḷutal = nīkkutal.
tura - tuṟa. tuṟattal = nīkkutal. tuṟa - tuṟavu - tuṟavi = paṟṟu nīkkiyavaṉ.
tuṟa - tuṟakkam = tuṟavāṟpeṟum vīṭu.
o. nō. viṭu - vīṭu = paṟṟa viṭukiyāṟ peṟum pēriṉpam.
tuṟakkam eṉṉum col mutalāvatu vīṭṭiyē kuṟittatu; piṉ, atippōṉṟē viṇṇiluḷḷatum iṉpantuykkap peṟuvatumāṉa (allatu aṅṅaṉam uḷḷatum peṟuvatumākak karutappaṭu vatāṉa) tēvarulakattik kuṟittatu. varkkam eṉṉum vaṭacol cuvar (uyarattiluḷḷatu) eṉṉum pakutiyaṭi yākap piṟantiruppatāl, ati itaṉoṭu mayakkuvatu cariyaṉṟu. mēlēḻulakaṅkaḷuḷ cuvarlōkam eṉpatoṉṟākak kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. cuvar eṉṉuñ colliṉ vērp poruḷi mēṟ celaviyalil uyartaṟ pakutiyiṟ kāṇka.
tūrttal = kuppi kūḷattik kūṭṭit taḷḷutal.
nututtal = nīkkutal, aḻittal, avittal.
iṉṉal nutukkum taṇkaviti vaḷḷal  (vināyaka pu. 46, 4).
neyyāl erinutuppēm eṉṟaṟṟāṟ keḷaviyāl 
kāmam nutuppēm eṉal   (kuṟaḷ, 1158).
(nū) - nī, nīttal = nīkkutal. nīttār = tuṟantār.
(nūṅku) - nīṅku. nūkku - nīkku.
(3) tuḷḷutal
tuḷḷutalāvatu kutittal, atu muṉṉōkkit tuḷḷutalum mēṉōkkit tuḷḷutalum eṉa iruvakittu. mēṉōkkum pōtu mēṟṟiciyum muṉṟiciyāki viṭuvatāl atuvum muṉṟiciyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatē.
ukaḷutal = tāvutal. ukaḷittal = kutittal.
kutittal = tāvutal, tāṇṭutal, kaṭattal kūttāṭutal.
kuti - kutiri = tāvic cellum vilaṅku
kūṟṟaṅ kutittaluṅ kikūṭum  (kuṟaḷ 269)
kutittal kaṭattal (vellutal)
kuti - kūttu - kūttaṉ.
tuḷḷutal = kutittal. tuḷḷal = kūttaṉ.
tuḷ - tuḷḷi - tuḷi = tuḷḷiviḻum nīrttivali, ciṟṟaḷavu. tuḷ - tuḷḷam = tuḷi. tuḷi - tumi.
(tūṇṭu) - tāṇṭu. tāṇṭutal = tāvutal, kutittal, kaṭattal.
tāy eṭṭaṭi tāṇṭiṉāl kuṭṭi patiṉāṟaṭi tāṇṭum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
tāṇṭu - tāṇṭavam = kūttu.
tāṇṭu - tāṇṭakam = 24 eḻuttu ākiya aḷavit tāṇṭic cellum aṭikaḷik koṇṭa ceyyuḷ.
tumukku (te.) = tāṇṭu.
(tūvu) - tāvu. tāvul = tāṇṭutal.
(4) teḷittal
teḷittalāvatu oṉṟi aḷḷi muṉṉāka eṟital allatu iṭutal. nīrit teḷikkumpōtu atu tuḷit tuḷiyākat tuḷḷi viḻutalāl, tuḷḷutaṟ karuttil teḷittaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
ukutal = muṉtuḷḷi viḻutal, cintutal. ukuttal (pi.vi.) = cintutal, tūvutal.
tuḷittal = tuḷi viḻutal (ta.vi.); tuḷikaḷic cintutal, teḷittal, teṟittal, iṟittal, cintutal (pi.vi.).tuḷi - teḷi. teḷittal = tuḷi tuḷiyāyc cintutal; malar, arici mutaliyavaṟṟic ciṟṟaḷavāyt tūvutal.
teḷi - teṟi. teṟittal = tuḷi tuḷḷi viḻutal, tuḷḷutal, viraliṉāl oṉṟi muṉṉōkkic cuṇṭutal allatu tuḷḷac ceytal.
tuḷḷu naṭiyit teṟinaṭi eṉpar.
teṟinaṭi marikkaṇa miriya  (akam. 224)
tāvupu teṟikku māṉ  (puṟam. 259)
tūvutal = teḷittal, iṟittal, citaṟutal.
tūvāṉam = iṟikkum maḻi, teṟikkum maḻittuḷi.
tūval - tuval. tuvalutal = tuḷittal, teḷittal.
tuval - tutali = tuḷi. tuvali - tivali.
tuvali - tuvāli = tuḷi, cūtakap perukku
tuval - tuvaṟu. tuvaṟutal = maḻi tūvutal (ta.vi.)
tuvaṟu - tuvaṟṟu. tuvaṟṟutal = tūvutal (pi.vi.)
tuvaṟu - tūṟu. tuvaṟṟu - tūṟṟu.
tūṟutal = maḻittuḷi viḻutal. tūṟu - tūṟal. tūṟṟu - tūṟṟal.
tūṟṟutal = patariyum maṇiyiyum pirittaṟkuk kūlatti muṉṉāka vāriyiṟittal, maṇṇivāri yiṟittal, paḻic coṟkaḷip parapputal. tūṟal = maḻit tuḷi, paḻiccol.
tūṟu = paḻiccol. avatūṟu = paḻippu.
tuṉṉal = ciṟu tivali.
nīrttivaliyik kuṟittaṟkut tuṉṉal eṉṟoru colluṇ miyālum, teṟikkappaṭum oru vakik kāy tel eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruppa tālum, tuḷ tuṉ eṉṉum īraṭikaṭkum potuvākat tul eṉṟoru vēruṇmi ūkikkappaṭum.
paṟavikaḷ puṇarcci vēḷiyiṟ ciṟappākavum, piṟa vēḷikaḷiṟ potuvākavum, tam uṭampiṉiṉṟu melliṟakukaḷit tūvuvatiṉāl, avaṟṟiṟkut tūvu eṉṉum peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu.
tūvu - tūvi.
tūvu - tūval = iṟaku, iṟakiṉālāṉa eḻutukōl. Pen eṉṉum  āṅkilac collum ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyatē. L. penna, feather.
uku tuḷi tūvu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, mutalāvatu muṉ cintutal eṉṟē poruḷpaṭṭu, piṉpu cintutal eṉṉum potup poruḷi uṇartti varukiṉṟaṉa. nīṇṭa kālakkaṭappiṉāl avaṟṟiṉ muṉmik karuttu iṉṟu maṟintu viṭṭatu. iṉṟum teḷittal viṉi muṉṉaṉṟi vēṟu puṟattil nikaḻāmi kāṇka. 
(5) muṟcellutal
iyaṟkiyāṉa celavellām muṟcelavē.

tuḷḷutal eṉpatu oru muṟi muṉṉōkkit tāvutal; muṟ celavu eṉpatu neṭukac cellutal. itu cellutal celuttu tal ākiya iraṇṭiyun taḻuvum.
uytal = muṉ cellutal, cellutal.
uyttal = celuttutal.

uppoy oḻuki  (puṟam. 116).

ēvu = ampu.

iṉi, eynaṉ - eyiṉaṉ - eyiṉ eṉṟamām.

turam = poṟuppu, talimi.
tura - turi = talivaṉ, ciṟṟaracaṉ.
muṉ. muṉṉutal = muṟpaṭutal, cellutal.
muḷ - (muṭu) - viṭu. viṭuttal = muṟ celuttutal, aṉupputal, viṭu - viṭi - viṭiyil - viṭiyilāṉ.
viṭu - viṭuppu. viṭi = celavu, cella uttaravu.
muḷ - (muy) - (muyam) - viyam = muṟceluttutal, celuttutal, ēvutal tūṇṭutal.
viyaṅkoḷ - viyaṅkōḷ = ēval, matippēval.
viyaṅkoḷḷutal = māṭu kutiri mutaliya vilaṅkit tūṇṭic celuttutal, tēroṭṭutal, tūṇṭutal, ēvutal.
tērviyaṅkoṇṭa pattu eṉṉum aiṅkuṟu nūṟṟup pirivup peyari nōkkuka.

(6) virivu (tuṭukku)
virivu allatu vēkam eṉpatu, oppu nōkkic collum uṟavup paṇpākum. oṉṟaṉ celavit taṉippaṭa vēkam eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. māṭṭu vaṇṭiyiviṭak kutiri vaṇṭiyum, kutiri vaṇṭiyi viṭa mitivaṇṭiyum, mitivaṇṭiyiviṭap pukivaṇṭiyum, pukivaṇṭiyiviṭa iyaṅki (Motor) vaṇṭiyum, vēkamākac cellum, orē vaṇṭiyi eṭuttuk koḷḷiṉum, ataṉ meṉ celavoṭu oppu nōkkiyē ataṉ kaṭuñcelavu vēkam eṉappaṭum.
ākavē, iyaṅkum poruḷ oṉṟāyiṉum palavāyiṉum, oṉṟiviṭa iṉṉoṉṟum orucelaviviṭa iṉṉoru celavum muṉ celvatē vēkam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. ātalāl, muṟcelavu eṉpatu iyalpāṉa celaveṉṟum, oru celaviṉum iṉṉoru celavu muṟpaṭuvatē vēkam eṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
virivu eṉpatu oru viṉiyi muntit toṭaṅki munti muṭippatiyum, vēkam eṉpatu oruviṉi ceytaṟkaṇ oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkuriya viṉi vītattiyum, kuṟikkum.
(i) lakara ṉakara vetuki
ol - olleṉa = virivāka.
oy - oyyeṉa = virivāka.
tuḷḷutal = virital. tuḷ - tuṇ - tuṇṭi = tuṭukkāṉavaṉ.
tuṇṇeṉal = virital.
tuṉital = virital.
tuval = virivu, tuvalutal = virital.
poḷ - poḷḷeṉa = virivāka.
muḷ - muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = virintu cellutal.
kaṭaṟpaṭi kuḷippa maṇṭi  (puṟam 6)
(muḷ) = (muy) - muyal = vēkamāka ōṭum ciṟu vilaṅku vaki.
(muḷ) - (maḷ) - maḻa - maḻamaḻa (virivuk kuṟippu)
(mul) - (mal) - val = virivu. vallē = virivāka.
val - valli = virivu.
oṉṟiṉa vallē ceyiṟ ceyka  (nālaṭi. 4.)
(ii) rakara vetuki
curu - karukku = virivu curukkāy vā eṉpatu vaḻakku.

caru - carēl (vi.ku.). caru - cara - caracara (vi.ku.).
cara - caraṭṭu (vi.ku.).
tura - turi = vēkam. turattal = āṇiyi muṭukkutal. tura - turattu (pi.vi.) turattutal = muṭukkutal, vēkamāy ōṭṭutal.
(puru) - para - parapara - paraparappu.
(puru) - paru - pari = vēkam. vēkamāṉa celavu, vēkamākac celluṅ kutiri. parital = ōṭutal.
(puru) - poru - porukku (vi.ku.) porukkeṉṟeḻuttāṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. porukku - pokku (kocci vaḻakku). porukka = virivāka.
(muru) - viru - viruviru - viruviruppu = virivu. viruviruveṉṟu pō eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
viru - viruṭṭu (vi.ku.).
viru - vira - viri - virivu. viri - virici.
(iii) ṟakara vetuki
kuṟu - kuṟukuṟu. kuṟu kuṟuttal = vēṇṭātatic ceyya virital. kuṟu - kuṟumpu = cēṭṭi.
cuṟu - cuṟucuṟu - cuṟucuṟuppu. cuṟu - cuṟuti = vēkam.
tuṟu - tuṟutuṟu. tuṟutuṟuttavaṉ - vēṇṭāviṉi allatu kuṟumpu ceytu koṇṭēyiruppavaṉ.
(nūṟu) - noṟu - noṟil = virivu.
(muṟu) - muṟukku = vēkam, tuṭukku.
(iv) ṭakara vetuki
(uṭu) - (oṭu) - ōṭu. ōṭutal = virintu cellutal.
kuṭu - kuṭukuṭu (vi.ku.). kuṭukuṭuveṉṟu ōṭukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.  kuṭukuṭuttāṉ = virivāḷaṉ (avacarakkāraṉ).

kāleṉakkaṭukkuṅ kaviṉpeṟu tērum (maturik. 388)

kaṭukutal = virital.

emmampu kaṭi viṭutum  (puṟam. 9.)  (mu.tā.)

tuṭu - tuṭukku = vēkam, tuṇivu, kuṟumpu. tuṭu - tuṭum (vi.ku.). tuṭu - tiṭu - tiṭum (vi; ku.). tiṭu - tiṭīr (vi.ku.) tuṭu - tuṭi - tuṭippu + oṉṟic ceyya virital. tuṭittal = virintu aṭittuk koḷḷutal.
(nuṭu) - noṭu - noṭunoṭu. noṭunoṭuttal = tuṭukkā yiruttal. noṭu - noṭukku (vi.ku.).
(puṭu) - poṭu - poṭukku (vi.ku.). poṭukkeṉṟu pōy viṭṭāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka. poṭu - poṭupoṭu (vi.ku.).
(puṭu) - paṭu) - paṭa - paṭapaṭa - paṭapaṭappu.
paṭa-paṭakku. paṭakkuppaṭakkeṉal = tuṭittal, accattāl neñcam tuṭittal.
muṭu - muṭuku - muṭukku, muṭukutal = virital.
muṭukkutal = vēkamāy ōṭṭutal.
muṭukuvaṇṇam = virintu cellum kuṟiviṇi vaṇṇam.
muṭukku - moṭukku (vi.ku.).
muṭu - moṭu - moṭumoṭu (vi.ku.).

kutu - kutukutu - kutukutuppu = viripu.

vicu - vici = vēkam.
(7) (naṭukkam) accam
uṭalum neñcāṅkuliyum virintu acivatu accattik kuṟikkumātalāl, virivuk karuttil accak karuttup piṟantatu.
tuḷ - tuṇ. tuṇṇeṉal = tiṭukkiṭutal, añcutal. 
tuṇ - tuṇukku. tuṇukkiṭutal = tiṭukkiṭutal. tuṇukku - tuṇukkam = naṭukkam, accam.
tuṇ - tiṇ - tiṭu - tiṭukku.
nuṭu - naṭu - naṭuṅku - naṭukku - naṭukkam.
vitu - vitir - vitirppu = naṭukkam. vitirvitirppu = naṭunaṭukkam. vitu - vitukku. vitukku vitukkeṉal = accattāl neñcam paṭakkup paṭakkeṉṟu aṭittuk koḷḷutal, iṅṅaṉam aṭittuk koḷḷutali verukku verukkeṉṟirukkiṉṟatu eṉpar. vitukku - virukku, - verukku - veruvu - veru = accam. veruvutal = añcutal. veru - veruḷ - veruḷi = accuṟuttum
pulluru. veruḷ - viraḷ - miraḷ. viraḷ - viraṭṭu. viraṭṭutal = accuṟuttutal, accuṟutti vēkamāka ōṭṭutal.
(8) vīcutal
oruvaṉ vēkamāy naṭakkumpōtu, avaṉ ki vēkamāy vīcuvatālum, aṅṅaṉam vīcumpōtu atu nīḷuvatālum, vēkamāy naṭappatik kuṟikkum vicu eṉṉum col, vēkamāy oṉṟi vīcuvatiyum nīṭciyiyuṅ kuṟikkum. coṟkaḷip piṟappittuḷḷatu.
vicu - viciṟu - viciṟi - civiṟi. viciṟutal = vēkamāy vīcutal, vīci yeṟital.
vicu - vicukku. vicukkutal = virintu kivīcutal. viciṟutal. vicukku vicukkeṉṟu naṭakkiṟāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku.
vicu - vīcu. vīcutal = vīciyeṟital, vārikkoṭuttal, nīḷutal, mikutal, nīḷavācaṉi varutal.
vīcu - vīccu = vēkam, vīcutal. ūñcalāṭṭu, nīḷam, mikuti.
vīccāy naṭakkiṟāṉ, kivīccup peritāyirukkiṟatu, ūñcali oru vīccu āṭṭiṉāṉ, vīṭu vīccāyirukkiṟatu, vīccāykkoṭu eṉpaṉa, muṟiyē mēṟkuṟitta poruḷkaṭkeṭuttukkāṭṭām. 
vīccu - vīccam = nāṟṟam.
kāṟṟāṉatu nīṇṭu celvatāl, ataṉoṭu kalanta vācaṉiyum nīṇṭu celkiṉṟateṉka. vīcutal eṉṉum col vaḻakkil tīya nāṟṟattiyē kuṟikkum.
vīcu - vīci = nīṇṭuvaḷarum mēlutaṭṭu mayir. talimayirum tāṭiyum nīṇṭu vaḷarvaṉavēyāyiṉum, kuṟukkāka nīṇṭu vaḷarvatu mīciyoṉṟē yātaliṉ, atu vīciyeṉappaṭṭatu.
vīci - mīci.
oru cila coṟkaṭkuk kaṟṟōr vaḻaṅkum vaṭiviṉum kallār vaḻaṅkum vaṭivē muntiṉatāyuḷḷatu.
(9) ūkkutal
ūkkutal ūkkamuṇṭākkutal. ūkkamāvatu muyaṟcikkētu vāṉa muṉippu. oruvaṉ taṉ maṉattālēṉum piṟaṉālēṉum oruviṉi ceyyat tūṇṭappaṭṭa viṭattē, avaṉiṭattu ataṟkuriya muyaṟci piṟakkum. tūṇṭutal eṉpatu muṟceluttutal. oru kutiri muṟcellumāṟu ataṉi ūrpavaṉ atit tūṇṭiviṭuvatu pōṉṟatē. oruvaṉ oru viṉi ceyyumāṟu avaṉatu uḷḷam avaṉit tūṇṭiviṭuvatum,
uḷ - uḷḷam = ūkkam. uḷḷutal = ūkkutal.
uḷḷa muṭimi yuṭimi poruḷuṭimi
nillātu nīṅki viṭum
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvaṉār ūkkamuṭimi yatikārattil (592) kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. (uḷḷam eṉpatip parimēlaḻakar āku peyarākak koṇṭatu poruntātu. maṉattik kuṟikkum uḷḷam eṉṉum collum, ūkkattik kuṟikkum uḷḷam eṉṉum collum, vevvēṟām. maṉattik kuṟippatu uḷḷiruppatu eṉṟum, ūkkattik kuṟippatu muṟceluttuvatu eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum).  (mu.tā.)
uḷ - (uy) - (uyal) - uñal - uñaṟṟu.
uñaṟṟutal = uḷḷan tūṇṭutal, muyaṟci ceytal, varunti yuḻittal.
muṉ - muṉi, muṉital = muṟpaṭutal, muyaṟciceytal, ūkkaṅ koḷḷutal. muṉi - muṉippu.
muḷ - (muy) - muyal - muyaṟci. muyal - muyaṟṟu. muyalutal = muṉintuḻittal.
muka - mucumucu - mucumucuppu = ūkkam.
	ūkārac cuṭṭu - 4) mēṟcelavu
tiṟanta veḷiyil nilamaṭṭattiliruntu uyirikaḷ muṟcellak kūṭiya ticikaḷ, pakkattici eṭṭum mēṟṟici oṉṟumāka oṉpatām. avaṟṟuḷ, makkaḷum maṟṟap paṟavāvuyirikaḷum muṟcellakkūṭiya ticikaḷ pakkatticiyākiya eṭṭē, paṟavi oṉṟē oṉpāṉ ticiyum muṟcellak kūṭiyatākum.
nilattilum nīrilumiruntu mēleḻum poruḷkaṭkellām muṟcela veṉpatu mēṟcelavē. mēṟcelavu muṟcelavil oruvakiyēyātalāl, muṟcelavik kuṟikkum ūkāramē mēṟcelaviyum uṇarttum eṉka. mēṟcellutal allatu mēṉmēṟcellutal eṉpatu, muṟcelaviyum micic celaviyum potuppaṭak kuṟittal kāṇka. ēṇ cēṇ mutaliya coṟkaḷ uyarattiyum toliviyum potuppaṭak kuṟittaliyum nōkkuka.
(1) eḻutal
eḻutalāvatu kiḷartalum tuḷḷutalum. tuḷḷutal eṉpatu muṉṉōkkit tuḷḷutalum mēṉōkkit tuḷḷutalum eṉa iruvakittu.
uṉṉutal = uyaraveḻutal.

ucumputal = uṟaṅkiṉavaṉ mella uṭampacitteḻutal. ucumpu - ucuppu (piṟaviṉi).
ukalutal = aliyeḻutal.
ukaḷutal = kutittal, ukittal = eḻutal, eḻupputal, uyarak kutittal.
utittal = uyaraveḻumputal;
kutittal = uyaraveḻumputal. kuti - koti. kontaḷittal = kaṭal kiḷarnteḻutal.
tuḷḷutal = kutittal.
tuḷ - tuḷumpu; tuḷumputal = mīṉ nīrmaṭṭattiṟku mēl mellat tuḷḷutal.
tuḷumpu - taḷumpu. taḷumputal = kalam acivatāl nīr ciṟitu tuḷḷutal. taḷumpu - tatumpu. taḷumpu - taḷampu.
tuḷumpu - tuṭumpu. tuṭumputal = tatumputal;
tuḷ - taḷ - taḷa - taḷataḷa - taḷataḷi - tattaḷi.  
taḷa - taḷi. taḷittal = kotittal.
poṅkutal = eḻutal, kotittal, mikutal.
muḷ - muḷi; muḷital - poṅkutal.
muḷ - muḷu. muḷuttal = uliyarici mellat tuḷḷutal.
muḷ - muṇṭu. muṇṭukal = tuḷḷutal, viti muḷi nilat tuḷḷiruntu muṭṭiyeḻutal.
mucumucuttal = nīr kotittal.
(2) puḷittup poṅkutal
cūṭṭiṉāṟ poṅkuvatu pōṉṟu puḷippiṉālum cila poruḷkaḷ poṅkiyeḻum.
ukiṉ - ekiṉ = puḷi. ukiṉ - ukiṉam - ekiṉam = puḷi.
nura - nuri = puḷippiṉāl eḻum ciṟu kumiḻi, ataṉ niṟamāṉa veṇmi. nuri veṇmiyāyiruntal kāṇka. nuri - nari = veṇmi. narittal = veḷuttal.
nariyāṉ puṟattiṭṭa cūṭu  (paḻamoḻi 48)
nutampu = kaḷ. notittal = puḷittup poṅkutal.
(puḷ) puḷi = puḷittup poṅkuvatu, puḷippuccuvi, puḷippup paḻam. puḷittal = puḷittup poṅkutal. puḷikka vitta mā eḻumpiyiruttalik koṇṭē atu puḷittu viṭṭateṉṟu aṟintu koḷḷutal kāṇka.
puḷi - puḷicci = puḷicci.
(puḷ) - (pura) - puri - piri = puḷitta mōr, pālip puḷikka vikkum mōr.
poṅku - poṅkal = kaḷ. poruṇi = kaḷ. peruku = tayir.
(muḷ) - muḷi. muḷital = poṅkutal. muḷitayir = naṉṟāyt tōynta tayir.
muṇṭakam = kaḷ. (muḷ) - mura - murappu. murattal = puḷittal. mura - mōr.
muramureṉavē puḷitta mōrum eṉṟu auviyār kūṟutal kāṇka.
mura - muri = nuri.
mukiṉi = puḷi.
(3) uvarttup poṅkutal
uvar nilamum poṅkiyeḻum.
(uḷam) - uḻam. uḻamaṇ = uvarmaṇ. (uḷam) - aḷam = uppu.
umaṇ = uppu. umaṇ - umaṇaṉ.
upputal = poṅkiyeḻutal. uppu = uvarttup poṅkiyeḻum cattu, uvarppuk kal.
uvar - uvari = uvar nīrkkaṭal.
(cuvar) - cavar. cavarttal = uvarttal.
poṅkutal - nilam uvartteḻutal.
porumutal - uḷam uvartteḻutal.
(4) uyarcci
uṭampāl uyartalum nilimiyāl uyartalum iṭattāl uyartalum eṉa uyartal mūvaki.
ū - u - mēl, uyarnta.
e.ṭu. uttuṅkaḷ.
uttari, uttariyam, uttūḷaṉam mutaliya pala vaṭacoṟkaḷil muṉṉoṭṭāyiruntu, uyarcci allatu mēluṟavuk karutti uṇarttuvatu ukaramē.
ulli - matil.
uḷ = mēṉmi.
ukattal = uyartal.
ukappē uyarvu  (tol. uriyiyal 8).
ukittal = uyaraveḻumputal.  (mu.tā.)
uccam - uyarcci, ucci = uccamāṉa iṭam. uttaram = uyarvu, uyaravirukkum viṭṭam, uyarnta vaṭatici.
kumarimali mūḻkip paṉimali eḻuntapiṉ, vaṭatici uyarntatu; teṉṟici tāḻntatu. itaṉāl, avi muṟiyē uttaram takkaṇam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. uttaram (u+taram) = uyarvu. takkaṇam (takku + aṇam) = tāḻvu.
upputal = eḻutal, paruttal, vīṅkutal. uppucam = vayiṟṟup porumal.
umpar = mēl, mēliṭam, ākāyam, tēvarulakam, tēvar.
umparāṉ = uyarniliyiliruppavaṉ.
umparam = mēliṭam, ākāyam, tēvarulakam.
umpal = uyaramāṉa vilaṅkākiya yāṉi.
uyar - uyaram, uyarvu, uyarcci, uyartti.
uvaṇam = uyarap paṟakkum paruntu, uvaṇam - uvaṇaṉ.
uyara uyarap paṟantālum ūrkkuruvi paruntākumā? eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
uvaṇi = tēvarulakam.
uvaccaṉ - ōccaṉ - ōcaṉ = teyvatti ēttupavaṉ.
uṟi = uyaram.
uṉ = uyarnta, mikunta.
v.L.: uṉmattam.
uṉṉutal = mūccaṭakki eḻutal, uyarak kutittal, uṉ - uṉṉatam.
uṉṉippu = uyaram;
ūṅku = uyarvu, mikuti;
ūrtal = ēṟutal, ēṟic cellutal, ūr - ūrti.
ūrttam = mēl > ūrtvam (Urdhva) - va.
oyyal = uyarcci. oyyāram = uyarnili.
oyil = oyyāram, uyarakkutittāṭum kummi.
ōṅku = ōṅkal = yāṉi, mali. ōṅku - ōkku - ōkkam = uyaram, perumi.
ōccutal = uyarttutal. ōccu - ōccam = uyarvu, kīrtti.
kaṭitōcci mella veṟika  (kuṟaḷ 572).
opputal = uyarttutal.
ōmputal = uṭal uyarumāṟu vaḷarttal, pēṇutal, kāttal.
orki = yāṉi.
ōvar = ēttāḷar.

(10) taṉmippeyar
māntaṉukku iyalpāka nāṉ eṉṉum akaṅkāra miruttalālum, avaṉ taṉṉalattiyē muṉṉalamākap pēṇutalālum, uyarcci kuṟikkum ēkārattiṉaṭiyākat taṉmip peyari amittuk koṇṭāṉ muṉṉit tamiḻaṉ eṉka.
orumi 	paṉmi
ēṉ	ēm (mutal nili)
yāṉ	yām (2 ām nili)
nāṉ	nām (3 ām nili)
vantēṉ vantēm eṉṉum taṉmi viṉikaḷil. ēṉ ēm eṉṉum taṉmip peyarkaḷ iṉṟum vikutiyāy vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. ivi piṟkālattil piṉvarumāṟu tirintaṉa.
ēṉ - eṉ - aṉ - al.
ēm - em.
ēm - ām - ōm
em - am - ōm
yām nām eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vēraḷavil oṉṟē yāyiṉum, tiripu vēṟupāṭu kāraṇamākavum vacati nōkkiyum. muṟiyē taṉittaṉmip paṉmiyākavum uḷappāṭṭut taṉmip paṉmiyākavum āḷappaṭṭaṉa.
muṉṉiliyil nī nīm (nīr) nīṅkaḷ eṉavum, paṭarkkiyil avaṉ avar avarkaḷ eṉavum, iḻiloppuyarvu ākiya munnili paṟṟiya peyarkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭamiyālum; taṉmiyilum. aracarum iṟivaṉum tammi yām eṉṟē cuṭṭuvatākak kūṟuvatu marapātalālum, yām nām eṉṉum oṟṟip paṉmip peyarkaḷiṉiṉṟu yāṅkaḷ nāṅkaḷ eṉṉum iraṭṭip paṉmip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa (4 ām nili).
mutalāvatu, yāṅkaḷ eṉpatu taṉittaṉmip paṉmiyākavum, nāṅkaḷ eṉpatu uḷappāṭṭut taṉmip paṉmiyākavum, vaḻaṅkiṉa. piṉpu, yāṅkaḷ eṉṉuñ col vaḻakkaṟṟup pōy viṭṭamiyāl, ataṟkup patilāka nāṅkaḷ eṉpatu tavaṟāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu (5 ām nili).
vēṟṟumit tiripu
eḻuvāy 	vēṟṟumiyaṭi
ēṉ, yāṉ	eṉ
ēm, yām	em
nāṉ	naṉ
nām	nam
yāṅkaḷ	eṅkaḷ
nāṅkaḷ	naṅkaḷ
tamiḻil, naṉ eṉpatu iruvaki vaḻakkilum, naṅkaḷ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakkilum, aṟṟaṉa. naṅkaḷ kōṉ vacutēvaṉ eṉṟu tirumaṅkiyāḻvār kūṟutal kāṇka.
teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum naṉ eṉṉum aṭi iṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.

(11) viṉāccol
oruvaṉi evaṉ eṉṟu viṉāvuvatu, ōr iṭattiluḷḷa pala poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟi uyara eṭuttukkāṭṭi ituvā eṉṟu kēṭpatu pōliruttalāl, viṉāc collum eḻucci allatu uyarcci kuṟitta ēkārac collaṭiyākap piṟantuḷḷatu.

ē - yā - ā. ēvar - yāvar - yār - ār.
ē - ā - ō. vantāṉā? vantāṉō? (5 ām nili)
peyar : ēvaṉ, ēvaḷ, ēvar, ētu, ēvi.
evaṉ, evaḷ, evar, entu, etu, eṉṉatu, ev, evi, eṉṉa.
yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar, yātu, yā, yāvi;
yāvar - yār, yāvatu - yātu.

ēṉ 					(kāraṇam)
ē e yā eṉṉum mūṉṟē viṉāvaṭikaḷāy amivatiyum, ā ō eṉṉum iraṇṭum īṟākavaṉṟi vēṟāka varāmiyiyum, nōkkuka. e ēyiṉ kuṟukkam; yā ataṉ tiripu. ātalāl, mūlaviṉāvaṭi ē eṉṉum oṉṟē.
(12) uyarcci kuṟikkum akara mutaṟ coṟkaḷ
u-a-ā.
(ukittal = uyaraveḻumputal. uki - aki.) akittal = uyarttutal.
(uḷ = mēṉmi. uḷ - (uṇ) - aṇ.)

aṇpal = mēlvāyppal.

aṇṇal = uyarvu, uyarntōṉ, aracaṉ, kaṭavuḷ,
aṇṇaṉ = periyōṉ, mūttōṉ, tamiyaṉ.

(kuti-kati). katittal = eḻutal.
(13) kuravar peyar
oruvaṉukku uyarntōrāṉa aiṅkuravaruḷḷum iru mutukuravar mikac ciṟantavarātaliṉ, avarik kuṟittaṟku uyarvu kuṟitta ukarac cuṭṭiṉ tiripāṉa akaratti aṭiyākak koṇṭa pala coṟkaḷ kiḷittuḷḷaṉa.
tanti	 	tāy
—— 		akki
accaṉ		acci
attaṉ		atti
appaṉ		ammi (avvi)
—— 		aṉṉi, aṉi (aṇi)
		aṉṉi (aññi), aṉi
āñaṉ		—— 	
———— 		ātti
  —— 		āy
kuṟippu : (1) kōṭiṭṭa iṭattiṟkuriya coṟkaḷ vaḻakkaṟṟaṉa pōlum.
(2) tāyik kuṟikkum cila coṟkaḷ aṉ īṟu peṟṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
v.L.: 		akki - akkaṉ
			ammi - ammaṉ
ivaṟṟiluḷḷa aṉ īṟu orukāl maṟaṅkuṟikka vantirukkalām.
(3) tāyik kuṟikkum pala coṟkaḷ muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟum peṟātum tamakkiyiyum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.

e.ṭu. 	tāy 		tamakki
		akki	akki
				tamakki
		acci		acci
		avvi	tavvi (tamavvi)
tanti peyarum iṅṅaṉam tamiyaṉik kuṟikkum.
e.ṭu. aiyaṉ - tamiyaṉ.
(4) kuravar peyarkaḷ cila vikutimāṟi avarkaḷiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantārik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. attaṉ - atti (tantiyōṭuṭaṉ piṟantavaḷ)
ammi - ammāṉ (tāyōṭuṭaṉ piṟantavaṉ)
(5) tanti tāy peyar muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟum varum.
tam + appaṉ = tamappaṉ - takappaṉ.
(tam + āy = tāy)
(6) kuravar peyarkaḷuṭ cila piṟavaṟṟiṉ tiripākat terikiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. 	attaṉ - accaṉ
		ammi - avvi
		aṉṉi - aññi
(7) āṇpāl kuṟikkum aṉṉīṟum peṇpāl kuṟikkum ikara vikāra vīṟukaḷum, mutaṟkālattil kuravar peyarkaḷoṭu cērntiruntatākat teriyavilli.
e.ṭu. 	appaṉ - appu
		ammi - ammu
(8) kuravar peyarkaḷ pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyarik kuṟittaṟkup pāl mayaṅkātu mīmiciyāka aṭukki varuvatuṇṭu.
e.ṭu.	appaccu (appacci) = pāṭṭaṉ.
		ammācci = pāṭṭi.
tāy peyarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu tāyiyē kuṟippatu muṇṭu.
e.ṭu.	ammaṇi (ammaṇi), ammaṉi.
tāypeyar pāṭṭiyik kuṟippatumuṇṭu.
e.ṭu.	avvi = tāy, pāṭṭi.
(9) ammi appaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, tamiḻ nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi ulaka meṅkaṇum avvan nāṭṭiṟ kēṟpat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
(10) mēṟkāṭṭiya irumutukuravar muṟip peyarkaḷi, vitappāka ammi appaṉ eṉpavaṟṟi, kuḻavi vaḷarppoli kaḷiṉ pāṟpaṭuttuvar mēli moḻi nūlvallār. āyiṉ, aṇṇaṉ eṉṉum peyar uyarntōṉukkum tami yaṉukkum potuvāyiruttalālum, aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar mutalāvatu periyōṉik kuṟittup piṉpu tirintum tiriyātum aiṅkura variyum potuppaṭac cuṭṭuvatālum, ammi appaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ mūppum arumiyum paṟṟip piṟarukkum pāl tavaṟātu vaḻaṅkutalālum, uyarcci kuṟikkum ukaravaṭiyiṉ tiripāṉa akara vaṭiyum uyarcci kuṟittalālum, avaṟṟic cuṭṭolikaḷiṉ pāṟpaṭuttuvatē cālacciṟappuṭittām.
āriya moḻikaḷellām tiripuṭi moḻikaḷātaliṉ, avaṟ ṟit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭa mēṉāṭṭār ammi appaṉ eṉṉum coṟkaḷi muṟiyē mā pā eṉṉuṅ kuḻavi yoli kaḷiṉiṉṟu tirintaviyākak koḷvatu viyappaṉṟu.
(14) piṉmi
kuṟukkāka vaḷarum vilaṅku paṟavikaṭku uṭampiṉ uccamāṉa iṭam allatu mēṟpuṟam mutukātaliṉ, ukaraccuṭṭiṟkup piṉmik karuttum ciṟupāṉmi urittām.
mēlum, kālamuṉ iṭamuṉ eṉa muṉmi iru vakippaṭṭu muṉmik karuttil piṉmik karuttum piṉmik karuttil muṉmik karuttum mayaṅkutalāl, ataṉālum muṉmic cuṭṭu piṉmik karuttit taḻuvuvatām. iṉi, mēl eṉṉuñ col uccam muṉmi piṉmikaḷi uṇarttuvatiyuṅ kāṇka. 
e.ṭu. mēl = uccattil, mēṉāḷ = muṉṉāḷ, mēl = piṉpu, ataṉ mēl = ataṉ piṉpu.
uppakkam = piṉpakkam.
uttaram = piṉcollum maṟumoḻi.
uttaram - uttaravu = vēṇṭukōḷiṉ piṉtarum āṇi allatu viṭi.
ummi = piṟkālam (etirkālam)
.................................................................................................. ummi
erivāy nirayattu vīḻvarkol eṉṟu
parivatūum cāṉṟōr kaṭaṉ
eṉpatu nālaṭiyār. (58)


	ūkārac cuṭṭu - 5) neruṅkal
(1) aṇukal
muṉṉōkkic cellum uyirikaḷ tām aṭiyak karutiya iṭatti mutalāvatu aṇukum. karuttilātu nīṇṭu cellum oru marakkiḷiyum kūṭa aṇmiyiluḷḷa oru poruḷi iyaṟkiyāka aṭukkum.
neruṅkal eṉpatu, toṭṭu neruṅkalum toṭātu neruṅ kalum eṉa irutiṟattatātalāl. ivviyal muṟcelaviyaliyum toṭutaliyaliyum arimā nōkkākat taḻuvum.
(uḷ) - aḷ. aḷḷutal = neruṅkutal.
aḷ - aṇ - aṇuku - aṇukkam.
aṇ - aṇṭu - aṇṭi. aṇ - aṇmu.
aṇ - aṭu.
aḷ - aru - aruku.
uṟutal = neruṅkutal.
kuḷ - kuḻu - keḻu - keḻumu. keḻumutal = kiṭṭutal.
(kuṭṭu) - kiṭṭu. kiṭṭutal = neruṅkutal.
kiṭṭu - kiṭṭam = aṇmi. kiṭṭa = neruṅka.
tuṉṉutal = neruṅkutal. tuṉṟutal = neruṅkutal.
tuṟutal = neruṅkutal.
tūrtal = neruṅkutal.
(nuḷ) - naḷ - naṇ - naṇuku.
(nuḷ) - (neḷ) - neru - neruṅku.
muṭṭutal = neruṅkutal. kiṭṭamuṭṭa eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. muṭṭaṭi = aṇmi.
muṇṭutal = neruṅkutal. muṇṭu - maṇṭu. 
maṇṭutal = neruṅkutal.
(2) ceṟital
iruporuḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟaṇukutal neruṅkalum, pala poruḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟaṇukutal ceṟitalum ākum. neruṅ kaliṉum ceṟivu mika aṇukkamākum.
(uḷ) - aḷ = ceṟivu.
uṟu - uṟa - uṟappu = ceṟivu.
(cuṟu) - ceṟu; ceṟuttal = ceṟital.
ceṟutta ceyyuḷ  (pulam. 53)
ceṟu - ceṟi - ceṟivu.
tuṉṉutal = ceṟital.
tutital = ceṟital. tuti - tati.
tuvaṉṟutal = ceṟital.
tuṟutal = ceṟital. tuṟu - tuṟavu - tuṟumu - tuṟumpu. tuṟumutal = ceṟital. tuṟumputal = ceṟital.
nuḷ - naḷ - naḷi = ceṟivu. naḷḷutal = ceṟital.
naḷiyeṉ kiḷavi ceṟivum ākum  (tol. uriyiyal, 25)
potuḷutal = ceṟital. muḷ - muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭu = neruṅkutal, ceṟital. muṟu - viṟu - veṟu. veṟuttal = ceṟital.
viṟu - viṟa - viṟappu = ceṟivu.
viṟappum uṟappum veṟuppum ceṟivē  (tol. uriyiyal, 49)
muṭi - miṭi. miṭital = ceṟital.
moyttal = ceṟital.
(3) oṭuṅkal
oṭuṅkalāvatu nerukkamātal. iru allatu pala poruḷkaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu neruṅkumpōtu nerukkam uṇṭākum. ataṉāl oṭukkam ēṟpaṭum. oṭukkam iṭiveḷiyoṭukkamum,  poruḷoṭukkamum, oḻukkavoṭukkamum, eṉa mūvaki.
ul - ulli = oṭuṅkiyāḷ. ul-ullāṭi = ulli. ulli - olli. ul - ol - ollaṭṭi = olli.
ul - ulku - (uṟku) - uṟkam - ukkam = oṭuṅkiya iṭi.
ul - ol - olku. olkutal = curuṅkutal; olku - oṟkam = oṭukkam. curukkam. taḷarcci.
ul - al - alku - alkul = curuṅkiya iṭi. alkutal = curukkutal. alku - aḵku. aḵkutal = curuṅkutal.
uḷ - oḷ - oru - oruku - orukku. orukkutal = oṭukkutal. orukku - erukku.
uḷ - uṭu - uṭuku - uṭukku = iṭiyiloṭuṅkiya kōṭaṅki.
uṭukku - uṭukki.
uṭuku - iṭuku. iṭukutal = neruṅkutal.
uṭukku - iṭukku - iṭukkam = nerukkam.
iṭukku = ciṟu cantu. iṭukku - iṭukkal.
iṭukkutal = nerukkutal, nerukkip piṭittal allatu ēntutal. piḷḷiyi iṭuppil iṭukkutal eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
iṭukku = iṭukki = nerukkip piṭikkuṅ karuvi.
uṭu - iṭu - iṭi = oṭuṅkiya maruṅkul. iṭu - iṭuppu.
iṭu - iṭukkaṇ = oṭukki allatu nērukki varuttum tuṉpam.
iṭu - iṭar. iṭarppaṭutal = tuṉpattāl allatu vaṟumiyāl nerukkuṇṇutal.
iṭu - iṭaṅku - iṭaṅkar = ciṟu vaḻi.
uḷ - iḷ - iṇṭu = ciṟiya iṭukku. iṇṭum iṭukkum eṉpatu vaḻakku.
iḷ - iṭṭu - iṭṭitu = oṭuṅkiṉatu. ciṟiyatu. iṭṭiṭi = ciṟiya iṭiveḷi. iṭṭēṟi = ēṟṟamāṉa ciṟiya vaṇṭippāti. 
iṭṭiki = ciṟu ceṅkal.
uṭu - oṭu - oṭuṅku - oṭukku - oṭukkam.
oṭuṅku - aṭaṅku - aṭakku - aṭakkam.
aṭaṅkutal = oṭuṅkiyamital. aṭakka voṭukkam eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
uṟa - uṟaṅku - uṟakku - uṟakkam. uṟaṅkutal = oṭuṅkit tūṅkutal. ūraṭaṅkutal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
(kuṭṭu) - kiṭṭu - kiṭṭi = keṇṭikkāli nerukkuṅ karuvi.
kuḷ - kuṭku - kukku. kukkutal = oṭuṅkutal.
kuḷ - kuṭu - koṭu - koṭuku. koṭukutal = oṭuṅkutal.
koṭu - (kōṭu) - kōṭaṅki - kōṭāṅki = uṭukku.
cuḷ - ceḷ - ceṟi. ceṟittal = nerittal, aṭakkutal.
nuḷ - neḷ - neru - neruṅku - nerukku - nerukkam. neru - neri. nerittal = neruṅkutal.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭuku - muṭukku = ciṟu cantu.
muṭukku - muṭukkar.
muḷ - (maḷ) - vaḷ = nerukkam.
	ūkāraccuṭṭu - 6) toṭutal
neruṅkalukku aṭuttu nikaḻakkūṭiya toṭarpu toṭutal. muṟcellum uyirikaḷ tām aṭiyak karutiya poruḷkaḷi aṭuttapiṉ avaṟṟit toṭum; taṟceyalāka etirppaṭṭa poruḷkaḷiyum toṭalām. iṅkut toṭutal eṉpatu kiyiṉāl toṭuvati maṭṭumaṉṟu, uṭampālum kuṇattālum toṭuvatiyum kuṟikkum.
(1) toṭutal tuṟi
melitāyt toṭutal
(uḷ) - aḷ - aḷavu - aḷāvu. aḷavutal = toṭutal.
uṟutal = tōlāl toṭutal. uṟu ūṟu = toṭṭaṟivu.
ul-ol-oṟṟu-ottu.
ottutal = meṉporuḷāl toṭutal.
ottu - ottaṭam. ottu - oṟṟu - oṟṟi.
tuvaḷutal = toṭutal. tuvaḷi - ottaṭam.
tuva - tuvi - tuvital = cāyantoṭutal.
(tūṇṭu) - tīṇṭu - tīṭṭu. tīṇṭutal = toṭutal.
toṭutal = uṟutal.
tōytal = nīriyum nilattiyun toṭutal.
kālnilan tōyāk kaṭavuḷi  (nālaṭiyār, kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu)
porutal = poruntit toṭutal. poruṅ katavu, porumuka veḻiṉi mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.
muttutal = mukattāl toṭutal.
(i) toṭum uṟuppu (puṟaṇi)
uri = tōl, marappaṭṭi. (ul - ur - uṟu. ur - uri).
(tol) - toli - tōl - tōṭu - ōṭu.
(toṉ) - tōḷ = toṭum uṟuppāṉa ki, allatu cuvalit toṭum puyam.
tuva - tuvakku = tōl.
(ii) toṭaṅkal
oru karuviyit toṭutalē atikkoṇṭu ceyyum viṉiyit toṭaṅkalākum. oru viṉiyit toṭaṅkal atit toṭutal pōlām.
uṟutal = toṭaṅkal.
v.L.: urikkaluṟṟāṉ = collat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ.
(tuṭu) - tuṭaṅku - tuṭakkam. tuṭu - toṭu.
toṭu - toṭaṅku - toṭakkam.

toṭutal = toṭaṅkutal. aṉṟu toṭṭu = aṉṟu toṭaṅki.
(tuva) - tuvaṅku - tuvakkam.
toṉṟutoṭṭu, aṉṟu toṭuttu, toṭṭakuṟi (toṭaṅki viṭṭa viṉikkuṟi) mutaliya maraput toṭarkaḷi nōkkuka.
(iii) toṭuppu
toṭuppāvatu iruporuṭkuṇṭāṉa toṭarpu. atu oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭṭapiṉ nikaḻvatu.
(tuṭu) - tuṭi. toṭu - toṭi.
toṭuttal = iṇittal.
toṭu - toṭukku. toṭukkutal = toṭṭāl viḻum niliyāyt toṭuttuvittal.
toṭu - toṭuppu = iṇippu, takāppuṇarcciyuṟavu.
toṭukku - tocukku = takāppuṇarcci.
toṭu - toṭucu = vippu. toṭucu - toṭicu.
toṭu - toṭuvu = vīṭṭit toṭarnta kolli.
toṭu - toṭuvi = toṭuttiruppatu, putiya yāṉiyip payiṟṟumpaṭi toṭuttuviṭṭa yāṉi, pāṅkaṉ, vippu.
toṭu - toṭar - toṭarpu.
(tol) - toṟṟu - tottu.
(iv) tuṭakku
tuṭakkāvatu toṭuppāluṇṭākuṅ kaṭṭu.
(tuṭu) - tuṭakku = kaṭṭu. toṭu - toṭakku = kaṭṭu.
tuva - tuvakku = kaṭṭu. tuya - tuyakku = kaṭṭu.
toṭu - toṭar - toṭarpu = kaṭṭu, kaṭṭappaṭṭa ceyyuḷ.
(v) toṭarcci
pala poruḷkaḷ muṟiyē oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭukkap paṭuvatāl toṭarcciyuṇṭākum.
toṭu - toṭar = varici.
aḷ - aṇ - aṇi = varici.
aṇi = aṇil = mutukil mūvarici allatu variyuḷḷa aṇil.
aṇ = aṭu = aṭukku = aṭukkam = aṭukkal.
toṭar = toṭari. toṭar = toṭarcci. toṭar = toṭarvu = toṭarpu.
toṭu = toṭakku = toṭakki = toṭari (caṅkili, koṭi).
toṭu = toṭal = toṭali = māli.
toṭu = toṭavu = toṭaval = māli.
toṭu – toṭi – māli . toṭi-toṭiyal= māli.
(2) muṭṭal tuṟi
i. valitāyt toṭutal
valiya poruḷkaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu vēkamākat tākkuvatu valitāyt toṭutal.
utittal = tākkutal, eṟṟutal.
oṭṭutal = tākkutal.
māntaruṟi nilattō ṭoṭṭalaritu  (kuṟaḷ, 499)
kuṭṭutal = muṭṭiyāl taliyit tākkutal.
kuṭṭu - koṭṭu. koṭṭutal = muṭṭiyāl oṉṟit tākkutal, kuccāl paṟiyi aṭittal, cuttiyalāl oṉṟit taṭṭutal, koṭṭuppiṭi, cempukoṭṭi mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.
coṭṭutal = taliyiṟ kuṭṭutal, paṟavi oṉṟik kottutal.
(tuṭṭu) - taṭṭu. taṭṭutal = kiyāluṅ karuviyālum koṭṭutal. taṭṭu - taṭṭāṉ.
noṭṭi (yiṭutal) = cuvi mikutiyāl aṇṇattil nāvic cērttuk koṭṭutal.
poṭṭu - paṭṭu. paṭṭutal eṉpatu taṭṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkiṉa paṇṭi viṉiccol, iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟatu. paṭṭutaṟku aṭiyāka vittuk koḷḷum kal paṭṭaṭi eṉṟum, paṭṭum aṟi paṭṭaṟi eṉṟum, paṭṭum cāli paṭṭacāli eṉṟum, vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
muṭṭutal = taliyālum mukattālum tākkutal. mukam eṉṟatu oru poruḷiṉ muṉi allatu muṟpakuti. muṭṭu = muṭṭiyil vāṅkum kuṇṭaṭi.
muṭṭu - moṭṭu = taliyil vāṅkum kuṭṭu. moṭṭu moṭṭeṉṟu taliyiṟ kuṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku. koṅku nāṭṭār kuṭṭi moṭṭukkāy eṉpar.
iṅkuk kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷ vallōci mikumāṟu ṭakaram iraṭṭiyavi yātaliṉ, avi perumpālum kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷkaḷi vaṉmiyākat toṭuvatiyē kuṟikkum. takaravoli ṭakaravoliyiṉum melliyatātaliṉ, takaramiraṭṭiya coṟkaḷ caṟṟu meṉmiyākat tākkuvatik kuṟikkum. takaram taṉittu varuvaṉavum, ṭakaram taṉittum meliyōṭu kūṭiyum varuvaṉavum, meli taṉittum iraṭṭiyum varuvaṉavum, iṅṅaṉamē.
kuttutal = kiyāl uṭampiṟ kuttutal, ulakkiyāṟ kūlattit tuvittal.
kuttu - kuṟṟu.
kummutal = kuttutal. kumittal = kuttutal.
muṇṭutal = mella muṭṭutal.
mottutal = uṭampiṟ kiyālum karuviyālum aṭittal.
mōtutal = muṭṭuvatiṉum caṟṟu melitāyt tākkutal.
kiyālum ulakkiyālum kuttutal pōl toṭutalaḷavāṉa kuttutalkaḷē iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭavi.
ii. muṭṭuk koṭuttal
muṭṭuk koṭuttalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟi muṭṭit tāṅkumpaṭi ceytal.
uḷ - aḷ - aṇ - aṇṭu. aṇṭutal = neruṅkutal, poruntutal, muṭṭutal.
aṇṭu - aṇṭi = muṭṭu.
aṇṭi (aṇṭak) koṭuttal = muṭṭuk koṭuttal.
potu = oppu, poruttam. potu - (pōtu) - pōtiki = tūṇmēl vikkun tāṅku kaṭṭi.
muḷ - muṭṭu.
muḷ - muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = tāṅkutal.
maṇṭukāl = muṭṭukkāl.
iii. olittal
uyirikaḷiṉ vāyolikaḷallāta maṟṟa volikaḷellām, poruḷkaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭuvatiṉālēyē allatu tākkuvatiṉālēyē uṇṭā kiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu muṉṉit tamiḻar aṟintu. toṭuvatiyum tākkuvatiyum kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷi ṉiṉṟu olipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷi amittuk koṇṭaṉar.
uyirikaḷiṉ vāyolikaḷum, eḻuttolikaḷāyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ uṭampuḷḷirunteḻuppappaṭum kāṟṟāṉatu, miṭaṟum mūkkum pōṉṟa uṟup piṭaṅkaḷiyum, aṇṇamum pallum pōṉṟa uṟuppukkaḷiyum, tākkuvatiṉālēyē uṇṭākiṉṟaṉaveṉ ṟaṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu :
unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit
taliyiṉum miṟṟiṉum neñciṉum niliip
pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum
aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ
uṟuppuṟ ṟamiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
ellā eḻuttuñ colluṅ kāli
eṉṉun tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl (83) ūkikkappaṭum.
ul - ulampu. ulamputal = alapputal.

col oli vaṭivāyiruttalāl, oli paṟṟiya pala coṟkaḷ colliyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.

tuṇatuṇattal, toṉutoṉuttal, toṇa toṇattal eṉpaṉa, alapputaliyum viṭātu pēcutaliyuṅ kuṟikkum.
nuḷ - naḷ (naḷḷeṉal) = ōr olikkuṟippu.
nuḷ - (noḷ) - noṭi = oli, col.
pul - pulampu. pulamputal = olittal.
oruvaṉ taṉiyāyiruntu taṉṉoṭu tāṉē pēcuvatu olittalaḷavā yiruppatāl, atu pulamputal eṉappaṭum. ataṉāl pulampu eṉṉum col taṉimiyik kuṟikkum.
pulampē taṉimi  (tol. uri. 35)
iṉi, tākkum allatu tākkappaṭum poruḷkaḷiṉ tiṇmikkum olikkun taṉmikkum ēṟpap palvēṟu vakiyāṉa ōcikaḷ piṟattalāl, avaṟṟik kuṟittaṟku vevvēṟeḻuttolikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap peṟum eṉṟaṟika.

(4) olikkuṟippuc coṟkaḷum virivuk kuṟippuc coṟkaḷum oṉṟu pōl tōṉṟiṉum vēṟupaṭṭavi.

iv. taṭṭal (taṭi)
oru poruḷ iṉṉoru poruḷi muṭṭiṉavuṭaṉ, muṉṉataṉ muṟcelavu taṭukkappaṭuvatāl, muṭṭaṟ karuttil taṭik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
oṭṭu - aṭṭu - aṭṭi = taṭi.
aṭṭu - aṭṭam = kuṟukku.
kuṟukku niṟpatu etuvum taṭiyākum. muṭṭappaṭum cuvar mutaliyavum kuṟukku niṉṟutāṉ taṭukkum. ātalāl, kuṟukku niṟṟal eṉiṉum taṭuttal eṉiṉum oṉṟē.
aṭṭaṅkāl = kuṟukkāka maṭakki vikkuṅ kāl.
aṭṭu - aṭṭaṇam. aṭṭaṇakkāl (aṭṭaṇaṅkāl) = kuṟukkāka maṭakkivikkuṅ kāl.
aṭṭap pallakku = kuṟukkākak kāvum pallakku.
aṭṭam pārittal = kuṟukkāka (pakkavāṭṭil) vaḷartal.
kuḷ - kuṟu - kuṟuku - kuṟukku = vaḻiyiṉ pakkavāṭṭu, vaḻittaṭi, mutukiṉ kuṟukkup pakuti.
kuḷ - (kuṭṭu) - kaṭṭu. kaṭṭutal = taṭuttal, vaḻiyiṟ kuṟukkākac cellutal.
kāṭi kaṭṭiṉāl pāṭikaṭṭum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
tuḷ - taḷ - taṭṭu. taṭṭutal = taṭuttal, nīruḷ mūḻkiṉavaṉukkut tari taṭṭutalum, muṟcelpavaṉukkuc cuvar taṭṭutalum taṭiyākum.
taṭṭu - taṭṭi = aṟiyi allatu vaḻiyit taṭukkum mūṅkiṟpāy allatu teṉṉaṅkiṭuku.

taḷ - taḷi = taṭukkum kaṭṭu allatu vilaṅku, kaṭṭu allatu piṇippu, ceyyuṭcīriṉ ōcip piṇippu.
taḷital = taṭuttal, piṇittal,
taḷ - taṭu - taṭi. taṭu - taṭuppu. taṭu - taṭukku = ciṟu taṭṭipōṉṟa pāy.
taṭu - taṭakku - taṭaṅku - taṭaṅkal.
taṭṭut taṭiyiṉṟi, taṭṭut taṭaṅkaliṉṟi eṉpaṉa maraput toṭarmoḻikaḷ.
taḷ - (taḻu) - (taku) - taki. takittal = taṭuttal, 
muḷ - muṭṭu = muṭṭuppāṭu, taṭi.
tīyatik kāṇṭalum kēṭṭalum nalviṉikkut taṭiyākak karututaliṉ, avaṟṟi muṟiyē, kaṇṭu muṭṭu kēṭṭumuṭṭu eṉpar camaṇar.
muḷ - (muṭu) - muṭi = nerukkaṭi, muṭi - muṭiñcal.
muṭṭu - (muṭṭam) - viṭṭam = mukaṭṭiṉ kuṟukkuttaram.
muḷ - (muṟu) - maṟu - maṟukku = kuṟukku. maṟukkāṭṭutal = kuṟukkāka niṉṟu iṭam valam cellātapaṭi taṭuttal. kuṟukkum maṟukkum eṉpatu vaḻakku.
mul - vil - vilaku - vilaṅku = kuṟukku, kuṟukkāka vaḷarvatu, taṭukkuṅ kaṭṭu (taḷi).
vilaṅkūṭaṟuttatu  (cilap. 16: 213).
vil - villaṭi = taṭi. vilaṅku - vilaṅkam - villaṅkam = taṭi.
vilaṅku - vilaṅkal = nāṭṭiṉ kuṟukkē niṉṟu oṉṟaṉ celavit taṭukkum mali.
kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kurīip pōla eṉṉum uvamiyi nōkkuka.
oruvaṉ ōr aṟiyāta nāṭṭil ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkumpōtu. vaḻiyil maliyiruppiṉ, taṭiyuṇṭu cuṟṟic celvatu allatu mīḷvatu iyaṟki. karikāl vaḷavaṉiṉ vaṭaticip paṭiyeṭuppu paṉimaliyāltaṭiyuṇṭatu.
v. kaṭi
oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟi muṭṭum muṉi ataṉ kaṭiyātalāl, muṭṭaṟkaruttil kaṭimik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
pakkam nōkkiyatum mēṉōkkiyatum eṉak kaṭi iruvaki. pakkam nōkkiyatu muṉi allatu kaṭi; mēṉōkkiyatu ucci. tali eṉpatu ivviraṇṭiṟkum potu. 
uccimik karuttum, kaṭimik karuttum oṉṟē. āyiṉum, oppiṉ muṭittal eṉṉum utti paṟṟi, mēṉōkkiya uyarcci kuṟitta coṟkaḷuṭaṉ mēṉōkkiya ucci kuṟitta coṟkaḷum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. eṉiṉum, naṭṭukku niṟkum nilittiṇip poruḷkaḷiṉ uccip peyarkaḷ uyartaṟ karuttiyum, paṭukkiyāyk kiṭakkum nilittiṇip poruḷkaḷiṉ kaṭip peyarkaḷ muṭṭaṟkaruttiyum, iruniliyum aṭiyak kūṭiya iyaṅku tiṇip poruḷkaḷiṉ uccip peyarkaḷ av iru karuttiyum, aṭippaṭiyāyk koṇṭavi yeṉṟaṟika.
paṭukkiyāyk kiṭakkum oru tūṇiṉ muṉi, atu naṭṭukku niṟkum pōtu ucciyāy māṟutal kāṇka.
kuṭu - kuṭumi = muṭi, muṭipu (tīrmāṉamāṉa koḷki)
koṇṭa kuṭumittit taṇpaṇi nāṭē  (puṟam. 32.)
kuṭu - (kaṭu) - kaṭi - kaṭici. kaṭiyaṉ = nāṟpāl variciyil iṟutiyil kūṟuppaṭum uḻavaṉ. kaṭicci - kaṭici = uḻatti.
kuṭu - koṭu - kōṭi = kaṭici.
tul - (tal) tali = kaṭi, ucci, mēluṟuppu.
irutali maṇiyaṉ, irutalik koḷḷi, irutalik kāmam mutaliya toṭarkaḷil, tali eṉpatu muṉiyi allatu kaṭiyik kuṟittal kāṇka.
naṭuvaṇa teyta irutaliyum eytum
eṉpatilum aḵtē.
muṇṭu = tali. muṭi = tali, ucci, uccik koṇṭi, talimayir.
muḷ - muḻu - muku - mukuṭam = muṭi. mukuṭam - makuṭam - muṭi, muṭikkalam, pāṭṭiṉ iṟutit toṭar.
mucci = taliyucci. mōciki = uccimuṭi; muñcam = uccayaṇi.
vi. elli
muṭṭuṅkaṭi oru poruḷiṉ muṭiviṭamāy allatu elli yāyiruppatāl, muṭṭaṟkaruttil ellik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
ul - ula. ulappu = muṭivu, aḷavu, elli.
ul - ol = elli, muṭivu.
ol - el - elli. el - elki.
el - ēl - ēlvi = kālavelli.
ōr - ōram = muṭiyum pakkam, pakkam.
ōrañcollutal = orupāṟ kōṭic collutal.
kuṭu - koṭu - kōṭi = kaṭici, elli, kaṭikōṭi, teruk kōṭi eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷik kāṇka.
tuku - (tiruku) - tiki = muṭivu. elli, tikital = muṭital, tīrtal. tīrmāṉamātal. mātam tikinta cūli, ataṉ vili iṉṉum tikiyavilli, eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
tiki - tici = muṭivu, elli, pakkam. (tukku) - tikku.
ticiccol = pala ticikaḷil vaḻaṅkum koṭun tamiḻc col.
ticiccol eṉpatu toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum tamiḻilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭātalālum, mayaṅkutalik kuṟikkum tiki eṉṉum collum tici eṉṟu tiritalālum, elliyik kuṟikkum tici eṉṉum col teṉ collēyām.
nī ticittatuṇṭō  (kampa. kikēci cūḻ. 18)
ivaṉ cinti tuḻāyt ticikkiṉṟatē  (tiv. tiruvāy, 4,6,1.)
ticippuṟutaluñ cīvarkku  (vētā. cūṭā. 110.)
eṉṉun toṭarkaḷil, mayaṅkutalik kuṟikkum tiki eṉṉuñ col tici eṉṟu tirintiruttal kāṇka. tici eṉṉuñ colliṉ mūlavaṭivāṉa tiki eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkāmiyiyum nōkkuka.
tici - (tēcu) - tēcam - tēyam - tēm = tici tēcam, pakkam, iṭam.
avaṉ maṟi tēe nōkki  (akam. 48)
tevvar tēettu  (puṟam. 6)
tici tēcam, eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pakkam eṉṉum poruḷil varutali, antat ticikkē pōkamāṭṭēṉ eṉṉum tamiḻ vaḻakkālum, ēkatēcam eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkālum, aṟiyalām. ēkatēcam = oru pakkam, oru pakuti.
tēcam eṉṉum col mutalāvatu elliyik kuṟittu, piṉpu ōr elliyil allatu pakkattil uḷḷa nāṭṭik kuṟittatu, o. nō. cīmi = elli, nāṭu.
tikkut tikkeṉṟu aṭittuk koḷḷukiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmiyālum, nāttaṭṭutal tikkutal eṉappaṭutalālum, tiki eṉṉuñ col kakaravoli koṇṭiruttalālum, tikku eṉṉum collum teṉ collē yeṉka.
muḷ - muḷi - viḷi - viḷimpu = ōram. viḷimpu - (viḷimpu) - 
vaṭimpu.

muṭṭu - maṭṭu = aḷavu. maṭṭu - maṭṭam = aḷavu, cariyāṉa aḷavu, tāḻnta aḷavu, tāḻvu.
kaṭalmaṭṭam, maṭṭappalaki, maṭṭakkutiri, maṭṭat tuṇi mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
vii. muṭital
ulattal = muṭital;
uṟutal = toṭutal, muṭṭutal. uṟu - iṟu - īṟu. iṟu - iṟuti. iṟutal = muṭital;
iṟu - iṟa - iṟappu = cāvu.
iṟuttal = kaṭamiyit tīrttal, vari celuttutal. iṟu - iṟi = kaṭami (vari).
iṟuttal = vaḻiccelavi viṭṭiruttal, taṅkutal, iṟu - iṟi = iṟivaṉ = eṅkun taṅkiyiruppavaṉ.
uṟu - aṟu - aṟuti = muṭivu.
aṟu - aṟavu = nīkkam.
tuvalutal = cātal.
tul - (til) - (tir) - tīr. tīrtal = muṭital, nīṅkutal, tīrmāṉam = muṭivu, muṭipu. tīr - tīrvi.
pol - poṉṟu. poṉṟutal = iṟattal, muṭital.
mul - muṟṟu, muṟṟutal = muṭital, iṟattal.
muḷ - muḷi - viḷi. viḷital = muṭital, iṟattal. muḷital = muṟṟutal, keṭutal. viḷi - vī. vītal = muṭital. cātal. muḷ - viḷ - viḻu - viku - vikuti = iṟuti. īṟu.
muḷi - (muḻi) - muṭi - muṭivu. muṭi - maṭi. maṭital = iṟattal.
muṭi - muci. o. nō. oṭi - oci mucital - muṭital. iṟattal.
mūṅkil pōṟ cuṟṟam muciyāmal vāḻntiruppīr eṉṉum tirumaṇa vāḻttu marapuriyik kāṇka.
muṭṭutal = muṭital.
muñcutal = muṭital. cātal. mūcutal = muṭital, cātal. muccutal = muṭittal, mūḻttal = mutirtal. mūḻ - mūy. mūyttal = muṭittal.
viii. maḻuṅkal
oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṭṭum poruḷkaḷuḷ, meliyatu orē muṟiyilum valiyatu pala muṟip piṉṉum muṉi maḻuṅki moṭṭiyākiṉṟaṉa. kūrākac cīviṉa eḻutukōl tēyvatu, eḻutappaṭum karuviyil atu palamuṟi muṭṭuvataṉālēyē.
(nuḷ) - (nuḻu) - naḻu - naḻuṅku. naḻuṅkutal = maḻuṅkutal. naḻuṅku - naḻukku - naḻukkam = maḻukkam, maḻuṅkal.
poṭṭu - poṭṭi = kaṇṇoḷi maḻukkam.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maḻu - maḻuku - maḻuṅku - maḻukku - maḻukkam. 
maḻukku - maḻukki, maḻuṅku - maḻuṅkal.
muḷ - muṭṭu - moṭṭi - maṭṭi. maḻu moṭṭi - maḻumaṭṭi.
mūḷ - (moḷ) - moḻukku - moḻukkaṭṭi - mokkaṭṭi = maḻukkamāṉatu.
moḻukku - mokku - mokki = kūriṉmi.
(moḷ) - moṇ - moṇṇi = maḻukkam, kūriṉmi.
moṇṇi - maṇṇi = maḻukkam.
maṇṇi - maṇi = maḻukkal, maḻuṅkalāytam.
maṇi - maṇiyaṉ = maḻuṅkalāytam.
ix. matimuṭṭu
matiyāṉatu kūriya kaṇpōṉṟiruttalāl, maṭami ataṉ maḻukkamākak kūṟappaṭum. kūriya matikkuk kūr eṉṟē peyar.
kūḻi = kuṭṭiyāṉatu, matikkuṟivu.
kūḻi māntartañ celkati  (tēvā: 462, 9)
muṭṭu - muṭṭāḷ. muṭṭu - muṭṭaṉ = mūṭaṉ.
muṭṭu - (muṭṭi) - maṭṭi. muṭṭu - moṭṭi = maṭṭi = mūṭaṉ. muṇṭu = maṭami. muṇṭam = aṟivillātavaṉ.
x. matimaḻukkam
moṇṇi = kūriṉmi. moṇṇiyaṉ = aṟivu maḻuṅkiyavaṉ.
moṇṇi - maṇṇi = maḻukkam, maṭami. maṇiyaṉ = matik kūrmiyaṟṟavaṉ.
maḻukku - makku = matiyaṟṟavaṉ. maḻukkaṭṭi - vaḻukkaṭṭi = matimaḻukkam, mūṭattaṉam.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu = matiyaṟṟavaṉ.
xi. moṭṭiyātal
muṉimaḻuṅkiyatu moṭṭiyākum.
moṭṭiyātal, uṇmiyāy moṭṭiyātalum aṇi vakiyil moṭṭiyātalum eṉa iruvaki.


uṭampiṟkut taliyiṉmiyum, talikkup pāki makuṭa miṉmiyum, maṇṭikku mayiriṉmiyum, uṟuppiṟku aṇi yiṉmiyum, mēlukku āṭiyiṉmiyum, nilattiṟkup payiriṉ miyum, muṭaṅkalukkuk kiyeḻuttiṉmiyum, marattiṟkuk kiḷiyiṉmiyum, kompuḷḷa vilaṅkiṟkuk kompiṉmiyum, vāluḷḷa uyirikku vāliṉmiyum, vāliṟku ataṉ nuṉiyiṉ miyum, ivi pōlvaṉa piṟavum, moṭṭimiyākak karutappaṭum.
kuṭṭu - kaṭṭu. kaṭṭāntari = veṟunilam.
kuḷ - (kūḷ) - kūḻi = moṭṭivāl, vālaṟṟatu. aḷavaṭiyiṉ iṟutic cīril mōṉi mutaliyaṉa villāta toṭi.
coṭṭu - coṭṭi = vaḻukki, vaḻukkit tali.
poṭṭu - poṭṭal = pāḻnilam, veṟunilam, tali vaḻukki.
pōṭu = moṭṭi.
mūḷi = uṟuppukkuṟi. aṇiyaṟṟatu. mūḷikkuṭam (vāyaṟunta kuṭam) mūḷikkātu mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka. mūḷi - mūḻi = mūḷikkāti.
muṇṭu - moṭṭiyāṉatu. kiḷi taṟitta kaṭṭi.
muṇṭu - muṇṭam = taliyillātatu moṭṭiyāṉatu, kiḷitaṟitta kaṭṭi, uṟuppukkuṟi, āṭiyillāvuṭampu.
āṭiyillāvuṭampi muṇṭakkaṭṭiyeṉṟum moṭṭik kaṭṭi yeṉṟum kūṟuvar.
muṭṭu - moṭṭi. moṭṭit tali = pākiyaṟṟa tali, mayir veṭṭiṉa tali. moṭṭi maṇṭi = maḻitta tali.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maṇ. maṇṇutal = maḻittal. intak katti maṇṇātu, eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
maḷ - maḻi - vaḻi. maḻittal = moṭṭiyaṭittal (tali vaṟaṇṭutal).
maḻittalum nīṭṭalum vēṇṭā  (kuṟaḷ 280)
muḷ - muṇṭu - muṇṭam = maḻitta tali. muṇṭaṉ = maḻitta taliyaṉ. camaṇaṉ, maḻikkum muṭiviṉiñaṉ. muṇṭi = maḻitta talicci, kimpeṇ. muṇṭi - muṇṭicci. muṇṭu - muṇṭi. o. nō. tuṇṭu - tuṇṭi.
muṇṭittal = talimaḻittal, muṇṭi = maḻitta taliyaṉ, maḻiviṉiñaṉ. muṇṭi + aṉam = muṇṭaṉam. o.nō. kaṇṭi + aṉam = kaṇṭaṉam.
muṇṭi + itam = muṇṭitam.
aṉam itam eṉpaṉa toḻiṟ peyar vikutikaḷ.
e.ṭu. 	vañci - vañcaṉam. tappu - tappitam.
moḻukku - moḻukkaṉ = vēlippāṭillāta aṇi; maḻuṅku - maḻuṅkaṉ = vēlippāṭillāta aṇi.
(moḻu) - mōḻi = moṭṭi. kompillā māṭu, maraaṭi muṇṭam.
ēḻiyik kaṇṭāl mōḻiyum pāyum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. mōḻimukam = (moṭṭi mukamuḷḷa) paṉṟi, mōḻal = paṉṟi.
moṇṭu = moṭṭikki. kikkuṟi. moṇṭu - moṇṭi = ki kuṟintavaṉ. moṇṭimuṭam eṉpatu vaḻakku.
noṇṭutal = kuṟinta kāloṭu naṭattal. oru kālāl naṭattal allatu tāvic cellutal.
noṇṭu - noṇṭi = noṇṭutal, noṇṭupavaṉ. ōraṭi kuṟukiya īraṭiccintu.
muṭam eṉpatu vaḷivu eṉṟum, moṇṭi eṉpatu kuṟi eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟika.
xii. maṅkal
maṅkal eṉpatu uḷi maḻuṅkal;
maḻuṅku - maṅku - maṅkal.
maṅkal niṟam = mañcaḷ niṟam. veḷḷiyumaṉṟip pacciyumaṉṟi iṭi nikarttāy maṅkiya niṟamākaviruttaliṉ, mañcaḷ niṟam mutalāvatu maṅkal eṉappaṭṭatu. maṅkal - mañcal - mañcaḷ, o.nō. kaḻaṅku - kaḻañcu,
ilaṅku - ilañcu - ilañci.
mañcal = mañcaḷ niṟamuḷḷa carakku.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil kaṇavaṉōṭu kūṭi vāḻum peṇṭir aḻakiṟkum uṭal nalattiṟkum maṇattiṟkum mañcaḷ tēyttuk kuḷippatu vaḻakkamātaliṉ, mañcaṭkum mañcaḷ niṟattiṟkum maṅkalat taṉmi ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu. tirumaṇa variciyil vikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷuḷ mañcaḷum oṉṟākum.
maṅkal - maṅkalam = mañcaṭpūccāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum makaḷir vāḻkki nalam, mañcaḷālum mañcaḷ niṟattālum kuṟikkap peṟum naṟceyti, makaḷir vāḻkki nalattiṉ aṭiyāḷamāṉa aṇi (tāli).
maṅkalam - maṅkali = kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭi vāḻpavaḷ, kaṇavaṉi iḻavātavaḷ.
maṅkalam - maṅkali = maṅkali.
maṅki = maṅkalamākiya maṇapparuvattāḷ.
maṇattiṟkuriyavar patiṉāṟāṭṭip paruvattāṉum paṉṉīrāṭ ṭip paruvattāḷum eṉṟu akapporuḷilakkaṇamum,
mākavā ṉikarvaṇki mānāykaṉ kulakkompar
īkivāṉ koṭiyaṉṉāḷ īrāṟāṇ ṭakaviyāḷ.  (maṅkala vāḻttup pāṭal, 2-4)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum kūṟutaliyum, paṉṉīrāṭṭip paruvattār maṅkiyeṉap paṭutaliyum, nōkkuka.
maṅku - makku - makkal = oḷimaḻuṅkal.
xiii. vaḻittal
maḻi - vaḻi. tali vaḻittal mukam vaḻittal eṉṉum vaḻakkuk kaḷik kāṇka. talivaḻittalit tali vaṟaṇṭutal eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar. vaḻittal eṉpatu muṇṭittal eṉṟu maṭṭum poruḷ paṭum. vaṟaṇṭutal eṉpatu talimayiric cuṟaṇṭiyeṭuttal pōl valitāy vaḻittalik kuṟikkum.
mayir peyarvatu pōṉṟē tōl peyarvatum oruvaki muṇ ṭaṉam allatu moṭṭiyātalāl, tōlip peyarppatum vaḻittal eṉappaṭum. maramuñ cuvarum pōṉṟa karaṭumuraṭāṉa poruḷ uṭampōṭu uracit tōlip peyarttu viṭṭāl, maram vaḻittuviṭṭatu cuvar vaḻittu viṭṭatu eṉpar. katti vaḻittalum karaṭu muraṭāṉa poruḷ vaḻittalum viṉivaṭivil ottiruttaluṅ kāṇka. vaḻital eṉpatu tōl peyartalākiya taṉ viṉiyik kuṟikkum. vaḻi - vaḻal, vaḻalutal = neruppi ṉāl ventu tōlurital. vaḻal - vaḻaṟṟu - vaḻaṭṭu. vaṟaṟṟutal = neruppil vāṭṭit tōlurittal. allatu mayir nīkkutal.
ōliyāl nākkiluḷḷa acaṭṭi nīkkutalum, cīppāl tali mayiric cīvutalum, maṇveṭṭiyāl vāykkāliluḷḷa cēṟṟi iḻutteṟitalum, ciṟaṅkiyāl oru poruḷi iḻuttut taḷḷutalum, ivi pōlvaṉa piṟavum, viṉiyilum viḷi vilum katti vaḻittali ottiruttalāl, aviyum vaḻittal eṉappaṭum.
vaṭittal = nāvaḻittal, nāvit tiruttutal.
vaṭiyā nāviṉ vallāṅkup pāṭi  (puṟam. 47)
vaḻi - vaṭi - vār. vaṭittal =talimayiri vārutal. 
vaṭikkoḷ kūḻi  (naṟ. 23.)

vārtal = urital (akam, 69), vaḻittaḷḷutal, kūṭṭutal, talimayiric cīvutal. vāri = cīppu, kuppi vāri mutaliya vāruṅ karuvi. cikkuvāri ciṇukkuvāri eṉpaṉa talimayir vāruṅ karuvikaḷ. vārkōl - viḷakkumāṟu. vāriyaṉ = kaḷattil nellik kūṭṭik kuvippavaṉ. aṅṅaṉam kuvittali mēṟ pārkkum aluvalāḷaṉ. paḷḷar ūrāṇmiyil āṭkaḷik kūṭṭupavaṉ. vāriyam - vāriyaṉ toḻil, mēṟpārvi, mēṟpārvikkuḻu.
kumpa aḷanta paṭiyiṉ mēluḷḷa kūmpiya pakutiyik kiyāl taṭṭutalum vaḻittali ottiruttalāl, tali taṭṭiyaḷattalit talivaḻittal eṉpar. aḷakkappaṭum poruḷ nīrpporu ḷāyiṉ paṭi maṭṭattiṟku mēl kūmpātoḻukumātaliṉ, atu vaḻital eṉṉun taṉ viṉiyāṟ kūṟappaṭum. koḷkalaṅkaḷilum ēri kuḷam pōṉṟa koḷḷiṭaṅkaḷilum nīr nirampiyōṭutali vaḻintōṭutal eṉpatu, immuṟi paṟṟiyē.
xiv. vaṭittal
vaḻi - vaṭi. cōṟu ventapiṉ nīri vaḻiyac ceytal vaṭittal eṉappaṭum. tāṉāy vaḻintōṭāta nīrik kalattic cāyttu vaḻiyac ceyvatu vaḻittaliṉum vēṟu paṭṭatātaliṉ, vaṭittal eṉappaṭṭatu. poruḷ vēṟu pāṭṭic col vēṟupāṭu kuṟittatu. ēri kuḷaṅkaḷil nīr nirampit tāṉāy vaḻiyātu maṭi allatu kaliṅku vaḻiyāyp pāyumāyiṉ, atuvum vaṭital eṉap paṭum. aṅṅaṉam pāyccutal vaṭittal eṉappaṭum. kūḻuṅ kañciyum pōṉṟa nekiḻporuḷ tāṉāy vaḻitaliyum vaṭital eṉpar.
vaṭi-vār. orukalattiṉiṉṟu nīri ūṟṟutalum ōr ulōkattik kāycci yūṟṟutalum vaṭittal allatu vārttal eṉappaṭum. kaṇṇīrpōlat tāṉāy vaṭital vārtal eṉappaṭum.
vār - vārppu - vārppaṭam = kāycciyurukki vārttuc ceytatu;
vēlvaṭittuk koṭuttal kollaṟkuk kaṭaṉē  (puṟam. 812)
oru kalattiluḷḷa nīri ūṟṟumpōtu atiluḷḷa maṇṭiyit taṭuttaṟkuk kalattiṉ vāyil tuṇikaṭṭuvatu. vaṭikaṭṭutal eṉappaṭum. iti vaṭārkkāṭṭār vēṭu kaṭṭutal eṉpar. veyilukkut taliyil tuṇikaṭṭuvatiyum oppumi paṟṟi vēṭukaṭṭutal eṉpatuṇṭu.
xv. vārtal (nīḷal)
vaṭiyum nīrpporuḷ nīṇṭu nērāka viḻumātaliṉ, vaṭitaṟ karuttil nīṭcik karuttum nērpuk karuttum tōṉṟiṉa. kāti nīṭṭi vaḷarttalik kātu vaṭittal eṉpar.
kuḻiviravu vaṭikātā  (tēvā. 1091,1)
vaṭi - vari - varici. vari - variccu - variccal = nīṇṭa kuccu. vaṭi - vār. vārtal = nīḷutal, nērātal, nīḷakkiḻittal.
vārtal pōkal oḻukal mūṉṟum
nērpum neṭumiyuñ ceyyum poruḷa  (tol. uriyiyal, 19)
vār - vāri = nīṇṭa peruṅ kampu. vār - vāri = nīṇṭa peruṅkampu. vārivati = āṟṟiṉ kuṟukkāka vāriyāl amikkappaṭṭa vaḻi, pālam. vārivati - vārāvati.
vār = nīṇṭa tōltuṇṭu.
vār - vāl = nīṇṭavuṟuppu. vāl - vālam = vāl, vāla
mukam = nīṇṭamukam.
vāḷ = nīṇṭa katti, vāḷam = nīṇṭa vār, kampi. vāḷ - vaḷ - vaḷpu = vār.
vār - vāram = colloḻukkamum iciyoḻukkamumuḷḷa pāṭal.
xvi. vaḻukki
moṭṭiyāṉa poruṭkaḷ, ciṟappāka maḻitta tali, vaḻukki yāyiruppatāl, maḻukkik karuttiṉiṉṟu vaḻukkik karuttup piṟantatu.
uḷ - (iḷ) - iḻu - iḻum. iḻumeṉal = vaḻu vaḻuppu.
muḻu - moḻu - moḻukki - maḻukki - vaḻukki. moḻu - maḻu.
maḻu - maḻa - maḻamaḻa - maḻamaḻappu = vaḻavaḻappu. maḻu - vaḻu. maḻa - vaḻa.
vaḻu - vaḻukku - vaḻukkal = vaḻuvaḻuppu. vaḻukkit tēṅkāy.
vaḻu = vaḻukki. vaḻukkāy = vaḻukkit tēṅkāy.
vaḻu - vaḷukki = vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa iḷantēṅkāy uḷḷīṭu.
vaḻutali = vaḻavaḻappāṉa kattirikkāy.
vaḻutuṇi = kattarikkāy.
vaḻuval = tēṅkāy vaḻukki.
vaḻuvaḻuppu = vaḻavaḻappu, meṉmi.
vaḻu - vaḻuku vaḻukutal = vaḻuvaḻuppāyiruttal.
vaḻu - vāḻi = vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa maram. maram vaḻukum eṉpatu viṭukati.
vaḻa - vaḻaku = vaḻavaḻappu, meṉmi.

xvii. vaḻukkal
vaḻukkiyāṉa iṭamum poruḷum vaḻukkum.
(uḻu) - iḻu - iḻukkal = vaḻukkal.
(nuḻu) - naḻu - naḻuṅku - naḻuṅkutal = vaḻuvutal; caṟukkutal.
vaḻu - vaḻuku - vaḻukku - vaḻukkal = caṟukkal, caṟukku nilam.
vaḻukkumaram = caṟukkumaram.
vaḻuvaḻuttal = vaḻukkutal. vaḻu - vaḻuvu. vaḻuvutal = caṟukkutal.
vaḻāal = vaḻukkuki.
vaḻūu maruṅkuṭiya vaḻāa lōmpi  (malipaṭu. 21)
xviii. naḻuval
vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa poruḷ kiyiviṭṭu naḻuvum.
muḻu - maḻu - vaḻu. muḻu - moḻu - moḻuppu. moḻupputal = maḻupputal.
moḻuppu - maḻuppu. maḻupputal = valakkāramāyp pēcik kaṭamiyiṉiṉṟu naḻuvutal.
vaḻu - vaḻuvu - vaḻuval = naḻuval. vaḻuvāṭi = kāriyatti naḻuvaviṭupavaṉ.
vaḻu - vaḻukku. vaḻukkutal = tapputal.
(nuḻu) - naḻu - naḻuvu - naḻuval.
naḻuvu - naḻuvi = piṭikoṭātavaṉ
naḻu - naḻuppu. naḻupputal = maḻupputal.
vaḻuval
neṟiyiṉiṉṟu vaḻukkalum vaḻuvalum tavaṟutalātaliṉ, vaḻukkaṟ karuttu vaḻuvaṟ karuttit taḻuviṟṟu.
(uḻu) - iḻu - iḻukku - iḻukkam = tavaṟu, tīyoḻukkam. iḻukku = tavaṟu.
muḻu - maḻu - vaḻu = tavaṟu. vaḻu - vaḻukku - vaḻukkam = tavaṟu. vaḻukkutal = tavaṟutal. vaḻukku = tavaṟu.
vaḻu - vaḻūu. vaḻu - vaḻāl - vaḻāal = tavaṟuki.
vaḻu - vaḻuvu - vaḻuval = tavaṟu, kēṭu.
vaḻu - vaḻumpu = kuṟṟam.
vaḻavaḻappu
vaḻukkum poruṭkaḷ cila oṭṭuntaṉmiyaṉavāyiruttaliṉ, vaḻukkalik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu piciṉpōl oṭṭukiṉṟa allatu neyppaciyuḷḷa cila poruḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
uḻu - uḻuntu = vaḻavaḻappāṉa payaṟu.
koḻu - koḻuppu.
(nuḻu) - (niḻam) - niṇam. o.nō. taḻal - taṇal.
(muḻu) - maḻu - vaḻu - vaḻumpu = niṇam.
vaḻu - (vaḻal) - vaḻali = cavukkāram (Soap).
muḷ - (maḷ) - vaḷ - vaṇṭi = vaḻavaḻappāṉa kāy. vaṇṭi - veṇṭi.
xix. kuṭṭiyātal (ciṟuttal)
muṉi maḻuṅkiṉa poruḷum mēṉmēlum muṉi tēyum poruḷum, kuṭṭiyākum. kuṟitalum kuṭṭiyātalē.
kuḷ - kuḷḷam. kuḷ - kuḷḷi. kuḷ - kuḷḷal.
kuḷ - kuṭṭi - kaṭṭi.
kuḷ - kūḷ - kūḷi = kuḷḷam, kuḷḷamāṉatu.
kūḷ - kūḻi = kuḷḷam. kuṭṭi, kuḷḷamāṉatu, kuṭṭiyāṉatu. kūḻi - kūḻiyaṉ.
kuḷ - (kuṇ) - kuṇil = kuṟuntaṭi.
kul - ku (kuṟumi muṉṉoṭṭu - diminutive prefix)
e.ṭu. kukkirāmam (va.)
o.nō. nal - na. nappiṉṉi, nakkīraṉ.
xx. taṭṭiyātal
mikak kuṭṭiyāṉa poruḷ taṭṭiyākum. uyaramāṉa poruḷkaḷellām caṟṟuk kuṟukiṉ kuṭṭiyum, mikak kuṟukiṉ taṭṭiyum, ām.
caṭṭu eṉpatu taṭṭutalik kuṟitta oru paḻantamiḻ viṉiccol. caṭṭuc caṭṭeṉṟu taṭṭukiṟāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku. taṭṭum poruḷum taṭṭappaṭum poruḷum taṭṭiyākum. taṭṭutalum oruvaki muṭṭutalē. muṭṭutal, viṉimutal muṭṭutalum karuvi muṭṭutalum eṉa iruvaki. ciṉiviṉi viṉimutal viṉi yokkum.
(cuttutal) = taṭṭutal. cuttu - cutti - cuttiyal = taṭṭuṅkaruvi. cuttu - cuṭṭu.
(cuṭṭu) caṭṭu - caṭi. caṭital = aṭikkappaṭum āṇi muṉi maḻuṅkit taṭṭiyātal.
caṭṭu - caṭṭam = taṭṭiyāṉa marappaṭṭi.
caṭṭakappi = taṭṭakappi.
caṭṭu - caṭṭuvam - caṭṭukam = caṭṭakappi.
caṭṭuvam = caṭṭakappi, taṭṭiyāṉa pāṉi.
caṭṭuvam - caruvam.
caṭṭam - taṭṭiyāṉa paṉiyōli. caṭṭam vārutal = ōliyiṉ arukukaḷi arintu eḻutuvataṟku ēṟṟatākkutal.
caṭṭam - caṭṭaṉ = ōliccuvaṭi payilum māṇavaṉ.
caṭṭaṉ + nampi = caṭṭanampi = māṇavar talivaṉākiya āciriyaṉ, talimi māṇavaṉ, caṭṭanampi - caṭṭampi.
caṭṭanampippiḷḷi - caṭṭampippiḷḷi - caṭṭām piḷḷi.
caṭṭam = maraccaṭṭam, kōṭiḻukkum caṭṭappalaki, nērmi, ceppam, muṟimi, nītiyoḻuṅku, varampu. viti.
māṇavar pārttu oḻuṅkāka eḻutuvataṟku mēl variyil variyappaṭṭiruppatu mēlvaric caṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
ōr amippakattiṉ karuma naṭappiṟkuriya vitiyoḻuṅku muḻuvatum caṭṭa tiṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
caṭṭam = viti, vitikkum atikāram, atikārattāṟ peṟṟa vurimi.
iṭṭamuṭaṉ mutaliyār vāṅki vanta kāḷitiṉam irupōruṇṇum
caṭṭamuṭaṉ koḷḷuṇṇum ...  (vētanāyakampiḷḷi taṉippāṭal)
caṭṭam - caṭaṅku = matavitippaṭi allatu oḻukka vitippaṭi naṭipeṟum karaṇam.
caṭṭam = paṭam kaṇṇāṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ nāṟpuṟamuṅ kōkkap paṭum maraccaṭṭam. caṭṭak kaṭṭil. caṭṭak katavu, caṭṭap parampu. caṭṭa vāḷ mutaliyaṉa nāṟpuṟamuñ caṭṭaṅ kōttavi.
capparam mukaṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuc caṭṭaṅkaṭṭutal āyatta viṉiyāyiruttaliṉ, caṭṭaṅ kaṭṭutal eṉpatu āyattañ ceytal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.
uṭampu uyirukkuc caṭṭam pōṉṟiruppatāl, atuvum caṭṭam eṉappaṭum.
caṭṭam - caṭṭakam (frame, outline).
caṭṭam - caṭṭi = uṭampu, uṭampiṉ mīntōl, atu pōṉṟa meyppi.
mīntōl peyara aṭipaṭṭavaṉi nōkki avaṉ uṭampu caṭṭi caṭṭiyāyk kaḻaṉṟuviṭṭatu eṉṟum, pāmpu mīntōl kaḻittalic caṭṭi cuḻaṟṟutal eṉṟum, kūṟutal kāṇka.
caṭṭam - caṭam = uṭampu. o. nō. paṭṭam - paṭam.
caṭam = (caṭal) - caṭalam = uṭampu.
o. nō. paṭam - paṭal - paṭalam (parappu).
caṭalam eṉṉum vaṭivu vaṭamoḻiyililli. cattiyum caṭalamum eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
cappu
cappu eṉpatu, oru nekiḻ poruḷ ētēṉum oṉṟi muṭṭalik kuṟikkuñ col. cāṇamum kaḷimaṇṇum paḻac catiyum pōṟ kuḻinta poruḷkaḷi, nilattiṟ pōṭum pōtum cuvaril eṟiyum pōtum, cappu eṉṟa oli kēṭkum. ataṉāl cappeṉṟu viḻuntatu eṉpar. avvoliyeḻap pōṭappaṭum kuḻi poruṭkaḷ taṭṭiyāṉa vaṭivaṅ koḷḷum. ataṉāl, cappu eṉṉum olikkuṟippiṉiṉṟu taṭṭiyātal karuttuṇarttum pala coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
(cuppu) - cappu - cappi = taṭṭi, taṭṭiyāṉatu, taṭṭiyāṉa toṭip pakuti.
cappik kāy, cappik kāl, cappit tirukki, cappi mūkku, cappi vāy, cappi yelumpu mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷik kāṇka.
cappu - cappaṭṭi = taṭṭiyāṉatu. ciṟaku, cappaṭṭiyaram = taṭṭiyaram.
cappu - cappal = cappaṭṭi. cappal - campal = vilit tāḻvu.
cappu - cappaṭi = cappaṭṭi.
cappi = taṭṭiyāṉa patar. patar pōṟ payaṉaṟṟavaṉ (pataṭi).
cappu - cappaṉ = payaṉaṟṟavaṉ.
cappu - cappari = mūṭaṉ. cappaṭṭi = maṭiyaṉ.
cappu - cappaṅki = mantaṉ.
cappu - capparam = moṭṭiyāṉa eṭupputtēr.
cappu - cappaḷi. cappaḷital = cappiyātal.
cappaḷittal = aṭiyiṟ cappiyāka uṭkārtal.
cappaḷi - cappaḷam - cappaṇam - cammaṇam.
cappaṇaṅ kūṭṭutal, cammaṇaṅ kūṭṭutal, eṉpaṉa vaḻakku.
cappaḷakkaṭṭi = cappiyāṉa tāḷakkaṭṭi (cappaḷāk kaṭṭi).
cappu - cappatti = taṭṭiyāṉa muttu.
cappu - cappāttu = (taṭṭiyāṉa) ceruppu.
cappāttu - cappātti = taṭṭiyāṉa allatu ceruppup pōṉṟa iliyuṭiya kaḷḷivaki.
vaṭavar taṭṭiyāṉa appa (roṭṭi) vakiyic cappātti eṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
cappu - (cappāḷi) - cappāṇi. o. nō. kaḷavāḷi - kaḷavāṇi.
cappāṇi = nilattiṟ cappiyāyirukkum muṭavaṉ, avaṉip pōl kuḻanti amarntiruntu kitaṭṭum nili.
taṭṭi eṉpatu uyarattiṟku māṟāṉa taṉmiyātalāl, ati yuṇarttum cappiyeṉṉum col potuvākat tāḻvāḷa niliyiyē kuṟikkum.
cappi = taṭṭiyāṉatu. tāḻntatu, payaṉaṟṟatu, cuviyaṟṟatu, keṭṭatu.
cappittuṇi = maṭṭamāṉa tuṇi. cappi nilam = payaṉaṟṟa nilam, cappi vākku (cappaṭṭi vākku) = payaṉaṟṟa col.
cuviyaṟṟatic cappeṉṟirukkiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakkāta lāl, cappu eṉpatē cappi yeṉṟu tirintiruttal uṇarappaṭum.
coṭṭuc coṭṭeṉṟu coṭṭukiṟatu, cotteṉṟu viḻuntatu, eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmiyālum, taṭṭuṅ karuvi yoṉṟu cutti eṉappaṭutalālum, caṭṭu cappu mutaliya coṟkaḷum tuvakkattil ukara mutalākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
taṭṭu
(tuṭṭu) - taṭṭu.
taṭṭutal = kiyāl allatu karuviyāl koṭṭutal; atāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟi valitāy muṭṭac ceytal.
taṭṭu - (taṭi) - taṟi. taṟital = caṭital.
taṭṭu = taṭṭiyāṉatu.
tulittaṭṭu, tērttaṭṭu, pūttaṭṭu, veṟṟilit taṭṭu mutaliya vaṟṟi nōkkuka. pātti variciyāṉa nilappakutiyum taṭṭu eṉappaṭum.
taṭṭukkūṭi = taṭṭiyāṉa kūṭi.
taṭṭu - taṭṭam = taṭṭiyāṉa kiṇṇam.
taṭṭu - taṭṭi = taṭṭiyāṉatu, aṟivili.
(tuṭṭu) - tiṭṭu (o. ku.). tiṭṭutiṭṭeṉṟu kēṭkiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
taṭṭi - taṭi. vāḻip paṭṭiyi vāḻittaṭi eṉpar.
toppu
toppu (o. ku.) oruvaṉ tuṇi tuvippatik kuṟikkumpōtu, topput toppeṉṟu tuṇiyi aṭikkiṟāṉ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
toppu - tappu - tapputal = tuṇi tuvittal.
tappu - tappi = aṭi (te.) taṭṭiyāṉatu. mūṅkiṟpaṟṟi, tappi vittuk kaṭṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
tappu - tappaḷam = paṟṟu.
toppu - tuvi. tuvittal = tuṇiyaṭittal, cammaṭṭiyā laṭittut taṭṭiyākkutal.
poṭṭu - paṭṭu
paṭṭutal eṉpatu taṭṭutalik kuṟitta oru paṇṭi viṉiccol. paṭṭaṭi eṉṉuñ colli nōkkuka.
paṭṭu - paṭṭam = paṭṭiyāṉa takaṭu, oru talivaṉuṭiya ciṟappip poṟittu avaṉatu neṟṟiyiṟ kaṭṭum takaṭu, attakaṭṭiṟ poṟikkappaṭum ciṟappup peyar, oruvaṉukku makkaḷ pukaḻcciyākavō ikaḻcciyākavō vaḻaṅkum ciṟappup peyar, taṭṭiyāṉa kāṟṟāṭi, marappaṭṭi, cīri (maravuri), cīripōṉṟa tuṇi.
paṭṭaṅ kaṭṭutal, paṭṭañ cūṭṭutal, paṭṭamaḷittal, paṭṭap peyar mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka.
paṭṭam - paṭam = tuṇi, tuṇiyil eḻutappaṭum ōviyam.
paṭam - paṇam = takaṭāṉa kācu.
paṭam - paṭām = tuṇi, tuṇikkaccu, yāṉiyiṉ mukapaṭām.
paṭam - paṭakam = tiriccīli, paṭamāṭam.
paṭam - paṭaṅku = āṭi, tiri, paṭamāṭam, tuppākkiyiṉ aṭi, pātattiṉ muṟpakuti.
paṭaṅku - paṭaṅkam = paṭamāṭam.
paṭam - paṭavu - paṭaku = pāy kaṭṭiya tōṇi.
paṭam - paṭal = taṭṭipōṉṟa aṭippu.
paṭal - paṭalam = mēṟkaṭṭi.
paṭal = paṭaliki = pūntaṭṭu.
paṭṭu - paṭṭā = paṭṭivāḷ, vaṇṭip paṭṭi, āvaṇa ēṭu, āvaṇam.
paṭṭu - paṭṭi = cīli, takaṭu, ēṭu, ēṭṭileḻutappaṭum aṭṭavaṇi, mūṅkiṟ piḷāccu, taṭṭi, taṭṭiyālamitta kāl naṭittoḻu, kāl naṭippaṭṭiyuḷḷa ciṟṟūr. pakkattuk kollikaḷiṟ pōy mēyum paṭṭimāṭupōṟ kaṭṭukkāva liṉṟit tiripavaṉ.
paṭṭi - paṭṭiyal = poruḷvili yaṭṭavaṇi, taṭṭiyāṉa variccal.
paṭṭi - paṭṭiki = ēṭu, paṭṭayam, cīli, arikkacci, mēkali, yōkappaṭṭi.
paṭṭu - paṭṭi = taṭṭiyāṉa poruḷ.
marappaṭṭi, vaṇṭippaṭṭi, vāḻippaṭṭi, poṭip paṭṭi, tōṭpaṭṭi, yōkappaṭṭi mutaliya peyarkaḷiyum, paṭṭiyaṭittal, paṭṭi tīrttal mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷiyum; paṭṭiyaram, paṭṭippuḻu mutaliya vaṟṟiyum nōkkuka.
paṭṭi - paṭṭiyam - paṭṭayam = paṭṭivāḷ, ēṭu, takaṭu, ceppēṭṭāvaṇam.
paṭṭi - paṟṟi - patti = taṭṭiyāṉatu.
mūṅkiṟpaṟṟi tēṅkāyppaṟṟi mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkuka.
paṭṭam - pāṭṭam. pāṭṭaram = taṭṭiyaram.
muṭṭu
muṭṭu - maṭṭu - maṭṭi = paṭṭi.
vāḻippaṭṭiyi vāḻimaṭṭiyeṉṟum poṭip paṭṭiyip poṭi maṭṭi eṉṟum, kūṟutal kāṇka.
maṭṭi = taṭṭiyāṉa ōlikkāmpu, taṭṭiyāṉa aṭikaruvi (bat).
paṉimaṭṭi pantaṭimaṭṭi mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷik kāṇka.
maṭal = maṭṭi, itaḻ. maṭal - maṭali - vaṭali = maṭṭi veṭṭappaṭāta iḷampaṉi.
(maṭi) - maṇi = taṭṭup palaki, taṭṭiyāṉa kaṭṭi.
aṭimaṇi arivāḷmaṇi mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkuka.
xxi. paṭartal
taṭṭiyākum poruḷ paṭarntu paravum.
paṭṭu = ciṟṟūr.
paṭṭu - paṟṟu = nilam, ciṟṟūr, mēkappaṭi, tappaḷam.
paṭam = paṭṭiyāka akalum pāmpiṉ kaḻuttu.
paṭam - paṇam - paṇi = paṭattiyuṭiya nalla pāmpu, pāmpu.
paṭaṅku - paṭaṅkāṉ = akaṉṟa pūrāṉ.
paṭal - paṭalam = parappu, iyaliṉum paranta nūṟpakuti, tiripōṟ kaṇṇiṟ paṭarum cati.
paṭal - paṭali = paṭarki, paranta iṭam, vāyakaṉṟa paṟi.
paṭali - patali = vāyakaṉṟa paṟi.
paṭal - paṭar = paravum mēkappaṟṟu.
paṭartal = paravutal, akalutal, cellutal.
paṭṭi - paṭi = parappu, kūṭṭam, cēṉipparappu. cēṉip pakuti, mēkappaṟṟu.
paṭāki = akaṉṟa koṭi, nāṭṭiṉ uṭpirivu, kūṭṭam.
paṭāki - patāki = peruṅkoṭi. aintu viralum neruṅkip paranta ki.
pāṭavari = vāḷavari.
(paṭa) - para - paravu. para - parappu. para - parattu (pi.vi.)
para - paravi = paranta kaṭal. para - parampu.
parampaṭittal= uḻuta nilattiṟ kaṭṭiyaṭittup paravac ceytal.
paratti = parantu oḻukupavaḷ. parākam = paravum pūttūḷ.
para - pār = parappu, tērttaṭṭu, pāṟi, nilappakuti, ñālam.
pārmutir paṉikkaṭal  (tirumuru. 45)
pār - pāṟi.
pār - pāri. pārittal = paravutal, paruttal, virittal.
para - pā - pāvu. pā - pāy = paranta taṭukku. pāytal = parattal.
palaki - akaṉṟa pāḷam. palāciṉi = parakka naṭutal.
pāḷ = iruppuc caṭṭam. pāḷam = palaki. pāḷi = viriyum maṭal.
pāḻi = akalam. perumi, nakar (pērūr).
akittal = aṭittal. akalutal = paṭartal, nīṅkutal, cellutal. akalam = mārpu.
akal = akaṉṟa maṇtaṭṭam.
akal - āl = paṭarumaram. ālpōṟ paṭarntu eṉṉum uvami marapi nōkkuka. āl - ālam.
āli = perumpūtam.
aṭittal = taṭṭutal. aṭartal = taṭṭiyuruvākkutal. 
aitaṭarnta nūṟpeytu  (puṟam. 29).
aṭar = takaṭu; pūvitaḻ.
ataḷ = tōḷ.
avittal = kuṟṟutal. aval = kuṟṟutalāl taṭṭiyāṉa arici.
aviḻtal = virital, virintu kaṭṭu viṭutal.
alittal - aṭittal. alaku = akalam, kattiyalaku.
alaku pākku = taṭṭiyāṉa pākku.
alacutal = iḻiyakalutal. alacal = iḻiyakaṉṟa tuṇi.
alartal = malartal.
alavi - parattimi.
kalattal = parattal. kalakka naṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
kaṭṭuri kalantakāli  (kampaṉ. kara. 68)
kala - kalavu - kalavam - kalāvam = arippaṭṭiki. mayiṟpīli.
kali = āṭi.
callā = alacal tuṇi, callāri = alacaṟ cīli.
callavaṭṭam = kēṭakavaki.
tuvittal = kuṟṟutal. tukil = tuṇi. tukiliki = tuṇikkoṭi. takaḻi = akal.
takaṇ = puṟpaṟṟi. takaṇ - takaṭu.
(tuṭṭu) - taṭṭu - taṭṭam - taṭam = akalam, perumi, akaṉṟa kuḷam. taṭam - taṭākam.
taḷam = taṭṭu, parappu, paṭi itaḻ. tāḷam = cālar. 
tālam = taṭṭu, yāṉikkātu.
nal - (nāl) - nālam - ñālam = paranta ulakam.
parumiyum akalamum oṉṟē. periya ili, periya tāḷ, periya taṭṭu, periya tuṇi, peruvaḻi eṉṟu akaṉṟa poruḷkaḷik kūṟutal kāṇka.
naṉam = akalam. naṉam - naṉavu = akalam.
malartal = virital.
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟku nēr mūlam akaramutalāyiṉum, aṭimūlam ukaramutala eṉpatu oru tali. itu ippaṭala muḻumikkum okkum.
xxii. amuṅkutal
amuṅkutalāvatu oru poruḷ aḻunta muṭṭutal.
uṟu - uṟuttu. (uḷ) - (aḷ) - aḻu - aḻuntu - aḻuttu - aḻuttam.
(um) - am - amuṅku - amukku - amukkam.
(num) - nem - (nemuṅku) - nemuṅkutal = amuṅkutal.
(nuḷ) - (neḷ) - neru - neruṅku - nerukku - nerukkam.
(3) kuttal muṟi
kuttal eṉpatu, moṭṭik karuvi kuttutalum kūrṅkaruvi kuttutalum eṉa iruvaki. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉatē īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum.
i. kuttutal
kuḷ - kiḷ, kuḷḷutal - nakattāṟ kiḷḷutal.
kuttu - kuntu.
kuttu - kottu - koṭṭu.
cuḷ. cuḷkaḷ eṉṟu kuttukiṟatu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
cuḷ - cur - curukku - cuṟukku (kuttaṟkuṟippu)
tūṇṭutal = kuttutal.
tuḷ - teḷ - teṟu. teṟutal - koṭṭutal.
tuḷ - taḷ - (tay) - ti. tittal = kuttutal. muḷ tikkum eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
nuḷ. muḷ. mucumucuttal = tiṉaveṭuttal.
ii. kuntutal
kuntutalāvatu nilattiṟ kuttuvatupōl uṭkārtal. kuṇṭi kuttutal eṉpatu nāki vaḻakku. kutta vittal eṉpatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku.
kuttu - kuntu. kuntutal = uṭkārtal.
kuttu - kutti - kuti. kutiṅkāl = nilattiṟ kuttum aṭippātam.
kuti = nilattiṟ kuttum viṟkōlaṭi.

iii. nērātal

ceṅkuttu naṭṭukkuttu mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷi nōkkuka.

katir - katiravaṉ.

piḷḷikkaṟṟāḷi, piḷḷik kiṇaṟu. piḷḷik kōṭṭi. piḷḷit takkāḷi, piḷḷippiṟi, piḷḷip peṭṭi, piḷḷi viḷātti (kuṭṭi viḷā) mutaliya puṇarp peyarkaḷil. piḷḷi eṉpatu ciṟumiyik kuṟikkumēṉum, atu iḷamip peyariṉ āṭci virivē eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum.  kuṭṭit tolkāppiyam eṉpatil kuṭṭi eṉpatum atuvē.

puḷ - poḷ - poṭṭu = ciṟiyatu, ciṟṟaḷavu.

poḷ - poṭi.
muḷ - muṭṭu = ciṟiyatu. muṭṭukkaḷ = ciṟuporuṭkaḷ.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maṇ - maṇi = ciṟiyatu.
maṇikkayiṟu, maṇikkāṭi, maṇikkuṭal, maṇikki, maṇittak kāḷi, maṇiccampā, maṇiccuṟā, maṇippayaṟu, maṇippuṟā, mutaliya puṇarppeyarkaḷil, maṇi eṉṉuñ col ciṟumiyik kuṟittal kāṇka.

māṇ - māṇi = ciṟiyatu, ciṟuvaṉ, maṇavātāṉ, māṇavaṉ.
karumāṇiyā yiranta kaḷvaṉē  (tiv. iyaṟpā, 2, 61.)
māṇ - māṇaval = ciṟuvaṉ, kaṟkuñ ciṟuvaṉ.

o.nō. piḷḷi = māṇavaṉ, māṇavi. paḷḷip piḷḷi eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
E. pupil, from L. pubillus, pupilla, dims of pupas, a boy; pupa. a girls.
E. pedant, from Gr. pis. pidos, a child.
māṇākkaṉ māṇākki eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷum, māṇavaṉ eṉṉum vaṭivamum, ataṉtiripāṉa māṇi eṉpatum, ivaṟṟukku aṭivaḻiyāṉa koṭivaḻic coṟkaḷum, vaṭa moḻiyililli.
(4) uṟittal
uṟittal eṉpatu aḻuttit tākkutal. ālaṅ kaṭṭiyum perumaḻittuḷiyum nilattil valvēkamāy viḻuvatiyum. oru pēroli kātil aḻuttamāyp paṭuvatiyum, veppam kāram puḷippu mutaliyavi uṭaliyō nāviyō tākkuvatiyum, uṟittal eṉpar. ivaṟṟuḷ, mutalatu parumit tākkum ēṉiya  nuṇmit tākkum ākum. iviyellām toṭutaliṉ vaki kaḷē. toṭutaleṉpatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟiṉ mēṟpaṭutal. paṇpi paṭutalum paṇpu paṭutalum eṉappaṭutal eṉa iruvaki. aḻuttamāyp paṭutalē uṟittal.
i. cuṭutal
cuḷḷeṉṟu kuttukiṟatu eṉpatu pōṉṟē, cuḷḷeṉṟu veyilaṭikkiṟatu eṉpatum vaḻakku. kūrṅkaruviyāṟ kuttuvatu pōṉṟatu vemmittākku.
uṟittal = veyil uṭampit tākkutal.
cuḷ - cuḷḷāppu = kaṭuveyil.
cuḷ - cuḷḷi - cūḷi = ceṅkal cuṭumiṭam.
cuḷ - (cuṭku) - (caṭkāṉ) - cukkāṉ = aḷaviṟantu cuṭappaṭṭa ceṅkal.
cuḷ - cur - curam = cuṭumpāli. cur - curaṉ - cūraṉ = katiravaṉ.
cur - cuṟu - cuṟukku (cuṭutaṟ kuṟippu).
cuṟu - cuṟīr (cu.ku.).
cuḷ - cuṭu - cuṭal - cuṭali. cuṭu - cūṭu.
cuṭu - cuṭar - cuṭarōṉ.
tuḷ - teḷ - teṟu. teṟutal = cuṭutal.
nīṅkiṟ ṟeṟūum  (kuṟaḷ. 1104).

vii. cenniṟam
neruppāṉatu civanta niṟamāyiruppatāl, neruppiṉ peyarkaḷi ṉiṉṟum appeyarkaḷiṉ aṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum cenniṟattik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ tirintuḷḷaṉa. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,
eriniṟat tilavam  (cilap. 5: 214.)
eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. erimalar = murukkumalar (cīvaka. 662.)
erimalar = centāmari (cīvaka. 2741).
ul - al - alattam - alattakam = cempañcuk kuḻampu.
al - ar - araṉ = civaṉ  (cenniṟattāṉ).
tamiḻaruḷ oru cārār iṟivaṉit tīvaṭiviṉaṉākak karutiyatāl, avaṉi araṉ eṉṟum civaṉ eṉṟum piṟavāṟum aḻittaṉar.
ar - arakku = civanta meḻuku.
ar - arattam = civappu. kuruti. arattam (arattam eṉpatē cariyāṉa vaṭivam. atu mutal keṭṭu vaḻaṅki vaṭacolleṉap piṟaḻavuṇarappaṭṭu, piṉpu ikaram muṉṉiṭṭeḻutappaṭu kiṉṟatu o.nō. araṅkaṉ - raṅkaṉ - iraṅkaṉ). - attam = civappu attam - atti = civanta kaṉitarum maram.
ar - aruṇam = civappu. aruṇaṉ = kālic ceṅkatirōṉ. aruṇamali = civaṉ aḻaṟpiḻampāka niṉṟa mali. aruṇamali - aṇṇāmali.
ar - (ār) - āriyam = civanta kēḻvaraku.
ul - (il) - ilanti = civanta kaṉitarum muṭceṭi.
(il) - (ir) - iratti = ilanti.
(ir) - irāki = kēḻvaraku.
ul - el - (er) eruvi = cemparuntu.
ul - uru - urumpu - urumparam - utumparam = civappu, cempu.
kul - kulikam = civappu, cātiliṅkam.

cuḷ - (coḷ) - cōṇ - cōṇam = civappu. cōṇa mali = aruṇamali. cōṇi = civappu.

cey = civappu. ceyyaṉ = civantavaṉ.

kaṟuppuñ civappum vekuḷip poruḷa  (tol. uri. 74)
cey - ceyali = civanta acōkan taḷir.

ceymmi - cemmi.

cēy - cē. cēttal = civattal. cē - cētu = civappu. cētu - cēttu = civappu. cēttu - cēntu - cēntaṉ = cēyōṉ (murukaṉ).
cētu - kētu = civappu.
cul - (cōl) - cāl - cāli = cennel.
(cōl) - cēl = ceṅkeṇṭi mīṉ.

cum - cem - cempu - ceppu kempu - kempu = civanta kal. centūḷ - centūḷam - centūram - centuram - cinturam = ceñcuṇṇam.

(coku) - cokkam = cempu. cokkaṉ = civaṉ.

cekkac cevēr eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.

kallā makkaḷ civappic cuvappu eṉṟu collal kavaṉikkat takkatu.
āriyar intiyāviṟkuḷ pukumuṉṉarē tamiḻar civa vaḻipāṭṭi ṉarāyiruntamiyālum, civaṉ eṉpatu tamiḻc collātalā lum, ataṟkut tamiḻ vaḻiyāyp poruḷ kūṟātu vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyāy naṉmi ceypavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvatu porun tātu. anti vaṇṇaṉ centī vaṇṇaṉ māṇikkam mutaliya peyarkaḷ civaṉukkup payiṉṟu varutal kāṇka.

tuppu - tuppam = arattam.
tumpu - tōmpu = civappu.

tāmparam - tāmpari - tāmari = cemmalarvaki.
tāmari - mari.


nāṭṭuppuṟattu makkaḷ iṉṟum tāmariyit tāmpari eṉṟē vaḻaṅkuvar. tāmari eṉpatu cemmalariyum muḷari eṉpatu veṇmalariyum tuvakkattiṟ kuṟittatākat terikiṉṟatu.
tuḷ - (taḷ) - taḷavu - taḷavam = cemmulli.

tuvaḷ - tuvaṇṭi = kāviyuṭi.
araṉ, civaṉ, cēntaṉ, cēyōṉ, cokkaṉ eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ. kariyōṉ, kaṇṇaṉ, māyōṉ, māl mutaliyavi tirumāliyum; kariyōḷ (karuppāy). kāḷi, māyōḷ, māri mutaliyavi kāḷiyiyum; karumi paṟṟik kuṟikkum oru poruṭ coṟkaḷāyiruttali nōkkuka. cokkaṉ eṉpatu aḻakaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ paṭumēṉum. civaṉik kuṟikkum pōtu apporuḷ paṭātu.
viii. terital
viḷaṅkum (oḷirum) poruḷkaḷ teḷivākat teritalāl, viḷakkattik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ teḷivākat teritalik kuṟikkum.
tulaṅkutal = viḷaṅkutal, teḷivākat terital. 
tul - tel - teṉ. teṉpaṭutal = terital, tōṉṟutal, pulap paṭutal.
tuḷaṅkutal = viḷaṅkutal.
tuḷaṅkumiḷam piṟiyāḷaṉ  (tēvā. 88, 10)
tuḷaṅkoḷi = mikkavoḷi. tuḷ - tuṇ - tuṇi = oḷi. (piṅ.).
tuḷ - teḷ. teḷḷutal = viḷaṅkutal. teḷivātal.
teḷḷuṅ kaḻalukkē  (tiruvāca. 10, 19).
teḷ - teḷi. teḷital = viḷaṅkutal, naṉṟāyt terital.
teḷi - teri. terital = viḷakkamātal, tōṉṟutal.
muḷ - viḷ - viḷaṅku. viḷaṅkutal = oḷirtal. teḷivākat terital.
ix aṟital
teriyum poruḷ aṟiyappaṭum.

x. ārāytal
teriyāta poruḷip paṟṟiya aṟivu ārāycci vaḻippaṭuvatām.
teḷḷutal = ārāytal.

teri - tēr. tērtal = ārāytal.
xi. teḷital
ārāycciyāl aiyam nīṅkit teḷivu piṟakkum. ataṉpiṉ teḷiyap paṭṭataṉ mītu nampikkiyuṇṭām. ataṉāl oru viṉi muyaṟcikku uṟutiyāṉa tīrmāṉañ ceyyappeṟum.
tuṇital = teḷivātal, uṟutiyāka muṭivu ceytal, tuṇivu = uṟuti, cūḻccit teḷivu.
tuṉṉal pōkiya tuṇiviṉōṉ  (puṟam. 23)
tuṇiccal = kaṭumaṉattiṭam.
tul - tel - teṉ - teṉpu = teḷivu. teṉpu - tempu.
teḷḷutal = teḷivātal. teḷ - teḷi. teḷital = aiyanīṅkutal, namputal.
teḷ - teṇ - teṭpu - teṭpam = teḷivu teṭṭavar = teḷintavar.
teri - tēr. tērtal= uṟutikoḷḷutal, muṭivu ceytal.
tēr - tēṟu. tēṟutal = teḷital, tiṭaṅkoḷḷutal, namputal, tuṇital.
tēṟu - tēṟṟu - tēṟṟam = teḷivu, uṟuti.
tēṟṟaṉ = uṇmiyaṟivuḷḷavaṉ. tēṟṟu tēṟṟaravu = tēṟṟutal, teḷivittal.
teḷivu eṉpatu kalakkattiṟku etirāyiruppatāl, uḷḷam aiyuṟavu nīṅki uṇmiyaṟivatum,oruvaṉnōyuṇṭaniliyil 

ēṟpaṭṭa kalakkam nīṅki avaṉ uṭampu valuppeṟuvatum, tērviṟku muṉṉuḷḷa aiyam nīṅkumāṟu māṇavaṉ tērcci kāṭṭuvatum. teḷital allatu tēṟutal eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum.
nīrtteḷivu
nīrt teḷivum kalaṅkal nīkkamē. maṇṇun tūciyum piṟavum kalantirukkum pōtu nīrūṭu pārttal iyalātu. avi nīṅkiya niliyil nīr paḷiṅku pōliruppatāl, ataṉ uḷḷum aṭiyum uḷḷa poruḷkaḷ teḷivāyt teriyum. aṅṅaṉam teriyum nīr teṇṇīr.
cilaviṭattu, ūṭu teriyāta nīrpporuḷum ataṉiṉun tiṇṇiya niliyuṭaṉ oppu nōkkit teḷivu eṉappaṭum.
tuḷ - tuṇ - tuṇi. tuṇital = teḷital.
tuṇinīr mellaval  (maturik. 283.)
tuḷ - teḷ - teṇ - teṭpu - teṭpam = teḷivu.
teḷ - teḷi - teḷivu.
teṭṭa = teḷinta.
mālkari teṭṭa matappaci  (kampa. carapaṅ. 8).
teḷ - teṭu. tiṇṇamillāta kañciyit teṭuteṭuveṉṟiruk kiṉṟateṉpar.
teḷ - teri - tēr - tēṟu = teḷivu. tēṟutal = teḷital.
tēṟu = nīrit teḷivikkum tēṟṟāṅkoṭṭi.
tēṟupaṭu ciṉṉīr pōla (maṇi. 23, 142).

tēṟṟu - tēṟṟā (tēṟṟāṅkoṭṭi, akkoṭṭi maram).
tēṟṟāviṉuṭiya vitiyik koṇṭu kalattē mellat tēṟṟak
kalaṅkiya nīriṟ citivu teḷiyumāṟu pōla  (kalit. 142, vari).
xiii. kaḷ teḷivu
kaḷḷum tēṉum potuvāka arittum vaṭikaṭṭiyum teḷivāṉa tāka eṭukkap peṟuvatāl, teḷivu eṉṉum col avviraṇ ṭiyum ākupeyarāy uṇarttiṟṟu.
iṉimiyum veṟiviḷippum kaḷḷukkun tēṉukkum potuviyal pātalāl, kaḷ tēṉ matu maṭṭu mutaliya peyarkaḷ avviraṇ ṭiyum potuppaṭak kuṟikkum.
iyaṟkik kaḷḷum ceyaṟkik kaḷḷum eṉak kaḷ iruvakip paṭum.
tul - (tel) - teṉ = (teḷivu, kaḷ) iṉimi.
teṉṉici pāṭum pāṇaṉ  (tiruvālavā. 56, 7).
teṉ - tēṉ = teḷivu, kaḷ, matu. tēṉ - tēm - tīm - tīvu = iṉimi. tēṉ - tēṉi. tēṉittal = iṉittal. tīviya = iṉimiyāṉa. tīm - (ti) - titti.
tuḷ - teḷ - teḷivu = patanīr, karuppañcāṟu. 
tēṟu - tēṟal = teḷinta kaḷ, tēṉ.
xiv. veṇmi
oḷiyāṉatu veṇmiyāyiruttalāl, oḷiyi allatu viḷakkattik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu (allatu collaṭi kaḷiṉiṉṟu) veṇmiyik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
neruppu civantateṉṟum, kaṭumiyāṉa katiravaṉoḷi veṇmi yāṉateṉṟum, potuvākak koḷḷappaṭum.
kuru - kuruku = veṇmi.
tul - tulliyam = veṇmi.
tuḷ - teḷ - teḷi. teḷital = veṇmiyātal.
pul - pal - pāl = veṇmi.
pāl - vāl = veṇmi.
muḷ - muru - muruntu = veṇmi.
muḷ - viḷ - viḷar. viḷarttal = veṇmiyātal.
viḷ - veḷ - veṇmi. veḷ - veḷḷi. veḷ - veḷu.
xv. veḷuttal
viḷ - viḷar. viḷarttal = veṇmiyātal, veṭkutal.

viḷ - veḷ - veḷḷeṉa = viṭiya, veḷḷeṉak kāṭṭi = veḷḷeṅkāṭṭi = viṭiyaṟkāli.
veḷ - veḷu. kiḻakku veḷuttal = viṭiyumuṉ kīḻttici veḷḷiyātal.
veḷuttal = veṇṇiṟamātal, vaṇṇāṉ tuṇikaḷi veḷḷiyākkutal allatu tuppuravākkutal.
veḷ = veḷku. veḷkutal = nāṇattāl mukam veḷuttal. veḷku - veṭku - veṭkam. veḷ - veḷiṟu, veḷiṟutal = ciṟitu veṇṇiṟamātal.
xvi. veṇmiyāṉa poruṭkaḷ
ul - el - elumpu - eṉpu.

viḷavam - vilvam = viḷāviṟku iṉamāṉa kūviḷam.
karuviḷam = marappaṭṭi karuttuḷḷa viḷāvaki.
