veḷ - veḷḷi. veḷ - veḷḷi.
veḷ - veḷicci = veṇṇiṟak koṇṭi mīṉ.
veḷ - veḷir - veḷiṟu = viḷiyāta veṇmaram. aṟiviṉmi.
kuṟippu : - kuruku eṉpatu kokki maṭṭuṅ kuṟiyātu nāri kāraṉṉam mutaliya paṟpala nīrppaṟavi vakikaḷik kuṟitta lāl, avaṟṟik kuṟikkumpōtu vaḷinta kaḻuttuḷḷatu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum.
xvii. tūymi
aḻukkellām perumpālum piṟa niṟamāyiruppatālum, aḻukkaṟṟa cāyantōykkāta āṭi veṇmiyāyiruppa tālum, cuvariṉ aḻukkip pōkka veṇcuṇṇam pūcuvatālum, veṇmi tūymik kaṭiyāḷamām.
tuḷ - tuy - tuyya = tūya. tūy - tu - tuppu - tuppuravu = tūymi.
tuy - tūy - tūymi. tūy - tū.
pul - pal - pāl - vāl. vāṉmi = tūymi. vālāmi = tūymiyiṉmi. vālaṟivu = tūya aṟivu.
mul - mulli = kaṟpu.
muḷ - veḷ - veḷḷi = tūymi, tūya āṭi, veṇpā. veḷ - veḷi = veṇpā.
ekkāraṇattiyiṭṭum vēṟṟuttaḷi viravātu taṉṟaḷi koṇṭē iyalum tūymiyuṭiya pā veṇpā (veḷ+pā).
veḷḷi = kaḷaṅkamaṟṟavaṉ, cūtuvātillātavaṉ.
veḷḷikkilli kaḷḷac cinti eṉṟār auviyār.
veḷ - veḷḷanti = kaḷḷaṅ kavaṭaṟṟavaṉ.
xviii. veṟumi
tūymi yeṉpatu vēṟṟup poruḷum vēṟṟup poruḷi yalpuṅ kalavātatātaliṉ, tūymik karuttil taṉimik karuttum taṉimik karuttil veṟumik karuttum tōṉṟum.
taṉikkaruppic cuttak karuppu eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
veṇmi = tūymi, taṉimi, veṟumi.
veḷḷili = ili tavira vēṟu pū kāy oṉṟumillāta koṭiyili. allatu uṇaviṉṟiyum vaḻaṅkappaṭum ili.
veṇpāṭṭam = mārāyamillātu veṟumiyāṉa pāṭṭam.
veṇṇilik kaṭaṉ = īṭillātu veṟumiyākak koṭukkap paṭum kaṭaṉ.
veḷ - veṟu - veṟumi = taṉimi, oṉṟumiṉmi. veṟu ili veṟṟili (veḷḷili.)
veṟuñcōṟu = kaṟivakiyillāt taṉic cōṟu.
māṭṭut tāmpaṇiyil (tāvaṇiyil) eṅku pārttālum veṟum māṭāyirukkum eṉpatu pōṉṟa vaḻakkik kāṇka.
veṟuvāy = oṉṟumillāta vāy.
veṟu - veṟumam - veṟumaṉ. veṟumaṉē pōy viṭṭāṉ eṉpatu. oṉṟuñ collātu allatu ceyyātu pōyviṭṭāṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvati nōkkuka.
veṟu - veṟṟu. veṟṟiṭam = oṉṟumillāta iṭam. veṟṟu vaṇṭi = veṟu vaṇṭi. veṟṟāḷ = taṉiyāḷ.
veṟu - veṟi. veṟittal = mukilum maḻiyumiṉṟi vāṉam veṟumiyātal.
veṟi - veṟittu - veṟikcu = veṟumi.
āḷiruntu pōyviṭṭa veṟṟiṭattik kaṇṭu. veṟic ceṉṟirukkiṟatu eṉpar.
xix. vaṟumi
poruḷillā veṟumiyē vaṟumi.
veḷ - (veṇku) - veṅku - veṅkaṉ = oṉṟu millātavaṉ.
veḷ - veṟu. veṟumpayal = oṉṟumillāta payal. 
veṟu - veṟṟu. veṟṟukkaṭṭi = oṉṟumillātavaṉ.
veṟṟeṉat toṭuttal = (ciṟanta) poruḷiṉṟic colli yaṭukkutal.
veṟu - vaṟu - vaṟumi = poruḷiṉmi.
vaṟuṅ kūval = nīrillāk kiṇaṟu.
orunāḷum pōkātavaṉ tirunāḷukkup pōṉāṉām. tirunāḷum veṟunāḷāyp pōṉatām eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. veṟunāḷ = (ciṟanta) nikaḻcciyaṟṟa nāḷ.
xx. vīṇmi
payaṉillā veṟumi vīṇmi.
viḷ - vīṇ.
veḷ - veṭṭi. veṭṭi vēli = payaṉaṟṟa vēli, vīṇ vēli.
veṭṭiyāḷ = vēli ceyyāta āḷ.
veḷ - veṟu. veṟum pēccu = payaṉaṟṟa pēccu.
veṟu - vaṟu - vaṟitu = vīṇāṉatu, vīṇāka.
xxi. veḷi
vāṉaveḷi oṉṟumaṟṟa veṟṟiṭamātalāl, allatu veṟṟiṭa māyt tōṉṟutalāl, atu veṟumi yuṇarttuñ coṟkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.
viḷ - viṇ = ākāyam. viṇ - viṇṭu = ākāyam.
viḷ - (vicu) - vicumpu.
viḷ - veḷ - veḷi = ākāyam.
veḷḷiṭi = veṟumiyāṉa iṭam.
vaṟitu niliiya kāyamum  (puṟam 20)
xxii. veḷippāṭu
veḷippāṭāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟaṉ uḷḷiruntu veḷi varutal.
viḷḷutal = veḷiviṭṭuc collutal.
viḷ - viḷampu - viḷamparam.
viḷ - viṭu. viṭutal = veḷivarutal, piñcu viṭutal. inta maram piñcu viṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
viṭuttal = veḷippaṭak kūṟutal.
viṭu - (viṭi) - (viṭicci) - viricci = teyvattāl viḷampappaṭu vatākak karutappeṟum naḷḷirā naṟcol.
pākkattu viricci = paṭimaṟavar ceṉṟu taṅkiya pakkattu ōrntu kēṭkum viricci.
viṭu - viṭi. viṭittal = veḷippaṭuttutal.
viḷ - veḷ - veḷi - veḷicci = kātiṟ puṟappaṭum koppuḷam.

veḷi - veṭi. veṭittal = veḷivarutal, piñcu viṭutal.

eṉpatu aṭiyārkku nallār mēṟkōḷ.

uṟippu eṉṉum paṇpup peyar vitantu cuṭṭuvatu kārattiyē. uṟikkum poruḷic cuḷḷeṉṟirukkiṟatu eṉpar. kāratti ericcal eṉṟu kūṟuvar. itaṉāl, uṟittalic cuṭutalō ṭoppak koṇṭami pulaṉām.

xxvi. ciṉattal

ēmap puṇiyic cuṭum.

cuṇṭucol = cuṭucol.
toṭutal : toṭutal (allatu muṭṭutal) eṉpatu, uḷḷiruntu toṭutal veḷiyiruntu toṭutal eṉa iru vakittu. karuppikkuḷḷiruntu tāy vayiṟṟik kuḻavi muṭṭutal uḷḷiruntu toṭutal; tāy vayiṟṟiṉiṉṟu piṟanta uyirikaḷ tammi aṭuttavaṟṟit toṭuvatu veḷiyiruntu toṭutal. marattuḷḷiruntu tuḷirum nilattuḷḷiruntu muḷiyum muṇṭi veḷivaruvatu pōla, tāy vayiṟṟiṉuḷḷiruntu kuḻaviyum muṇṭi veḷivaruvatu, ataṟkut tōṟṟam. ākavē tōṟṟamum uḷ tōṟṟam veḷit tōṟṟam eṉa iruvakittu. muṭṭikkuḷ kuñcum karuppikkuḷ kuṭṭiyum tōṉṟuvatu uḷ tōṟṟam, avi piṟappatu veḷit tōṟṟam. ākavē, cilavaṟṟiṟku oru tōṟṟamum cilavaṟṟiṟku iru tōṟṟamum uḷavām.

vii. ceytal
putitākac ceyyappaṭum poruḷ ēṟkeṉavē ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ aḷavi mikuttalāl, mikuttaṟ karuttil ceytaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. āṅkilattilum make eṉṉum colli L. magnus (great) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu tirippar. oru taṉippaṭṭavarkkō oru kūṭṭattārkkō uḷḷa poruḷ pōtātapōtu tēviyāṉa aḷavu atu perukkappaṭum. viḷivup perukkam eṉṟu aṭikkaṭi kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. payir vaḷarttalip payir ceytal eṉṟum, poruḷīṭṭutalip poruḷ ceytal eṉṟum kūṟuvatiyum nōkkuka.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭu - karu.
kaṭuttal = mikutal. karuvi = tokuti (group). karumi = perumi.
āyutattik kuṟikkum karuvi eṉṉum col tūya teṉ collāta lālum, ataṉōṭu toṭarpuḷḷa karaṇam eṉṉum col ceykiyeṉṟu poruḷpaṭutalālum, ivviraṇ ṭiṟkum karu eṉpatu pakutiyāyiruttalālum, karu eṉṉumcol 

mikutip poruḷ tarutalālum, mikuttaṟ karuttu ceytaṟ karuttip piṟappittaṟkēṟṟatātalālum, ceytal eṉap poruḷpaṭum karuttal eṉpatoru viṉi muṉṉoru kālattu vaḻaṅkip piṉṉar vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉatākat terikiṉṟatu.
karu - karumam = ceyki. toḻil. o. nō. paru - parumam.
karu - (karum) - karumam. o.nō. uru - urum - urumam.
karum - kam = toḻil, kammiyar toḻil. 
īṅ kammum  (tol. eḻut. 328)
kam - kammiyam - kammiyaṉ = kammāḷaṉ.
kam - kammāḷaṉ.
karumam - kammam = kammiyar toḻil.
kammañ ceymākkaḷ  (nālaṭi. 393)
kammavāru = payir vēli ceyyum teluṅka vakuppār.
karu - karuvi = ceyyum āyutam.
karu - karaṇam = ceyki, caṭaṅku, maṇac caṭaṅku, uṭaṉpaṭikki, āvaṇam (deed), āvaṇam eḻutuvōṉ, kaṇakkeḻutuvōṉ.
karaṇam - karaṇattāṉ = kaṇakkaṉ.
karaṇam = ceyyuṅ karuvi. akakkaraṇam puṟakkaraṇam eṉpavi uṭampiṟku akamum puṟamumuḷḷa karuvikaḷākiya uṟuppukkaḷ.
karu - kār. kār - kāraṇam. kār - kāriyam.
karuniṟattiyum tōṉṟutaliyuṅ kuṟikkum karu eṉṉum collum, kār eṉṟu tirital kāṇka. kārttal = karuppātal, tōṉṟutal.
aṇam iyam eṉpaṉa vikutikaḷ. o. nō. kaṭṭaṇam, ēraṇam, kaṇṇiyam, vāriyam.
karumam karuvi eṉpaṉa mutaṟkāla vaṭivaṅkaḷ. ataṉāl, avi marapākap paṇṭi nūlkaḷiṟ payiṉṟu varuvaṉa. karaṇam kāraṇam kāriyam eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyarkku muntiṉa iṭik kāla vaṭivaṅkaḷ.
kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇara (tol. 1088).
ākkan tāṉē kāraṇa mutaṟṟē  (tol. 504)
āriyam naṉṟu tamiḻtī teṉavuritta
kāriyattāṟ kālakkōṭ paṭṭāṉi  (nakkīrar).
(cuḷ) - ceḷ - cey. cey - ceḻu - ceḻuttal = mikutal. ceḻu ceḻuttal = mika vaḷappamātal.
cey - ceyki = ceyal, payirttoḻil, uṭaṉ paṭikki, āvaṇam (deed).

iii. kuppuṟutul
pūkkaḷ malarvatu tam mukattit tiṟappatu pōlum, avi kuvivatu ati mūṭuvatu pōlum, iruttalālum; pakalil malarntu iravil kūmpum ilikaḷum malarkaḷum, perum pālum, malarumpōtu mēṉōkkiyum kūmpum pōtu kīḻ nōkkiyum iruttalālum, māntaṉ mēṉōkkip paṭuttiruttal malarnta nilikkoppākavum, kīḻ nōkkip paṭuttiruttal kūmpiya nilikkoppākavum, collappaṭum.

kavi - kaviki = kuṭi.

kavikaṇ = kuṭaṅkiyāl maṟikkappaṭṭa kaṇ. mukil kuṭipōl vaḷintu paṭutalum kavital eṉappaṭum.

iv. virumpik kākkum talivaṉ
oru poruḷi virumpukiṟavaṉ atip pōṟṟik kāppāṉā taliṉ, virumpaṟ karuttu kāttaṟ karuttiyun taḻuvum.
e.ṭu. pēṇutal = virumputal, pōṟṟutal. tanti tāyp pēṇ eṉṉum auviyār kūṟṟi nōkkuka. oru talivaṉukku avaṉ kīḻppaṭṭavar māṭṭaṉpu iruttal vēṇṭumātalāl, virumputal kuṟitta cila coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu talimi kuṟitta coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.

nampu naya eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum orēyaṭiyiṉiṉṟu tirintaviyē.
nāyaṉ = talivaṉ, aracaṉ, kaṭavuḷ, tanti.
nāyaṉār pōṉanāḷ iṉṟeṉṟu
akattiluḷḷōrellāruṅ kūṟi  (cīvaka. 2097, uri)
eṉṉumiṭattu, nāyaṉār eṉpatu tantiyik kuṟittatu. nāyaṉ nāyccumār eṉṉun toṭar talivaṉ taliviyarik kuṟikkum.
iṟivaṉaṭiyāriyum talivarōṭoppak karutum vaḻakka muṇmiyāl, nāyaṉ nāyaṉār eṉṉum peyarkaḷ aṭiyāri yuṅ kuṟikkum. vaḷḷuvaṉārkku nāyaṉārp paṭṭam vaḻaṅkuvatu avaratu pulamit talimi paṟṟi yākum.
nāyaṉ - nāyar = cēra (maliyāḷa) nāṭṭup paṭit talivar vaḻivanta kulattār. nāyaṉ eṉpatu taṇṭanāyaṉ eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. nāyaṭu nāyuṭu eṉpaṉa teluṅku vaṭivaṅkaḷ.
nāyaṉār - nayiṉār = camaṇarkkum civa kulattārkkum vaḻaṅki varum paṭṭap peyar. mutalāvatu itu talimi paṟṟi avviru cārāriṉ talivarkkē vaḻaṅkiyatu. tantiyik kuṟikkum nāyaṉā nayiṉā eṉpavi teluṅku vaṭivaṅkaḷ.
nāyaṉ - nāyanti - nayinti = āṇṭāṉ, aṭimi vittāḷuṅ kulattalivaṉ, cila kulattār paṭṭappeyar.
nāyanti eṉpatu viḷi vaṭivil nāyantē (nāyaṉtē) eṉṟākum.
nāyaṉ - nāyakaṉ = talivaṉ, kaṇavaṉ, aracaṉ, kaṭavuḷ.
nāyakam = talimi.
nāyakaṉ - nāyakar = cila kulattār paṭṭap peyar.
nāyakaṉ - nāykaṉ = vaṇikar talivaṉ.
nāykaṉ - nāykkaṉ = cila kulattār paṭṭap peyar.
nāyakaṉ - nāyakkaṉ = cila kulattār paṭṭap peyar.
nāykkaṉ nāyakkaṉ eṉpaṉavum paṭit talimi kuṟikkum. iv vaṭivaṅkaḷ mutalāvatu kulat talivarkku vaḻaṅkiyavi.
nāyaṉ - (nāyar) - nāyiṟu = kōḷkaḷuḷ talimiyāṉa katiravaṉ ataṟkuriya kiḻami.
nāyiṟu - ñāyiṟu. nāyiṟu eṉpatē ulaka vaḻakku.
kuṟippu : nāyaṉ eṉṉum peyar tanti peyarākavum kulap paṭṭap peyarākavum vaḻaṅkum vaḻakku vaṭamoḻiyililli. talivaṉ kaṇavaṉ eṉṟa poruḷilēyē avi aṅku vaḻaṅkuvaṉa. mēlum, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyililli. nāya nāyaka (nayaka) eṉṟa iru vaṭivaṅkaḷē aṅkuḷa.
ñāyiṟu eṉṉuṅ kiḻamip peyar toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatu.
nāyakaṉ eṉṉuñ collukku vaṭa moḻi yakarātiyiṟ kāṭṭappaṭum vēr nī eṉpatu. atu celuttutali allatu naṭattutalik kuṟikkum. accollum nū eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa nī eṉpatē. nīttal = celuttutal. nīyāṉ = kalañceluttu vōṉ.

vii. maṇattal (puṇartal)
taḻuvaliṟ ciṟantatu maṇam.
maṇattālāvatu kāmakkātalar taḻuvik kūṭutal.

kaṭiyiṟ kāvalum  (maṇi. 18, 98)

ix. iṇiyātal
āṇum peṇṇumāka iruvar allatu iraṇṭu cērntu puṇarvatālum, iyaṅkutiṇi yuyirikaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa āṇum peṇṇumākak kūṭi vāḻtalālum, uḻaviṟkum vaṇṭikkum aṭikkaṭi iru kāḷikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭuvatālum, ki kāl mutaliya cila vuṟuppukkaḷ iv viraṇṭāyiruppatā lum, āṭi mutaliya poruṭkaḷ naṉkoṭiyil potuvāka iṇiyāka vaḻaṅkappeṟuvatālum, iṇiyik kuṟittaṟkuk kalattalik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu pala coṟkaḷ piṟap pikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
uttal = poruntutal. utti = viḷiyāṭṭiṟku iruvar cērum iṇi. uttikaṭṭutal eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.

ii. kalakkam

nīru maṇṇuṅ kalappatu kalaṅkal. atu poruṭkalakkam. pala āṭkaḷ kūṭiyirukkum pōtu kuṟippiṭṭa oruvar yār eṉṟu teriyātu tiṇṭāṭuvatum. pala vaḻikaḷ kūṭumiṭattil cella vēṇṭiya vaḻi etuveṉṟu teriyātu mayaṅkuvatum. oṉṟic ceyvatu ati viṭṭuviṭuvatu eṉṉum eṇṇaṅkaḷ maṉattil tōṉṟum pōtu oṉṟit tuṇiyātu ūcalāṭuvatum, maṉakkalakkamām.

(5) kāṇal tuṟi
akak kaṇṇāṟ kāṇalum puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇalum eṉak kāṇal iruvaki. muṉṉatu karutal, piṉṉatu nukartal.
kāṇal
i. kaṇṭaṟital
kaṇṭaṟitalāvatu puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇal.
kaṇṭukēṭ ṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟi vatellām kaṇṭaṟivē yāyiṉum, aivaki nukarccip poruḷkaḷiṉ vaṭiviyum kāṇutaṟ ciṟappup paṟṟik kaṇṇiṉ toḻilē kāṭci eṉa vitantu kūṟappaṭum.
aimpulaṉkaḷum poruḷkaḷoṭu poruntuvataṉālēyē nukarcciyum aṟivum ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(uḷ) - aḷ - aṟi - aṟivu.
kuḷ - kaḷ. kaḷḷutal = poruntutal.
kaḷ - karu - kari = kaṇṭavaṉ (cāṭci).
kaḷ - kaṇ = kāṇum poṟi. kaṇṇutal = pārttal 
(nāmatīpa nikaṇṭu).
kaṇ - kāṇ - kāṭci = pārvi, aṟivu.
kāṇ - kāṇam = mēṟpārvi.
kāṇ - kāṇi. kāṇittal = mēṟpārttal.
kaṇkāṇi - kaṇkāṇam. kaṇkāṇi = mēṟpārppavaṉ.
tul - tuṉṟu - tōṉṟu. tōṉṟutal = kaṇṇoṭu poruntu tal, terital, urukkoḷal, utittal, piṟattal.
pullutal = poruntutal.
pul - pulam - pulaṉ = poruntiyaṟiyum aṟivu.
pul = aṟivu, aṟivu nūl, ilakkaṇam.
pulam - pulami. pulam - pulavaṉ.
pulaṉātal = aṟiyappaṭutal. pulappaṭutal = pulaṉoṭu poruntutal.
pulam - pular. pulartal = kaṇṇukkut terital, viṭital.
pular - pulari = viṭiyal.
pular - palar - palār, palāreṉṟu viṭintatu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
ii. karutiyaṟital
karuti yaṟitalāvatu akak kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭaṟital.
maṉam oru poruḷoṭu allatu ceytiyoṭu poruntutalē karutalām.
uttal = poruntutal. utti = poruttam poruntumuṟi, porutta mākak koḷḷum ūkam.
uṉṉutal = poruntutal, karututal, tiyāṉittal.
uṉ - uṉṉam = tiyāṉam.
uṉṉittal = poruttamāka ūkittal.
uṟutal = poruntutal, karututal.
ōrtal = poruntutal, karututal; karutiyaṟital.
kuḷ - kaḷ (karu) - karutu - karuttu. cuḷḷutal = kūṭutal, poruntutal.
kaḷ - kaṇ. kaṇṇutal = poruntutal, karututal, matittal.
kaṇ = kaṇṇiyam = matippu.
(cuḷ) - ceḷ - ce. cettal = poruntutal, ottal. karututal. cettal = karututal.
aravu nīruṇal cettu  (kalit. 45).
veppuṭiyāṭūuc cettaṉeṉ  (patiṟṟap. 86)
cettal = ottal.
veḷḷāṅ kurukiṉ piḷḷi cettu (aiṅ. 151).
ce - cettu - cittu. o. nō. centuram - cinturam.
cittu = karuttu, aṟivu, karutiyati aṭiyum tiṟam. cittu - cittaṉ.
muṉṉutal = poruntutal, karututal muṉ - muṉṉam = karuttu, kuṟippu.
muṉ - maṉ. maṉṉutal = poruntutal karututal. 
maṉ - maṉam = karutum akakkaraṇam.
maṉṉuntiṟam mantiram. tiram= tiṟam. ṟakarattiṟku muntiyatu rakaram.
maṉ + tiram = mantiram = karuttu valimi, eṇṇattiṉ tiṇṇam.
uḷḷatti allatu āciyi aṭakkiya muṉivaṉ, taṉ karuttu valimiyāl tāṉ karutiyati niṟivēṟṟu moḻiyum, tāṉ kaṇṭupiṭitta tappāta uṇmikaḷic collumoḻiyum, mantiramākum.
niṟimoḻi māntar āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa  (tol. cey. 178)
niṟimoḻi māntar maṟimoḻi karuttu valimiyoṭu kūṭiyatu eṉpati āṇiyiṟ kiḷanta eṉṉun toṭar kuṟippāy uṇarttum. karuttu valimiyoṭu kūṭāviṭattu oru moḻiyum oru payaṉum uṟātu. mantiram vāymoḻi eṉavum paṭum. vāymiyāṉa moḻi allatu vāykkum moḻi vāy moḻi. tirumūlar aruḷiya tirumantiramum nammāḻvār iyaṟṟiya 
tiruvāymoḻiyum, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ iru parattaric cavitta moḻikaḷum pōlvaṉa. mantiraṅkaḷām. mantirattik kāṭṭum moḻiyiyum mantiram eṉṟatu ōr āku peyar.
eṇṇiya eṇṇiyāṅ keytupa eṇṇiyār
tiṇṇiya rākap peṟiṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 666).
ātalāl, tiṇṇiya vuḷḷattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭatē vāymoḻi eṉa aṟika. maṉattukkaṇ mācilaṉāṉa oruvaṉ, oruvari vāḻvikkavō cāvikkavō vāy tiṟantu oṉṟic colla vēṇṭuvatilli. uḷḷattil uḷḷiṉālum pōtum. kaṭavuḷi vēṇṭum maṉṟāṭṭu eṅṅaṉam uriyiṉṟi uḷḷattilum nikaḻa muṭiyumō, aṅṅaṉamē mantiramum eṉka.
niṟimoḻi mānta rāṇiyiṟ kiḷanta eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattiṟkup pērāciriyar uritta uri varumāṟu:
itu mantirac ceyyuḷuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu itaṉ poruḷ: - niṟimoḻi māntareṉpatu, colliya colliṉ poruṇmi yāṇṭuṅ kuṟiviṉṟip payakkac collum āṟṟaluṭiyār eṉṟavāṟu. avar āṇiyamaṟ kiḷakkap paṭṭup puṟattārkkup pulaṉākāmaṉ maṟittuc colluñ coṟṟoṭarellām mantira meṉappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
avi vallārvāyk kēṭṭuṇarka. tāṉē eṉṟu pirittāṉ, ivi tamiḻ mantirameṉṟaṟkum, pāṭṭāki aṅkatameṉappaṭu vaṉavum uḷa, avi nīkkutaṟkumeṉa uṇarka. avi :
āriya taṉṟu tamiḻtī teṉavuritta
kāriyattāṟ kālakkōṭ paṭṭāṉic - cīriya
vantaṇ potiyi lakattiyaṉā rāṇiyāṟ
centamiḻē tīrkka cuvā  - eṉavum,
muraṇil potiyiṉ mutaṟputtēḷ vāḻi
paraṇa kapilarum vāḻi - yaraṇiya
lāṉanta vēṭkiyāṉ vēṭkōk kuyakkōṭa 
ṉāṉantañ cērka cuvā - eṉavum,
ivi teṟkaṇ vāyil tiṟavāta paṭṭimaṇṭapattār poruṭṭu nakkīrar oruvaṉ vāḻavum (oruvaṉ) cāvavum pāṭiya mantiram aṅkatap pāṭṭāyiṉa mēl pāṭṭuri nūl (391) eṉpuḻi aṅkatameṉṟōtiṉāṉ iṉṉa mantiratti. iḵtu oruvaṉi iṉṉavāṟṟāṟ perumpāṉmiyuñ capittaṟ poruṭṭākaliṉ ap peyarttāyiṟṟu. ik karuttē paṟṟip piṟarum,
niṟimoḻi māntar perumi nilattu
maṟimoḻi kāṭṭi viṭum  (kuṟaḷ. 2)
eṉṟāreṉka
amiccaṉ araciyaṟ karumaṅkaḷi eṇṇum allatu cūḻun tiṟaṉum mantiram eṉappaṭum. tiṟamiyākac cūḻntu tappātu vāykkum vaḻivakikaḷic collutalāl, amiccaṉ aracaṟkuk kūṟum aṟivuriyum mantiram eṉappaṭṭatu. mantiram kūṟupavaṉ mantiri.
mantirik kaḻaku varumporu ḷurittal
eṉṟār ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyarum.
iii. karutum māntaṉ
māntaṉi maṟṟa vuyirikaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu maṉṉuntiṟaṉākiya pakuttaṟivē. iyaṅkum uyirikaṭkellām karutut tiṟamiruppiṉum, atu māntaṉiṭattiṟpōl maṟṟa vuyirikaḷiṭattil vaḷarcciyaṭiyavilli.
makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar (1522).
poruḷkaḷi uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉa ilakkaṇa nūlār iru pakuppākap pakuttatum, aṅṅaṉam pakuttaṟ kēṟpa muṉṉarē potumakkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ viṉikaḷik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷi vevvēṟu vikuti koṭuttuk kūṟi vantatum, avaṟṟiṉ pakuttaṟi vuṇmiyiṉmi paṟṟiyē maṉṉuntiṟam ciṟantiruttal paṟṟiyē māntaṉ maṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
āṅkilac collārāycciyāḷarum man eṉṉum peyarukku thinking animal eṉṟē poruṭ kāraṇaṉ kāṭṭuvar (E. man, from A.S. munan, to think).
maṉ = maṉṉum (karutum) uyiriyākiya māntaṉ.
maṉpati = makkaṭ kūṭṭam.
maṉṉuyir = makkaṭ kulam.
niṉṉaḷan taṟital maṉṉuyirk karumiyiṉ  (tirumuru. 276)
eṉṟār nakkīrar.
tammiltam makkaḷ aṟivuṭimi mānilattu 
maṉṉuyirk kellām iṉitu  (kuṟaḷ, 68)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
maṉṉuyir eṉpataṟku maṉṉāniṉṟa vuyir eṉṟu parimēlaḻakar urikkum uri poruntātu. maṉṉāniṟṟal = nilipeṟutal.
maṉ - māṉ - māṉavaṉ - māntaṉ.
maṉ - manu (va.).


(6) poruttal tuṟi
pal uṟuppukkaḷiyum pakutikaḷiyum poruttuvatu poruttu.
i. uṟuppup poruttu
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṇ - kaṇu. kaṇ - kaṇi. kaṇukkāl = kaṇikkāl. kaḷ - kaḷam = uṭampiyum taliyiyum poruttum kaḻuttu.
kaḷam - (kaḷattu) - kaḻuttu.
kaḷ - kaṭṭu
kuḷ - koḷ - koḷi = pāṭṭu.
pul - pol - poru - porun - poruntu - poruttu.
puḷ - (pūḷ) - pūṇ - pūṭṭu. puḷ - (puṇ) - puṇar - puṇarppu.
puḷ - poḷ - poṭṭu = neṟṟiyelumpup poruttu.
muḷ - muḷi = muṭṭu, kaṇu.
muḷi - muḻi - moḻi = kimuṭṭu, karumpukkaṇu.
muḻi + ki = muḻaṅki. muḻi + kāl = muḻaṅkāl.
muḻi - muḻam = muḻaṅkiyaḷavu.
muḷ - muṭṭu = ki kāṟ poruttu.
muṭṭu - muṭṭi = muḻaṅkāṟ poruttu, kikkaṇu, maṭakkiya muṭṭik kiyāṟ ceyyum maṟpōr.
muṭṭu - muṭu - maṭu - maṭi = poruttu, koḷuttu.
muṭṭu - mūṭṭu = poruttu.
muṇṭu = marakkaṇu, uṭaṟcantu. muṇṭu - muṇṭam = kaṇukkāṟ poruttu.
kaṭṭutal
(uḷ) - iḷ - iḻi - iṇi. (iḷ) - iya - iyi - ici, icittal = kaṭṭutal.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu. kaṭṭu - kaṭṭil.
kaṭṭu - kaṭṭaṇam = pāṭi.
puḷ - (puṇ) - puṇi - piṇi.
puṇ - puṇar. puṇarttal = kaṭṭutal.
puṇ - puṇi - piṇi. piṇittal = kaṭṭutal.
puḷ - poḷ - poṭṭu - poṭṭaṇam = kaṭṭu.
poṭṭaṇam - poṭṭalam.
muḷ - muṭṭu - mūṭṭu - muṭṭi.
ii. muṭital
ul - al. allutal = kūṇṭu muṭital.
kuḷ - (kuy) - kuyil. kuyilutal = neytal, piṉṉutal.
nuḷ - neḷ - ney - neyavu - necavu.
pul - pol - poru - poruntu - poruttu.
pul - (puṉ) - piṉ.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭi. muṭital = piṉṉutal.
iii. tittal
uttu - attu. attutal = tittal.
oṭṭutal = tittal.
kuttutal = tittal.
tuṉṉutal = tittal. tuṉ - tuṉṉam = tiyal. tuṉṉakāraṉ - tiyaṟkāraṉ.
tuḷ - (taḷ) - (tay) - ti. tittal = tuṇi palaki mutaliyavaṟṟi icittal.
ti - (ticcu) - taccu - taccaṉ.
vaṇṭik kuṭattil ārikaḷip poruttalit tittal eṉpar.
poḷ - poṭṭu - pottu.
pottutal = mūṭṭutal. pollam pottutal eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
muḷ - mūḷ - mūṭṭu - mūṭṭi - mūṭi = mūṭṭappaṭṭa kōṇippi, mūṭṭutal = tittal.


(7) mūṭal tuṟi
mūṭalāvatu. koḷkalaṅkaḷiṉ vāyiyum pōrppavaṟṟiṉ ōraṅkaḷiyum porutti maṟittal.
i. mūṭutal
um - umi - umital = vāy mūṭi eccil umiḻtal. umi - umiḻ.
cuḷ - (cūr) - cārttu - cāttu. cāttutal = mūṭutal.
tum - tumi. tumital = umital. tūrttal = kuḻiyiyum tuḷiyiyum mūṭutal.
potu - pottu. pottutal = mūṭutal, maṟittal. potu - potukku. potukkutal = maṟittal.
muḷa - mūḻ. mūḻttal = mūṭutal. mūḻ - mūḻal = mūṭi.
mūḻ - mūṭu - mūṭi. mūṭu - mūṭam - mōṭam = vāṉam mūṭiya mantāram, mantāram pōṉṟa maṭami.
mūṭu = aṟivili. mūṭam - mūṭaṉ.
mūḻ - mūy. mūytal = mūṭutal, vāymūṭi eccil umiḻtal.
mūy - moy. moyttal = mūṭutal.
ii. mūṭiya poruṭkaḷ
um - umi = nelli mūṭiyuḷḷa toli.
um - ūm = vāypēcāta ūmi.
ūm - ūmi. ūm - ūmaṉ.
vāy pēcātavaṉi vāyi um eṉṟu vittuk koṇṭiruk kiṟāṉ eṉpar.
kum - kommi = kampu tiṉi kēḻvaraku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ umi.
cum - coṅku = cōḷa umi.
mūḻ - mūku - mūkaṉ = ūmiyaṉ. mūku - mūṅku = ūmi.
mūṅku - mūṅki = ūmi.
mūku - mūkā. mūkāttal = ūmiyāyiruttal.
iii. pōrttal
uṟu - uṟi.
kuḷ - kuṭi = caṭṭi.
kumpu - kuppu - kuppā = tūrippi.
kuppu - kuppāyam = meyppi, caṭṭi. kuppāyam - kuppācam.
cul - (cōl) - cāl - cālvi = pōrvi.
poru - pōr - pōrvi.
poru - potu - poti. potital = pōrttal.
poti - potir. potirtal = pōrttal.


(8) paṟṟal tuṟi
paṟṟalāvatu, ki kāl mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷip piṟa poruḷ kaḷōṭu poruntip piṭittal. maṉattil oṉṟik koḷḷutalum paṟṟutalōṭokkum.
i. piṭittal
viralkaḷip porutti oru poruḷip paṟṟutalē piṭittal. piṟavuṟuppukkaḷāl oṉṟip paṟṟutalum oppumipaṟṟip piṭittal eṉappaṭum.
kuḷ - koḷ. koḷḷutal = paṟṟutal. koḷ - kōḷ. ēṟukōḷ tīkkōḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkuka.
koḷkompu - koḻukompu = paṟṟukkōṭu.
kutu - katu - katuvu. katuvutal = paṟṟutal. katu - kātu = oliyip paṟṟum cevi.
pul - (puṟṟu) - paṟṟu.
puḷ - (piḷ) - piṇṭi - piṇṭam. piṇṭam = tiraṭci. piṇṭittal kikkuḷ cōṟṟi allatu māvit tiraṭṭutal, cērttal, piṭittal. piṇṭi - piṭi. o. nō. kaṇṭi - kaṭi. taṇṭi - taṭi.
piḷḷiyār piṭikkak kuraṅkāy muṭintatu eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyiyum, poriviḷaṅkāy piṭittal. koḻukkaṭṭi piṭittal, mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷiyum nōkkuka.
piṭittal = kikkuḷ tiraṭṭutal, kikkuḷ māttiraḷiyit tiraṭṭutal pōl oṉṟip paṟṟutal, kiyiṟ piṭittuk koṇṭatu pōl aḷavik kuṟittal.
piṭi = piṭikkappaṭum kāmpu allatu aṭip pakuti, kikkuḷ piṭikkum aḷavu.
caṭṭi mutukiṟ piṭikkiṟatu, cōṟu pāṉiyaṭiyiṟ piṭittuk koṇṭatu. vīṭṭil tīppiṭittu viṭṭatu. campaḷattil ōr urupāvip piṭittu viṭṭārkaḷ. eṉakkup puḻuṅkalaricitāṉ piṭikkum, eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vaḻakkukkaḷellām uṇmiyākavum aṇi vakiyilum paṟṟaṟ poruḷiyē kuṟittal kāṇka.
pol - (poṟṟu) - poṟṟi - poṟi = piṭikkuṅ karuvi, cūḻcciyam, iyantiram. elippoṟi pulippoṟi mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkuka. aimpulavuṟuppuk kaḷum poruḷkaḷiṉ taṉmiyip piṭittaliṉ, poṟiyeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
ii. koḷḷutal
oruvar oṉṟip peṟṟapiṉ atik kiyiṟ koḷḷutalāl, koḷḷutal eṉṉuñ col, viliyiṉṟip peṟutaliyum vilikku vāṅkutaliyuṅ kuṟikkum. viliyiṉṟip peṟutal, vantatip peṟutalum valintu peṟutalum eṉa iru vakittu.
koḷḷutal = peṟutal, vāṅkutal, vilikku vāṅkutal.
koḷ - koḷḷi = koḷḷum vili.
koṇṭāṉ = peṟṟavaṉ. vilikku vāṅkiṉavaṉ, oruttiyi maṉiviyākak koṇṭavaṉ.
koḷ - koṇku - koṇkaṉ = kaṇavaṉ.
koḷ - koḷvu - koḷvaṉi.
koḷ - koḷnaṉ - koḻunaṉ = kaṇavaṉ.
koḻunaṉ - koḻuntaṉ = koḻunaṉoṭu kūṭap piṟantavaṉ. koḻunti (pe. pā).
koḷ - koḷḷi = peruvāriyāka valintu koḷvatu (cūṟi), peruvāriyāka uyirkaḷik koḷḷum nōy.
koḷ - koṇṭi = koḷḷi.
koḷ - kōḷ = koḷḷi. ākōḷ nirikōḷ mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷik kāṇka.
koḷ - kōḷ - kōṭal. cūṟikōṭal = koḷḷiyaṭittal.
koḷḷutal = maṉattiṟkoḷḷutal. karututal, namputal.
koḷ - kōḷ = karuttu. koḷ - koḷki = karuttu, nampikki, kōṭpāṭu = kuṟikkōḷ, neṟimuṟi.
iii. koḷuttutal
koḷuttutalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟik koḷḷa vittal; atāvatu paṟṟavittal.
kuḷ - koḷ - koḷuvu - koḷuvi. koḷ - koṇṭi = koḷuvi.
koḷ - koḷu. koḷuttutal = paṟṟavittal.
aṟivu koḷuttutal tīk koḷuttutal eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷik kāṇka.
koḷ - kōḷ = koḷuttuki, kuṟaḷi.
tīkkōḷ kōḷ mūṭṭutal mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷik kāṇka.
koḷ - koṭku - koṭki - kokki = koḷuvi.
koḷḷi - neruppup paṟṟiya kaṭṭi. kalakamūṭṭupavaṉ. pol - poru - poruntu - poruttu.
viḷakkuk koḷuttutal viḷakkup poruttutal eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷik kāṇka.
muḷ - mūḷ - mūṭṭu. tīppaṟṟavittal tī mūṭṭutal eṉpaṉa vaḻakku. mūṭṭutal = poruttutal.
mūṭṭu - mūṭṭam.
mūṭṭu - māṭṭu. māṭṭutal = koḷuvutal. tīppaṟṟa vittal. māṭṭu = māṭṭi. talippāki māṭṭi caṭṭi māṭṭi eṉpaṉa māṭṭuṅ karuvikaḷ.
māṭṭu = māṭṭal = talimuṭiyil māṭṭum aṇi.
māṭṭu = ceyyuḷil ōriṭattiluḷḷa toṭari maṟṟōriṭat tiluḷḷa toṭaroṭu poruttip poruḷ kōṭal.
iv. kollutal
arimā puli mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ ōr uyirik kollaṟkē paṟṟutalāṉum, kollum vilaṅkukaḷum makkaḷum ōr uyiriyip paṟṟutaluṅ kollutalōṭokkumātalāṉum, paṟṟutaṟ karuttil kollutaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. āṅkilattilum paṟṟutalik kuṟikkum seize eṉṉum col. kollutalik kuṟittal kāṇka.
koḷ - kōḷ = koli. koḷḷutal = paṟṟutal, kollutal.
kōḷari = kolluñciṅkam.
pulikōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēy kōṭpaṭṭāṉ mutaliya toṭarkaḷ, puliyiṉālum pēyiṉālum kollappaṭṭatik kuṟittal kāṇka.
koḷ - kol - koli.
kol - kollaṉ = marattik kolpavaṉ, taccaṉ, kammiyaṉ, iruppukkollaṉ.
maraṅkol taccaṉ  (cilap. 5:29)
aivakik kol toḻilkaḷuḷ mutalāvatu eḻuntatu taccu.
(9) oṉṟal tuṟi
iru allatu pala poruḷkaḷ oṉṟākap poruntutal oṉṟal.
i. oṭṭic cellutal
ūrtal = nilattiyoṭṭic cellutal, uṭampiyoṭṭi nakartal.
uṭu - uṭumpu = nilattiyum cuvariyum oṭṭip paṟṟum uyiri.
ūṇāṉ = nilatti oṭṭip paṭarum koṭi.
ōṇāṉ = nilattiyum marattiyum oṭṭic cellum ūruyiri. ōṇāṉ - ontāṉ - onti - ōti.

naṭṭuvattiṟku mattaḷam ciṟanta tuṇiyāvatu. ātalāl naṭṭuvaṉōṭu eṉṟum mattaḷakkāraṉ kūṭiyēyiruppāṉ. ataṉāl, naṭṭumuṭṭu, naṭṭuvaṉum muṭṭuvaṉum, eṉṉum iṇimoḻikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. naṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum colli nōkki muṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum collum muṭṭu eṉṉum colli nōkki naṭṭu eṉṉum collum etukipaṟṟiyamintaṉa.

āriyar nāvalantēyattiṟku varumuṉṉarē, tamiḻar ici nāṭakak kalikaḷil ciṟantiruntatiṉāl, avar avaṟṟi iyalōṭu cērttut tamiḻi muttamiḻāka vaḻaṅki vantaṉar. talikkaḻakat tamiḻ muttamiḻākavē iruntatu.
ellā nūṟkum potuvāṉa col vaḻakki ulaka vaḻakku ceyyuḷ vaḻakku eṉṟum, poruḷ vaḻakki ulakiyal vaḻakku nāṭaka vaḻakku eṉṟum, ivviruvakiyāka vakuttiruntaṉar. ulakiyal vaḻakku uṇmiyāṉatu; nāṭaka vaḻakku puṉinturi yāṉatu. ivviraṇṭum kalanta akapporuṭ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkip pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku eṉṉum peyarāṟ kuṟittu ataṉpaṭiyē toṉṟutoṭṭup pāṭi vantaṉar muṉṉit tamiḻar.
nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam
kaliyē paripāṭ ṭāyiru pāṅkiṉum
uriya tākum eṉmaṉār pulavar
eṉṟu cārpu nūlārāṉa tolkāppiyar kūṟutal kāṇka. (akattiṇiyiyal, 53).
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa naṭippup paṟṟiya coṟkaṭkellām vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamākak kuṟikkappaṭuvatu nrutta eṉṉum vaṭivoṭu naṭṭa (va.) eṉṉum vaṭiviṉ ovvāmiyi eṉpatākum.
viii. aḷavu
ōr aḷaviṉāl orumuṟi aḷakkappaṭṭa poruḷ aḷanta karuviyōṭotta aḷaviṉatāyiruppatāl, oppumik karuttil aḷavuk karuttup piṟantatu. eṇṇal eṭuttal mukattal nīṭṭal eṉṉum nālvakiyaḷavuḷ muṉṉatoḻinta ēṉi mūṉṟilum, iṉṉatutāṉ aḷavu karuvi eṉṉum yāppuṟavilli. makkaḷel lārkkum potuvāṉa aḷavu karuvikaḷ eṅkaṇum peru vaḻak kāka vaḻaṅkivariṉum, eṉṟum evarum tam vacatikkēṟpa etiyum aḷavu karuviyāka vittuk koḷḷalām. parumaṉilō neṭumiyilō, oru poruḷukku otta aḷavu iṉṉoru poruḷiṟ koḷḷuvatē aḷattal eṉka. vacatipaṟṟic cila aḷavu karuvikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruppiṉum, uṇmiyil aḷakkum poruḷukkum aḷakkappaṭum poruḷukkum vēṟupāṭilli. aḷakkappaṭum poruḷik koṇṭē aḷakkavuñ ceyyalām. tuṇiyāl tuṇiyi aḷappatiyum kāyāl kāyi niṟuppatiyuṅ kāṇka.

iv.  māṟupaṭutal
uṟu - uṟaḻ - uṟaḻcci. uṟaḻtal = karuttu māṟu paṭ ṭuriyāṭal, coṟkaḷ tōṉṟalum tiritalumākap puṇartal.

muṉ - muṉi. muṉital = kōpittal, veṟuttal.

ii. tiraṇṭa aṭi
marañceṭikoṭikaḷiṉ aṭi, avaṟṟiṉ maṟṟap pakutikaḷi viṭap paruttiruppatāl, parumik karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa pala coṟkaḷ aṭiyik kuṟikkat tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
uḷ - aḷ - aṇṭu. aṇṭutal = neruṅkutal, muṭṭutal, poruntutal (kūṭutal), tiraḷutal, paruttal.
aṇṭu - aṇṭi - aṭi = paruttatu, parutta aṭi ō.nō. taṇṭu - taṇṭi - taṭi.
aṭi = marattiṉ aṭippakuti, mūlam, āti, paḻimi marattaṭi pōṉṟa pātam, kāl, ceyyuḷaṭi, aṭiyaḷavu (foot) oṉṟaṉ aṭippākam.
aṇṭu - aṇṭi = uṭampiṉ aṭittuḷi (anus), aṇṭi taḷḷutal = aṭittuḷip pakuti veḷi varutal (Prolapsus ani).
aṇṭi taḷḷutali aṇṭu taḷḷutal eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar. 
aṇṭimā = paḻattiṉ aṭiyiṟ koṭṭiyuḷḷa mara muntiri.
aṇṭimāmpaḻam, aṇṭimāṅkoṭṭi aṇṭik koṭṭi eṉpaṉa teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
aṇṭutal eṉṉuñ coṟku mēṟkuṟitta poruḷkaḷuḷḷami kīḻvarun toṭarkaḷālum mēṟkōḷālum aṟiyappaṭum.
(1) kiṭṭutal, neruṅkutal. (itu veḷippaṭi).
(2) muṭṭutal. aṇṭi koṭuttal = muṭṭuk koṭuttal.
aṇṭu - aṇṭi - aṭi.
paṭṭaṭi = taṭṭum aṇikal, aṭikal.
(3) poruntutal, ottal, takutal, ēṟṟal.

aṇṭip piḻittal = oruvaṉip paṟṟukkōṭākak koḷḷutal.

ā oru toḻiṟ peyar vikuti.
e.ṭu. uṇ - uṇā = uṇavu.

kuḻu - kaḻu - kaḻi = karumputtaṇṭu. karuppaṅkaḻi eṉpatu vaḻakku.

taḷ - taṭṭu = cōḷam karumpu mutaliya payirkaḷiṉ aṭi.
taṭṭu - taṭṭi.

iv. pūppaṭital
nilittiṇiyil. tiraṇṭa allatu parutta mutalum ciṉiyum mutircciyaṭiyum. ataṉāl, tiraṭcipaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ cila pūppaṭitali uṇarttum.

mūppu cākkāṭṭiṟ kētuvātalāl, iḷami aḻiyāmiyik kuṟittatu.

pūppaṭintavaḷit tiraṇṭavaḷ eṉpatu koṅku nāṭṭu vaḻakku. o. nō.  E. virgin, pom L. virgo, to swell.
kumar - kumari = kaṉṉi kaṉṉiyāṉa kāḷi (turkki). kaṉṉimi, aḻiviṉmi.
kumar - kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ. iḷiñaṉ, murukaṉ. cēyōṉip paṇṭittamiḻar iḷiñaṉākavē karutiyiruntatāl, avaṉ kumaraṉ eṉṟum murukaṉ eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭāṉ. (muruku = iḷimi. murukaṉ = iḷiñaṉ).
kumaraṉ kumari eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷik kumāraṉ kumāri eṉa nīṭṭi makaṉiyum makaḷiyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiyatu piṟkālattu āriya vaḻakku. tamiḻil iḷiñaṉ iḷiñi eṉṟē avi poruḷ tarum.
kumaraṉ kumari eṉṉum teyvaṅkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻarāl vaṇaṅkappaṭṭu varupavi.

vii. perumi (ciṟappu)
matippiṟkum pukaḻiṟkum ētuvāṉa aṟivāṟṟalatikāra celvaṅ kaḷiṉ parumiyē perumi.
uravōṉ = periyōṉ.
kuru - perumi, periyōṉ. kuru - kuravu - kuravaṉ = periyōṉ. aracaṉ āciriyaṉ tāy tanti aṇṇaṉ ākiya aivarum aiṅkuravar eṉṟum, tāy tantiyar iru mutukuravar eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭutalāl; kuru eṉṉum collukkup periyōṉ eṉpatē mūlapporuḷākum. kuravar eṉṉuñcol peṟṟōri vitantu kuṟittal pōla, kuru eṉṉum col āciriyaṉi vitantu kuṟikkiṉṟa teṉka.
kuru - kuri - perumi.
kul - kal - kaṉ - kaṉa - kaṉam = perumi.
cuḷ - (cūr) - cīr - ciṟa - ciṟappu.
pul - pal - paru - peru - perumi.
muḷ - maḷ - maṇ - māṇ - māṇpu. maṇ = māṭcimi. 
māṇ - māṭci. muku - makam = perumi.
makam - makattu - makantu - māntu - māntaṉ = paṭippiṟ periyavaṉ.
muṉ - maṉ - māṉ - māṉam = perumi.
kuṟippu : makaṉ eṉṉum peyar makam eṉpataṉiṉṟu tirinta tākavuṅ koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu. āyiṉum, piḷḷi eṉṉum iḷamip peyar āṇpiḷḷi peṇpiḷḷi eṉap periyōrkkum vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, makaṉ makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ piḷḷiyarkkum periyōrkkum potuvāyirutta lāṉum, iḷamipaṟṟiya makaṉ eṉṉum peyarē periyōṉi yuṅ kuṟittatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
aiyaṉ : nerukkam ceṟivu toṭutal poruntal oṉṟal tiraṭci parumi perumi eṉpaṉa, muṟiyē oṉṟiṉiṉṟoṉṟeḻunta toṭarccik karuttukkaḷ.
uḷ - aḷ - aṇa. aṇṇutal = neruṅkutal.
aḷḷal = nerukkam.
aḷ = ceṟivu. aḷḷutal = ceṟital.
aḷ - aḷa - aḷavu - aḷāvu. aḷavutal = toṭutal, poruntal, kalattal.
aḷ = paṟṟirumpu.

aḷḷutal = cērttal, poruttal, pūṭṭutal.
aḷḷukkaṭṭutal = irumput takaṭṭāl iṟukkutal. 
aḷ = pūṭṭu, vaṇṭivillit tāṅkuṅ kaṭṭi.
aḷ = vaṉmi. vaṉmik kuṇam tiraṭci parumi perumi tiṇmi mutaliyavaṟṟāl ēṟpaṭuvatu.
aḷ - (ay) - ai = perumi. periyōṉ. talivaṉ tanti, aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, kaṇavaṉ.
ai - aiyaṉ (ai + aṉ) = periyōṉ, mūttōṉ. uyarntōṉ, talivaṉ, tanti, aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, muṉivaṉ, (antaṇaṉ), pārppāṉ (kōyiṟkāriyam pārppavaṉ). tēvaṉ, cāttaṉ.
aiyaṉ - aiyi (peṇpāl) = periyōḷ, talivi. āciriyi, āciriyaṉ maṉivi, tuṟaviṉi (tavappeṇ). kāḷi, (turkki), malimakaḷ (pārvati).
ai allatu aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar, mutalāvatu mūppu kāppu aṟivu tavam mutaliyaṉapaṟṟi, periyōṉ allatu talivaṉ eṉṟa poruḷiyē kuṟittatu. iṉṟum periyōriyellām aiyā eṉṟē aḻittal kāṇka.
eṉṉi muṉ nillaṉmiṉ  (eṉṟa kuṟaḷil. 771)
ai talivaṉik kuṟittatu.
aiṅkuravar eṉṉum aivakippaṭṭa periyōruḷ, mutalvaṉ tanti. piṟappi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa āriyakkula muṟippaṭi, tamiḻaruḷ ēṟṟiḻivupeṟṟa oru cār mēlōrum orucār kīḻōrum, tantiyi muṟiyē aiyā eṉṟum aiyaṉ eṉṟum, toṉṟutoṭṭu (āriyar teṉṉāṭu varukikku muṉpiruntum, tamiḻ kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟiyatiliruntum), aḻittu varukiṉṟaṉar. aiyā eṉpatu aiyaṉ eṉpataṉ viḷi vaṭivam. aṉṉīṟṟup peyar ulaka vaḻakkil uyarvu kuṟiyāmi paṟṟi, aiyā eṉṉum viḷivaṭivamē aiyaṉ eṉṉum eḻuvāy vaṭivattiṟkup patilākavum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
tantiyi nikarttavaḷ tāy. aiyi eṉṉum peṇ pāṟpeyar. potuvākap periyōḷik kuṟikkum pōtu tiriyātum, tāyik kuṟikkumpōtu āy eṉṟu tirittum, varum. āy - āycci (iraṭṭip peṇpāl), āyaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ peyar iṭiyaṉiyē kuṟittaliyum. āycci eṉṉum peṇpāṟ peyar iṭicciyik kuṟittalōṭu tāy pāṭṭi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkutali yum nōkkuka.

aṉṉiyun tantiyum muṉṉaṟi teyvamātalālum, tirumaṇa mākumvari makkaḷ peṟṟōrōṭēyē uṟitalālum, ētēṉumoru tuṉpaṅkaṇṭu araṟṟum pōtum or iṟumpūtu kaṇṭu viyakkumpōtum; ciṟārum iḷiñarum peṟṟōri viḷittal iyalpu. itaṉāl, peṟṟōrik kuṟikkum coṟ kaḷiṉiṉṟu, irakkak kuṟippiṭic coṟkaḷum viyappuk kuṟippiṭic coṟkaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
peṟṟōr	irakkakkuṟippiṭic                        viyappukkuṟipiṭic
   peyar 	col 		col
appaṉ 	appa, appā,  		appa, appā,
		appappa, appappā  	appappa,
					appappā	
accaṉ 	accō 		accō
ammi 	ammā, ammavō 	amma, ammā
		ammakō, 		ammamma
aṉṉi 	aṉṉō 		aṉṉō
iṅṅaṉamē, ai aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷiṉiṉṟum, aiya, aiyavō - aiyakō, aiyē, aiyō, aiyiyō mutaliya irakkak kuṟippiṭic coṟkaḷum, ai, aiya, aiyō mutaliya viyappuk kuṟippiṭic coṟkaḷum; piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
cuṭṭolik kālattiṟku muntiya kuṟippolik kālattil tōṉṟiya viyappuṇarvolikaḷuḷ oṉṟu āy eṉa neṭiṉ mutalākavē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. atu piṉṉar āy - ay (ai) eṉak kuṟukiyirukkalām. avvuṇar volik kuṟukkam vēṟu; aḷ eṉṉum aṭip piṟantu tantiyik kuṟikkum colliṉiṉṟu tirintu iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum ai (ay) eṉṉum viyappiṭiccol vēṟu.
ai viyappākum  (tol. 868)
tantikku aṭuttavaṉ tamiyaṉ. tam eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟa aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarē, tamiyaṉ eṉpatu.
muṉṉiṉṟu moyyavintā reṉṉiyar  (pu. ve. 8, 23)
eṉpatil, aiyar eṉpatu tamiyaṉmārik kuṟittatu.
uṟavumuṟiyallāta periyōruḷ, tantikku neruṅkiyavaṉ āciriyaṉ. āciriyar illaṟattārum tuṟavaṟattārumāka iru cārār. ekkulattārāyiṉum illaṟam naṭattum āciriyariyum, aiyar eṉṟaḻippatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku, muṉivarāṉa āciriyar eṅkum aiyar eṉṉum peyarkkuriyar.
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa   (tol.  1091)
eṉpatil, aiyar eṉpatu muṉivarik kuṟittatu. piṅkalam muṉivar tokutiyi aiyar tokuti eṉa vakuttukkūṟum. muṉivar palavakiyilum mikap periyār eṉpatu, periyārit tuṇik kōṭal periyārip piḻiyāmi eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷati kāraṅkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻnāṭu pukunta pirāmaṇar iruvaki nilippaṭṭu āciriyat toḻiliyē mutalāvatu mēṟkoṇṭamiyāl, avar aiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. pūcāriya rāṉa purōkitarum orucār āciriya vakuppiṉarē. itu paṟṟiyē, tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kiṟittavak kurumārāṉa pātirimārum aiyar eṉa aḻikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉar. nāṭṭup pātirimāri nāṭṭiyar eṉpar. kuru eṉṉum peyar kaliyāciriyaṉiyum matavāciri yaṉiyum potuppaṭak kuṟippatu. avviruvakiyāriṉ toḻi loppumiyi uṇarttum.
avar eṉṉum teṉcol teluṅkil vāru eṉat tirivatāl, avarkaḷ eṉṉum uyarvīṟṟiṟ kotta vāru eṉṉuñ colloṭu kūṭi aiyavāru eṉa niṟkum teluṅkup peyar, aiyavāru eṉat tirintu māl neṟiyār (viṇavar) āṉa teluṅkup pirāmaṇarik kuṟikkum aiyakāru eṉpatu, āṅkilattil aiyaṅkār eṉṉum vaṭivaṅ koḷḷum.
oru nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa āṭcit talivaṉ aracaṉātaliṉ, avaṉum aiyaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ.
oru peṇṇukku vāḻkkit talivaṉ avaḷ kaṇavaṉātaliṉ, avaṉ avaṭku aiyaṉāvaṉ.
eṉṉikku mutavātu
eṉpatil, ai eṉpatu kaṇavaṉik kuṟittatu.
iṉi, ellā vulakaṅkaṭkum oru peruntalivaṉāṉa iṟivaṉukku. aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarurimi collāmalē viḷaṅkum. aiyi eṉṉum peyar kāḷiyiyum, mali makaḷiyuṅ kuṟittalāl, aiyaṉ eṉpatu civaṉik kuṟittal tēṟṟam. muḻumutaṟṟeyvam perun teyvam ciṟuteyvam eṉat teyvam muttiṟappaṭutalāl, piṟkālattil civaṉ makaṉākak kūṟappeṟṟa vaṇikat teyvamāṉa cāttaṉ. aiyaṉ eṉṟum aiyaṉār eṉṟum āṇpāl vaṭivilum uyarvup paṉmi vaṭivilum kuṟikkap peṟuvaṉ.
iṅṅaṉam, aiyaṉ eṉṉum col, palvēṟu poruḷ kuṟittut toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻ nāṭṭil tirintum tiriyātum vaḻaṅki yiruppavum; aṇuvaḷavum āriyat toṭarpaṟṟa paṟiyaruḷḷiṭṭa cila tamiḻp paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ aiyaṉ eṉṉum colliyē tanti peyarākak koṇṭiruppavum; vaḻakkiṟkum varalāṟṟiṟkum moḻi nūṟkum muṟṟum māṟāka, āriyaṉ eṉṉum varuṇap peyariṉ citivē aiyaṉ eṉum tamiḻc col eṉa, ōriru taṉip paṭṭavar nūliṟ kūṟiyiruppatuṭaṉ ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyiluṅ kuṟittiruppatu. mikamika varuttattakka toṉṟām.
perumik karuttu tiraṭcik karuttiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa parumik karuttiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyiruppatāl, nerukkamum ceṟivum puṇarppum vaṉmiyum kuṟikkum aḷ eṉṉum aṭiccol liṉiṉṟu, muṟkūṟiya muṟippaṭi, ai eṉṉum collum ataṉvaḻi aiyaṉ aiyi mutaliya coṟkaḷum tirintiruppatu, iyaṟkikkum ēraṇattiṟkum ēṟṟatē.
aiyaṉ eṉṉuñ coṟku mūlam ai yātalāl, atu ārya eṉṉuñ colloṭu poruntāmiyiyum kaṇṭu koḷka.
viii. tiṇmi
pala aṇukkaḷ allatu poruḷkaḷ mika neruṅkic cērvatāl tiṇmi uṇṭākum.
uṟu - uṟa - uṟappu. uṟa - uṟi. uṟital = kaṭṭiyātal.
uṟu - iṟu - iṟuku - iṟukku - iṟukkam.
iṟukkutal = iṟukumāṟu amukkik kāṭṭutal.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu - kaṭṭi = iṟukiyatu. kaṭṭi - keṭṭi.
kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḻ - kāy. kāḻppu = viram. kāyttal = uḻippāl ki iṟukutal, piñcu mutirntu iṟukutal, kāy kāyttal.
cuḷ - ceḷ - ceṟi - ceṟivu.
tuḷ - tiḷ - tiṇ - tiṭpu - tiṭpam.
tiṇ - tiṇuku - tiṇuṅku - tiṇukkam. tiṇ - tiṇi.
nuṟu - neṟi. neṟittal = viṟappākutal.
muḷ - muṟu - muṟuku - muṟukal = cūṭṭiṉāl iṟukiyatu.
muṟu - muṟa - muṟamuṟappu = viṟappu.
muṟu - viṟa - viṟa - viṟappu.
viṟu - veṟu - veṟi. veṟittal = ceṟital.
viṟa - viṟi. viṟittal = kuḷirāl iṟukutal.
muḷ - (moḷ) - moy. moyttal = iṟukutal.
ix. tiṇṇam
tiṇṇam eṉpatu taṭṭiyāṉa poruḷiṉ parumaṉ. uram = tiṇṇam.
ōli = tiṇṇiya ili. melliya takaṭṭi ōliyāyiruk kiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkamiruppiṉum, iliyi viṭa ōli tiṇṇa māyiruttalik kavaṉikka.
ōṭu = tiṇṇamāṉa kāyttōl.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu - kaṭṭi - keṭṭi. keṭṭik kāppu = tiṇṇamāṉa kāppu.
tuḷ - teraḷ - tōḷ - tōṭu = tiṇṇamāṉa paḻattōl, ōli.
tuḷ - (tiṉ) - tiṇ - tiṇṇam.
tul = til - tir - tiram - tiṟam - tiṟaṉ.
muḷ - muraḷ - muraṇ = tiṇṇamāṉa cippi.
x. kaṉam
tiṇmiyāṉatum tiṇṇamāṉatum kaṉakkum.
kuru = kaṉam. kuru - kurūu.
pacumaṭ kurūuttiraḷ  (puṟam. 32).
kul - kal - kaṉ - kaṉa - kaṉam.
(puḷ) - (paḷ) - paḷu - paḷuvu.
xi. valimi
kaṉamuḷḷatu valiyatu
uram - uraṉ. uram - uravu - uravōṉ = valiyōṉ.
uṟu - eṟuḻ = vali.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu - kaṭṭi - keṭṭi.
tul - til - tir - tiram - tiṟam - tiṟaṉ tiṟal.
tiṟam - tiṟami.
poru - pōr = valimi.
mul - muṉ - muṉpu = valimi.
mul - mal = valimi.
mal = val - vali - valimi.
val - valu - valuvu.
val - vaṉ - vaṉpu - vaṟpu.
val - valam = valimi, veṟṟi.
valakki = payiṟciyiṉāl valimi peṟṟa ki.
valam = valakkip pakkam. valam - valavaṉ = valap pakkattuk kāḷi, ūrtiyi valamākap poṟitirittōṭṭupavaṉ. valam varutal = nakari valamākac cuṟṟivarutal.
muḷ - moḷ - moy = valimi. moy - moympu = valimi.
muḷ (maḷ) - vaḷ = valimi.
xii. kaṭiṉam
valiyatu kaṭiṉamāṉatu.
kul - kal = kaṭiṉamāṉatu. kal - kaṉṟu. kaṉṟutal = kāy kiḻaṅku mutaliyaṉa kaṭiṉamātal. kaṉṟu - kaṇṭu.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭu - kaṭumi.
kaṭu - kaṭiṉam. kaṭu - kaṭṭam.
vaḷ - (vay) - vayir. vayirttal - vayiraṅ koḷḷutal, ceṟṟaṅ koḷḷutal. vayir = kompu. vayir - vayiram - viram.
vayir - vayiri. vayirittal = kaṭiṉamātal.
vayir - vayiri - viri = paki, pakivaṉ.
xiii. uṟuti
uram - uraṉ.
uṟu - uṟuti, uṟutali.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṭṭu = uṟuti.
kaṭṭu - kaṭṭi - keṭṭi.
tul - til - tir - tiram - tiṟam - tiṟaṉ.
tuḷ - (tiḷ) - tiṇ - tiṇṇam.
tiṇ - tiṭam.
tiṇ - tiṇṇam - tiṇṇakkam = neñcaḻuttam.
tiṇ - tiṭam - tiṭāri - tiṭārikkam = neñcuram.
ūkārac cuṭṭu - vaḷital
vērum āṇiyum pōṉṟa nīṇṭa poruṭkaḷ, tām muṭṭiṉa poruḷōṭoṉṟāviṭattuc cāyum. iyaṅku tiṇiyuyirikaḷ cuvarum maliyum pōṉṟavaṟṟāl taṭiyuṇṭaviṭattup pakkamākat tirumpic cellum.
(1) vaḷital tuṟi
i. kōṇutal
kōṇutalāvatu, nērākac cellum poruḷ oru pakkamākac cāytal.
ul - ol - olku. olkutal = cāytal.
uṟu - iṟu - iṟa - iṟappu = kūriccāyvu.
iṟa - iṟavāṇam, iṟavāram = kūriccāyvu.
kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōṇ. kōṇutal = cāytal.
kōṇ - kōṭu. kōṭutal = cāytal.
kōṇ - kōṇam = cāyviṉāl uṇṭākum mūli.
cuḷ - caḷ - caḻi. caḻital - kalamum peṭṭiyum pakkamāka amuṅkic carital.
caḷ - caru - caruvu. caruvutal = cāytal.
caru - cari - carivu. carital = cāytal.
cari = aṭivāram.
caru - cār. cārtal = cāytal.
tūṇil cāyntu koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ eṉpatum, tūṇil cārntu koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ eṉpatum oṉṟē. cērtalik kuṟikkum cār eṉṉum collum, cāytalik kuṟikkum cār eṉṉum collum vevvēṟu.
cār - cāral = maliccarivu, cāyntu peyyum maḻi.
cāralaṉ = cāralnāṭaṉ, malināṭaṉ.
cāralaṉ - cēralaṉ - cēral - cēraṉ = mali nāṭaṉ, muttamiḻ vēntaruḷ oruvaṉ.
caḷ - cāḷ - cāṭu. cāṭutal = cāytal, cāṭu - cāṭṭam - cāyvu. cāṭu - cāṭi = nēraṉmi, cāyal oppu.
cāḷ - cāy - cāyvu. cāyvu carivu eṉpatu vaḻakku.
cāyuṅkālam - cāyaṅkālam - cāyṅkālam = katiravaṉ cāyum vēḷi (eṟpāṭu).
cāy - cāyi = niḻal. niḻal cāytal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. cāy - cāyal = niḻal, orumaruṅku oppu.
(tali) cāyttal = tūṅkutal. cāy - cayaṉam (va.) 
cāy - cā - cāvu. cātal = cāyntu viḻutalpōl iṟattal.
tul - til - tiru - tirum - tirumpu. tirumputal - cāytal.
uccivēḷikkuppiṉ uṭampu niḻal cāyvati, aṭittirumputal eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
mul - (mūl) mūli = kōṇam.
muḷ - muṭa - muṭaṅku - muṭaṅki = mūli. mūli muṭaṅki eṉpatu vaḻakku.
muḷ - (mūḷ) - (māḷ) - māṇ - māṇal = cāyvu, vaḷivu. kōṇal māṇal eṉpatu vaḻakku.
māḷ - māṭu - māṭi = cāyvu. cāṭimāṭi eṉpatu vaḻakku. nērākap paḻikkāmal cāyvu pōṉṟa nēral muṟiyil paḻittal, cāṭimāṭiyāyt tiṭṭutal eṉappaṭum.
maḷ - vaḷ - vāḷ- vār. vārtal = cāytal, carital.
vār - vāram = carivāṉa iṭam. aṭivāram = malic carivu.
tāḻvāram = kūriccarivu.
vāḷ - vāṭu - vāṭṭam = cāyvu. vāṭṭam cāṭṭam eṉpatu vaḻakku. aṅkaṇam vāṭṭam cāṭṭamāy irukka vēṇṭum eṉpar.
ii. vaḷital
vaḷitalāvatu, koṭiyum melliya kampiyum pōṉṟa nīṇṭa tuvaḷporuṭkaḷ vaṭṭamākum vari mēṉmēlum palapaṭiyāyk kōṇutal.
uṟu - iṟu. iṟutal = vaḷital.
iṟu - iṟa = vaḷinta peruṅkūṉi (prawn)
iṟa - iṟā - iṟavu. iṟā - iṟāl - iṟāṭṭu.
iṟa - iṟi = peṇṭiriṉ vaḷinta muṉki.
kul - kula - kulavu. kulavutal = vaḷital. kulavu - kulāvu.
kul - kuṉ - kuṉi. kuṉital = vaḷital.
kuṉ - kūṉ = vaḷinta mutuku. kūṉutal = vaḷital.
kūṉ - kūṉi = ciṟṟiṟāl.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuḻiyam = vaḷitaṭi.
kuḷ - (kuṇ) - kuṇa - kuṇaku - kuṇakku = vaḷivu. kuṇakutal = vaḷital.
kuḷ - kūḷ - kūḷi = vaḷinta vāḻippaḻam.

e.ṭu. Five turns, ten fold.
(tuṭam) - taṭam = vaḷivu. taṭam - taram = muṟi.

xv. varutal
ōriṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟavaṉ tirumputalē varutalām. iṉṟu ceṉṉikkuc ceṉṟu nāḷikkut tirumpuvēṉ eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟil, tirumpuvēṉ eṉpatu tirumpivaruvēṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
cellutal varutal eṉpaṉa ticinōkki vēṟupaṭṭaṉa vēyaṉṟi, viṉivaṭivil vēṟupaṭṭaṉavalla. oruvaṉ ōriṭatti ṉiṉṟu maṟṟōriṭattiṟkuc cellumpōtu, avviṉi puṟappaṭṭa iṭatti nōkkic cellutal eṉṟum, pukum iṭatti nōkki varutal eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. ātalāl, oruvaṉ ōriṭattiṟkup putitāy variṉum mīṇṭuvariṉum, ticipaṟṟi iraṇṭum oṉṟākavē koḷḷappaṭum.
vaḷ = vaḷivu. vaḷ - varu - vār - vā - va.
o. nō. taḷ - taru - tār - tā - ta.
varu : varukiṟāṉ, varuki, varuvāy, varavu.
vār : vārāṉi (varuki), vārum, vāruṅkaḷ (ēvaṟ paṉmi).
vār, vā (ēval orumi).
va; vantāṉ, vammiṉ (ēvaṟpaṉmi).
varukiṟāṉ varuvāṉ eṉṉum nikaḻkāla etirkāla muṟṟu vaṭivukaḷilum. varuki eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarilum, varu eṉpatu pakutiyāyiruttalāṉum; vār eṉṉum orumi yēvalilum, vārum vāruṅkaḷ eṉṉum paṉmiyēvalilum, vārtal vārāṉi eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarkaḷilum, vār eṉpatu pakutiyāyirutta lāṉum; vā eṉṉum ēvalorumi vaṭiviṉiṉṟu vātal vāvu eṉa ētēṉum oru toḻiṟpeyar piṟavāmiyāṉum, varutal eṉṉum viṉikku rukaraṅkūṭiya varu eṉpatē pakutiyām. vaḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu mutalāvatu tiriyak kūṭiyavi var - varu - eṉpaviyē.
iṅku varutal viṉikkuk kūṟiyatit tarutal viṉikkuṅ koḷka. kōr eṉṉum col kō eṉak kuṟintatupōla, varu eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa vār eṉṉuñ collum vā eṉak kuṟinta teṉka. nō eṉṉum viṉi iṟantakālattilum ēvalilum no (nontāṉ, nommāṭā) eṉak kuṟukiyatupōla, vā tā eṉpaṉavum va ta eṉak kuṟukiṉa.
vār = vā (ēvalorumi).
vantikka vāreṉa  (paripā. 20, 70)
lāraṭā vuṉakkiyātu tāṉartam makaḷaṭukkimō  (pārata. vēttira. 12).
xvi. varital
varitalāvatu eḻututal. eḻututal eṉpatu, eḻuttu eḻutu taliyum paṭam varitaliyum potuppaṭak kuṟikkum. muṟ kālattil paṭaveḻuttē (Heiroglyphic or picture-writing) tamiḻakat til vaḻaṅkiyatāl, eḻuttu eṉṉum col paṭattiyum varivaṭiviyum oruṅkē uṇarttiṟṟu.
paṭamum eḻuttum perumpālum vaḷikōṭukaḷālāvatāl, vaḷitaṟ karuttil varitaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
irutiṇiyuyirikaḷiṉ uṭampilum iyalpākak kāṇappaṭum kōṭukaḷ potuvāka vaḷintēyiruttaliṉ, kōṭu eṉṉum peyar mutalāvatu vaḷikōṭṭiyē kuṟittup piṉpu oppumi paṟṟi nēr kōṭṭiyum kuṟittatu.
kōṭutal = vaḷital. kōṭu = vaḷikōṭu, vari (kōṭu). 
varikkutiri varippuli mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kōṭukaḷi nōkkuka.

vaḷ - var - vari. varital = vaḷital, vaḷittu allatu cuṟṟikkaṭṭutal.
vari = vaḷikōṭu, kōṭu, kōṭṭuvaṭivāṉa eḻuttu, varivu, varaṇaṉi, varaṇikkum pāṭṭu allatu kātaṟ pāṭṭu, kaṭṭu.
āṟṟuvari kāṉalvari mutaliya icippāṭṭu vakikaḷi nōkkuka.
varittal = eḻututal, varital, pūcutal, kātaliyiṉ tōḷilum mārpilum varital, avaḷi maṇattal, vaḷittu allatu cuṟṟikkaṭṭutal.
vari - varaṉ = maṇavāḷaṉ.
vari - varaṇam (vari + aṇam) = eḻuttu, varivu, pūccu, niṟam, kulam, vaki, ōcivaki, paṇ, pāṭṭu.
varaṇam (va.) - varaṇi (va.). varaṇittal = varital, collāl varital.
varaṇi - varaṇaṉi (varaṇi + aṉi). varaṇam varaṇi varaṇaṉi eṉpaṉa teṉ collaṭiyāyp piṟanta vaṭanāṭṭuc coṟkaḷ.
vaḷ - vaṇ - vaṇṇam = eḻuttu, varivu, pūccu, niṟam, vaki, ceyyuḷ, ōcivaki.
vaṇṇam - vaṇṇakam = arākam eṉṉum vaṇṇavuṟuppu. vaṇṇaka vottāḻicikkali toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkum tamiḻc ceyyuḷ vaki.
vaṇṇattāṉ - vaṇṇāṉ = āṭikku niṟamūṭṭupavaṉ. 
vaṇṇattuppūcci - vaṇṇāttippūcci.
vaṇṇam - (vaṇṇakaṉ) - vaṇṇakkaṉ = kaṭṭi nāṇayattiṉ vaṇṇatti (urittu) nōṭṭañ ceypavaṉ.
vaṇṇam - vaṇṇi. vaṇṇittal = varaṇittal.
vaṇṇi - vaṇṇakam = varaṇittup pukaḻki.
o. nō. cuḷ - cuṇ - cuṇṇam - cuṇṇakam. cuṇṇittal = nīṟākkal.
vari - varuvu. varuvutal = kōṭiṭutal. varuvūci = kōṭiṭum ūci.
vari - vari = kōṭu. kīṟal. varital = ōviyam varital, eḻuttu eḻututal, kaṭṭuri allatu nūl eḻututal, kātaliyiṉ tōḷilum mārpilum toyyil varital, avaḷi maṇattal.
kuṟippu : - vaṇṇam vaṇṇakam eṉpaṉa toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum tamiḻilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷātalāṉum, avaṟṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭuvaṉa taṉittamiḻ yāppu vakiḷātalāṉum, akkuṟiyīṭukaṭku mūlam taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷē. 
iṉi, vaṇṇam eṉṉum col, vaki eṉṉum poruḷil, avvaṇṇam ivvaṇṇam evvaṇṇam eṉa iruvaki vaḻakkilum peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkutaliyum nōkkuka. 
niṟam eṉṉum varaṇap peyarum tiṟam eṉṉum vakip peyarum icivakiyik kuṟittal pōṉṟē. avviraṇṭiyum kuṟikkum vaṇṇam eṉṉum peyarum ici vakiyik kuṟitta teṉka. tolkāppiyattil 20 vaṇṇaṅkaḷum, avinayattil 100 vaṇṇaṅkaḷum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.
vaṇṇan tāṉē nālin teṉpa
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (cey. 210) vaḻinūṉmuṟiyiṟ kūṟuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
xvii. varampu
ōriṭattiṉ elli kōṭṭiṉāl kuṟikkappaṭuvatu vaḻakka mātaliṉ, kōṭiṭutal elli kuṟittaliyuṇarttiṟṟu. 
varital =kōṭiṭutal, elli kuṟittal, variyaṟuttal, vilakkutal.
vari = kōṭu, ellikkōṭu, aḷavu, elli.
variyaṟuttal, aṭimutal muṭivari, ituvariyum, itu varikkum mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷik kāṇka.
vari - varampu = kōṭu, elli, aḷavu, tāṇṭak kūṭāta ellipōṉṟa aṟaviti, caṭṭam.
varampu - varappu - vayalelliyākiya cuṟṟuttiṭar.

(2) iyaṅkal tuṟi
i. acital
acital eṉpatu, ētēṉumoru poruḷ iṭavalamākavēṉum muṉ piṉṉākavēṉum cāytal allatu vaḷitalōyātaliṉ, vaḷital karuttil acitaṟ karuttup piṟantatu.
ul - ula - ulavu - ulāvu. ulāvutal = acital.
ul - al - ali; alital = acital. ali - aci.
al - āl - āṭu. āṭutal = acital.
ul - ulu - uluṅku - ulukku. uluṅkutal = acital. 
ulukkutal = acittal.
uluṅku - aluṅku - aṉuṅku - aṉukku.
ulukku - alukku = kamakam. alukkutal = kurali iṉimipaṭa vaḷittal allatu acittal.
aluṅku - alaṅku - alaṅkal = acital.
ulu - uluppu. ulupputal = acittal.
ul - ol - olku. olkutal = vaḷital, acital.
uḷ - (uy) - uyal. uyalutal = acital.
(uy) - (uya) - iya - iyaṅku - iyakku - iyakkam = acivu, kiḷarcci. iyaṅkutal = acital.
kul - kulu - kuluṅku - kulukku. kuluṅkutal = acital,
(cul) - cal - cali. calittal = acital, acittal. acittut teḷḷutal. caliyaṭi = callaṭi.
tuḷ - tuḷa - tuḷaṅku - tuḷakku - tuḷakkam.
tuḷaṅkutal = acital.
tuḷ - (tuy) - tuyal. tuyalutal = vaḷital, acital.
nuḷ - nuṇ - nuṇaṅku. nuṇaṅkutal = vaḷital, acital.
nuḷ - nuṭa - nuṭaṅku. nuṭaṅkutal = vaḷital, acital.
ii. iyaṅkutal
oru poruḷ acivatiṉāl atu caṟṟu iṭam peyarkiṉṟatu. ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu iṉṉōriṭattiṟkup peyartalum aciviṉ pāṟpaṭum. āṅkilattilum move eṉṉuñ col, acitaliyum iṭam peyartaliyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka.
ul - ula - ulavu - ulāvu. ulāvutal = iyaṅkutal.
ul - ol - olku. olkutal = naṭattal.
olku - oḻuku - oḻukku - oḻukkam = naṭatti.
oḻukutal = naṭattal, mukkaraṇattāl naṭattal.
ul - al - ali - aci. acital = naṭattal, mella naṭattal.
uḷ - (uy) - uya - iya - iyaṅku - iyakku - iyakkam = celavu. iyaṅkutal = cellutal.
uya - uyavu. uyavu ney = vaṇṭi cellutaṟku vēṇṭum macaku.
uya - uyal - iyal. iyalutal = naṭattal, nikaḻtal, nikaḻa muṭital, cellutal.
iya - iyavuḷ = celuttuvōṉ, kaṭavuḷ.
iyal = naṭatti, taṉmi.
iyal - iyalpu = taṉmi.
iyal - iyaṟki = taṉmi, taṉmiyāka allatu tāṉāka naṭappatu.
cul - cel - celavu.
iii. varuntutal
aciviṉālum celaviṉālum uṭampiṟkut taḷarcciyum varuttamum piṟakkum. ataṉāl, acivum celavum kuṟitta coṟkaḷ taḷarcciyum varuttamuṅ kuṟikkum. uḷḷattiṉ acivu tuṉpam, uṭaliṉ acivu varuttam.
ul - al - allal = tuṉpam.
al - allā. allāttal = tuṉpuṟutal.
alacutal = acital, varuntutal, alacaṭi = tuṉpam.
alital = acital, varuntutal. alittal = varuttutal.
āṟalittal = vaḻippōkkari varuttip paṟittal.
aliccal = tirital, varuttam.
ali - aci - acivu = taḷarcci, varuttam, tuṉpam.
cul - cal - cali - calippu. calittal = taḷartal, varuntutal.
cil - cel - cellal = tuṉpam.
cellal iṉṉal iṉṉā miyē  (tol. uri. 6)
tuḷaṅkutal = acital, varuntutal.

(3) muṟital tuṟi
muṟital
muṟitalāvatu, viṟappāṉa nīḷ poruḷkaḷ oṉṟi muṭṭic cāyumiṭattu allatu vaḷiyumpōtu oṭital, ivviyaṟki vitiyaṟintē, varicciyum karumpiyum oṭikka vēṇṭiyaviṭattu muḻaṅkāli muṭṭavittuc cāyppar.
uṭu - oṭu - oṭi. oṭu = vaḷivu, neḷivu. oṭu - oṭukku, ulōkak kalaṅkaḷiṉ neḷivi oṭu allatu oṭukku eṉpar. ati nīkkutaṟku oṭuttaṭṭutal allatu oṭukkeṭuttal eṉṟu peyar.
oṭital = muṟital. oṭi - oci.
uṟu - iṟu. iṟutal = vaḷital, muṟital.
nuḷ - noḷ - noṭi. noṭittal = vaḷital, oṭital.
nuḷ - neḷ - neru - neri - nerical = neruṅki muṭṭutal, uṭital.
kaṇṇāṭi maṭkalam mutaliya poruḷkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṭṭi nerintupōvatu, avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷ cāyntu muṟitalē.
nerital = vaḷital, muṟital.
neṟital = vaḷital. neṟittal = puṟavitaḻoṭittal. 
muḷ - muṟu - muṟi. muṟital = vaḷital, oṭital.
muṟi = muṟinta tuṇṭu, cīṭṭu.

(4) tirukal tuṟi
tirukal
oru nīṇṭa poruḷ muḻu vaṭṭamākātu mēlum mēlum pala neḷivukaḷāka allatu vaḷivukaḷākat toṭartal tirukalām.
cuḷ - curu - curi. curiyāṇi = muṟukkāṇi.
tuḷ - (tiḷ) - tiru - tiruku - tirukal. tiruku - tirukku = tiruki vikkum aṇi allatu āṇi, muṟukku, māṟupāṭu, vañcaṉi.
tirukum āṇi tirukāṇi, tirukalāṉa kaḷḷi tiruku kaḷḷi.
tiruku = koṇṭit tirukupōl tirukivikkum aṇi.
tiruki = tēṅkāy tiruki pōṉṟa karuvi.
tiru - tiri. tirital = muṟukutal. tirittal = muṟukkutal.
tiricaṭi tiritāṭi tirimaruppu mutaliyaṉa muṟukkuṇṭa poruḷ kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ.
puḷ - puru - puraḷ - puraḷi - puraṭi - piraṭi - piruṭi = yāḻ muṟukkāṇi, tirittuk kūṟum poy.
puru - puri = muṟukkuṇṭa nūl, kayiṟu, purital = muṟukutal.
puru - puraḷ - puraṇṭi - piraṇṭi = puraṇṭirukkum allatu muṟukkuṇṭirukkum koṭi.
muḷ - muṟu - muṟuku - muṟukal. muṟuku - muṟukku = tirukalāka vuḷḷa palakāram, muṟukkum āṇi muṟukkāṇi.
tirukal murukal eṉpatu vaḻakku.
muḷ - (maḷ) - vaḷ - vaḷi = caṅku (muṟukiyiruppatu).
(5) vaṭṭat tuṟi
vaḷitta poruḷiṉ irumuṉiyum toṭumāṟu muḻu vaḷivāṉatē vaṭṭam.
i. vaṭṭamāṉavi
uṟu - iṟu - iṟāl = vaṭṭamāṉa tēṉkūṭu. iṟattal = vaḷital.
uḷ - (aḷ) - (āḷ) - āḻi = vaṭṭam, mōtiram, cakkaram.
kul - *(kula) - kulavi - kuravi = vaṭṭamāka niṉṟāṭuṅ kūttu.
kuḷ - (kuru) - kuruku = vaḷiyal.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu - kuṇṭalam = vaṭṭam, cuṉṉam.
kuḷ - kuṭa - kuṭam - (kuṭakam) - kaṭakam = vaḷiyal, vaḷinta matil, vaṭṭamāṉa peṭṭi.
(kuṭuku) - kiṭuku = vaṭṭamāṉa kēṭakam, vaṭṭamāṉa aṟi, kēṭakam pōṉṟa teṉṉantaṭṭi.
kiṭuku - kiṭukam - kēṭakam.
kuṇa - kuṇaku - kuṇakku - kuṇukku = kaṉatta kātu vaḷiyam.
kuḷ - koḷ = kōḷ - kōṭu -kōṭṭam= vaṭṭam, māvaṭṭam. kōram = vaṭṭil.
cul - cil = cakkaram.
cul - cuṉ - cuṉṉam = cuḻi, vaṭṭam. cuṉ - cuṉṉi = cuḻi.
cuḷ - cuḻi = vaṭṭam. cuḻittal = vaṭṭam varital.
cuḷ - cuṭṭi = vaṭṭamāṉa aṇi, vaṭṭamāṉa poṟi. cuṭṭittali = caṭṭiyuḷḷa tali.
cuvaḷ - cavaḷ - (cakaḷ) - cakaṇṭi = vaṭṭamāṉa paṟi.
cakaṇṭi - cakaṭi = cakkaram, cakkaramuḷḷa vaṇṭi, paṟi.
cakaṭi - cakaṭu - cakaṭam = vaṇṭi, vaṭṭil.
cakaṭu - cakaṭi. cakaṭu - cākāṭu. cakaṭu - cāṭu.
cakaṭi - cakkaṭi - cakkaṭā. 
cuḷ - (caḷ) - caru - cari = vaḷiyal vaki.
cuḷ - cuḷi - cuḷiyam - coḷiyam = cuṉṉam.
cuḷ - (cur) - cūr - cūrppu = kikkaṭakam.
puḷ - puru - puri - pari - pariti = vaṭṭam, vaṭṭamāṉa katiravaṉ. pariti - paruti.
pari - parici - vaṭṭamāṉa kēṭakam.
pari - paricu - parical = vaṭṭamāṉa kūṭittōṇi.
puḷ - (paḷ) - paṇṭi = cakkaram, cakkaramuḷḷa vaṇṭi.
paṇṭi - pāṇṭi - pāṇṭil = vaṭṭam. akal, vaṭṭamāṉa kiṇṇam, cālar.
muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭi = cuḻi, cuṉṉam.
muḷ - (maḷ) - (maṇ) - maṇi = vaṭṭamāṉa veṇkalam.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭiyiṭutal = kāli maṭittal allatu maṭakkutal.
maṇṭutal = vaḷital, maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam, vaṭṭakiyāṉ nāṭṭuppirivu, nāṟpāṉ nāḷ vaṭṭam.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali, maṇṭalittal = vaṭṭamātal, muḻumi yātal, niramputal.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam = vaṭṭam, vaṭṭamāṉa kōḷ, (katiravaṉ tiṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa), vaṭṭamāṉa kaṇṇāṭi.
maṇṭila yāppu, maṇṭalittal, nilimaṟi maṇṭilam, aṭimaṟi maṇṭilam mutaliyaṉa toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varum tamiḻ yāppilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ. aṭinirampi varutal maṇṭila yāppākum. vaṭṭaravuk karuttu muḻumi allatu niṟivuk karuttit taḻuvum.
(maḷ) - vaḷ - vaḷḷam = vaṭṭamāṉa kalam.
vaḷ - vaḷḷi - vaḷiyal.
vaḷ - vaḷi. vaḷi - vaḷiyam. vaḷi - vaḷiyal, vaḷivi.
vaḷ - vaṭṭu = vaṭṭamāṉa karuppukkaṭṭi, vaṭṭamāṉa ōṭu.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭi = vaṭṭamāṉa peṭṭi.
vaṭṭi - vaṭṭil = vaṭṭamāṉa kalam.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭam - vaṭṭaki, vaṭṭāram.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaṇam = vaṭṭamāṉa kēṭakam. vaṭṭaṇam - vaṭṭaṇi. vaṭṭaṇittal = vaṭṭamātal, vaṭṭamākkutal.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaṇi = vaṭṭam, kēṭakam, cālar.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaravu = vaṭṭa vaṭivu.
vaḷ - vaṇṭu = vaḷiyal, vaṭṭamāṉa pūcci.
vaṇṭu - vaṇṭi = cakkaram, cakkarattiyuṭiya ūrti. vaṇṭi - vaṇṭil.
vaḷ - vāḷ - vār - vārppu = vaḷiyal.
vāḷ - vāḷi = mūkkilum kātilum aṇiyum kampi vaḷiyam.
ii. cūḻtal
cūḻtalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟic cuṟṟi vaḷital.
uḷ - uṭu. uṭuttal = cuṟṟik kaṭṭutal. uṭu - uṭuppu. uṭu - uṭi.
uṭuttal = cūḻak koṇṭiruttal.
nīrāruṅ kaṭaluṭutta nilamaṭanti  (maṉōṉmaṇīyam).
