tūculāyk kiṭanta  (cīvaka. 550).
ūr = katiravaṉ tiṅkaḷic cūḻum vaṭṭakkōṭu.
ceṅkatir taṅkuvatō rūruṟṟatu  (kampa. carapaṅ. 9)
ūrkōḷ = nilāvic cuṟṟiyirukkuṅ kōṭṭi.
kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōṭu - kōṭṭi = nakaric cūḻntirukkum matil, nilāvic cūḻntirukkum ūrkōḷ.
cul - cuṟṟu - cuṟṟam. cuṟṟutal = cūḻtal.
cuḷ - cūḻ. cūḻtal = nāṟpuṟamum vaḷital, aṅṅaṉam vaḷital pōl oru kāriyam paṟṟiya ellāvaṟṟiyum eṇṇutal. cūḻ - cūḻcci - cūḻccam = mantiram (ālōcaṉi), nuṇṇaṟivu, viraku (upāyam).
cūḻcci - cūḻcciyam = mati nuṭpamāṉa amippu.
cūḻ - cūḻal = cuṟṟuccārpu, iṭam.
cuḷ - (cūḷ) - cūṭṭu = cakkarattiṉ cuṟṟuccaṭṭam.
cūṟutal = cūḻtal.
cūṟiḷa vimiyōr  (pārata. kāṇṭava. 31)
puḷ - puri - purici = nakaric cuṟṟiyuḷḷa matil.
puri - pari. parittal = cūḻtal.
kuruti parippa  (akam. 31)
parivēṭam = ūrkōḷ.
muḷ - muṟu - muṟṟu - muṟṟuki = matiliccūḻtal.
muḷ - (maḷ) - vaḷ - vaḷavu = vīṭṭiṉ cuṟṟuppuṟam.
vaḷ - vaḷākam = cuṟṟiyuḷḷa nilappakuti.
vaḷ - vaḷi - vaḷical - vaḷavu.
vaḷ - vaṭṭi = cakkarattiṉ cūṭṭu.
vaḷ - vaṭṭu - vaṭṭam = ūrkōḷ.
mul - (mal) - val - vala. valattal = vaḷital, cūḻtal.
vala - vali = cūḻntu oṉṟip piṭikkum kayiṟṟuk karuvi.
vaḷ - vāḷ - vār - vāri = nilattic cūḻnta kaṭal.
vār - vāraṇam = kaṭal. vārtal = vaḷital, cūḻtal.
vāraṇam - vāraṇaṉ = kaṭal teyvam.
vāraṇaṉ - varuṇaṉ.
varuṇaṉ mēya perumaṇa lulakamum  (tol. akat. 5)
iii. curuḷtal
curuḷtalāvatu, oru nīṇṭa tuvaḷporuḷ oru vaḷiyamākavō pala vaḷiyamākavō amital. atu pōṉṟa tōṟṟamum iyakkamum ataṉpāṟpaṭum.
kuḷ - kuḻal. kuḻalutal = curuḷutal.
kaṭikuḻaṉṟa karuṅkuḻalkaḷ  (cīvaka. 164)
kuḷ - (kuru) - kuruḷ = curuḷ.
(kuru) - kuṟaṇṭu. ruṟaṇṭutal = curuḷtal.
kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōṭu - caṅku. kōṭutal = vaṉital, cuḻital.
cuḷ - (curu) - curuḷ = curuṇṭa poruḷ.
curuḷ - curuḷi.
curuḷ - curuḷi - curuṇi.
curuḷ - curuṭṭu - curuṭṭi - curuṇṭa mayir. curuṭṭu = curuṭṭap paṭṭatu. curuṭṭu - curuṭṭi.
(curu) - curi. curital = curuḷtal.
curiyirum pitti  (poruna. 160)
curital = cuḻital.
veḷḷic curicaṅkoṭu  (til. tiruvāya. 7,1,3)
curimukam = caṅku.
curi = uṭampuc cuḻi.
curineṟṟikkāri  (kalit. 101)
curi - curiyal = nīrccuḻi. curi - curintu = nīḻccuḻi.
cuḷ - cuḻi = uṭampuccuḻi, atiyuṭiyavaṉ ceyyuṅ kuṟumpu, nīrccuḻi.
puḷ - puri. purital = curuḷtal.
purikkuḻaṉ maṭanti  (cīvaka. 2688)
purital = cuḻital. puri = caṅku.
puriyoruki paṟṟi  (tiv. iyaṟ. 1, 31)
valampuri = valamākac cuḻinta caṅku iṭampuri = iṭamākac cuḻinta caṅku.
muḷ - muṭa - muṭaṅku - muṭaṅkal = ōliccuruḷ, tirumukam.
muṭaṅkutal = vaḷital, curuḷtal.
muḷ - vaḷ - vaḷi = caṅku. vaḷital = cuḻital.
vaḷ - vaṇṭu = caṅku. vaṇṭu - vaṇṭal = nīrccuḻi.
vaḷ - vāḷ - vār - vāraṇam = caṅku.
iv. cuṟṟutal
cuṟṟutalāvatu, oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟic cuṟṟivarutal allatu vaṭṭamiṭutal.
ul - al - āl, ālutal = cuṟṟi āṭutal.
āl - ālā = vāṉattil vaṭṭamiṭṭup paṟakkum paṟavi.
āl - āli = karumpālipōl cuṟṟiyāṭum iyantiram.
āl - ālatti = kaṇṇeccil kaḻittaṟku viḷakkiyāvatu, mañcaḷ nīriyāvatu oruvar taliyic cuṟṟiyeṭuttal.
ālatti - āḷatti = valivu melivu camaṉ eṉṉum munnili yilum cuṟṟip paṇṇicittal.
āl - ālu - āṭu. āṭutal = cuṟṟutal. āṭu - āṭṭu - āṭṭam.
ul - ula - ulavu. ulavutal = cuṟṟi varutal.
ulavu - ulāvu. ula - ulā - ulāttu.
ulā = veṟṟivēntaṉ talinakaric cuṟṟivarum pavaṉi, atippaṟṟiya nūl.
ulavu - uravu. ulāvu - urāvu.
ula - ulavi = ulavivaruṅ kāṟṟu.
kuḷ - koḷ - koṭṭu. koṭṭutal = cuṟṟutal. cuṟṟivarutal. koḷ - koṭpu.
kāluṇa vākac cuṭaroṭu koṭkum muṉivarum  (puṟam. 43)
koṭum puli koṭkum vaḻi  (ciṟupañ. 80)
koḷ - kōḷ = aṇṭattic cuṟṟivarum viṇmīṉ.
cuḷ - cuḷi - coḷi - coḷiyam. coḷiyamāṭutal = tiruṭum nōkkattuṭaṉ vīṭṭic cuṟṟi varutal.
cul - cula - kalavu - kalāvu. culāvutal = cuṟṟutal.
culāvu = cuṟṟi vīcuṅ kāṟṟu.
cul - cuṟṟu.
cuḷ - cūḻ. cūḻtal = cuṟṟutal.
cūḻkatir vāṉ viḷakkum  (pu. ve. 9, 16)
tuḷ - (tiḷ) - tiru - tiri. tirital = cuṟṟutal.
cuṭaroṭu tiritarum muṉivarum  (cilap. 12)
muḷ - vaḷ - vaṭṭu - vaṭṭam = cuṟṟu, oru kōḷ vāṉa maṇṭalatti orumuṟi cuṟṟivaruṅkālam, viyāḻaṉ cuṟṟivaruvatu viyāḻa vaṭṭam.
vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaṇi - (1) vaṭṭamāṉa celavu.
māppuṇṭara vāciyiṉ vaṭṭaṇimēl  (kampa. paṭit. 97)
(2) iṭacāri valacāriyākac cuṟṟuki.
cuṟṟivarum vaṭṭaṇiyil  (periya. pu. ēṉāti. 29)
v. kaṭṭutal
oru poruḷi nūlālum kayiṟṟālum cuṟṟik kaṭṭuvatāl, cuṟṟutaṟ karuttiṟ kaṭṭutaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
cuṟṟutal = āṭiyi ariyic cuṟṟik kaṭṭutal.
puri - pari - vari. varital = kaṭṭutal. varittal = kaṭṭutal. vari = kaṭṭum araciṟi. uḻavar kaṇṭu mutalil āṟiloru pakutiyi aḷantu cākkiṟ kaṭṭiyataṉāl, araciṟi vari eṉappaṭṭatu. arici piṭittal eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam ariciyic cākkiṟ piṭittu vāṅkutalik kuṟikkumō, aṅṅaṉamē vari kaṭṭutaleṉpatum varip pakutiyic cākkiṟ kaṭṭi varittaṇṭalāḷariṭam oppu vittalik kuṟikkum.
muḷ - muṭu - muṭi - muṭiccu = vaḷittuk kaṭṭiya kaṭṭu, muṭiccup pōṉṟa marakkaṇu.
muṭi = vaḷittuk kaṭṭiya kaṭṭu. muṭital = vaḷittuk kaṭṭutal.
muṭi - muṭippu = muṭiyappaṭṭa poruḷ.
vi. cuḻalutal
cuḻalutalāvatu, oru poruḷ taṉṉit tāṉē cuṟṟutal. cuḻaṟṟutal aṅṅaṉañ cuṟṟuvittal.
ul - ulam = cuḻaṟci. ulamvā - ulamā - ulamaral.
ulamarutal = cuḻalutal, maṉam cuḻaluvatupōl varuntutal.
uḻalutal = cuḻalutal. uḻal - uḻali = uḻalum āliyulakki allatu vāyiṟ kuṟukkumaram.
uḻal - uḻaṟṟu - uḻaṟṟi = cuḻaṟci.
uḻal - uḻaṉṟi = māṭṭuk kaḻuttil kaṭṭum uḻalit taṭi.
ul - al - āl - ālu - āṭu. āṭutal = cuḻalutal.
kuḷ - kuṟu - (kaṟu) - kaṟa - kaṟaṅku = kāṟṟāṭi. kaṟaṅkutal = cuṟṟutal, cuḻalutal.
kuṟu - kiṟu - kiṟukiṟu = kiṟukiṟuppu = taliccuṟṟu.
cuṟṟum poruḷi nōkkik kiṟukiṟuveṉṟu cuṟṟukiṟatu eṉpatiyum, kiṟukiṟu vāṇam kiṉṉaṟu vāṇam eṉṟu collik koṇṭu ciṟuvar cuṟṟiyāṭi viḷiyāṭuvatiyum, tali valikkumpōtu kiṟukiṟu veṉṟu varukiṟatu eṉṟu col vatiyum nōkkuka.
kiṟu - kiṟukku = taliccuṟṟu, mūḷikkōḷāṟu, pittiyam. oruvar nīṇṭanēram cuṟṟiyāṭuvatāl talimayakkam uṇṭātal kāṇka.
kiṟukiṟuttal = tali cuṟṟutal, mayakkamātal.
talicuḻaṉṟu kiṟukiṟuttu  (pirapōta. 18, 54)
kiṟu - kiṟaṅku - kiṟakkam = mayakkam. kiṟaṅkutal = mayaṅkutal.
kuḷ - koḷ - koṭpu = cuḻaṟci. koḷ - kōḷ = katiravaṉic cuṟṟum viṇmīṉ.
vaḷivalaṅ koṭku māttiram  (maṇi 12, 91)
cuḷ - cuḻal - cuḻaṟci - cuḻal - cuḻaṟṟu - cuḻaṟṟi - cuḻaṭṭi.
curittal = cuḻalutal.
curukku maṇṭalan tūṅkunīrc curippuṟa vīṅka  (kampa. irāvaṇaṉ tērēṟu. 30)
cuḷ - cuṟu - cūṟi = cuḻittaṭikkuṅ kāṟṟu.
cūrttal = cuḻalutal. cūrppu = cuḻaṟci.
tuḷ - (tiḷ) - tiri. tirital = cuḻalutal. cūṟivaḷi = cūṟāvaḷi.
valantiriyāp poṅki  (pu.ve.9, 12)
tirittal = cuḻaṟṟutal.
eḵku valantirippa  (tirumuru. 111)
tiri - tiriki = tirikal.
tivi - teru - terum = cuḻaṟci. terumvā - terumā - terumaral.
alamaral terumaral āyiraṇṭum cuḻaṟci  (tol. uri. 13).
puḷ - (puy) - puyal (Cyclone).
muḷ - vaḷ - vaḷi = vaḷintu vīcuṅkāṟṟu.
vaḷ - vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaṇi = vāḷ pōl paṭik kalattic cuḻaṟṟutal.
vii. alital
oru poruḷ cuṟṟumpōtum cuḻalumpōtum pala iṭattic cārtalāl, cuṟṟutaliyum cuḻalutaliyuṅ kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ cila, aṅkumiṅkum alitalik kuṟikkum.
ul - al - ali.
uḻalutal = alital.
cuḻalutal = alital. cuṟṟutal = alital.
tirital = alital.
(6) uruṭcit tuṟi
uruṭci eṉpatu kaṉavaṭṭam. parappu maṭṭum uḷḷatu vaṭṭam, parappum kaṉamum uḷḷatu kaṉavaṭṭam.
kuṇṭuruṭci nīḷuruṭci eṉa uruṭci iruvakippaṭum.
i. uruḷtal
uruḷtalāvatu, uruṭcip poruḷkaḷ puraṇṭu puraṇṭōṭutal.
ul - al - ali. alital = uruḷtal, alittal = uruṭṭutal.
tēṟal kallalit toḻukum  (puṟam. 115)
ali = uruḷum tiri.
uruḷ - uruḷi = uruḷum cakkaram.
uruḷ - uruṭci. uruḷ - uruṭṭu - uruṭṭal. uruṭṭu - uruṭṭi.
ii. kuṇṭuruṭci (uruṇṭi)
pantupōṉṟatu kuṇṭuruṭciyākum.
ul - ula - ulaku = uruṇṭiyāṉa ñālam, atu pōṉṟa kōḷ.
ulaku - ulakam.
uruḷ - uruṇṭi - uṇṭi.
kuḷ - kuḷiki = uruṇṭa māttiri.
kuḷi - kuḷiyam = uruṇṭi, maruntu (māttiri).
kuḷiyam - kuḻiyam = vācaṉiyuruṇṭi.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = uruṇṭi.
kul - kōl - kōli = uruṇṭi, ciṟṟuruṇṭi.
kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōḷam = uruṇṭi.
kōḷ - kōḷā = uruṇṭikkaṟi.
cuḷ - cuḻi - cuḻiyam = uruṇṭip palakāram.
puḷ - (poṇṭu) - pōṇṭā = uruṇṭip palakāram.
muḷ - muḻi - viḻi = kaṇṇiṉ karuviḻi, viḻi.
mūkkum muḻiyum eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
muḷ - (muṭam) - muṭal - muṭali = uruṇṭi.
vaḷ - vaṭṭu - vaṭṭaṇi = uruṇṭi.
(vaṭam) - vaṭakam = tāḷikkum uruṇṭi.
iii. uruṇṭa tiraṭci
uruṇṭa tiraṭciyāvatu kuṇṭāyiruntum muḻuvuruṇṭi yallātatu.
ul - ulam = uruṇṭa tiraṭci, uruṇṭa kal.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭatu.
kuṇṭukkal, kuṇṭuk kōtumi, kuṇṭuc campā, kuṇṭumaṇi, kuṇṭu malliki, kuṇṭūci mutaliya peyarkaḷ, uruṇṭa tiraṭcip poruḷkaḷik kuṟikkum.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭā = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kalam.
kuṇṭu - kaṇṭu = nūṟpantu.
kuṇṭu - kuṭṭu = muṭṭi (te.)
kuṭam = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kaṭṭi allatu kalam.
kum - kumpu - kumpam = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kalam.
kumpaccuri = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa curi.
kumpu - kumpā = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kalam.
kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōḷam - kōcam = muṭṭi.
kuḷ - koḷ - koṭṭi = uruṇṭi taliyaṇi. 
cul - cūl = muṭṭi.
ñamali tanta maṉavuccū luṭumpiṉ  (perumpāṇ. 132).
cul - cuṉ - (cuṉi) - ciṉi = muṭṭi.
cuḷ - (coḷ) - cōḷam = uruṇṭa kūlam.
muḷ - muṭṭi.
muḷ - muṭṭu - muttu = uruṇṭa kal allatu viti.
āmaṇakku muttu, kurukku muttu, puḷiya muttu, vēppamuttu mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷik kāṇka.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maṇ - maṇi = uruṇṭa kūlam allatu viti, uruṇṭa pāci.
neṉmaṇi, kuṉṟimaṇi mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷik kāṇka.

iv. nīḷuruṭci
uruḷi pōṉṟatu nīḷuruṭciyām.
ul - ulam - ulakki.
uruḷ - uruḷi - uruṭi (-rōti).
muṉpu kūṭaliyal - tiraḷal tuṟiyil kuṟikkap peṟṟa ulakki kuṇṭalam mutaliya orucila coṟkaḷ tiraṭcikkarut toṭu uruṭcik karuttum koṇṭaviyeṉa aṟika.

(9) ūkāraccuṭṭu 
tuḷittal
oṉṟi valitāyt toṭṭaporuḷ kūrnuṉiyuḷḷa tāyirup piṉ, atu vaḷiyāviṭattu ataṉāl muṭṭappaṭṭa poruḷit tuḷikkum.
vēr maṇṇiyum, ūci tuṇiyiyum, pūcci ēṭṭiyum, āṇi marattiyum, ampu uṭampiyum, kampi nilattiyum, piṟa piṟavaṟṟiyum tuḷikkum.
oru poruḷi viṉimutal (karuttā) tuḷippilum, viṉi mutalāṟ kiyāḷap peṟum karuvi tuḷippiṉum, collākka neṟikku iraṇṭum oṉṟē.
valitāyt toṭum poruḷkaḷuḷ, kūr nuṉiyuḷḷavi caṟṟu uḷḷiṟaṅku mātalāl, ataṉiyē tuḷittaliṉ tuvakka niliyākak koḷḷalvēṇṭum. kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷit tuḷippaṉavellām kūrnuṉi yuḷḷaṉavākavē irukkum.
(1) kuḻittal tuṟi
kuḻittalāvatu paḷḷam uṇṭupaṇṇutal.
i. kuttal
kuttal eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ uḷḷiṟaṅkumāṟu kūrpporuḷāṟ kuttutal. kuttu - kutti - katti.
toṭutaliyaliṟ kūṟiya kuttal kuttit toṭutal eṉṟum, iṅkuk kūṟiyatu kuttit tuḷittal eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟika.
(ur) - ar - ara - araṅku. araṅkutal = ampu mutaliyaṉa tittal.
kuttu - kutti = kuttuṅ karuvi, koḻuvoṭu cērnta kalappip pakuti.
kuttu - kottu - koṭṭu. kaḷikkottukaḷik koṭṭu mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.
(tuḷ) - (taḷ) - (tay) - ti. muḷ tittal = muḷ uḷḷiṟaṅkutal.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷi. poḷital = uḷiyāṟ kottutal.
poḷ - po. pottal = kuttit tuḷittal.
poḷ - potu. potuttal = muṉ mutaliyaṉa pāytal.
potu - potir. potirttal = kuttutal.
ii. kīṟutal
kīṟutalāvatu kūrṅ karuviyāṟ kutti nīḷa iḻuttal.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kīḷ. kīḷtal = kiḻittal, piḷattal.
kiḷ - kiḻi, kīḷ - kīḻ, kīḻtal = kiḻittal = piḷattal.
kuḷ - kuṟu - kiṟu - kiṟukku. kiṟukkutal = kīṟivarital, kōṭu kīṟutal.
kiṟu - kīṟu - kīṟal.
kīṟu - kīṟṟu = kīṟiyavari, kīṟiya tuṇṭu, tuṇṭu, taṭṭit tuṇṭu.
kīṟṟu - kīccu. kīccutal = kōṭu kiḻittal.
kuḷ - (kuṟu) - kūṟu = pakuti. tuṇṭu.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷi - poḷittal = kiḻittal.
puḷ - (puṟu) - pūṟu - pīṟu. pīṟutal = nakattāṟ kīṟutal.
iii. poṟittal
poṟittalāvatu, kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷiṟ kuḻitteḻutul.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuḻittal = kuḻitteḻututal.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷi. poḷital = veṭṭutal, eḻuttu veṭṭutal.
poḷi - poṟi - poṟittal = kuḻit teḻututal.
puḷ - piḷ - viḷ - veṭṭu.
iv. kiṇṭutal
kiṇṭutalāvatu poruḷkaḷik kiḷittal allatu kuṭital.
uḷartal = talimayirik kiṇṭutal, arumpik kuṭital.
uḻutal = kiṇṭutal, mayirik kōtutal.
kuṭital = kiṇṭutal. kuṭi - kaṭi.
neykuṭi tayiriṉ  (paripā. 16, 3)
kaṭi - kaṭali = kaṭiyappaṭum payaṟu.
kaṭi - kaṭiccal.
kuḷ - koḻu - kōḻi = nilattiyum kuppiyiyum kiḷikkum paṟavi. koḻu = nilatti uḻum kāṟu.
koḻu - koḻutu. koḻututal = mayir kuṭital.
koḻutu - kōtu.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kiḷar - kiḷaṟu - kiḷāṟu. kiḷāṟutal = kiṇṭutal.
kiḷ - kiḷi. kiḷittal = kiṇṭutal, kiṇṭiyatu pōṟ pirital.


kiḷ - kēḷ - kēḷvi. kēṭṭal = kiṇṭutalpōl viṉavutal, viṉaviyataṟku oruvar viṭukkum viṭiyic cevikoṭuttal, cevimaṭuttal.
kiḷ - kiṇṭu - keṇṭu. keṇṭutal = kiḷittal. tōṇṭutal.
keṇṭu - keṇṭi = nīruṇkalattiṉ kiḷi pōṉṟa uṟiñci, atu uḷḷa kalam.
kiḷ - keḷ - kēḷ - kēḻal = nilattik kiṇṭum paṉṟi, ati yotta yāṉi.
tul = tuṉ. tuṉṉutal = uḻutal. kiṇṭutal, tuṉṉūci = kalappik kutti.
tuḷ - tuḻa - tuḻavu. tuḻa - tuḻā - tuḻāvu. tuḻāvutal = kiṇṭutal.
tuḷ - toḷ - toy. toytal = uḻutal.
nuḷ - naḷ - naṇṭu. naṇṭutal = kiṇṭutal.
naṇṭaṟcōṟu = tippoṅkaliṟ kiṇṭic camitta cōṟu.
naṇṭal = kiṇṭik kuḻinta cōṟu. naṇṭal kiṇṭip paṭikkiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
naṇṭal piṇṭal = kūḻuṅ kaṭṭiyumāy ākkiya cōṟu. cōṟṟi naṇṭal piṇṭalākki viṭṭāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku.
naṇṭu = nilattik kiṇṭic cellum nīruyiri.
naṇṭu - ñaṇṭu.
nuḷ - neḷ - neṇṭu. neṇṭutal = kiṇṭutal.
neṇṭu = naṇṭu. neṇṭu - ñeṇṭu = naṇṭu. ñeṇṭutal = kiṇṭutal.
muḷ - muṇṭu. muṇṭutal = paṉṟi mukattāṟ kiḷittal.

7. aṟuttal
aṟuttalāvatu, vāḷāl nīṭṭuvākil palamuṟi tuḷittuk kīṟutal allatu tuḷittut tuṇittal.
(ur) - ar - araṅku. araṅkutal = ampu mutaliyaṉa tittal. (tuḷittu aṟuttal)
araṅku - arakka. arakkutal = kiḷitaṟittal, veṭṭutal.
tārun tōḷumarakki  (vināyaka. pu. 42, 4).
araṅku = aṟukkappaṭṭa aṟi. vaṭṭāṭṭiṟkum cūtāṭṭiṟkum vakutta kaṭṭam, naṭattiṟkum nāṭakattiṟkum vakutta nilam allatu mēṭi.

araṅkiṉṟi vāṭṭāṭi yaṟṟē nirampiya
nūliṉṟik kōṭṭi koḷal  (kuṟaḷ. 401).
araṅkēṟṟam = mēṭiyēṟik kalvittiṟamum kalittiṟamum kāṭṭal.
oru vīṭu pala aṟikaḷ koṇṭiruppati araṅkaraṅkāyirukkiṟatu eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku.
araṅku - araṅkam = nāṭakavaraṅku, cūtāṭumiṭam, pōrkkaḷam, nīrāl aṟukkappaṭṭa āṟṟiṭikkuṟi, kāvirikkum koḷḷi ṭattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa āṟṟiṭik kuṟiyic cērnta tiruvaraṅkam.
araṅkam - araṅkaṉ = tiruvaraṅkattil paḷḷikoṇṭirukkum tirumāl.
araṅkam tiruvaraṅkam araṅkaṉ eṉpaṉavē toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum vaṭivaṅkaḷ. raṅkam srīraṅkam raṅkaṉ eṉpaṉa iṭikkālattil mutaṟkuṟiyākat tirinta vaṭa coṟkaḷākum.
araṅku allatu araṅkam eṉṉum coṟku aṟukkappaṭṭatu eṉpatē mūlapporuḷ; ar(ur) eṉpatē mūlam. raṅkam eṉṉum taliyaṟṟa coṟku mūlamumilli; mūlap poruḷu milli.
ar - aṟu - aṟuppu.
aṟu - aṟuvu - aṟuvaṭi.
aṟu - aṟuvi - aṟukkappaṭum tuṇi, tuṇi.
aṟu - aṟi = tuṇṭam, kaṭṭiṭappakuti, aṟukkappaṭṭa allatu vakukkappaṭṭa iṭam, araṅku, cūtāṭum kaṭṭam, pātti.
araṅku aṟi eṉṉum iru coṟkaṭkum uriya poruḷkaḷ ēṟattāḻa oṉṟāyiruppatālum, av iru coṟkaṭkum ar eṉpatē mūlamātalālum, aṟiyeṉṉum col taṉittamiḻc col eṉpatu veḷḷiṭimali yātalālum, araṅku allatu araṅkam eṉṉum collum teṉ collēyeṉṟu teḷika.
vi. piḷattal
piḷattalāvatu oru poruḷi orē muṟiyil veṭṭit tiṟattal allatu tuṇittal. tāṉāyt tiṟattalum piḷattalē.
uṭi - uṭippu, kuḷ - kaḷ - kaṇ - kaṇi - kaṇicci = piḷakkuṅ kōṭari.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kiṟu - kīṟu - kīṟal.
tuḷ - tuṟa - tuṟappu = tiṟattal.
tuṟa - tuṟavu = tiṟavu. tuṟa - tiṟa - tiṟavu.
tiṟa - tiṟappu - tuṟappuk kuccu = tiṟavukōl.
tuṟa - tuṟavi = tiṟanta veḷiyiṭam.
maṇṭi piḷattali maṇṭi tiṟattal eṉṟu kūṟuvatu vaḻakku.
tiṟa - teḷi. teṟittal = piḷattal, uṭital.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷi. poḷital = veṭṭutal, piḷattal.
poḷ - pōḻ. pōḻtal = piḷattal. pōḻ = tuṇṭu.
puḷ - piḷ - piṭu. piṭutal = kiyāṟ piḷattal.
piḷ - piḷavu = tuṇṭu.
piṭu - pitu - pitir. pitirtal = piṇṭu utirtal.
piḷ - viḷ - viṭu. viṭutal = piḷattal. viḷḷutal = piḷattal.
vii. veṭittal
veṭikkum poruḷ oṉṟip piḷantukoṇṭu veḷivaru vatāl, piḷattaṟkaruttil veṭittaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
puḷ - piḷ - piṭu - piṭuṅku - piṭāṅku = piṭuṅkum vēṭṭu.
piṭāṅku - pīraṅki.
aṭittiruntatu tiṭumeṉat tāṉē tiṟantuviṭṭāl, piṭuṅkiviṭṭateṉpar. piṭāṅku vēṭṭip pīraṅki eṉpatu tañci vaḻakku.
piḷ - viḷ - viṭu - veṭu - veṭi.
viḷ - (veḷ) - veṭṭu - vēṭṭu.
viii. virital
piḷanta poruḷ viriyum.
noḷ - noku - nokku = veṭippu.
kum - kam - kamar = veṭippu.
puḷ - piḷ - viḷ.
viḷḷutal = piḷattal, virital.
viḷ - viḷa - viḷavu. viḷavutal = kamarātal.
viḷ - viri - virivu. virital = piḷattal, veṭittal. pittac cūṭṭiṉāṟ pātattil veṭikkum veṭippip pitta virivu eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
viri - viral = kiyiṉiṉṟu virintatu.
viḷ - viru - viruvu = nilacciṟu veṭippu.
viḷ - viṭu - viṭar = mali veṭippu, kuki.
viṭutal = piḷattal, virital. viṭu - viṭavu = viṭar.
viḷ - (viy) - viyam - viyaṉ - viyal = virivu, akaṟci, parappu.
viyal - (viyalaṉ) - viyāḻaṉ = katiravaṉic cuṟṟum kōḷkaḷellā vaṟṟilum periyatu.
viyaleṉ kiḷavi akalap poruṭṭē  (tol. uriyiyal, 66.)
viḷ - veḷ - veṭi - veṭippu. veṭittal = piḷattal.
ix. vāy virital
piḷ - piḷā - piḻā = vāyakaṉṟa ōlik koṭṭāṉ.
piḻā - piṭā = vāyakaṉṟa nārppeṭṭi.
piṭā - piṭaku - piṭakam = taṭṭu, peṭṭi.
piḷ - (peḷ) - peṭṭi = ōli nār maram mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa vāyakaṉṟa ēṉam.
peṭṭi - peṭṭakam.
(peḷ) - pēḷ - pēḻ = piḷavu, virivu, akaṟci, pēḻvāy =akaṉṟa vāy.
pēḻ - pēḻi = vāyakaṉṟa ceppu allatu marakkalam.
x. pirital
piḷanta poruḷ vēṟākap piriyum.
pul - pil (vil) - vilaku - vilakku - vilakkam.
vilaku - vilaṅku. vilaṅkutal - vilakutal.
puḷ - puy. puyttal = pirittal, piṭuṅkutal, puy - piy.
piy - (pey) - peyar. peyartal = pirital, vilakutal. peyar - pēr.
puḷ - piḷ - piri - pirivu - piriviṉi.
piḷ - piṭu - piṭuṅku. piṭuṅkutal = peyarttal, paṟittal.
piṭu - (pitu) - pituṅku - pitukku - pitukkam.
pituṅkutal = peyartal, peyarttu veḷivarutal.
(pitu) - pitir. pitirtal = peyartal, pituṅkutal.
piḷ - viḷ. viḷḷutal = vilakutal.
viḷ - viṭu - vīṭu = viṭutal, nīṅkutal, celavu viṭṭirukkum iṭam (illam), paṟṟuviṭuki, pēriṉpa niliyam.
viṭu - viṭuti. viṭu - viṭutali.
viṭutal = pirital, nīṅkutal, viṭṭicittal = pirintolittal.
xi. viṭuttal
viṭuttalāvatu cikkalāṉa poruḷi viṭuvittal.
puḷ - piḷ - piy - (piyi) - pici = piyppatupōl viṭukkum viṭukati.
piḷ - piṭu - pitu - pitir = viṭukati. pitir - putir.
piḷ - viḷ. viḷḷutal = pitir viṭuttal. viḷ - viṭu viṭuttal = piṭṭuc collutal, viṭuvittal. viṭu - viṭi = pitirpōṉṟa aṟiyā viṉāvi viṭuvittalpōṟ kūṟum maṟumoḻi.
xii. cēy pirital
piḷ - piṟa - piṟappu. piṟa - piṟavi. piṟa - piṟanti = piṟavī,
iṉam (genus).
tāyum piḷḷiyum vēṟuvēṟu pirintuviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu pēṟukālattiṟ peṇṭirāṟ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. tāy vayiṟṟiṉiṉṟu cēy piritalē piṟattalām.
xiii. malam pirital
(pul) - (pil) - pēl.
puḷ - puḻukki - piḻukki.
(puḷ) - piḷ - (piy) - pī.
piḷ - pīḷ - pīḷi - pūḷi = kaṇ malam.
piḷ - (viḷ) - viṭṭu - viṭṭi.
xiv. nīrpporuḷ pirital
(pul) - pil - piliṟṟu. piliṟṟutal = cintutal, pīccutal.
pil - pīl - pīr = oru pīccu.
pīl - pīṟṟu - pīccu. o-nō: kīṟṟu - kīccu.
piḷ - piḻi - piḻiyal.
piḷ - piy - pey - peyal.
xv. pakutal
poruḷkaḷ pala pakutikaḷāyp piritalē pakutalām.
puḷ - (paḷ) - (paḻu) - paku. o. nō. toḻu - toku.
pakutal = pirital. pakkicittal = pirittolittal.
paku - pakuti = pirivu. kūṟu, kaṇṭumutalil āṟiloru kūṟāṉa araciṟi, pakucolluṟuppāṟaṉuḷ oṉṟāṉa mutaṉili.
pakuti vikuti eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ poruḷum, prakruti vikruti eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷum, vevvēṟām. pakuti = kūṟu. vikuti = īṟu. vikutal = muṭital. prakruti = iyaṟki (muṉ ceyyappaṭṭatu), iyalpu. vikruti = vikāram. pakutiyum tiripi (vikāratti) aṭiyak kūṭumātalālum, canticāriyi iṭinili eṉpavum tiripi yuṇṭu paṇṇutalālum, vikutiyeṉṉum uṟuppiṟkut tiripu eṉṉum poruḷ poruntātu. pakuti vikutikaḷ muṟiyē mutaṉili iṟutiniliyeṉṟu peyar peṟutalālum, avaṟṟiṟkuk kūṟu īṟu eṉpaṉavē coṟporuḷ eṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum. kaḷattil mutalāvatu aracaṉukkuc celuttappaṭum pakuti ciṟappākap pakuti eṉṟē peyar peṟṟatu pōl, pakucolluṟuppukkaḷuḷ mutaṉmiyāṉatum pakuti yeṉṟē peyar peṟṟa teṉka. ākavē, pakuti vikutiyeṉpaṉa  prakruti vikruti eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripallaveṉṟu teḷintu koḷka. pakuti vikutiyeṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē prakruti vikruti eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷit tirittuk koṇṭu, avaṟṟiṟkup poruntap puḷukalāka vēṟu poruḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar vaṭavar eṉka.
paku - pakam = pakuti, āṟu eṉṉum toki.
vēḷāḷaṉviḷivu, aracaṉ teṉpulattār teyvam viruntu okkal tāṉ eṉṉum aṟuvarkkup pakirntaḷikkappaṭṭata ṉālum, pakucol pakuti vikuti canti cāriyi iṭinili vēṟupāṭu (vikāram) eṉṉum āṟuṟuppukkaḷāyp pakukkap paṭṭataṉālumē, pakam eṉṉum coṟku āṟu eṉṉum tokipporuḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu.
paku - pakam - pakavaṉ = pakuttaḷittuk kāppavaṉ, palarkkum paṭiyaḷappavaṉ, āṇṭavaṉ.
vēḷāḷaṉ aṟu cārārkkam, aracaṉ araciyal viṉiñarkkum, āṇṭi aṭimiyarkkum, paṭiyaḷappatu pōla; āṇṭavaṉum palarkkum uṇavip pakuttaḷittuk kāppavaṉ eṉṉum karuttil, avaṉip pakavaṉ eṉṟaṉar. iṉṟum āṇṭavaṉip paṭiyaḷakkiṟavaṉ eṉṟu potumakkaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka. Lord eṉṉum āṅkilac collum itē karuttup paṟṟiyeḻun tiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
E. Lord. O.E. hlaford (loafward) = bread. keeper.
iṉi, avaravarkkuriya naṉmi tīmikaḷi vakuppavaṉ eṉṟumām.
pālvari teyvam  (col. 53)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyati nōkkuka.
iṉi, paku - pakavu - pakavaṉ eṉṟumām, eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum poruḷ oṉṟē.
paku - pakuppu. paku - pakal - pāl. paku - paki.
paku - pakku - paṅku. pakku - pakkam - pakkal.
paku - pakir - pakirvu. paku - pakar. pakartal = poruḷkaḷip pakuttu vili kūṟutal.
paku - pāku = pakuti, pakkam, pakkamāyiruntu pēṇupavaṉ (pākaṉ).
pāku - pākaṉ. pāku - pākam. pāku - pāki.
pāku - pākku = pakuti, kūṟu. pākku - pākkiyam = kūṟu, naṟkūṟu, pēṟu.
alareḻa āruyir niṟkum ataṉip 
palaraṟiyār pākkiyat tāl.  (kuṟaḷ. 1141)
pālvāykkuḻavi eṉṉum cilappatikārat toṭarukku (15:23). mēṟpeṟakkaṭava nalla pakutiyit taṉṉiṭattē yuṭiya kuḻavi eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟutal kāṇka.
pākku eṉṉum colli bhag eṉa eṭuttolippataṉālēyē atu vaṭacoṟpōl tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. iyam eṉpatu ōr īṟu. o. nō. kaṇ - kaṇṇiyam, paṇ - paṇṇiyam.
pākku - pākkam = pakkam, pakkamāṉa iṭam, paṭṭiṉap pakuti, pākkam - vākkam.
pākku - pāṅku - pāṅkar. pāṅku - pāṅkaṉ.
paku - pā - pātti. pā - pātu. pātiṭu - pātīṭu.
paku - vaku - vakuti. vaku - vakuppu.
vaku - vaki. vaku - vakuntu = vaki, vakutta vaḻi. vaḻivakuttal, vaḻivaki eṉpaṉa marapu.
vaku - vakku = vaki, vaḻi.
vaku - vakir - vakiṭu = vakirnta ucci, uccikkōṭu.
vaku - vāku - vākku = pākkam, tici.
iṭakki vākku, kāṟṟuvākku mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷik kāṇka.
paku eṉṉum col teṉcol eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ :
(1) vaṭamoḻiyil paku eṉṉuñ coṟku mūlamākak kāṭṭap paṭum bhaj eṉpataṟku āṇivērēṉum varalāṟēṉum illi.
atōṭu bhaj (to divide) eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭatāka bhanj (to break) eṉṟoru mūlam kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. uṇmiyil iraṇṭum oṉṟē. tamiḻp pakutiyiṉ tirinta vaṭivukaḷē vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamākak kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. pakutikkum, muntiyatu mūlam allatu vēr.
(2) Bhaj eṉṉum mūlattiṉ tirivukaḷāka, bhakti, bhaga, bhagavan, bhaga, bhagya mutaliya cila coṟkaḷē vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻilō nāṟpatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa coṟkaḷ paku eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu tirintuḷḷaṉa.
(3) pakuti pakkam pākam mutaliya pala coṟkaḷum paku eṉṉum orē pakutiyiṉiṉṟu tirintirukkavum, avaṟṟiṉ tiripukaḷāṉa prakruti pakṣam bhaga mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ vevvēṟeḻuttuk kaḷik koṇṭaṉavāy vevvēṟu mūlattaṉavākak kāṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
xvi. tōṇṭutal
tōṇṭutalāvatu oru poruḷiṉ parappu paḷḷamākak kuṭiyap paṭal. uḷ - aḷ - (aḻu) - aku - akaḻ - akaḻi.
kul - kal. kallutal = tōṇṭutal. kal - kalam = tōṇṭappaṭṭa ēṉam. kal - kaṉ, kaṉṉutal = tōṇṭutal. kaṉ - kaṉṉam = tōṇṭutal. tuḷittal, tuḷi, tuḷiyāṉa kātu. kaṉṉakkōl = tōṇṭuṅ karuvi.
kaṉ - kaṉi = tōṇṭappaṭṭa curaṅkam.
kul - kil. kil - kīḷ = kīḻ. kīḻtal = tōṇṭutal.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuṭi. kuṭital = tōṇṭutal.
kuḷ - (kuy) - kuyil. kuyilutal = tōṇṭutal. kuyil - kuyiluvam = marattik kuṭintu ceyyappaṭṭa icik karuvi.
cul - cūl. cūlutal = tōṇṭutal.
tuḷ - (tūḷ) - tūṇi = tōṇṭappaṭṭa kalam, ōraḷavu. 
tuḷ - turu - tūri - tūriyam = kuṭintu ceyyappaṭṭa icikkaruvi.
tuḷ - toḷ - toṭu. toṭutal = tōṇṭutal.
toḷ - tōḷ.
kēṭpiṉuṅ kēḷāt takiyavē kēḷviyāl
tōṭkap paṭāta cevi. (kuṟaḷ. 418)
tōḷ - tōṇṭu. tōṇṭu - tōṇṭi.
tōḷ - tōṇ - tōṇi = tōṇṭappaṭṭa marakkalam.
nuḷ - noḷ - nōḷ - nōṇṭu. nōṇṭutal = tōṇṭutal.
puḷ - poḷ - poy. poytal = tuḷittal, tōṇṭutal.
xvii. tēṭutal
nilattiṟkuḷḷirukkuṅ kiḻaṅkiyum, eṟumpukaḷ cērttu vittirukkum aṭippulliyum, poṉṉiyum, putiyaliyum, makkaḷ tōṇṭiyeṭuppa taṉāl,tōṇṭaṟ karuttil tōṇṭu taṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. kiṇṭutalum tōṇṭutaliṉ pāṟpaṭum,
tuḷ - tuḻa - tuḻavu - tuḻāvu. tuḻāvutal = tēṭutal.
tuḷ - tura - turappu.turapputal = tuḷittal, tēṭutal.
tura - tira - tirakku. tirakkutal = tēṭutal.
tuḷ - toḷ - tōḷ - tōṇṭu - tēṇṭu - tēṭu - tēṭṭu - tēṭṭam.
nuḷ - noḷ - nōḷ - nōṇṭu - nēṇṭu - nēṭu. nēṭutal = tēṭutal.
nōṇṭu - nōṭu. nōṭutal = tēṭutal, āytal, ārāytal, pārttal.
nōṭu - nōṭṭam = āyvu, ārāycci.
nōṭu - nāṭu, nāṭutal = tēṭutal, virumputal, āytal.
nāṭu - nāṭṭam = tēṭṭam, viruppam, tēṭum pārvi, pārvi, kaṇ.
nāṭṭa miraṇṭum matiyuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
kūṭṭi yurikkuṅ kuṟippuri yākum  (tol. 1042)
xviii. paḷḷamuṅ kuḻiyum
tōṇṭiya iṭam paḷḷamuṅ kuḻiyumākum. avi iyaṟkiyum ceyaṟkiyum eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. paḷḷattiṉum āḻntatu kuḻi.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuḻivu. kuḷ - kuṇṭu. kuṇṭuṅ kuḻiyum eṉpatu vaḻakku. kuḻi - kuḻici - kuḻinta pāṉi.
kuḷ - kuṭṭu - kuṭṭam = paḷḷam, āḻam.
kuḷ - (kaḷ) = (kay) - kayam = paḷḷam.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kīḷ - kīḻ = paḷḷam. kīḻ - (kiḻaṅku) - kiṭaṅku.
cuḻittal = kaṇ kuḻital.
nuḷ - noḷ - noḷḷal = kaṭkuḻivu. noḷ - noḷḷi.
noḷ - neḷ - ñeḷ - ñeḷḷal = paḷḷam.
noḷ - noṭi = paḷḷam.
puḷ - paḷ - paḷḷam. paḷ - paḷḷaṉ = paḷḷamāṉa maruta nilattil vāḻpavaṉ. uḻavaṉ.
paḷ - (pay) - payam - payampu = paḷḷam.
paḷ - paṇ - paṇṇi = paḷḷam, kuḻi, neṟkuttum paḷḷam, vayal.
paḷ - paṭu - paṭukar = paḷḷam, paḷḷamāṉa vayal, maruta nilam.
puḷ - poḷ - poy = kuḻi.
muḷ - maḷ - maṭu = paḷḷam.
maḷ - maḷḷaṉ = paḷḷamāṉa marutanilattil vāḻpavaṉ, uḻavaṉ.
paḷḷaṉ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakkum maḷḷaṉ eṉpatu ceyyuḷ vaḻakkumākum.
maḷḷa ruḻupaka ṭurappuvār  (kamparā. nāṭ. 18).
xix. nīrnili
paḷḷamāṉa iṭattil nīr taṅkuvataṉāl, paḷḷattik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ cila, nīrniliyik kuṟittaṉa. nīr niliyik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ cila, nīriyuṅ kuṟikkum.
kuḷ - kuḷam.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = ciṟu kuṭṭi.
kuḷ - kuṭṭu - kuṭṭi = ciṟu kuḷam.
kuḷ - (kaḷ) - (kay) - kayam = kuḷam.
tuḷ - toḷ - toḻuvi = maṭu.
toḷ - tōḷ - tōy - tōyam = nīr, kaṭal.
puḷ - poḷ - poy - poyki = kuḷam.
puḷ - paḷ - pāḻi = ciṟu kuḷam.
paḷ - paṭu = nīrnili.
paṭu - paṭuki = nīrnili, āṟṟōrattu nilam.
paṭuki - paṭukar = nīrnili.
paḷ(pal) - payam = nīr; payam - payampu = nīr, nīrnili.
paḷ - paṇ = nīrnili. paṇ - pāṇi = nīr.
muḷ - maḷ - maṭu = nīrkkiṭaṅku.
xx. tōṇṭappaṭṭa nīrnili
kuḷ - kiḷ - kiṇaṟu. kiḷ - keḷ - kēḷ - kēṇi.
tuḷ - tura - turavu = kiṇaṟu.
xxi. kīḻmi
paḷḷam nilamaṭṭattiṟkuk kīḻāyiruppatāl, paḷḷattik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟum collaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum kīḻmiyik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. kīḻmi nilam (iṭam) paṟṟiyatum niliyip paṟṟiyatum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. nilam paṟṟiyatum, nilattiṟku uṭpaṭṭatum mēṟpaṭṭatum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kīḻ - kīḻmi.
kīḻ - kiḻakku = kīḻ, kīḻiṭam, iḻivu.
kāṇiṟ kiḻakkān tali  (kuṟaḷ. 488)
kiḷiiya kuralē kiḻakkuvīḻn taṉavē  (kuṟun. 337)
kiḻakkiṭu poruḷō ṭintu mākum  (tol. 1226)
kuḷ - (kaḷ) - (kay) - kayam - kayami = kīḻmi.
kīḻ eṉpatu mutalāvatu oṉṟaṉ uṭpakutiyiyē kuṟittatu. nilattiṟ kuḷḷiruppatu nilattiṉ mēluḷḷa makkaṭku aṭippuṟat tiliruppatāl, kīḻ eṉṉuñ col piṉpu nilattiṟku mēluḷḷa veḷiyiṭattilum aṭitticiyik kuṟippatā yiṟṟu.
xxii. tāḻtal
tāḻvu eṉpatu kīḻnili.
uḷ - (iḷ) - (iḻu) - iru. irutal = tāḻtal. tāḻntu viḻutal.
ikuttal = tāḻtal.
māriyi ṉikutaru villumiḻ kaṭuṅkaṇi  (malipaṭu. 226)
kaṇaṅko ṭōkiyiṟ katuppikut tacii  (malipaṭu. 44)
kuḷ - kuṇ - kuṇam = tāḻnta kīḻttici. kuṇam - kuṇakku = kiḻakku.
kuḷ - kiḷ - kīḻ = kiḻakku. kīḻ - kīḻkku - kiḻakku.
tuḷ - (taḷ) - taḻu - tāḻ - tāḻvu; tāḻ - tāḻmi.
tāḻ - tāḻppu = tāḻttal, kālan tāḻttal, tāḻ - tāḻam.
taḻu - taku - takku = tāḻvu. tāḻkural toṇṭiyit takkut toṇṭi eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
takku - takkaṇam = tāḻnta teṉ tici.
teṟkilirunta kumari maliyum nilamum muḻuki, vaṭakkil kaṭalirunta iṭattil paṉimali yeḻuntatāl, teṉtici tāḻntu vaṭatici uyarntatu. itaṉāl, teṉtici takkaṇam eṉṟum vaṭatici uttaram eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. takkaṇam tāḻvu; uttaram uyarvu. tamiḻakap parappiṉpaṭi, nilamaṭṭattil tāḻntatum uyarntatumāṉa kīḻttici mēṟṟicikaḷum, muṟiyē, kīḻmēl allatu kiḻakku mēṟku eṉṟum, kuṇam kuṭam allatu kuṇakku kuṭakku eṉṟum kūṟuppaṭutal kāṇka.
taṉ - taṇ - taṇa. taṇattal = tāḻttal, kālantāḻttal.
taḷ - taṇ - taṇi - taṇivu. tāḻttal, kālan tāḻttal.
puḷ - paḷ - paḷḷi - kīḻmaṭṭamāṉa (kuṭṭiyāṉa) āṭu.
paḷ - paṭu - paṭi = kīḻmaṭṭam.
paṭi - pāṭi = kīḻmaṭṭamāṉa (tāḻvāṉa) kaṭṭil.

paṭu - pāṭu - pāṭi = tāḻvāṉa kūriyuḷḷa kuṭiyiruppu allatu pācaṟi.
paḷ - paṇ - pāṇi. pāṇittal = tāḻttal.
xxiii. taṭpam
kuḷircci cūṭṭit taṇippatāl taṇittaṟ karuttil kuḷirccik karuttup piṟantatu. veppa nāṭṭil cūṭu taṇivu mika mutaṉmi yākak karutappaṭum.
tuḷ - taḷ - taṇ - taṇam, taṇ - taṇmi.
taṇ - taṭpu - taṭpam.
taṇ - taṭu = taṭumam = kuḷircci, nīrkkōvi.
xxiv. kīḻātal
kīḻātalāvatu aṭitticiyaṭital. viḻutalum, paṭuttalum, nilattiṟ kiṭattalum, kīḻātalām.
uḷ - (iḷ) - iru. iruttal = kīḻamartal, amartal, (iḷ) - iṭu. iṭutal = kīḻiṭutal, koṭuttal. iṭu - iṭam.
ul - (il) - (īl) - īṉ - īṉutal = kīḻiṭutal, peṟutal. 
maṇaṟkīṉṟa veṇmuttam
īṉ - ī. ītal = iṭutal.
kuḷ - (kiḷ) - kiṭa - kiṭakki. kiṭattal = nilattiliruttal, paṭuttiruttal.
puḷ - paḷ - paḷḷi - paṭukki, paṭukkiyaṟi, paṭukkum vīṭu, vīṭu, kōyil, iṭam, vīṭukaḷ cērnta ūr.
paḷḷi koḷḷutal = paṭuttal. paḷḷiyeḻucci = paṭukki viṭṭeḻun tiruttal. makkaḷ perumpālum vīṭṭiṟku veḷiyē vēli ceytalālum, iraviṟ paṭuttaṟkē vīṭṭit tēṭutalālum, paṭukkiyik kuṟikkum paḷḷiyeṉṉum col, vīṭṭiyum pēriṉpa vīṭākiya kōyiliyum, aṭiyār paḷḷikoḷḷum maṭattiyum, vīṭukaḷ cērnta ūriyum, kuṟitta teṉṟaṟika.
paḷ - paṭu. paṭuttal= kīḻātal, viḻutal, kiṭattal, tūṅkutal, paṭutal = viḻutal, pōrkkaḷattil viḻuntiṟattal, iṟattal.
paṭu - paṭukki.
paṭu - paṭi. paṭital = viḻutal.
niṉṟāṉ iruntāṉ kiṭantāṉ taṉ kēḷalaṟac 
ceṉṟāṉ eṉappaṭuta lāl

eṉṉum nālaṭic ceyyuḷāl, iruttal eṉpatu kīḻamar talik kuṟittal peṟappaṭum.
xxv. iṟaṅkutal
iṟaṅkutalāvatu, mēṟṟicip poruḷ kīḻtticiyaṭital. av iṟakkam uḷḷiṭattatum veḷiyiṭattatum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. muṉ kālil tippatu uḷḷiṟakkamum, paṟavi viṇṇiliruntu maṇṇiṟku varuvatu veḷiyiṟakkamum ākum.
uḷ - iḷ - iḷi - iḻi - iḻivu. iḻital = iṟaṅkutal.
iḷ - (iṟa) - iṟaṅku - iṟakku - iṟakkam.
kum - kumuṅku. kumuṅkutal = iṟaṅkutal.
puḷ - puḻu - puku. pukutal = iṟaṅkutal.
iḷi, iḻi, iṟa, iṟaṅku mutaliya coṟkaḷ ukara mutalavāy mūlavaṭi viliruppiṉ vēṟu poruṉpaṭumātaliṉ, ammayakkatti nīkkutaṟkē ikara mutalavāyiṉa veṉṟaṟika. collākka neṟimuṟikaḷuḷ mutaṉmiyāṉavaṟṟuḷ immayakkoḻippum oṉṟākum.
xxvi. paḻittal
paḻittalāvatu, matippil iṟakkutal. oruvarip paḻittal, mēliruntu kīḻum mēṭṭiliruntu paḷḷattiṟkum iṟakkutal pōṉṟatām.

tuḷ - (taḷ) - taḻu - tāḻ - tāḻcci.
puḷ - paḷ - paḻi - paḻippu.
paḻi - (vaḻi) - (vayi) - (vay) - vi.
vay - vayavu - vacavu. vay - vayi - vaci.
xxvii. patital
oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟiṟ paṭitalē patitalām.
paṭital = viḻutal, patital, uṟuttutal = patittal.
paṭi - pati - pativu. pati - patippu.
patital = paṭital, taṅkutal, kuṭiyiruttal.
ōriṭattil taṅkiyiruttalip pativāyiruttal eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
pati = pativāyirukkum iṭam, vīṭu, ūr, nakar.
pati - vati - vaci - vācam. (va).
vatital = taṅkutal, kuṭiyiruttal.
pati = pātam = nilattiṟ patiyum uṟuppu allatu pākam. patiyum pātam pati pāta mūlam pati pātamūlap paṟṟuṭiyāṉ eṉṟu iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭi kuṟittu varum kalveṭṭut toṭarkaḷi nōkkuka.
pātam - pāti = pātam paṭṭu uṇṭākum vaḻi.
xxviii. paṇital

xxix. puḻi
puḻi yeṉpatu pakkavāṭṭilum oru poruḷilumuḷḷa āḻnta paḷḷam.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuṭi = puṭi, kaviki. vilāp puṭiyi vilākkuṭi eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
viritta kaviki puṭiyuḷḷatāyiruppatāl, kaviki kuṭi yeṉṉappaṭṭatu. kuṭi - kūṭi = kuṭiyuḷḷa oli nārp peṭṭi.

xxx. paḻimi

kīḻ = muṟkālam

xxxi. paḻakutal

iv. patar (uḷḷīṭaṟṟatu)

muḷ - mōḷ. mōḷutal = ciṟunīr viṭutal.
mōḷ - mōṭṭiram - mōttiram - mūttiram.
vaṭamoḻiyil mōḷ eṉṉum pakuti allatu viṉi illi. mūttiram eṉṉum viṉippeyarē uḷḷatu. viṉip pakuti yiṉiṉṟu viṉippeyar amiyumēyaṉṟi, viṉippeyariṉiṉṟu viṉippakuti amiyātu.

(3) taḷartal tuṟi
oruporuḷ tuḷi viḻuvatāl kaṭṭuviṭṭut taḷarcciyaṭi kiṉṟatu.
i. toḷatoḷattal
toḷatoḷattalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟuḷ iṟukap poruntātavāṟu tuḷiyiruttal.
tuḷ - (taḷ) -taḷar - taḷarcci. taḷartal= toḷa toḷattal.
taḷ - taḷa - taḷatti = taḷarcci.
tuḷ - toḷ - toḷa - toḷatti = taḷarcci.
toḷa - toḷatoḷa - toḷatoḷappu = taḷarcci.
toḷ - toy. toytal = taḷartal.
ii. kuḻital
tuḷiviḻunta poruḷpōl cuṭṭuviṭutal kuḻital.
poruṭ kuḻivum maṉakkuḻivum eṉak kuḻivu iruvaki.
uḷ - uḷi = kuḻinta cēṟṟu nilam, cēṟu.
uḷ - ūḻ - ūḻal = taḷarcci, taḷarntacati.
uḷ - aḷ - aḷḷal = cēṟu.
aḷ - aḷaṟu = cēṟu, āḻnta cēṟu pōṉṟa narakam.
aḷ - aḷḷi = veṇṇey.
aḷ - aḷi = cēṟu. aḷital = kuḻital, kuḷukuḷuttal, aṟakkaṉital.
uḷ - iḷ = iḻutu = ney.
kum - kumi - kumital = kuḻital, kuḻiya vēkutal.
kuḷ - kuḷu - kuḷukuḷu. kuḷ - kuḻi - kuḻivu.
kuḷ - kūḻ = kuḻinta uṇavu, uṇavu.
kuḷ - koḷ - koḷa - koḷakoḷa - koḷa koḷappu.
cuḷ - coḷ - coḷu. coḷuttal = cēṟātal. cōṟu kuḻital.
cuḷ - caḷ - caḷḷal = cēṟu.
caḷ - caḻu - caḻuṅku - caḻukkam = nekiḻcci.
tuḷ - taḷa - taḷar - taḷarcci.
tuḷ - toḷ - toḷku = cēṟu. toḷḷutal = nekiḻtal.
toḷ - toḷḷam = cēṟu. toḷ - toḷḷi = cēṟu. toḷḷi - toḷi = cēṟu.
toḷ - toy. toytal = taḷartal. toy - toyyal = cēṟu. toy - toyyil = kuḻampu.
nuḷ - noḷ - noḷa - noḷanoḷa. noḷa noḷattal = nekiḻtal.
noḷ - noḷu - noḷunoḷu. noḷunoḷuttal = nekiḻtal, kuḻital.
noḷ - noḷil = cēṟu.
noḷ - neḷ - neḷu - neḷuneḷu. neḷuneḷuttal = nekiḻtal, kuḻital.
neḷ - ney. neḷu - neku - nekiḻ.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷapoḷa. poḷapoḷattal = nekiḻtal, oḻukutal.
muḷ - (moḷ) - moḻu - moḻumoḻu. moḻu moḻuttal = cati taḷartal.
moḻu - moḻuku - meḻuku = nekiḻnta neyp poruḷ.

iii. meṉmiyātal
kuḻinta poruḷ meṉmiyākum.
uḷ - (iḷ) - iḷa. iḷattal = meṉmiyātal.
iḷa - iḷantāri. iḷa - iḷami - iḷimi.
mul - mel - meṉmi.
mellutal = pallāṟ kaṭittarittu meṉmi yākkutal.
mel - meli - melivu. melital = meṉmiyātal.
mel - mella = metuvāka.
mel - meḷ - meḷḷa = metuvāka. meḷ - meḷḷam.
mel - melu - metu - metuvu - metuku.
metu - mettu = metti = mellaṇi. metteṉal = meṉmiyā yiruttal.
iv. iḷakutal
iḷakutalāvatu maṉamum poruḷum mikak kuḻital.
ur - uru - uruku - urukku - urukkam.
ur - ar - ara - araṅku. araṅkutal = urukutal.
uḷ - aḷ - aḷi = aṉpu, aruḷ, koṭi.
aḷittal = aruḷutal, koṭuttal.
aḷ - aruḷ.
uḷ - (iḷ) - iḷa - iḷaku - iḷakku - iḷakkam.
iḷa - iḷakkari - iḷakkāram.
iḷa - ira - iraṅku - irakkam.
kuḷ - kaḷ - kari. karital = iḷakutal, nīrātal.
cuḷ - caḷ. caḷḷutal = iḷakutal.
nuḷ - neḷ - (neḷu) neku. nekutal = iḷakutal.
v. cōrtal
kuḻinta poruḷ cōrum. poruṭ cōrvum maṉaccōrvum eṉac cōrvu iruvaki.
cuḷ - coḷ = vaṭiyum vāynīr. coḷ - cōḷ - cōr.
coḷ - coḷu - coḷucoḷu. coḷuttal = kuḻintuvaṭital.
cuḷ - caḷ - caḻa - caḻaṅku, caḻaṅkutal = cōrtal.
caḻaṅku - caḻakku - taḷarcci.
caḷ - caṉi. caḷittal = taḷartal, cōrtal.
caḷ - (cāḷ) - cāḷi = vaṭiyum vāynīr.
tuḷ - taḷ - taḷar - taḷarcci.
tuḷ - toḷ - toy. toytal = taḷartal.
puḷ - poḷ - poḷa. poḷapoḷattal = vaṭital, oḻukutal.
vi. kulital
kaṭṭuviṭṭa poruḷ kuliyum.
uli - uli. ulital = cōṟu keṭutal.
uḷ - uḷaṟu - uḻaṟu. uḻaṟutal = nāttaḷartal.
uḷ - (uḷu) - uku. ukutal = keṭutal. ukkal = pataṉaḻital.
uku - ukam = aḻivu, kēṭu, ūḻi.
uḷ - ūḷ - ūḷi = keṭṭa ney.
ūḷ - ūḻ. ūḻttal = pataṉaḻital, keṭutal.
ūḻ - ūḻal = kēṭupāṭu, keṭṭatu.
ūḻ - ūḻi = kēṭu, aḻivu, oraḻiviṟkum iṉṉō raḻiviṟkum iṭippaṭṭa kālam.
ūḻ - ūcu. ūcutal = uṇavu keṭutal.
ūḻ muṭṭi - ūmuṭṭi = keṭṭa muṭṭi.
kul - kuli. kulital = keṭutal.
kuḷ - kuḷaṟu - kuḻaṟu. kuḻaṟutal = nāttaḷartal.
tul - tol - toli. tolital = taḷartal. keṭutal.
tuḷ - taḷ - taḷar.
nuḷ - noḷ - noḷa - noca. nocanocattal = cōṟu keṭutal.
noḷ - no. nontupōtal = uṇavu keṭutal.
vii. noyyatātal
kulinta poruḷ noyyatākum. noymmi eṉpatu valiyiṉmi, kaṉamiṉmi, ciṟumi mutaliyavaṟṟit taḻuvum.
ul - (il) - ilavu = noyya pañcu. ilavu - ilavam. ilavu - ilaku.
il - ili = noyya ili vaki.
uḷ - eḷ - eṇmi. eḷ - eḷi - eḷimi.
eḷ - ēḷ - ēṭṭi = eḷimi.
ēḷ - ēḻ - ēḻami. ēḻ - ēḻi.

uḷ - iḷ - iḷi - iḷippu - eḷimi.
iḷ - iḷa - iḷappu - iḷappam.
cuḷ - cuḷu - cuḷuvu.
nuḷ - noḷ - noy - noyppu - noyppam = ilēcu, eḷimi, ciṟumi.
noyyavaṉ = ciṟiyavaṉ.
viii. uṭal taḷartal
ul - ol - olku. olkutal = taḷartal.
olku - oṟku - oṟkam.
uḷ - oḷ - (oḷ) - oy. oytal = kikāl taḷartal.
uḷ - eḷ - ey - eyppu = taḷarnta mutumi.
uḷ - iḷ - iḷa. iḷattal = uṭal meṉmiyātal.
iḷ - iḷi - iḷippu = eyppu.
kutu - kutal - kutali = taḷarcci.
cuḷ - coḷ - cōr.
tuḷ - toḷ - toḷḷāṭu - taḷḷāṭu.
tuḷ - taḷ - taḷar.
toḷ - toy. toytal = cōrtal.
nuḷ - noḷ - noḷku. noḷkutal = iḷittal.
ix. nōtal
uṭalum uḷḷamum taḷartalāl nōvuṇṭām.
uḷ - uḷi. uḷital = nōtal. uḷi - uḷiccal.
uḷ - iḷ - ira - iraṅku - iraṅkal = varuntutal.

tul - tol - toli. tolital = varuntutal.
nuḷ - noḷ - noy - noyvu = maṉavaruttam.
noy - no = varuntu (ēvalorumi).
no - novvu = nōvu. novvutal = varuntutal.
no - nontali = valiyiṉmi (palavīṉam).
noḷ - noḷḷā - noḷḷāppu = varuttam.
noḷ - nōḷ - nōḷi = piṇiyuṇṭa nili.
nōḷ - nōy - nō - nōvu.
x. melital
uḷ - iḷ - iḷi. iḷittal = melital.
cuḷ - cuḷḷal = melivu. cuḷḷalaṉ = melintavaṉ.

nuḷ - noḷ - nōḷ - nōy - nōyntāṉ - nōyñcāṉ = melintavaṉ. nōytal = melital.
muḷ - mel - meli - melivu.
xi. tuṉpam
uṭalum uḷḷamum nōkakkūṭiya nili tuṉpam.
kum - kumi = tuṉpam, aḻivu.
tul - tuṉ - tuṉpu. tul - tol - tolli = tuṉpam.
tuḷ - (tuy) - tuyar - tuyaram.
tuḷ - toḷ - toy - toyyal = tuṉpam.
toy - to - tontaravu, tontari = tuṉpam.
tontarittal = tuṉpuṟuttutal.
nuḷ - noḷ - noy - no - nompu - nompalam = tuṉpam.
xii. nilitaḷartal (vaṟumiyaṭital)
ul - ol - olku - oṟku - oṟkam = vaṟumi.
olkutal = vaṟumiyaṭital.
uḷ - eḷ - eṇmi. eḷ - eḷi - eḷimi = vaṟumi.
eḷ - ēḷ - ēṭṭi = vaṟumi.
ēḷ - ēḻ - ēḻami. ēḻ = ēḻi.

nuḷ - noḷ - noṭi. noṭittal = taḷartal. keṭutal.
noy - no - nontaki = vaṟumi. no - nontali = vaṟumi.
mul - mel - meli. melital = vaṟumiyaṭital.
xiii. tōṟṟal
meṉmiyāl allatu taḷarcciyāl tōlviyuṇṭām.
tul - tol - toli. tolital = taḷartal, tōṟṟal.
toli - tolivu = tōlvi. tol - tōl - tōlvi.
mul - mel - meli - melivu = tōlvi.
meliveṉpatu muṇarntēṉ  (kampa. mutaṟpō. 181).

mel - vel. vellutal = melittal, tōṟkaṭittal.
vel - veṉ = veṟṟi, vel - vēl = veṟṟi.
vel - veṟṟi.
metu - mettu. mettutal = melittal, vellutal.

(4) nīḷal tuṟi
nekiḻum poruḷkaḷ nīḷutalāl, nekiḻccik karuttil nīṭcik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. ceṅkuttu paṭukki eṉṉum iruvaki vākilum nīṭci nikaḻum.
i. nīḷutal
nekiḻ - nīḷ - nīḷam. nīḷ - nīṭci.
nīḷ - nīr = nekiḻum (nīḷum) poruḷ.
nīr - nīl = nīlam = kaṭal nīriṉ niṟam.
nīḷ - nīṭṭu = nīṭṭappaṭum oli.
nīṭṭu - nīṭṭam.
nīḷ - nīṭu.
nīḷ - neḷ - neṭu - neṭṭu - neṭṭi.
neṭu - neṭil, neṭṭu - neṭṭam.
neṭṭu = neṭumi, neṭuntūram, neṭṭam = neṭumi, ceṅkuttu.
nīṭci eṉpatu ceṅkuttu vākilum koḷḷappaṭutal.
uruvattāl nīṇṭa uyarmara mellām
paruvattā laṉṟip paḻā
eṉṉum auviyār kūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
keṭṭu - naṭṭu - naṭṭam;
naṭṭamāy niṟkiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkik kāṇka.
ii. niṟṟal
niṟṟalāvatu oru poruḷ ceṅkuttāka nīṇṭiruttal.
nīḷ - (niḷ) - nil.
nil - nili - niliyam.
nili - nilippu = nilivaram.
nil - nilavu. nilavutal = nilittal.
nil - nilu - niluvi.

iii. niṟuttal
niṟpittalum tuliyi eṭuttu eṭiyaṟitalum ceṅkuttāka ūṉṟutalum, niṟuttal.
nil - niṟpu - niṟpāṭṭu. nil - nilu - niluttu. nilu - niṟu - niṟuttu - niṟuttam.
niṟu - niṟi. niṟu - niṟuvi.
niṟu - niṟuvu - niṟuvaṉam (tāpaṉam)
neṭu - naṭu. neṭu - neṭṭu - naṭṭu - nāṭṭu.
(5) pukutal tuṟi
pukutalāvatu tuḷikkuṭ cellutal kūrmiyāṉa allatu kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷ oṉṟaṟkuḷ putitāyt tuḷittuc cellutalum pukutalē.
i. uṭpukutal
uḷ - oḷi - (oḷ) - oṭṭu - oṭṭutal = oṉṟit tuḷikkuṭ pukuttutal.
tuḷ - turu - tūr. tūrtal = pukutal.
pukutalit tūrtal eṉpatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku. 
tūr - tūri = mīṉ pukum poṟi.
nuḷ - nuḻu - nuḻuntu - noḻuntu. noḻuntutal = taliyi uḷ nuḻittal.
nuḻu - nuḻi.
puḷ - puḻu - puku - pukuti = maṉivāyil, vaḻi, varuvāy. puku - pukutu.
pukuti - pukuṭi = vāyil.
puku - pukur. pukurtal = pukutal. pukur - pūr. pūrtal = pukutal.
puku - pō - pōku - pōkku. pō - pōtu. 
muḷ - (muḻu) - muḻi.
ii. uṭkoḷḷutal
uṭkoḷḷutalāvatu vāykkuṭ pukuttutal allatu uṇṇutal.
uḷ - uṇ - uṇa - uṇā - uṇavu.
uṇ - ūṇ - ūṭṭu - ūṭṭam.
ūṭṭu - ūṭṭi = ūṭṭappaṭum paṉṟi.
uḷ - uṟi - uṟiñcu (olikkuṟippōṭu kūṭiyatu) uṟiñcutal = nīri uḷḷiḻuttal.
kuḷ - koḷ - koṇṭi = uṇavu.
cumpu - cūmpu - cūppu (olikkuṟippōṭu kūṭiyatu) cūpputal = cāratti allatu catti uḷḷiḻuttal.
tul - tuṉ - tuṟṟu = uṇavu. tuṟṟutal = uṇṇutal.
tuṟṟu - tuṟṟi = uṇavu.
tuṉ - tiṉ - tīṉ - tīṉi.
tiṉ - tiṉṟi - tiṟṟi = uṇavu.
tīṉ - tīṟṟu. tīṟṟutal = ūṭṭutal.
tuḷ - tuy. tuyttal = uṇṇutal, nukartal.
tum - tu. tuttal = uṇṇutal. tu = uṇavu.
tu - tuppu = uṇavu.
nuḷ - nuḻu - (nuku) - nukar. nukartal = uṇṇutal tuyttal (aṉupavittal).
nuḷ - noḷ - noṇṭu - noṇṭal = nukarki.
puḷ - puḻu - puku - pukā = cōṟu. pukā - pukavu = uṇavu.
puḷ - puci - poci. pocittal = uṇṇutal.
muḷ - muṭu - maṭu. maṭuttal = vāyiliṭal, uṇṇutal.
maṭu - maṭi = uṇavu, cōṟu.
muḷ - (muci) - moci. mocittal = tiṉṉutal.
iii. moḷḷutal
moḷḷutalāvatu kalatti nīruṭpukutti nīr koḷḷutal.
kul - kōl. kōlutal = moḷḷutal. kōl - (kōlaki) - kōraki = akappi, kōraki - kōrikki.
kuḷ - koḷ. koḷḷutal = mukattal.
koḷ - kōḷ - kōy = kaḷmukakkuṅ kalam.
nuḷ - noḷ. noḷḷutal = mukattal.
muḷ - muḻu - (muku) - muka. mukattal = moḷḷutal, moṇṭa ḷattal, muka - mukavi.
mul - (muḻi) - mūḻi - akappi.
muḷ - moḷ. moḷ - moṇṭi - monti = kaḷ mukakkuṅ kalam, monti pōṉṟa tōṟkaruvi. (ici).
moṇṭi - maṇṭi = nīrmukakkuṅ kalam, irappōr kalam, atupōṉṟa taliyōṭu, taliyiṉ mēṟpakuti, maṇṭi pōṉṟa tōṟkaruvi (ici).

iv. muḻukutal
muḻukutalāvatu oru poruḷ, iṉṉoṉṟiṟkuḷ pukuntu amiḻtal.
um - am - amiḻ - āḻ. āḻ - āḻam. āḻ - āḻi. 
kuḷ - kuḷi. kuḷittal = uṭpukutal, muḻukutal, nīruṭ pukutal.
koṭiyāṉ kūrṅkaṇi kuḷippa  (pu.ve. 10:10, koḷu).
eṅku marumat tiṭik kuḷippa  (pu.ve. 7:23).
kaṭaṟpaṭi kuḷippa maṇṭi  (puṟam. 6).
muttukkuḷittal = muttukkaḷi nīruḷ mūḻkiyeṭuttal.
muḷ - (maḷ) - maṇ. maṇṇutal = kuḷittal, muḻukutal.
mutūr vāyiṟ paṉikkaya maṇṇi  (puṟam. 79)
maṇṇumaṅkalam = aracaṉ nīrāṭṭu viḻā.
muḷ - muḻu - muḻuku - muḻukku. muḻuku - mūḻku.
KGF - (KG§F) - K§F (j.É.); muḻukku - mukku - (pi.vi.)
v. viḻuṅkutal
viḻuṅkutalāvatu, ōr uṇavupporuḷi muḻukkutal pōl tiṭumeṉa vāyvaḻi uṭpukuttutal.
kuḷ - kuṭi.
nuḷ - noḷ. noḷḷutal = viḻuṅkutal.
nuḷ - nuḻu - (nuḻuṅku) - nuṅku, nuṅkutal = viḻuṅkutal.
nuṅku - noṅku - nokku. noṅkutal = viḻuṅkutal. nokkutal - uṇṭu kuṟiyac ceytal.
puḷ - paḷ - (paru) - paruku.
muḷ - muḻu - muḻuṅku - viḻuṅku.
muḻuṅku - (muḻukku) - muṭukku = orumuṟi viḻuṅkum nīr aḷavu. muṭukku - maṭakku.
viḻuṅku - (viḻukku) - viṭukku = maṭakku.
muḻukku - mukku. mukkutal = oṉṟi vāynīruḷ muḻukkit tiṉṉutal.
avali mukkittiṉ, eḷḷi nakkittiṉ
eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
pācavaṉ mukkit taṇpuṉaṟ pāyum  (puṟam. 63).
mukku - mokku. mokkutal = vāynīruḷ nirampa muḻukkit tiṉṉutal.
vi. oḷittal
oḷittalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟuḷ pukuntu maṟital.
uḷ - oḷ - oḷi.
kuḷ - kuḷi. kuḷittal = maṟital.
yāṉaṟital añcik kuḷittu  (kalit. 98)
puḷ - puy. puytal = maṟital.
kōlap pakaṟkaḷiṟoṉṟukaṟ puyya (tiv. iyaṟ. tiruvirut. 40)
puḷ - paḷ - paṭu - (patu) - patuṅku - patukku - patukkam.

(6) turuval tuṟi
turuval
turuvutalāvatu oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟuṭ pukuntu ūṭuruvic cellutal.
uḷ - uru - uruvu.
uḷ - (uṭu) - ūṭu - ūṭi = ūṭucellum iḻi.
kuḷ - (kuru) - kōr - kō. kōrvi - kōvi. kōrttal = nūlit tuḷiyiṉ ūṭu uruvac ceytal.
tuḷ - tuḷi. tuḷittal = turuvutal.
tuḷ - turu - turuvu.
mul - (mūl) - mūlam = vāyil, ūṭu.
ōr aṭippiṭattiṉ ūṭu ceṉṟu kaṭattal eṅṅaṉam turuvutal eṉappaṭumō, aṅṅaṉamē oru tiṟappiṭattiṉ ūṭuceṉṟu kaṭattalum turuvutal eṉappaṭum. oru tuḷiyūṭu cellutalum oru nāṭṭūṭu cellutalum turuvutal eṉappaṭutal kāṇka.
ūkārac cuṭṭuk karuttu vaḷarcci
ūkāraccuṭṭu, muṟkūṟiyapaṭi mutalāvatu muṉmiyik kuṟikkum. muṉmiyeṉpatu, kālamuṉ, iṭamuṉ, muṉṉili, muṉṉuṟuppu, muṟpakuti, muṉi (nuṉi) mutaliya pala karuttukkaḷit taḻuvum.
tōṉṟaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu muṟpaṭaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. oru marattil tōṉṟiya taḷir muṉṉāl nīṇṭu vaḷarvatum, ōr uṭampil tōṉṟiya taḷir muṉṉāl nīṇṭu vaḷarvatum, ōr uṭampil tōṉṟiya uṟuppum koppuḷamum muṉṉukkut taḷḷiyum puṭittum varuvatum, ivi pōlvaṉa piṟavum, muṟpaṭalākum.

muṟpaṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu muṟcelavuk karuttup piṟakkum. iṭampeyarum irutiṇiyuyirikaḷum ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu iṉṉōriṭattiṟkuc celvatellām muṟcelavē. piṟcela vellām, perumpālum, uyirikaḷ ciṟitu vaḷarcciyuṟṟa piṉpum kuṟuntoliviṟkum vēṇṭumeṉṟē nikaḻttap peṟuvatāl, iyaṟkiyāṉa celavellām potuvāka muṟcelaveṉṟēyaṟika. kīḻiruntu mēṟcelvatum oruvaki muṟcelavātaliṉ, muṟcelavu eṉpatu eḻutal allatu uyartaliyun taḻuvum.
muṟcelavuk karuttiṉiṉṟu neruṅkaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. oru marakkiḷi nīṇṭu vaḷarntu iṉṉoru kiḷiyi aṭuppatum, irutiṇiyiyaṅkuyirikaḷum muṉ ceṉṟu tāntām virumpum iṭattiyum poruḷiyuṅ kiṭṭuvatum, neruṅkalākum.
neruṅkaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu toṭutaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. oru poruḷ etticiyilāyiṉum mēṉmēlum ceṉṟu koṇṭē yiruppiṉ, ētēṉumoru poruḷit toṭṭē tīral vēṇṭum. muṉṉōkkic cellum uyirikaḷum tām virumpiya poruḷiyum iṭattiyum toṭṭaṭiyum. toṭutal eṉpatu, melitāyt toṭutal valitāyt toṭutal eṉa irupāṟpaṭṭu, tīṇṭutal, taḻuvutal, muṭṭutal, kuttutal, uṟittal mutaliya pala karuttukkaḷit taḻuvum.
toṭutaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu kūṭaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. kūṭal eṉpatu, oṉṟiya kūṭal, oṉṟāk kūṭal eṉa iru vaki. maṇṇoṭu maṇṇum nīroṭu nīruṅ kūṭuvatu oṉṟiya kūṭal; uṭaloṭu uṭalum kūlattōṭu kūlamuṅ kūṭuvatu oṉṟāk kūṭal. oṉṟāk kūṭal mīṇṭum toṭṭuk kūṭal toṭātu kūṭal eṉa iru tiṟattatu.
kūṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu vaḷitaṟ karuttup piṟakkum. oṉṟāk kūṭaliṉ cila poruḷkaḷ vaḷivatuṇṭu. oṉṟiyoṉṟu muṭṭiya iruporuḷkaḷ oṉṟātaviyāyiṉ, avaṟṟuḷ meliyatu kampipōl nīṇṭiruppiṉ vaḷiyum. kallimuṭṭiya vērum kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷi muṭṭiya āṇiyum vaḷital kāṇka. vaḻiccelvōṉ teruvaṭitta cuvari muṭṭit tirumpuvatum, vēṟṟiṭañ ceṉṟavaṉ viṉimuṟṟi mīḷvatum, aṇivakiyil vaḷitaliṉ pāṟpaṭum. vaḷital eṉpatu, cāytal, kōṇal, tirukal vaṭṭam, cuṟṟal, cūḻtal, uruṭci cuḻaṟci mutaliya pala karuttukkaḷit taḻuvum.
kūṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum maṟṟoru karuttu tuḷittaṟ karuttākum. muṭṭiya piṉ vaḷiyāta valiya poruḷ, tāṉ muṭṭiyatit tuḷittum cellum. āṇi caṭṭattiyum, vaṇṭu marattiyum, pūcci puttakattiyum. māntaṉ maliyiyum tuḷikkalām tuḷittal eṉpatu, kuḻittal, tōṇṭutal, tuḷiyiṭutal, pukutal, turuvutal ākiya pala karuttukkaḷit taḻuvum. tuḷittup pukutalum, tuḷikkuṭ pukutalum eṉap pukutal iru vakittu. uyirikaḷ tattam uṟiviṭattiṟkuṭ pukutal tuḷikkuṭ pukutalākum. oṉṟit turuviya poruḷ iṟutiyil veḷippaṭum. atu tōṉṟal allatu muṉvarutal pōṉṟatākum. ataṉpiṉ nikaḻakkūṭiyavi. tōṉṟal mutal turuval vari kūṟiyuḷḷa pala nikaḻccikaḷē. ivi oru poru ḷiṉ kālamellām mīṇṭum mīṇṭum nikaḻntu koṇṭē yirukkumātaliṉ, ūkārac cuṭṭuk karuttu vaḷarcci muṟṟuṟum elli turuvaṟkaruttē. ākavē, tōṉṟal mutal turuvalvari yuḷḷa karuttukkaḷellām oru cuḻal cakkaramātal kāṇka.
ūkārac cuṭṭāl muṟiyē uṇarttappeṟuṅ karuttukkaḷil, mutaṉmiyāṉaviyē iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa.  (mu.tā.)
ūṅku
ū - ūṅku = muṉ.
ūṅkutal = muṉ cellutal
ūkkutal = muṟceluttutal, uḷḷatti viṉiyil muṉ cellat tūṇṭutal

ūci
ūci - va. cūci (i. vē.) su#ci. 
uḷ - aḷ = kūrmi. uḷ - uḷi = kūriya veṭṭuk karuvi. 
uḷi - ukir = kūriya viraluṟuppu.
uḷi - uci - ūci = kūrmi (patiṟṟup. 70:7), kūriya kuttuk karuvi allatu tiyaṟkaruvi (piṅ), ciṟumi.
o. nō: iḷi - ici, vāḷi - vāci - vāciki.
ūcik kaṇavāy, ūcikkaḻutti, ūcikkaḷā, ūcikkāy, ūcikkār, ūciccampā, ūcittakari, ūcittūṟal, ūcit toṇṭi, ūcippāli, ūcippuḻu, ūcimalliki, ūci miḷakāy, ūci mulli, ūcivēr eṉpaṉa kūrmi allatu ciṟumi paṟṟit toṉṟu toṭṭup palvēṟu poruḷkaṭku vaḻaṅkivaruñ coṟkaḷām.

eḻuttūci, kuttūci, tuṉṉūci, tiyalūci eṉpaṉavum attaki yaṉavē. eḻuttūci = eḻuttāṇi. miyeḻut tūciyiṉ ... eḻut tiṭṭāḷ (cīvaka. 1767).
mīṉtēr koṭpiṟ paṉikkayam mūḻkic
ciralpeyarn taṉṉa neṭuveḷ ḷūci
neṭuvaci paranta vaṭuvāḻ mārpiṉ  (patiṟṟup. 42: 2-4)
eṉpatāl, puṇtikkum ūciyum paṇṭit tamiḻakat tiruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
kollat teruvil ūci viṟpatā? eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
matiṟṟaliyik kippaṟṟum pakivar kiyip potukkum ūcip poṟikaḷum, akkālattuk kōṭṭi matilmēl vikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
aiyavit tulāmuṅ kipeya rūciyum  (cilap. 15: 213).
vaṭamoḻiyil cūci eṉṉum coṟku mūlamilli. tiyalik kuṟikkum civ eṉṉum colli mūlamākakkāṭṭa virumpuvar.
iṅṅaṉamiruntum, cūci eṉpatiṉiṉṟu ūci eṉpatu tirintuḷḷa tāka, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka tamiḻ, akara mutaliyiṟ kāṭṭiyiruppatu ettuṇi neñcaḻuttam!  (va.va.96-97).
ūṭṭi
caṅkiṉāl kuḻantikaḷukkup pāl ūṭṭuvatāl ataṟku ūṭṭi eṉṟu peyar. makkaḷiṉ kaḻuttil muṉpuṟattuḷḷa caṅku pōṉṟa puṭippum ūṭṭi eṉappaṭum. caṅku eṉavumpaṭum  (col. 13.)
ūtā maṇi viḷiyāṭṭu
pala piḷḷikaḷuḷ mūvar kikōttuc cāṭ pūṭ tiri eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu mūṉṟutaram ki acittut tiri eṉṟu coṉṉavuṭaṉ kiyi yuruvik kimēṟki vippar. mēṟki, akaṅki mēṉōkkiyiruntāl veḷḷi eṉṟum, puṟaṅki mēṉōkkiyiruntāl karuppu eṉṟum, collappaṭum.  iv viraṇṭil evvakiyilēṉum, taṉiyāka vitta piḷḷi vilakiviṭa vēṇṭum. piṉpu, ēṉiyiruvarum mutaliṟ cērāta vēṟoru piḷḷiyuṭaṉ kikōttu, muṉpōṉṟē oru piḷḷiyip pirikka vēṇṭum. mūvarum orē vakiyāyk ki vittiruntāl, mīṇṭuṅ kōkkavēṇṭum iṅṅaṉam mummūva rāyk kikōttu ovvoruttiyip pirittapiṉ, iṟutiyil iruvar iruppar. avviruvaruṭaṉ, mutaṉmutaṟ pirinta piḷḷi oppukku eṉṟu collik kikōkkum. muṉpu piriyāta iruvaruḷ yārēṉum orutti taṉivakiyiṟ kivippiṉ, avaḷ vilakiviṭa, aṭuttavaḷ akappaṭṭukkoḷvāḷ.
akappaṭṭukkoṇṭavaḷ caṟṟut toliviṟpōy niṟka, avaḷukku muṉṉamē kāṭṭappaṭṭa oru maṇimāli allatu vēṟōr aṇi, oru piḷḷiyiṭam oḷittu vikkappaṭum. tolivil niṟpavaḷ, varalāmā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, varalām eṉṟu coṉṉapiṉ vantu, maṇimāli vittirukkiṟavaḷ eṉṟu oru piḷḷiyi ūkippāyc cuṭṭikkāṭṭum. illātavaḷ eḻuntu niṟpāḷ. aiyattiṟkiṭamāyiruppiṉ, avaḷ maṭi mutaliya vaṟṟip piṭittup pārkkalām. orumuṟi kaṇṭupiṭikkat tavaṟi ṉavaḷ maṟumuṟiyuṅ kaṇṭupiṭittal vēṇṭum. maṇimāli vittiruppavaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittu viṭiṉ, maṟṟellāp piḷḷikaḷuñ cērntu, ūtāmaṇiyik kaṇṭupiṭittāl ki taṭṭuvōm eṉṟu pāṭik kitaṭṭi makiḻvar. itu teruvilum ūrppoṭṭalilum viḷiyāṭap peṟum.

ūtu eṉṉum olik kuṟippuc col
ū - ūtu.
māntaṉ moḻi iyaṟki moḻi (Natural Language), ceyaṟki moḻi (Artificial Language) eṉa irunilippaṭum. cuṭṭuc coṟkaṭku muntiyatu iyaṟki moḻi; pintiyatu ceyaṟki moḻi. ivi muṟiyē muḻittal moḻi (Inarticulate Speech), iḻittal moḻi (Articulate Speech) eṉavum paṭum.
iyaṟki moḻiyic cērnta eḻuvaki yolikaḷuḷ, oppoli (Imitative) eṉpatu oṉṟākum. aḵtu aḵṟiṇi yoliyum uyartiṇi yoliyum eṉa iruvaki.
e-ṭu: 
aḵṟiṇiyoli :	kū, kūvu (coo), kari (cry, crow), ūḷ -ūḷi 				(howl), piḷiṟu (blare), im - imir (hum).
uyartiṇiyoli :	cappu (sup, sip), tuppu (spit), mukku, vikku 					(hiccup), ūm (hum), ām (haw), mūcu, ūtu.
uyartiṇiyolikaḷ, vāyoli, mūkkoli, mūccuk kāṟṟoli eṉa mūvakiyām. capputalum tupputalum vāyoliyuṭaṉum, mukkutalum ūṅkoṭṭutalum mūkkoliyuṭaṉum, mūcutalum ūtutalum mūccuk kāṟṟoliyuṭaṉum nikaḻum.
mūccuk kāṟṟum mūkkuvaḻi varuvatum vāyvaḻi varuvatum eṉa iruvakittu. mūcutal mūkkuvaḻik kāṟṟālum, ūtutal vāyvaḻik kāṟṟālum nikaḻum. mūcutalāvatu, ceviyuṟumāṟu kāṟṟi veḷiviṭṭum uḷḷiḻuttum urakka mūccu viṭutal. mūcumūceṉṟu iḷikkiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. 
mūcu - mūccu. o.nō: pēcu - pēccu.
ūtutalāvatu, nerupperittalum viḷakkaṇittalum cūṭāṟṟu talum tukaḷip pōkkutalum mutaliya ceyalkaṭku, vāyvaḻik kāṟṟi veḷiviṭutal.
kāṟṟu veḷivarum ōci, perumpālum cakara voliyālum, ciṟupāṉmi cakarattiṟku orupuṭiyiṉamāṉa takara voliyālum, kuṟikkappaṭum. mūcu eṉṉum collum, kāṟpantiṟkuk kāṟṟaṭip patik kuṟikkum pucukku eṉṉum olik kuṟippum, cakaratti yuṭiyaṉa. Gas eṉṉum āṅkilac collilum cakaramiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. vāṉ elmāntu (Van Helmo#nt) eṉpavar gas eṉṉum colli, khos (chos) eṉṉum kirēkkac colli ṉiṉṟu tirittār eṉpatu, attuṇip porutta muṭittaṉṟu. kāṟṟiṉ toḻilik kuṟikkum vīcu, viciṟu, vicukku (whisk) eṉṉum coṟkaḷum cakarattik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
nerupperittaṟkuk kāṟṟi ūtumpōtu, ūkārattiyē olittaṟkēṟṟa itaḻ kuvivum, cakara takaram pōṉṟa uracoli kaḷiyē (fricatives) olittaṟkēṟṟa vāyniliyum, amin tiruttalāl, anniliyil vāyvaḻik kāṟṟi veḷiviṭuñ ceyali, ūcu, ūtu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē kuṟikka muṭiyum. rakaram uruḷoliyātalāl (trill) attuṇip poruttamāṉa taṉṟu.
kāṟṟi veḷiviṭumpōtum uḷḷiḻukkumpōtum amiyum vāynili, ēṟattāḻa oṉṟē. ataṉāl veyilil alintavaṉ iḷippāṟa uṭkārumpōtu vāyvaḻik kāṟṟi veḷi viṭutaliyum, uṟippāṉa uṇavi yuṇpavaṉ vāyvaḻik kāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttaliyum, ūcu eṉṉum orē col kuṟikkalāyiṟṟu.
ūceṉṟu uṭkāra nēraṅ kiṭiyātu, ūcu ūceṉṟu avvaḷavu cōṟṟiyum uṇṭuviṭṭāṉ, eṉṉum vaḻakku kaḷi nōkkuka.
nerupperittal pōṉṟa ceyaliṟ kāṟṟi veḷiviṭutali, ūtu eṉṉuñ col kuṟittatu. cakara takaram oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu pōliyāka varum.

uṭalūtāripaṭ ṭoḻi  (tiruppu. 904)
ūtāri eṉṉuñ colli, utāra eṉṉum vaṭacolli ṉiṉṟu tirintatākac ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akara mutali kāṭṭiyiruppatu, uṇmikku māṟāṉatum muṟṟum porutta maṟṟatumām. utāra eṉṉum vaṭacol īkit taṉmiyik kuṟippatākum, ūtāri eṉṉum teṉ collō aḻippāḷiyik kuṟippatākum. aḻippāḷi vīṇcelavu ceypavaṉēyaṉṟi aḷippāḷiyallaṉ. tamiḻukku immiyum utavātu piṟamoḻi vaḷarccikku ērāḷamāy vārik koṭukkum tamiḻa aracaṉi allatu celvaṉi, ūtāriyeṉiṉ, atu mikap poruttamē.

ūti - Skt. vata (vāta.)
ūcu, ūtu eṉṉum coṟkaḷiluḷḷa cakara takaraṅkaḷ vāyvaḻik kāṟṟup paṟitali uṇarttutalāl, avi āriya moḻikaḷilum vaṭa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa c, t eṉpaṉa pōl veṭi(pput) takippolikaḷ (Plosive Stops) ākāveṉṟum, iraṭṭitta viṭattē aṅṅaṉamākumeṉṟum, aṟintukoḷka.
tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya toṉmūtiyaṉ moḻiyātaliṉ, annilamirunta iṭatti yoṭṭiya teṉṉāṭṭut teṉkōṭi yil vaḻaṅkum tamiḻolikaḷi, tamiḻ vāyilākattāṉ aṟiya  muṭiyumē yaṉṟip piṟa vaṭanilat tirimoḻikaḷ vāyilāy aṟiya muṭiyāteṉṟum, aṅṅaṉam aṟiya muyalvār kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kuruṭarpōla iṭarppaṭuvāreṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
 hoot, v.i. & t., & n, l. Make loud sounds ... (of steam whistle or motor car or driver) Sound (intr) ... ME huten, imit.
“hooter, n. In vbl senses, esp. siren, steam whistle, esp. as signal for work to begin or cease, [ER]”
eṉṟu erutantuṟi naṭappu āṅkilac ciṟṟakaramutali  (The Concise Oxford Dictionary of Current English) kūṟuvatāl, ūtu eṉṉuñ colloṭu hoot eṉṉum coṟku orumaruṅku toṭarpirukka lāmō eṉa aiyuṟa iṭamēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.
ūtu - pūtu - pūttu. pūttup pūtteṉṟu aṭuppūtik koṇ ṭirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
ūtu - ōtu. ōtutal = 1. kātiṟkuḷ ūtuvatu pōl, camaya kuravaṉ arumaṟi mantirattic caminta māṇavaṉukkuc collutal.
2. marumac ceytiyi oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉ kātiṟkuḷ mellac collutal.
ūti (vātam) mutaliyaṉa
mūccum pēccum uṭporuḷ iṭamāṟṟamum veḷiyēṟṟamum taṉittum piṟatātukkaḷōṭu kūṭiyum nikaḻttuvatu ūtiyiṉ toḻilkaḷ. uṇṭataṉ cerimāṉattiṟku utavuvatu pittanīr, taci nārkaḷiṉ maḻamaḻappāṉa iyakkattiṟku uyavuney pōṟ payaṉpaṭuvatu kōḻi. ai. allatu aiyam eṉpatum kōḻik koru peyar.  (ti.ma. 941)
ūr
ūr - va. rōh (i.vē.)
ur - uṟu - uṟi = uyarat toṅkiviṭum tūkku.
uṟu - uṟi = uyaram (piṅ.)
ur - ūr. ūrtal = 1. ēṟutal. ūrpiḻipu ... vantaṉṟu (aiṅ. 101). 2.ēṟiccellutal.
civiki poṟuttāṉō ṭūrntāṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 37)
vaṭamoḻiyār kāṭṭum ruh (to ascend) eṉṉum mūlam ūr eṉpatiṉ muṟimāṟṟut tiripē (metathesis)..
ūr - ūrttam. o. nō: nēr - nērttam. ūrttam - ūrtva (urdhva) uyarnta, tūkkiya (i.vē.).  (va.va. 98)
ūrppeyar
ūrppeyarkaḷum ūrppeyar īṟukaḷum vevvēṟu kāraṇam paṟṟiyavi:
āṟi eṉpatu āṟṟūr;
puttūr eṉpatu putiya ūr;
mūtūr eṉpatu paḻiya ūr;
pērūr eṉpatu mānakar.
paṭṭi eṉpatu kālnaṭit toḻuvamuḷḷa ciṟṟūr.
paṟṟu eṉpatu taṉippaṭṭavarkku allatu oru cārārkku uriya ciṟṟūr allatu ciṟṟūrt tokuti.
aṭaṅkāppaṟṟu eṉpatu aracaṉ āṇikku aṭaṅkātavar vacikkum ūr.
paḷḷi eṉpatu putta camaṇa maṭamuḷḷa ūr.
pāḷiyam eṉpatu paṭiyirukkum ūr. 
paṭṭu eṉpatu pāḷiyat talivarāṉa ciṟṟūr aracarkku viṭappaṭṭa ciṟṟūr allatu ciṟṟūrt tokuti.  (col)
ūrppeyar
maṅkalam eṉpatu pārppaṉar irukkum ūr.
vāṭi eṉpatu vēṭṭuvar allatu iṭiyar irukkum ūr.
paṇṭāra vāṭi eṉpatu kuṭikaḷukkuriya ūr.
nattam eṉpatu pārppaṉar allātār vāḻum ūr.
kuṭi eṉpatu oru kuṭumpattārē allatu kulattārē vacikkum ūr.
kuṭikkāṭu eṉpatu kuṭikaḷ vacikkum kāṭṭūr.
kuṭiyēṟṟam eṉpatu makkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya ūr. (kuṭiyēṟṟam eṉpatu iṉṟu kuṭiyāttam eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkum).
kallāṅkuttu eṉpatu kaṭiṉanilattūr
murampu eṉpatu kaṟpāṅkāṉa mēṭṭu nilattūr
pēṭṭi eṉpatu canti kūṭum ūr.
eyil eṉpatu matil cūḻnta ūr.
puram puri eṉpaṉa aracar talinakar
viṇṇakaram eṉpatu tirumālkōyil uḷḷa ūr  (viṇṭu + nakaram = viṇṇakaram; viṇṭu - viṣṇu. nakar, nakaram - maṉi, kōyil) ēri, kuḷam, kōṭṭi, kōyil mutaliya īṟukaḷil muṭiyum pērkaḷik koṇṭa ūrkaḷ, avvav vīṟukaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭum iṭattik koṇṭavi eṉpati evarum collāmalē aṟintu koḷḷalām.
ēri eṉṟu muṭiyum pērik koṇṭa ōr ūril ēri illā viṭiṉ atu oru kālattil iruntu piṉṉart tūrntu pōyiṟ ṟeṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
puram paṭṭu mutaliya cilapeyarkaḷum ikkālattil ciṟappup poruḷ iḻantu potup peyarāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl ikkālap puttūrp peyarkaḷ varalāṟṟuc ceyti yoṉṟum uṇarttā.  (col. 29).
ūruṇi
ūruṇpatu ūruṇi, itu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku  (ti.ma. 131).
ūḻ
paḻam piṟappukaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa iruviṉippayaṉ ceyta vaṉiyē ceyta muṟippaṭi ceṉṟaṭiyum iyaṟki yoḻuṅku. itu muṟippaṭi varuvatāl muṟi eṉṟum ūḻ eṉṟum, avaravarkkuriya iṉpa tuṉpap pakutikaḷi vakuppatāl pāl eṉṟum, vakuttāṉ eṉṟum, teyva ēṟpāṭu pōliruppatāl teyvam eṉṟum, pālvari teyvam eṉṟum peyar peṟum. iṉi māṟā iyalpāy iruppatāl iyaṟki eṉṟum peyar peṟuvatām.  (ti.ma. 214).
ūṟu
uṟuvatu ūṟu. uṟutalāvatu uṭampiṉ uḷḷum puṟampum paṭutal allatu toṭutal.  (ti.ma. 51).
ūṟu eṉpatu nērcci. uṟuvatu ūṟu; paṭuvatu pāṭu eṉpatu pōla.  (ti. ma. 945).
ekara okara iyaṟki
tolkāppiyaṉār tamatu nūlil, akkālattil vaḻaṅki vanta eḻuttukaḷ evvitam varivaṭivil eḻutappaṭṭu vantaṉaveṉ patik kuṟippiṭavilli : āyiṉum cila eḻuttukaḷ puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟpati māttiram kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. av veḻuttukaḷ kuṟṟiya lukaram, kuṟṟiyalikaram, makarakkuṟukkam, āytam, meyyeḻuttu, ekaram, okaram eṉpaṉa.
uyireḻuttukaḷuḷ a,i,u,e,o ākiya aintum tamakku iṉamāṉa neṭilkaḷi uṭiyaṉa. a,i,u ākiya mūṉṟukkum iṉamākiya neṭilit terivikka ak kuṟil uruvattukku ōr atikappaṭiyāṉa kuṟi cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl ekaram okaram ākiya iraṇṭiṉ vakiyil kuṟilik kuṟikkumpoḻutu atikappaṭiyāṉa puḷḷi cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṟiliṉ nīṭciyē neṭil. ātaliṉ neṭiliṉ vēṟupāṭu viḷaṅkak kuṟiluruvattukku atikappaṭiyāṉa kuṟiyiṭṭu neṭili uṇarttuvatē iyaṟki. ekara okaraṅkaḷ antap potuvitikku māṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āciriyarum a,i,u ākiyavaṟṟukkuriya neṭiluruvattik kuṟippiṭāmal, iv viraṇṭi maṭṭum ciṟappāka eṭuttuk kūṟukiṟār.
ātaliṉ, ekara okaraṅkaḷukkuk kuṟil illāmal neṭilē vaḻaṅkum oruvakiyiṉiṉṟum ivveḻuttukaḷiṉ uruvaṅ kaḷip peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟē nām eṇṇavēṇṭi yirukkiṟatu. vaṭamoḻiyil ekara okaraṅkaḷ neṭiliyē kuṟikkum. avaṟṟiṟku vaṭanāṭṭār iṭṭa kuṟikaḷi avvāṟē tamiḻilum neṭilukku mēṟkoṇṭu, an nāṭṭārāl vaḻaṅkappaṭāta kuṟili yuṇartta vēṟu kuṟikaḷi amikka vēṇṭi vantatu pōlum. puḷḷiyiṉāl māttirik kuṟivi aṟivittalip piṟa puḷḷiyiṭṭa eḻuttukaḷāl aṟiyalām. atip pōlavē ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉ tiṟattilum neṭili ṉiṉṟum kuṟinta kuṟili uṇarttap puḷḷiyiṭṭaṉar eṉṟē koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu tiru. ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉār tam paṇṭit tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷ eṉṉum cuvaṭiyil (pak. 86-7) varintuḷḷār.
ilakkaṇa nūlār eḻuttukaḷiṉ varivaṭivukaḷik kuṟippatē yaṉṟi, avaṟṟi varaṇikkum vaḻakkam eṅkumilli; avaṟṟi varaṇikkavum muṭiyātu. ṭakara pakaram pōṉṟa iraṇṭoru nērkōṭṭu varivaṭivukaḷi maṭṭum ōraḷavu vaṇṇikkalām. āyiṉ, ataṉāṟ peṟum payaṉilli. eḻuttukaḷi eḻututalē, avaṟṟiṉ varivaṭivik kāṭṭutalaṉṟi vēṟākātu.
puḷḷiyeṉpatu eḻuttukaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭṭuk kuṟiyēyaṉṟi uṇmiyāṉa varivaṭivākātu. iti yuṇarātu, perumpulavarum ciṟanta uriyāciriyarumāṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, makara vaṭivip paṟṟi vaḻuppaṭa vurittuviṭṭār.
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē  (tol. eḻuttu. 14)
makara varivaṭiviṉ aṭippuḷḷiṭṭa puḷḷi, makarak kuṟukkattiṉ vaṭivām eṉpatē itaṉ poruḷ.
makarak kuṟukka vaṭivu. teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa vaṇṇamālikaḷil iraṇṭām ṭakarattiṟkuḷḷa varivaṭivi orupuṭi yottatu. tamiḻ makara vaṭivu iṉṟu pōṉṟē aṉṟum iruntatu. mēṟ kuṟitta nūṟpā makarak kuṟukka varivaṭivu paṟṟiyatēyaṉṟi, makara varivaṭivu paṟṟiyataṉṟu. atip piṟaḻavuṇarntu, pakara varivaṭivuḷ ḷiṭṭa puḷḷiyē makara varivaṭivam eṉṟu urittatoṭu. ati varintum kāṭṭiviṭṭār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. avar uri varumāṟu :
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē  (nūṟpā)
itu pakarattōṭu makarattiṭi varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceykiṉṟatu.
makaram atikārappaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār.
itaṉ poruḷ : uṭpeṟupuḷḷi - puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟ peṟum puḷḷi, uruvākum - makarattiṟku vaṭivām eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyāvatu mēṟcūttirattāṉ meykaṭkuk kūṟum puḷḷi. īṇṭu uruveṉṟatu kāṭcip poruḷi uṇartti niṉṟatu.
utāraṇam; kappi kappi (kammi) eṉa varum iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟal.
iṉi, uyirmey vaṭivu paṟṟiya puḷḷi yillā ........... āṟē (17) eṉṉum nūṟpāvuriyilum.
arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyi ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyi vaḷitteḻutiṉār eṉṟu tam vaḻuvik kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭiviṭṭār. uruvutirin tuyirttal eṉpatu uyirmey vaṭivukaṭkuriyatē yaṉṟi, makara vaṭiviṟ kuriyataṉṟu.
meyyiṉ aḷavē ariyeṉa moḻipa  (eḻuttu 11)
avviyal niliyum ēṉi mūṉṟē  (eḻuttu 12)
ariyaḷavu kuṟukal makaram uṭittē
iciyiṭaṉ arukum teriyuṅ kāli  (eḻuttu 13)
eṉṟu muṉvarum nūṟpākkaḷiyum.
meyyiṉ iyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal  (eḻuttu 15)
ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē  (eḻuttu 16)
eṉap piṉvarum nūṟpākkaḷiyum, puḷḷipeṟutalaṉṟi ōreḻuttiṟkum tolkāppiyar vaṭivu kūṟāmiyiyum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar nōkkiyilar.
mēlum, makaravaṭiviṟku akattum puṟattum puḷḷiyiṭuvatu ēṭṭeḻuttiṟku iṭarppāṭāṉa teṉpatiyum, pakara vuṭ puḷḷiyi vaḷitteḻutum pakuttaṟivu kūṭap paṇṭit tamiḻaṟiñarkku illātiruntirukkātu eṉpatiyum avar karutiyilar.
ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉārum makarak kuṟukka vaṭivu kūṟum nūṟpā veṉṟu kaṇṭapōtu, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kāṇātu pōṉatu āṉi yaṭiccaṟukkalē.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vaḻuvuri. tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ ēṟkeṉavē paṉmuṟi vaṭivu māṟiṉa eṉṟu kūṟum eḻuttumāṟṟakkārarukku, oru pōlic cāṉṟākavum vāyttuviṭṭatu.
makarak kuṟukkam paṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpāvum ataṟku iḷampūraṇar uritta vaḻuvuriyum varumāṟu:
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvā kummē  (tol. eḻuttu 14)
itu. pakarattiṉ makarattiṭi varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceytal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) uḷpeṟu puḷḷi uruvu ākum - puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟ peṟum puḷḷi makarattiṟku vaṭivām. (aḵtiṉmi pakarattiṟku vaṭivām).
e-ṭu: p, (p) eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka. kappi, kappi (kampi) eṉavarum.
ivvuriyik kaṇṭē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum mayaṅkiṉār pōlum! iṉṟuḷḷapaṭiyē makara vaṭivam muṉpum iruntatu. makaraṅ kuṟukum pōtē uṭpuḷḷi peṟum. e-ṭu: m.
makarak kuṟi vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷēyum oru puḷḷiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
kīḻiyāriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta kālam tōrā. ki.mu. 2000. avarkkaṉṟu ilakkiyamum illi; eḻuttumilli; tamiḻarō ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ talikkaḻakak kālattilēyē muttamiḻilakkiya vilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭavar. mēlum, uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvakiyeḻuttuk koṇṭa neṭuṅkaṇakku mutaṉmutal tamiḻilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. iṉi, camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiya vētamoḻi yuṭpaṭa āriya moḻikaṭkellām tamiḻ muntiyatum mūlamumāṉa moḻiyeṉṟu, ataṉ muccuṭṭeḻut tukaḷē muḻucceviṭaṉukkuṅ kēṭkumāṟu muḻaṅkip paṟiyaṟi kiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, kiranta eḻuttum tēvanākariyum tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē tirintuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu, ūṉṟi nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākāmaṟ pōkātu. inniliyil, tamiḻ ēkāra ōkāra eḻuttukaḷi vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭateṉpatu, pāṭṭaṉ tirumaṇattip pēraṉē naṭatti vittāṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟatē.
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē am moḻikkuriya eḻuttukaḷ amikkappaṭum. ayaṉmoḻiyiluḷḷa ciṟap peḻuttuc coṟkaḷ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭiṉum, ac coṟkaḷē yaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappeḻuttukaḷ mutaṟkaṇ vaḻakkup peṟā. ēkāra ōkāra vaṭivukaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu vantaṉaveṉiṉ. av vīreḻuttukaḷiyum, uyirākavō uyirmeyk kūṭṭilō, mutalākavō iṭiyākavō kaṭiyākavō koṇṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil illātiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
ē, ēkkam, ēṅku, ēcu, ēṭāci, ēṭu, ēṇi, ēṇi, ētu (viṉā), ēntu,  ēppam, ēmā, ēmāḷi, ēy, ēr, ērāḷam, ēri, ēl, ēla, ēlam, ēvu, ēḻu, ēḻi, ēṟpāṭu, ēṟṟam, ēṟṟu, ēṟu, ēṉ mutaliya ēkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum.
ō, ōkkāḷam, ōṭu (peyar), ōṭu (viṉi), ōṭi, ōṅku, ōci, ōṭṭam, ōṭṭu, ōṭṭi, ōṭṭi, ōṭam, ōṇāṉ, ōtam, ōtu, ōmal, ōytal, ōrpaṭiyāḷ, ōram, ōri, ōli, ōviyam mutaliya ōkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum.
av vīruyirum meyyōṭukūṭi mūviṭattum varum nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷum, kallā makkaḷum vaḻaṅkum aṭippaṭi yulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷāyiruppati nōkkumpōtu, ti.nā.cuppiramaṇiyaṉāriṉ aṟiyāmi ettuṇi aḷavaṟṟa teṉṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
mutaṟkālattil māntaṉ vāyil mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya neṭi luyirkaḷ piṉṉark kuṟilkaḷākak kuṟukiya piṉpē moḻi vaḷarcciyaṭintiruppatāl, ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷi iruvaki yilum mutalilum mūviṭattuṅ koṇṭa iruvaki vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, āyirakkaṇakkākap perukiyuḷḷaṉa. ēkāra ōkāra neṭiṟcoṟkaḷiṉum, ekara okarak kuṟiṟcoṟkaḷ ēṟattāḻa irumaṭaṅku perukiyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
ā ī ū eṉṉum neṭilkaḷiyum a i u eṉṉum avaṟṟiṉ kuṟilkaḷiyum amikkat terinta tamiḻarkku ē ō eṉṉum neṭilkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ e o eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷiyum amikkat teriyavilli yeṉpatu. muṉ piṟanta mummakaviyum vaḷartta peṟṟōrkku, piṉpiṟanta irumakavi vaḷarkkat teriyātu pōyiṟṟeṉpatu pōṉṟatē.
tolkāppiya mutalatikāra mutaliyalākiya nūṉmarapiluḷḷa 33 kuṟu nūṟpāviṟkuḷḷēyē. ekara ēkāra okara ōkārac coṟkaḷ eṅṅaṉam iyalpāka vantuḷḷaṉa eṉpatip piṉvarum nūṟpākkaḷālum aṭikaḷālum uṇarntu koḷḷalām.
